[\[1b\]]
[\[1b.1\]{D4156}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_bud+d+ha tsa ri ta ma hA kA bya nA ma/_bod skad du/_sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[1b.2\]]thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/dpal ldan yon tan tshogs kyis gang /_/bdag nyid chen po sems can gyi/_/don mdzad yang dag sangs rgyas dang /_/chos dang dge 'dun la phyag 
[\[1b.3\]]'tshal/_/bu ram shing par mthu mnyam bu ram shing pa'i rgyud/_/thub dka' shAkya rnams la spyod pa rnam dag pa/_/'phrog byed zla ba lta bur skye dgu rnams la sdug_/rgyal po zas gtsang 
[\[1b.4\]]zhes bya'i ming can byung bar gyur/_/dbang po dang mtshungs de yi bdag po ldan ma yin/_/nus mthu rjes mtshungs gzi brjid lha mo bde sogs dang /_/pad+mo can 'dra phun sum tshogs pa sa 
[\[1b.5\]]bzhin brtan/_/sgyu ma chen mo zhes bya dpe med sgyu 'dra byung /_/mi skyong rgyal po 'di ni de dang thabs cig tu/_/dga' ba nyer ldan rnam thos sras kyi dpal bzhin rol/_/de nas yang dag ting 
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]'dzin ldan pa'i rig ma bzhin/_/sdig bral ma de mngal ni rnam par gzung bar gyur/_/mngal dang nye bar ldan pa nyid kyi sngon rol du/_/de ni gnyid song glang po'i dbang po dkar po zhig_/rang nyid la 
[\[2a.2\]]ni rab tu zhugs sogs mthong gyur la/_/de yi mtshan ma nyam thag pa nas thob ma yin/_/lha dang rab tu mtshungs pa de yi lha mo de/_/mngal gyis rigs kyi dpal ni rab tu 'dren byed cing /_/sgyu ma ngal ba dang ni mya ngan 
[\[2a.3\]]sgyu med par/_/gtsang la nyon mongs med pa'i dgon par blo gros mdzad/_/de ni lum bi zhes bya'i nags mthar gyur pa'i sa/_/sna tshogs ljon shing sna tshogs shing rta mngon dga' bar/_/bsam gtan la bzod dben 
[\[2a.4\]]pa'i nags kyi mtha' bzhed ma/_/'gro ba'i [(]chad [,]ched [ )]dang gnas phyir mi skyong la zhus so/_/de yi chos dang ldan la 'phags pa'i zhe 'dod dag_/rnam par shes nas ya mtshan dga' ba yis gang ste/_/zhi ba'i grong nas sa 
[\[2a.5\]]yi bdag po gshegs gyur te/_/de yi dga' ba'i rgyu yin rnam par rgyu phyir min/_/dpal dang ldan pa'i nags der rgyal po'i bdag ldan ma/_/rab tu bltam pa'i dus ni yang dag mngon gzigs shing /_/bla re nye bar 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]phul ba'i gzims mal rab thob ste/_/mi mo stong phrag dag gis mngon par dga' bar byed/_/de nas skar ma rgyal rab ldan par gyur pa'i tshe/_/brtul zhugs legs par 'dus byas lha mo de yi ni/_/rtsib logs dag nas 'jig rten phan phyir sras bltams te/_/tshor 
[\[2b.2\]]ba dang ni bral zhing nad dang bral ba nyid/_/ji ltar brla skyes brla nas rgya chen lag pa nas/_/dbang po dang mtshungs nga la nu ni spyi bo nas/_/mtshan ldan nyid kyang lag pa'i cha shas gsang ba nas/_/rnam pa de ltar de yi skye ba byung bar gyur/_/mngal nas mngon par nges par byung ba gzugs mdzes te/_/nam 
[\[2b.3\]]mkha' dag las 'phros 'dra skye gnas las ma skyes/_/bskal pa gcig min rnams su bsgoms pa'i bdag nyid ni/_/gang zhig yang dag rab tu shes na skyes gyur blun po min/_/nyi ma gzhon nu sa lam mngon par babs pa bzhin/_/gsal ba dang ni brtan pas gang zhig mdzes pa ste/_/de ltar 
[\[2b.4\]]shin tu gsal yang nges par rtog pa na/_/ri bong gis mtshan ji ltar mig dag 'phrogs par gyur/_/de ni rang gi lus kyi 'od rab 'bar ba yis/_/sgron ma'i 'od kyang gsal bar byed pa ltar bcom ste/_/rin thang chen po 'dzam bu chu gser mdzes pa'i mdog_/phyogs rnams thams cad rnam 
[\[2b.5\]]par snang zhing gsal bar mdzad/_/'khrugs pa ma yin yon po'i skyon dag rab bsal zhing /_/shin tu brtan pa dang ldan rnam par yangs pa'i 'gros/_/de ltar mi g.yo rab tu brtan pa'i gom pa bdun/_/drang srong skar ma bdun dang rab mnyam gshegs par gyur/_/byang chub phyir dang 'gro la phan 
[\[2b.6\]]pa'i phyir skyes shing /_/'di ni bdag gi srid pa'i skye ba tha ma zhes/_/seng ge'i 'gros 'dra phyogs bzhi dag tu rnam gzigs nas/_/snod ldan rnams kyi don byed dag kyang smras par gyur/_/nam mkha' dag las rab babs zla ba'i 'od zer dkar/_/grang dang dro ba'i chu rgyun rnam pa gnyis kyi 
[\[2b.7\]]shugs/_/lus la yang dag reg pas bde ba bskyed pa'i phyir/_/mdzes shing yid 'ong de yi spyi bor babs par gyur/_/dpal ldan bla re bres par gser gyis 'bar zhing mtshan/_/bai DUr+ya yi rkang pa'i gzims mal la gzims pa/_/de la gus phyir gser gyi pad+ma'i phyag mnga' ba'i/_/
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]gnod sbyin bdag rnams kun nas yongs su bskor nas gnas/_/mi mngon gyur pa'i lha rnams kyis kyang nam mkha' la/_/gang gi mthu las mgo bo yis ni rab btud cing /_/kun nas gdung ba skyob pa'i gdugs dkar rnams bzung ste/_/byang chub ched du mchog 
[\[3a.2\]]gi bsngags pa dag kyang smras/_/'das pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la lhag par mchod byas pa'i/_/lto 'phye che rnams khyad 'phags chos la sred pa'i phyir/_/gang la rlung g.yab kyis g.yab gus pa'i mig gzir cing /_/man dA ra ba'i me tog rnams kyis yang dag 'thor/_/de bzhin gshegs 
[\[3a.3\]]pa bltams pa'i yon tan gyis tshim pa'i/_/gtsang mar lhag par gnas pa'i rnam dag sems can rnams/_/sdug bsngal nyid du bying ba'i 'gro la phan pa'i phyir/_/lha rnams 'dod chags bral ba na yang dga' bar gyur/_/gang gis rab tu bltams tshe ri rgyal phur bu nyid/_/rlung gis 
[\[3a.4\]]bsnun pa'i gru ltar sa gzhi g.yos gyur zhing /_/tsan+dan dang bcas ut+pal pad+ma'i snying po yi/_/char pa dag ni nam mkha'i sprin dang bral las babs/_/yid 'ong reg na bde ba'i rlung dag byung gyur te/_/lha yi na bza' dag ni mngon par 'bebs byed cing /_/nyi ma de nyid lhag par mngon par 
[\[3a.5\]]gsal ba ste/_/zhi ba'i 'od zer 'dus ma byas pa'i me 'bar ro/_/shar dang byang gi mtshams kyi khang pa'i phyogs gcig na/_/bsil ba'i chu yi khron pa rang byung gyur pa ste/_/pho brang btsun mo'i 'khor rnams ya mtshan gyur rnams kyis/_/gang la bya ba rnams ni stegs bzhin rab tu byas/_/
[\[3a.6\]]chos dang ldan pa'i lha dang 'byung po'i tshogs rnams kyis/_/de ni lta ba'i don du nags tshal rab gang ste/_/rmad byung ngo mtshar gyur pa nyid kyi shing las kyang /_/me tog rnams ni dus ma yin par nges par brul/_/'tshe bar byed pa'i sems can rnams kyang de yi tshe/_/phan tshun dag tu phyin te gnod pa mi 
[\[3a.7\]]byed la/_/'tsho ba'i 'jig rten dag na nad gang ji snyed pa/_/de dag rnams kyang 'bad pa med par de tshe bcom/_/mkha' 'gro'i bya ni mtho min sgra sgrogs [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]kyang /_/chu klung dag ni chu yang zhi bar bab gyur la/_/phyogs rnams rab snang dge ba'i nam mkha' rab mdzes shing /_/lha yi rnga rnams mkha' 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]la rab tu grags par gyur/_/rgud par gyur la mgon ni nye bar thob nas bzhin/_/'jig rten dag kyang mchog tu rab zhi thob gyur la/_/'jig rten rnams kyi thar pa'i bla ma rab bltams tshe/_/'dod pa'i lha nyid kho na dga' ba med par gyur/_/shin tu ngo mtshar sras kyi skye ba mthong gyur nas/_/mi bdag brtan pa 
[\[3b.2\]]yin yang rnam par 'gyur nas song /_/rab dga' skyes pa nyid dang yid mi bde skyes te/_/brtse ba las ni mchi ma rnam pa gnyis byung ngo /_/mi ma yin pa'i sras po nyid kyi nus pa dang /_/ma yi rang bzhin kyang ni stobs chung nyid kyi phyir/_/grang dang dro ba'i chu dag 'dres pa'i chu klung bzhin/_/lha mo 'jigs pa 
[\[3b.3\]]dang ni dga' bas gang bar 'gyur/_/lhag ma spangs te 'jigs pa nyid ni shes byas nas/_/yid 'ong 'dod pa'i ched du rtogs pa ma yin pa'i/_/bud med rgan mo rnams kyis bde legs gyur byas shing /_/gtsang mar byas te lha rnams dag la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de yi mtshan nyid dag kyang shes shing 
[\[3b.4\]]thos gyur nas/_/spyod dang thos dang tshig la grags thob bram ze rnams/_/rnam rgyas zhal ni ya mtshan dga' ba yis gang ste/_/dga' dang 'jigs par gyur pa'i mi yi lha la smras/_/mi dag sa la bu ma gtogs pa'i khyad 'phags pa/_/gzhan gang cung zad 'dod min zhi ba de yi phyir/_/khyod 
[\[3b.5\]]kyi sgron ma 'di ni rigs kyi sgron ma ste/_/khyod nyid dga' bar mdzod cig dga' ston da ltar bgyi/_/de phyir brtan par rnam dpyod med par dgar mdzod dang /_/rigs ni nges pa nyid du mchog tu 'phel bar 'gyur/_/'di ni khyod kyi bu zhes skyes par gyur pa ste/_/'jig rten sdug bsngal dag 
[\[3b.6\]]gis mngon par bcom pa'i mgon/_/mchog gyur 'di la mchog gyur gser gyi 'od mnga' zhing /_/sgron ma'i 'od zer dag gis mtshan rnams gang gyur pas/_/de nyid rtogs par gyur pa'i thub par 'gyur ba'am/_/yang na mi bdag sa stengs 'khor los sgyur bar 'gyur/_/sa yi bdag po nyid la 
[\[3b.7\]]gal te dga' 'gyur na/_/stobs dang chos dag gis ni de'i tshe sa stengs su/_/rgyal po kun gyi thog tu 'di ni gnas 'gyur te/_/skar ma'i 'od rnams la ni nyi ma'i 'od bzhin no/_/de ni gal te thar bzhed nags su gshegs gyur na/_/mkhyen dang de nyid kyis ni de tshe sa stengs su/_/lugs kun 
[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]zil gyis mnan nas 'di ni gnas 'gyur te/_/mtho ba kun la ri rab ri yi rgyal po bzhin/_/ji ltar lcags rnams la ni gser dag mchog yin la/_/ri rnams la ni ri rab chu bo rnams la mtsho/_/gza' rnams la ni zla ba gdung byed rnams la nyi/_/de ltar khyod kyi 
[\[4a.2\]]sras ni rkang gnyis rnams kyi mchog_/gang gi spyan dag mi 'dzums mig gzir rnam par spangs pa ste/_/brtan zhing shin tu ring la dkar min rdzi ma dang /_/gzi brjid ldan pa yin yang 'on kyang zhi ba nyid/_/'di ni kun nas gzigs pa'i spyan mnga' ci la min/_/de nas gnyis skyes 
[\[4a.3\]]rnams la mi bdag gis smras pa/_/bdag nyid chen po mi skyong snga mas ma mthong ba/_/mthong zhes khyad 'di gang yin 'di la rgyu mtshan ci/_/de nas bram ze rnams kyis de nyid la smras pa/_/blo dang thos pa las dang grags pa rnams la ni/_/mi skyong sngon dang phyi ma nyid ni tshad ma yin/_/dngos po'i 
[\[4a.4\]]lugs la 'dir ni bya ba rgyu yin la/_/'dir yang bdag gi dper brjod dag ni mkhyen par mdzod/_/gang zhig rgyal po'i bstan bcos shes ldan ngan spong dag_/rig byed drang srong de gnyis kyis ni ma byas pa/_/de dag gi sras zhi ba pa wa sangs dang ni/_/phur bu yis ni dus kyis de nyid bskyed par 
[\[4a.5\]]gyur/_/nyams gyur snga ma rnams kyis ma mthong rig byed gang /_/dbyangs can ma yi bu yis kyang ni slar smras pa/_/nus med gnas 'jog gis ni ma byas gyur pa gang /_/rgyas pa yis ni de ltar rnam pa mang por brjod/_/'pho byed drang srong chen pos ma bsdebs gyur pa gang /_/tshig gi rkang pa 
[\[4a.6\]]grog mkhar gyis ni dang por bskyed/_/gso ba gang yang rgyun shes kyis ni ma mthong ba/_/phyi nas de ni drang srong rgyun shes bu yis byas/_/gang yang gnyis skyes nyid ni kun [(]shas [,]shes [)]ma rnyed pa/_/de ni gting dpogs bu yis rgyal po la thob bo/_/bu ram shing pas dang por ma bsdams gyur pa gang /_/
[\[4a.7\]]rgya mtsho la ni dug bcas kyis kyang mtshams bzung ngo /_/slob dpon nyid du bram ze'i rnal 'byor cho ga la/_/gzhan rnams kyis ma thob pa skyed byed gshegs par gyur/_/khyab 'jug gi ni grags pa'i las rnams gang yin pa/_/de rnams la ni dpa' bo la sogs stobs med gyur/_/de phyir lang tsho dang ni 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]rigs dag tshad ma yin/_/la la la lar mchog nyid nyer thob 'jig rten na/_/rgyal dang drang srong rnams kyis snga mas ma byas pa/_/de dang de dag rnams ni bu rnams dag gis byas/_/de ltar gnyis skyes de rnams kyis ni yid ches te/_/mi skyong dbugs 'byin dang yang mngon par dga' gyur la/_/
[\[4b.2\]]mi 'dod the tshom dag ni yid las rnam bstsal te/_/rab tu dga' ba nyid la lhag par 'dzegs par gyur/_/dga' ba yis ni gnyis skyes dam pa de rnams la/_/bkur sti sngon du 'gro ba'i nor rnams rab phul te/_/ji skad smras bzhin 'di ni sa yi bdag por gyur/_/rgas pa thos nas nags rnams 
[\[4b.3\]]su ni 'gro bar shog_/de nas mtshan ma rnams dang dka' thub stobs las ni/_/skye ba mthar mdzad bltams pa de ni rtogs gyur nas/_/dam pa'i chos la gus pa'i shAkya'i dbang phyug gi_/gnas su dkar min drang srong chen por gshegs par gyur/_/tshangs pa'i dpal nyid kyis kyang dka' thub dpal 
[\[4b.4\]]gyis ni/_/'bar ba'i tshang rig rnams kyi tshang rig de la ni/_/rgyal po'i bla mas gus pa dang ni bkur sti'i phyir/_/mi dbang pho brang du ni rab tu spyan drangs so/_/de ni sa bdag btsun mo'i 'khor gyi nye logs su/_/gzhon nu bltams pa la ni dga' ba'i shugs kyis 'ongs/_/dka' thub mchog las dang 
[\[4b.5\]]ni rgas pa la brten nas/_/brtan pa nags kyi 'du shes kyis ltar rnam par zhugs/_/de nas thub pa gdan la gnas pa de la mi skyong gis/_/zhabs bsil mchod yon sngon 'gro yang dag rab mchod nas/_/sngon tshe gnas mchog la ni mtha' can lhas bzhin des/_/ji ltar nye bar spyod la snyan gsan 
[\[4b.6\]]phab pa'o/_/bdag gi rigs ni rjes su bzung phyir nor ldan des/_/gang zhig bdag la gzigs phyir bcom ldan nyer gshegs la/_/bka' drin gnang bar mdzod cig zhi ba ci bgyi bgyi/_/kho bo slob ma lags so thugs rjes mdzad par rigs/_/de ltar mi skyong gis ni dngos po thams cad kyis/_/
[\[4b.7\]]nye bar gsol btab gyur pa'i thub pa ji lta bzhin/_/rab rgyas yangs pa'i spyan ni ya mtshan dang bcas pas/_/zab ba dang ni brtan pa'i tshig rnams smras pa'o/_/chos 'dod gtong ba dang ldan 'gron la sdug cing phangs/_/bdag nyid chen po khyed la 'thad pa 'di yin la/_/sems 
[\[5a\]]
[\[5a.1\]]pa rjes 'gro ye shes lang tsho rjes mthun pa/_/khyod kyi blo gros gnyen gang de ltar bdag la yin/_/de dang de ni gang gis mi skyong drang srong des/_/phra mo'i chos kyi nor rnams rab tu thob byas nas/_/rtag par cho ga bzhin du mchod sbyin byas gyur pa/_/dka' thub rnams 
[\[5a.2\]]kyis phyug cing nor rnams dag gis dbul/_/bdag gi nye bar 'ongs la dgos pa gang yin pa/_/bdag gi de ni gson mdzod dga' bar nyer song khyed/_/byang chub rtogs pa'i ched du khyod kyi sras bltams zhes/_/lha yi ngag dag bdag gi nyi ma'i lam du thos/_/ngag de thos nas dang ni 
[\[5a.3\]]yid la bsgoms nas dang /_/mtshan ma rnams kyis shes nas de nas nye bar 'ongs/_/brgya byin gyi ni rgyal mtshan yang dag bsgreng ba bzhin/_/shAkya'i rigs kyi rgyal mtshan blta bar 'dod pa yis/_/zhes pa'i tshig 'di de ltar thos par gyur nas ni/_/mi dbang rab tu dga' bas kun nas g.yos 
[\[5a.4\]]gyur te/_/yum gyi pang du son las gzhon nu blangs nas ni/_/dka' thub nor can la ni yang dag bstan par gyur/_/de nas drang srong che des zhabs la 'khor los mtshan/_/phyag dang zhabs kyi sor mo dra bas 'brel ba dang /_/glang po bzhin du mdoms nub smin ma mdzod spur bcas/_/
[\[5a.5\]]ya mtshan dang bcas rgyal po'i sras ni mthong bar gyur/_/lha mo'i pang na zhugs pa me yi bu bzhin du/_/ma ma'i pang du bzhugs pa mngon par mthong nas ni/_/byung gyur rdzi ma'i mtha' ru mchi ma rnams byung ste/_/dbugs med gyur nas gyen du lta la zhal phyogs gyur/_/mig dag mchi 
[\[5a.6\]]mas yongs gang dkar min mthong nas ni/_/bu la brtse ba'i phyir ni mi skyong 'dar gyur te/_/mchi ma dang ni gre ba kha zhing ldab ldib bcas/_/thal mo sbyar zhing yan lag btud de dris par gyur/_/gang gi lus ni lha las bar na cung zad cig_/rang gi skye ba ngo mtshar mang po'i sgron ma ste/_/gang 
[\[5a.7\]]gi don ni mchog tu 'gyur bar smra ba ste/_/rab tu mthong nas brtan pa gang phyir mchi ma 'ong /_/bcom ldan gzhon nu brtan pa'i tshe can yin snyam mam/_/bdag gi mya ngan ched du skyes pa min nam ci/_/bdag la chu yi snyim pa ci zhig ltar rnyed de/_/'di ni dus kyis 'thung phyir nye bar 'ongs ma yin/_/
[\[5b\]]
[\[5b.1\]]bdag gi snyan grags gter ni mi zad yin snyam mam/_/nges par bdag gi rigs kyi lag nor yin snyam ci/_/bde bar pha rol du ni rab tu 'gro snyam mam/_/gnyid par gyur kyang bu ni phye ba'i mig gcig yod/_/bdag gi rigs kyi myu gu skyes pa ma rgyas par/_/yongs su skams pa'i skal ba 
[\[5b.2\]]can zhig min snyam mam/_/khyab bdag myur bar gsungs shig bdag la zhi ba med/_/gnyen 'dun rnams kyis bu la brtse ba khyed kyis rig_/de ltar mi 'dod blo yis 'khrul ba byung gyur pa/_/rig nas mi dbang rgyal la thub pa des smras pa/_/bdag gis smras pa gang de the tshom 
[\[5b.3\]]med pa de/_/mi skyong khyed kyi blo gros gzhan gang ma gyur cig_/'di yi gzhan gyur mtshon min bdag gi rnam 'gyur te/_/'on kyang rang la slu bar rab mtshon rnam par 'khrugs/_/skye ba zad pa'i rnyed par bla min rtogs pa po/_/'di skyes bdag gi 'gro ba la ni dus yin no/_/rgyal srid rnam 
[\[5b.4\]]par spangs nas yul rnams la mi gnas/_/'bad pa drag po rnams kyis de nyid thob byas nas/_/'di ni 'gro la gti mug mun pa rnam 'joms phyir/_/ye shes rang bzhin nyi ma rab tu 'bar bar 'gyur/_/sdug bsngal rgya mtsho nad rnams bkram pa'i dbu ba can/_/rga ba'i dba' 
[\[5b.5\]]rlabs 'chi ba'i shugs ni drag po las/_/'gro ba nyam thag bying bar gyur rnams 'di yis ni/_/ye shes gru bo chen po yis ni sgrol bar byed/_/shes rab chu yi shugs la brten pa'i tshul khrims ngogs/_/ting nge 'dzin ni bsil ba'i brtul zhugs ngang pa 'phyo/_/'di yi mchog gi chos 
[\[5b.6\]]kyi chu bos rab bskor la/_/sred pas nyam thag gson pa'i 'jig rten 'thung bar byed/_/sdug bsngal gyis nyam thag dang yul gyis bsgribs rnams dang /_/'khor ba'i 'brog dgon 'jigs par gnas pa rnams la ni/_/lam rab chud zos rnams la lam 'gro rnams bzhin du/_/
[\[5b.7\]]'dis ni rnam par dag pa'i lam dag 'chad par 'gyur/_/yul gyi bud shing can gyi 'dod chags me yis ni/_/'jig rten skye bo rnam par bsregs par gyur pa la/_/sos ka'i tha mar sprin chen gyis ni char bzhin du/_/'di yi chos kyi char gyis rab tu sim byed 'gyur/_/sred pa'i sgo 
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]gtan ma rig mun pa'i sgo glegs can/_/sgo la skye dgu rnams kyi 'thon pa'i rgyu las ni/_/'di yis mchog rab kun nas rnyed par dka' ba yi/_/dam pa'i chos kyi sgras ni rnam par 'byed par 'gyur/_/rang gi las kyi zhags pa rnams kyis yongs dkris shing /_/
[\[6a.2\]]sdug bsngal dag gis mngon par zil non rten med pa'i/_/'jig rten rnams kyi rdzogs pa'i byang chub brnyes nas kyang /_/chos kyi rgyal po 'dis ni 'ching ba mthar mdzad 'gyur/_/rmongs sam 'dod pa'i bde ba yis sam rgyags las rung /_/'di yi mthar thug chos gang thos par mi 
[\[6a.3\]]byed pa/_/mi yi 'jig rten 'dir de mya ngan bya ba'i gnas/_/de phyir khyed nyid 'di la mya ngan ma byed cig_/yon tan rgya mtsho 'di las 'khrul gyur bdag gi ni/_/bsam gtan rnams ni thob kyang don byas med nyid de/_/de yi dam chos ma thos pa la kho bo ni/_/mtho ris dag na 
[\[6a.4\]]gnas kyang phun tshogs med pa snyam/_/de ltar don thos bshes dang bcas shing btsun mor bcas/_/yid mi bde ba dor nas mi dbang dgar gyur la/_/rnam pa de ltar bdag gi bu ni 'di'o zhes/_/des ni bdag nyid la ni snying po ldan par bsams/_/'phags pa'i chos rnams kyis 
[\[6a.5\]]kyang 'gro bar 'gyur ro zhes/_/bsam pa'i rnam par gzung bya snying la byas gyur te/_/nges par 'di ni mi sdug chos kyi phyogs min la/_/rigs rgyud nyams pa las ni 'on kyang 'jigs pa mthong /_/de nas bu la rnam par 'khrugs pa'i rgyal po la/_/bu yi nges pa'i de nyid rig par byas 
[\[6a.6\]]nas su/_/gus bcas gyen du gzugs mthong dkar min thub pa ni/_/rlung gi lam gyis ji ltar 'ongs par gshegs par gyur/_/blo gros byas pa'i sring mo'i bu yang mthong nas ni/_/thub pa'i gsung mnyan pa dang de yi lugs la ni/_/rnam mang rjes su brtse ba yis ni legs pa des/_/
[\[6a.7\]]sdug pa'i bu bzhin du ni sbyor ba byed du bcug_/sras po skyes pas tshim pa'i mi yi bdag po yang /_/yul la song ba'i 'ching ba rnams ni rnam spangs te/_/sras kyi yid 'ong sras po bltams pa'i las dag ni/_/rigs dang 'dra bar ji lta ba bzhin byed du bcug_/zhag bcu yongs su 'das par 
[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]gyur pa nyid kyi tshe/_/mchog gi dga' ba rab ldan snyoms par gyur pa'i yid/_/sras las mchog tu 'gyur phyir lha yi mchod pa ni/_/bzlas brjod dang ni sbyin sreg bkra shis la sogs byas/_/gzhan yang brgya dang stong phrag yongs su gang ba'i grangs/_/brtan dang stobs ldan bu rnams 
[\[6b.2\]]gser gyi rwar bcas shing /_/rgas pa nye bar 'ongs min swa ma ldan pa'i ba/_/rang gi sras 'phel phyir na gnyis skyes rnams la byin/_/de nas snyoms pa'i bdag nyid kyis ni rnam mang yul/_/rang sems tshim par byed pa'i bya ba rnams byas nas/_/yon tan ldan zhing dge ba'i yud tsam nges pa na/_/
[\[6b.3\]]grong khyer rab tu 'jug la dga' bas blo gros mdzad/_/de nas mche gnyis mche ba las byung rin thang che/_/dkar po'i me tog gis spras nor bu'i sgron/_/khri la dge ba'i phyir ni lha mo sras ldan ma/_/lha rnams la ni rab tu btud nas bsnyen par gyur/_/de nas gnas brtan skye mo 
[\[6b.4\]]rjes 'brang sras mgon ma/_/bdag po ldan ma grong la mdun du rab zhugs nas/_/mchod ldan lha na lha rnams kyis ni brgya byin bzhin/_/mi bdag nyid kyang grong ba'i tshogs kyis gshegs par gyur/_/de nas gdong drug skyes pas yid ches dbang phyug bzhin/_/shAkya'i rgyal pos grong 
[\[6b.5\]]khyer rnam par khyab mdzad de/_/zhal ni dga' bas rab gang 'di dang 'di zhes pa'i/_/srid 'phel rgyas pa dang ni grags dang snyan pa mdzad/_/de nas mi yi bdag po'i sras bltams cher skyes pas/_/ser skya zhes pa'i grong khyer ljongs dang bcas pa de/_/gnod sbyin grong na phun tshogs 'dod 
[\[6b.6\]]ba skyes pa na/_/lha yi bu mo rnams kyis bkram bzhin dga' bar gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_gzhon nu sku bltams pa'i le'u ste dang po'o//_//skye dang rga ba mthong song bdag skyes bdag pham de'i/_/skyes pa nas 
[\[6b.7\]]brtsams mi nyid nyi mas rgyal po de/_/chu yi shugs rnams kyis ni chu bo sin d+hu bzhin/_/nor dang glang po rta dang bshes rnams 'phel bar gyur/_/nor rnams dang ni rin po che dang bzang po'i gser/_/de dang de ni byas dang ma byas pa nyid kyang /_/yid la re ba yi rang bzhin du khur gyur pa/_/
[\[7a\]]
[\[7a.1\]]de la gcig tu phan pa'i cha shas gter rnyed gyur/_/gang zhig pad+ma dang mtshungs gnyis 'thung rnams kyis kyang /_/dkyil 'khor 'di la nye bar 'ong bar mi nus pa'i/_/gangs dang ldan pa'i glang chen chang gis dregs de rnams/_/'bad pa las kyang gzhan du de la nye 
[\[7a.2\]]bar gnas/_/sna tshogs mtshan mas mtshan zhing gsar ba'i gser sga dang /_/de bzhin gzhan rnams dag gis brgyan byas rngog ma 'phyang /_/stobs dang byams pa dang ni nor gyis thob pa yi/_/'di yi mgyogs 'gro rnams kyis grong khyer phye mar byas/_/de bzhin 'di yi yul 'khor 
[\[7a.3\]]du ni tshim dang rgyas/_/legs la rdul ldan yon tan ldan pa 'o mar ldan/_/rab mchog be'u rnams dang bcas pa'i ba rnams ni/_/'o ma mang po 'jo ba mang po byung bar gyur/_/'di yi dgra bo dbus gnas nyid du song gyur pa/_/dbus gnas gyur pa mdza' bshes nyid du rab tu song /_/
[\[7a.4\]]khyad par du ni bshes gnyen brtan por song ba ste/_/'di yi phyogs dag gnyis te dgra yang yod ma yin/_/de bzhin du ni 'di yi 'jam rlung 'brug gi sgra/_/glog gi phreng ba 'khyil ba yis ni brgyan pa'i sprin/_/rdo char dang ni thog lhung nyes pa rnams kyis gzhan/_/yul du dus la 
[\[7a.5\]]lha yis char pa phab par gyur/_/zhing gi las kyis ngal dub byed pa nyung ngus kyang /_/'bru ni 'bras bu ldan pa dus sbyor bzhin skyes te/_/de rnams nyid kyang 'di yi sman du byung gyur la/_/bcud dang snying po yis kyang mngon par lhags pa nyid/_/g.yul du rab tu zhugs la kun du 'joms 
[\[7a.6\]]pa bzhin/_/lus la the tshom za bar byed pa'i dus na yang /_/rang bzhin gnas shing bde ba nyid du nad med par/_/dus kyi dbang gis mi mo rnams ni rab tu btsas/_/brtul zhugs ldan rnams ma gtogs nor rdzas dman na yang /_/mi rnams pha rol rnams las don du gnyer mi byed/_/
[\[7a.7\]]mngon par don du gnyer la 'phags pa nor 'phra yang /_/de tshe 'ga' zhig dag ni rgyab kyis phyogs ma gyur/_/de tshe rgyal po de yi rgyal srid la ni dga'/_/gnyen la zhe sa med min mi ster ba yang med/_/brtul zhugs med pa nyid min [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]med 'tshe med pa/_/sgra mi bsgrags kyi sras po 
[\[7b\]]
[\[7b.1\]]lta bu byung bar gyur/_/der yang skyed mos tshal dang lha gnas dka' thub gnas/_/khron pa rdzing dang dge bcu 'du ba'i gnas rnams kyi/_/bya ba rnams ni mtho ris bzhin du nyer thob nas/_/chos 'dod rnams kyis mngon sum du ni rab tu mdzad/_/mu ge dang ni 'jigs dang nad rnams las 
[\[7b.2\]]grol zhing /_/dgar gyur skye bo mtho ris gzhan du mngon par dga'/_/bdag mos bdag po la 'am mo yis bdag por rung /_/phan tshun log par mngon par spyod pa dag kyang med/_/dga' ba'i ched du 'dod la 'ga' zhig gis ma bsnyen/_/'dod pa'i don du nor ni 'ga' zhig 
[\[7b.3\]]gis ma bsrungs/_/nor gyi don du chos la 'ga' zhig gis ma spyad/_/chos kyi don du 'tshe ba 'ga' zhig gis ma byas/_/rkun po sogs dang dgra yang nyams par rang gnas med/_/pha rol mya ngan med pa ji lta ba nyid bzhin/_/rang gi 'khor lo pha rol 'khor lo las grol te/_/rgyal po 
[\[7b.4\]]de la bde dang lo legs byung bar gyur/_/de yi tshe na rgyal po de yi rgyal srid la/_/de skyes pa na nyi ma'i sras po'i yid shes bzhin/_/dga' bar spyad par gyur la sdig pa rab nyams shing /_/chos rnams 'bar zhing nyon mongs rnams ni zhi bar gyur/_/gang phyir rnam pa de ltar don kun 
[\[7b.5\]]grub pa dang /_/rgyal po'i rigs kyi phun sum tshogs pa byung gyur te/_/de phyir mi skyong gis ni sras po de yi mtshan/_/don kun grub pa zhes bya 'di ni rab tu mdzad/_/lha mo sgyu ma yang ni lha yi drang srong mtshungs/_/bu yi nus mthu rnam par spangs pa mthong nas ni/_/bltams la rab tu 
[\[7b.6\]]dga' mas bzod par ma nus te/_/de nas gnas pa'i ched du ltar ni gshegs par gyur/_/de nas lha yi mngal dang mtshungs pa'i gzhon nu la/_/byams pa'i rang bzhin gyis kyang khyad par med pa yi/_/ma yi nu mo mdangs mnyam pa yi rang bzhin la/_/yang dag skyed btsud 
[\[7b.7\]]bdag las skyes pa lta bur gyur/_/de nas nyi ma gzhon nu 'char rir gnas 'dra dang /_/rlung gis rab tu bus par gyur pa'i me bzhin dang /_/skar ma'i bdag po mun min phyogs la gnas pa ltar/_/gzhon nu de ni rim gyis yang dag 'phel bar gyur/_/de nas rin thang chen po'i tsan+dan 
[\[8a\]]
[\[8a.1\]]rnams dang ni/_/rin chen phreng ba sman gyi snying por bcas rnams dang /_/ri dwags rab tu sbyar ba'i gser gyi shing rta rnams/_/'di yi ched du gshegs kyi khyim rnams dag nas blangs/_/lang tsho nyid dang rjes su mthun pa'i rgyan rnams dang /_/gser gyi rang bzhin 
[\[8a.2\]{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]glang chen rta rnams dang /_/gser dang dngul gyis bris pa'i mi chung gzugs rnams dang /_/shing rta ba glang byung dus yang dag sbyar rnams blangs/_/de ni lang tsho rjes mthun yul la nye spyod pa/_/de rnams kyis ni yul la nyer spyad la/_/dpa' dang brtan pa yis dang blo dang 
[\[8a.3\]]dpal gyis kyang /_/byis pa yin yang byis min rab mtshungs byung bar gyur/_/lang tsho gzhon nu shin tu 'das nas yang dag par/_/'du byed las ni rab tu thob nas dus su ni/_/lo mang dag gis rtogs bya nyin zhag nyung ngu yis/_/rang dang rjes su mthun pa'i rigs pa 
[\[8a.4\]]rnams ni blangs/_/drang srong chen po dkar min las ni sngon rol du/_/de yi nyes legs don gyi skal ba thob nas ni/_/nags su gshegs par 'gyur zhes shAkya'i rgyal po yis/_/'dod pa rnams la chags pa skyed du bcug par gyur/_/brtan pa'i tshul khrims spyod pa can gyi 
[\[8a.5\]]rigs de la/_/ngo tsha dang ni dul ba nyer ldan legs ldan ma/_/grags 'dzin ma zhes grags pa rnam par yangs pa ma/_/btsun mor mngon par brjod pa'i dpal mo 'di la blangs/_/kun 'gyed gzhon nu dang ni rab tu mtshungs pa yis/_/mchog gi lus kyis snang zhing 
[\[8a.6\]]gsal ba de nyid ni/_/shAkya'i rgyal po'i mna' ma de dang thabs cig tu/_/bde sogs ma dang mig stong ldan bzhin mngon par dga'/_/yid kyang dpyod par byed la cung zhig rab log pa/_/gang gis mthong mi 'gyur zhes rjes su bsams byas nas/_/sa la rab tu spyod 
[\[8a.7\]]min lding khang lto nyid du/_/gnas dag mi skyong gis ni de la rnam par bstan/_/de nas gzhal med nam mkhar sa la spyod pa bzhin/_/ston kha'i dus kyi chu gter sprin ni dkar po yi/_/dus sbyor kun du bde ba'i gzhi yi lding khang la/_/bud med rnams kyi sil snyan rgya chen rnams kyis rnam 
[\[8b\]]
[\[8b.1\]]par gshegs/_/gser gyis gdams pa'i rnga shing can gyi rdza rnga'i skad/_/mi mo'i lag pa'i rtse mos mngon par brdungs pa yi/_/lha mchog ma yi gar dang mnyam pa'i gar rnams kyis/_/ti se'i gangs ltar de yi pho brang mdzes par gyur/_/ngag dang gzo dang rol dang rnam 
[\[8b.2\]]'gyur rnams kyis kyang /_/rgyags dang rtse dang dgod pa dngar mo rnams kyis dang /_/smin ma bskyod pa yis dang phyed mig lta rnams kyis/_/de la der ni mi mo rnams ni dga' bar gyur/_/de nas de ni 'dod pa bsten par mkhas pa yi/_/dga' bas dregs pa'i bud med rnams 
[\[8b.3\]]kyis bzung gyur la/_/gzhal med khang ni rgyab nas bsod nams las can bzhin/_/gzhal med khang ni rgyab nas sa la ma gshegs so/_/mi skyong gis kyang de nyid bskyed pa'i ched las ni/_/de yi 'byung bar 'gyur ba'i don gyis bskul gyur te/_/sdig pa las ni dga' bral zhi la 
[\[8b.4\]]mngon dga' zhing /_/legs la skal bar bgos shing dul la bsnyen par gyur/_/brtan min bzhin du 'dod pa'i bde la ma zhen la/_/ma rungs skyed ma la ni yang dag chags ma gyur/_/brtan pas dbang po'i rta ni stobs las rnam thul zhing /_/gnyen dang grong khyer pa rnams yon tan rnams kyis 
[\[8b.5\]]rgyal/_/gzhan gyi sdug bsngal phyir ni rig pa ma brjod la/_/ye shes dge ba gang yang de la lhag par brjod/_/des ni rang gi skye dgu rnams la ji lta bar/_/de ltar skye dgu rnams la dge ba'i bsngags pa mdzad/_/skar ma rgyal snang shes ldan sras po lhag pa'i lha/_/ji lta bar ni de yi tshe ring phyir mchod des/_/chung min me la 
[\[8b.6\]]bsreg bya rnams ni bsregs gyur la/_/gnyis skyes rnams la gser dang ba lang rnams kyang byin/_/'gram stegs chab rnams kyis dang yon tan chab rnams kyis/_/gtsang sbra'i ched du lus dang yid la khrus byas te/_/rig byed du ni nye bar bstan pa'i chang rnams dang /_/zhi ba bdag 
[\[8b.7\]]skyes snying la zhi ba'i bde la 'thung /_/gang zhig don dang mi ldan zhi ba ma smras la/_/gang zhig dga' ba dang bral de nyid ma brjod de/_/zhi ba de nyid med dang rtsub pa'i de nyid phyir/_/ngo tsha ba yis bdag nyid kho na brjod ma nus/_/don ldan 'dod pa rnams dang mi 'dod pa rnams la/_/chags 
[\[9a\]]
[\[9a.1\]]pa'i skyon la brten nas rab tu 'thob min te/_/dge ba'i tha snyad gtsang ma la ni bsnyen gyur la/_/mchod sbyin la yang ma mos de ltar ji lta bzhin/_/sred pa dang ldan mngon par 'ongs pa de ma thag_/sbyin bya chu 'dra rnams kyis skom pa bcad gyur 
[\[9a.2\]]la/_/g.yul las ma gtogs ye shes kyi ni sta ri yis/_/dgra bo rnams kyi nga rgyal chen po bcom par gyur/_/gcig ni rnam par dul la des ni mdun bsrungs shing /_/bdun po nyid ni don la lnga po dag ni bsrungs/_/sde tshan gsum po thob la sde tshan gsum rtogs 
[\[9a.3\]]shing /_/sde tshan gnyis po shes la sde tshan gnyis po spangs/_/nyes pa byas kyang rab tu bskyangs nas gzhom bya la/_/'tshe bar mi byed khro bas kyang ni gzigs ma yin/_/de rnams dag la 'bras bu zhi bas bcings gyur la/_/de rnams dag ni 'dor ba tshul ma yin phyir mthong /_/
[\[9a.4\]]drang srong rnams kyis mchog gi brtul zhugs spyad gyur la/_/yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]yang dag bzung ba'i sha khon zhugs rnams dor/_/yon tan dri ldan snyan dngags rnams ni thob gyur la/_/dri ma can du byed pa'i rdul rnams rnam par dor/_/rab tu mi gnas gtor ma'i dpya ni ma blangs 
[\[9a.5\]]la/_/pha rol dngos po rnams la mngon par rlom ma byas/_/dgra rnams la ni chos ma yin pa ma smras la/_/khro ba rnams ni snying gis gzung ba ma yin no/_/zhi ba'i bdag nyid sems ni rnam par rab dang la/_/rnal 'byor rab tu ldan pa'i dbang po rnams ci ltar/_/der ni 
[\[9a.6\]]de ltar sa bdag rab tu sgyur ba can/_/'bangs dang grong khyer ba rnams de lta nyid du byas/_/de nas mdzes pa'i 'o ma 'dzin pa dang ldan ma/_/grags 'dzin ma ni rang gi grags pa 'dzin ma la/_/zas gtsang sras po'i sras po sgra gcan sgra yi zhal/_/sgra gcan 'dzin zhes bya ba 
[\[9a.7\]]dus su skyes par gyur/_/de nas 'dod pa'i bu mnga' mchog gi yid ches te/_/rigs rgyud 'phel ba rab mtshon sa skyong rgyal po ni/_/ji lta nyid du sras po rab tu skyes la dga'/_/de lta nyid du sras po'i sras po skyes la dga'/_/bdag gi bu yi bur gyur pa la brtse ba ni/_/bdag 
[\[9b\]]
[\[9b.1\]]bzhin gang las yin zhes dga' ba skyes gyur te/_/dus su des ni cho ga de dang de dmigs shing /_/bu la dga' ba mtho ris bzhin du 'dzegs par gyur/_/rgyal po'i khyu mchog grags dang snyan pas mtshan rnams kyis/_/dang po'i skal ba rnams kyi lam la gnas nas ni/_/dkar 
[\[9b.2\]]po ma dor dka' thub rnams la dka' thub dang /_/'tshe ba dor ba'i mchod sbyin rnams kyis mchod par gyur/_/rgyal po'i dpal gyis dang ni dka' thub dpal gyis kyang /_/bsod nams las can de ni de nas 'bar gyur la/_/rigs dang blo dang mdzad spyod kyis kyang gsal ba ste/_/
[\[9b.3\]]gzi brjid stong gi 'od zer bzhin du rab tu 'phro/_/rang byung las byung mchod byed sngags ni mchod byas nas/_/sras gnas ched du brtan pa'i dpal gyis bzlas byas shing /_/dka' thub byed pa'i las rnams byas gyur skye dgu rnams/_/skyed 'dod skye dgu'i bdag bzhin dang po'i dus su'o/_/
[\[9b.4\]]mtshon cha spangs shing bstan bcos rnam par shes gyur la/_/nye bar zhi la bsnyen zhing nges la rnam par bzod/_/dbang ldan bzhin du yul rnams gang la ma bsnyen la/_/yab mes bzhin du yul rnams kun la gzigs par gyur/_/des kyang bu yi ched du rgyal srid bzung gyur la/_/bu ni 
[\[9b.5\]]rigs kyi don du rigs ni grags pa'i phyir/_/grags pa mtho ris ched du mtho ris bdag nyid phyir/_/chos kyi don du bdag nyid gnas la 'dod par 'gyur/_/de ltar des ni dam pa rnams kyis nges thob pa'i/_/thos pa las grub chos ni rnam pa sna tshogs byas/_/gang las bdag 
[\[9b.6\]]gi bus ni bu yi ngo mthong nas/_/nags tshal nyid du 'gro mi 'gyur zhes mgon bslangs so/_/bdag la yang dag blangs pa'i dpal ni srung 'dod pa'i/_/sa skyong rnams kyis sa la bu ni bsrung ba ste/_/chos ni 'dod pa'i ma yi dbang po de yis kyang /_/yul rnams 'dor ba 
[\[9b.7\]]med par bu ni chos las bsrungs/_/byang chub sems dpa' yul la bde ba'i ro shes thams cad kyang /_/sras skyed pa na dpe med sems pa nags su gshegs gyur te/_/de phyir nye bar bsags pa'i las ni rtsa nas skyed pa'i phyir/_/de ni zhi ba ma thob de srid dga' ba bsnyen par gyur/_/
[\[10a\]]
[\[10a.1\]]sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_btsun mo'i 'khor na gnas pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o//_//de nas re zhig dus na 'jam po'i rtswa rnams dang /_/khu byug pho ni gyen du skad 'don rkang 'thung rnams/_/glu 
[\[10a.2\]]rnams dang ni nges ldan skyed mos tshal rnams ni/_/pad+ma'i 'byung gnas kyis brgyan des ni thos par gyur/_/de nas bud med skye bo rnams ni dga' ba yi/_/grong khyer skyed mos tshal gyi yid 'ong dngos thos te/_/khyim gyi nang du mngon par bkag pa'i glu bzhin du/_/phyi rol rab 
[\[10a.3\]]tu 'gro ba'i ched du blo mdzad do/_/de nas sras su mngon brjod yid 'ong de yi ni/_/dgongs pa mi skyong gis ni nges par thos gyur nas/_/brtse ba'i phun sum tshogs pas lang tshor 'ongs pa ru/_/ltad mo dag la rnam par gshegs par bka' stsal to/_/gzhon nu shin tu gzhon nu'i 
[\[10a.4\]]sems dang ldan pa ni/_/yang dag skyo ba'i sems su ma gyur zhes sems zhing /_/nyam thag tha dad skye bo kun nas 'dus pa ni/_/rgyal po'i lam du phyir la rnam par zlog pa ste/_/nying lag dman pa rnams dang dbang po ma tshang rnams/_/'khogs pa nad pa la sogs bkren rnams phyogs rnams 
[\[10a.5\]]su/_/de nas mchog tu zhi bas yang dag rab ston nas/_/rgyal po'i lam rnams dag ni mchog tu mdzes par byas/_/de nas dpal ldan rgyal po'i lam ni byas pa na/_/dpal ldan rnam par dul ba rjes 'brangs gzhon nu ni/_/gzhal yas khang pa'i logs nas dus su mngon babs nas/_/
[\[10a.6\]]mngon par rjes gnang byas pa mi skyong la mngon gshegs/_/de nas mi dbang bu la mchi ma byung gyur te/_/mgo la nye bar bsnams te yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]rnam gzigs nas/_/song cig ces ni tshig gi bka' dag stsal gyur la/_/brtse ba dag las de ni yid kyis ma btang ngo /_/de nas de 
[\[10a.7\]]ni 'dzam bu chu gser sga 'dzin pa'i/_/legs par bslabs pa'i mgyogs 'gro bzhi yis sbyar ba yi/_/dbang po ma nyams mkhas shing gtsang la 'od zer 'chang /_/gser gyi rang bzhin shing rta la ni yang dag 'dzegs/_/de nas 'bras spos me tog 'bar ba rab bkram zhing /_/phreng ba 
[\[10b\]]
[\[10b.1\]]rnam dpyangs rab tu g.yo ba'i ba dan can/_/zla ba skar ma dang bcas bar snang la bzhin du/_/'dra ba rjes 'brang lam ni rab tu thob par gyur/_/ya mtshan gyur las rab tu rgyas pa'i mig rnams ni/_/ut+pal sngon po'i phyed 'dra rnams kyis bkram pa ni/_/rgyal po'i lam la dal bu 
[\[10b.2\]]dal bus gshegs gyur te/_/grong khyer skye bo rnams kyis kun nas mngon par dga'/_/kha cig de la zhi ba'i yon tan gyis bstod la/_/de bzhin gzhan rnams gsal ba nyid kyis phyag 'tshal zhing /_/kha cig dag ni 'di la mngon du phyogs nas kyang /_/dpal dang tshe yi rnam par rgyas la 
[\[10b.3\]]shis pa brjod/_/zhar ba sgur po nad pa mi'u thung rnams kyi sa/_/tshogs pa rnams ni rigs chen rnams la nges dral nas/_/mi mo rnams ni nges par zhugs gnas phra rnams las/_/lha yi rjes 'gro rgyal mtshan bzhin du rab tu btud/_/de nas gzhon nu nges par gshegs zhes 
[\[10b.4\]]gtam dag ni/_/bsgo bya skyes las bud med rnams kyis thob gyur na/_/bkur 'os skye bos mngon par rjes gnang byas pa rnams/_/blta bar 'dod pas lding khang shod du song bar gyur/_/yon tan ska rags brul zhing gegs byas de rnams ni/_/gnyid log rab tu sad pas lta 
[\[10b.5\]]byed 'khrugs pa rnams/_/gnas lugs gtam gyis rnam par brgyan rnams rnam gzhag ste/_/ya mtshan gyur pas dogs med par ni yongs su song /_/gzhal yas khang gi skas kyi 'og gi sgra rab dang /_/dril chung sgra rnams dang ni gdu bu'i sgra rnams kyis/_/rnam par skrag gyur khyim gyi 
[\[10b.6\]]bya yi tshogs rnams ni/_/phan tshun shugs rnams las kyang yang dag kun nas gtor/_/gang zhig 'dir ni yan lag rab mchog ma rnams ni/_/myur ba rab tu skyes gyur tsam tsam skyes rnams kyis/_/lo ma 'dzin rnams rgyas shing skyed smad shing rta ni/_/yangs pa rnams kyis lci ba nyid phyir 
[\[10b.7\]]'gro ba bzung /_/mtha' gcig legs par rab tu sbyar ba'i rgyan rnams ni/_/rnam par sbas pa rnam par gsal bas ngo tsha bas/_/skyen por 'gro bar nus pa yin yang gzhan ma yin/_/'gro ba nges par bzung ste myur min song bar gyur/_/phan tshun rab tu bcer bas ril por gyur pa 
[\[11a\]]
[\[11a.1\]]rnams/_/yang dag drud las bskyod gyur rna lung 'khyil ba can/_/de dag rnams kyi de tshe sgra bcas rgyan rnams ni/_/rlung bgrod skar khung rnams su rab tu zhi bar gyur/_/rlung bgrod skar khung rnams las rnam par 'thon pa rnams/_/phan tshun thang chen gyur pa'i rna 
[\[11a.2\]]lung 'khyil ba rnams/_/bud med rnams kyi 'dam skyes 'dra ba'i 'dam skyes zhal/_/lding khang rnams su bkod pa rnams ni rnam par mdzes/_/de nas khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]lang tsho ma rnams kyis khyab cing /_/ya mtshan la ni rab phye skar khung rnams kyis ni/_/lha mo dang bcas gzhal med 
[\[11a.3\]]rnams kyi nam mkha' bzhin/_/dpal dang ldan pa'i grong khyer kun nas mdzes par gyur/_/skar khung rnams kyis rnam par yangs pa ma yin las/_/phan tshun 'gram par bkod pa'i rna lung can rnams kyis/_/'dam skyes pad+ma rnams kyi tshogs rnams bcings pa bzhin/_/rab 
[\[11a.4\]]myos mchog ma rnams kyi zhal ni mdzes par gyur/_/gzhon nu de la lam du rnam par blta ba yi/_/bud med de rnams sa la 'gro 'dod ltar mdzes shing /_/gyen du gdong phyogs de la rab tu lta ba yi/_/mi rnams gnam la 'gro bar 'dod bzhin mdzes par gyur/_/lus dad dpal 
[\[11a.5\]]gyis mchog tu 'bar bar gyur pa yi/_/rgyal po'i sras de mthong nas bud med de rnams ni/_/nges par dgongs pa gzhan min bsam pa dag pa yis/_/'di yi chung ma skal ba bzang zhes dal bus smra/_/gzugs kyi dngos su me tog tog can dang mtshungs shing /_/rnam par ring zhing 
[\[11a.6\]]rgyas pa'i dpung pa can 'di ni/_/dpal nyid dor nas chos la nye bar gshegs zhes te/_/de la de dag rnams kyis zhe sa kho na byas/_/rnam par dul la gtsang zhing brtan pa'i cha lugs can/_/grong pa rnams kyis de ltar rgyal po'i lam khyab pa/_/de ni sngon du gzhon nus mthong nas 
[\[11a.7\]]dga' bas te/_/bdag gi srid pa gzhan ma cung zhig brtags pa 'dra/_/grong khyer de yang mtho ris bzhin du rab dga' ba/_/mngon par gzigs nas gtsang mar gnas pa'i lha rnams kyis/_/sa gzhi skyong ba'i sras po yang dag bskul don du/_/'khogs pa'i mi rgan rab tu 'gro bar 
[\[11b\]]
[\[11b.1\]]sprul gyur to/_/de nas gzhon nu yis ni rga bas zil non zhing /_/mi rnams las ni tha dad dbyibs can de gzigs nas/_/g.yo ba med cing de nyid du ni zhugs pa'i spyan/_/chags par gyur cing kha lo 'dzin pa la smras so/_/mngon par nyer 'ongs mi de su yin 'dren pa kye/_/skra rnams 
[\[11b.2\]]dkar zhing dbyug pa la ni lag pas brten/_/smin mas kun nas mig g.yogs 'khogs shing yan lag drud/_/rnam 'gyur 'di ni tha mal gang 'dod yin nam ci/_/de skad ces smra shing rta rab tu 'dren pa po/_/lha mchog de rnams nyid kyi blo ni rmongs byas shing /_/don ni bsrung bya 
[\[11b.3\]]yin yang skyon med mthong gyur te/_/de yis sa skyong bdag nyid skyes la nges par gsol/_/gzugs kyi gnod byed stobs kyi rnam par za ba po/_/rgan pa rnams kyi 'joms byed mya ngan rnams kyi sa/_/dran pa rnams kyi nyams byed dbang po rnams kyi dgra/_/'di ni rgas pa zhes bya gang gis 
[\[11b.4\]]'di bcom mo/_/'dis kyang byis pa nyid dang 'o ma 'thung gyur cing /_/dus kyi sa la yongs su gnyid log gyur pa ste/_/rim gyis lus rab ldan pa'i lang tshor gyur nas kyang /_/rim gyis de nyid kho nas rga bar nye bar song /_/de skad ces smras rgyal po'i bdag nyid skyes pa ste/_/cung zhig 'dar 
[\[11b.5\]]la kha lo sgyur la 'di skad smras/_/skyon 'di kho bo la yang 'byung ba min nam ci/_/de bzhin 'di la kha lo sgyur ba yis gsol to/_/'di ni na tshod rab mchog tshe dang ldan pa'i yang /_/gdon mi za bar dus kyi dbang gis 'byung bar 'gyur/_/de bzhin rgas pa gzugs kyi rnam par nyams pa 
[\[11b.6\]]bor/_/'jig rten pa yi shes pa nyid dang mthong ba nyid/_/de nas sngon gyi bsam pa dag pa'i blo mnga' ba/_/rnam rgyas bskal par bsags pa'i bsod nams las can de/_/rdo rje chen po'i sgra la nye bar ba lang bzhin/_/rgas pa thos nas gzhon nu kun nas yi mug gyur/_/dbugs ni ring du 
[\[11b.7\]]brgyus nas rang gi mgo bsgul zhing /_/'khogs pa de la spyan ni rnam par zhugs nas su/_/rga ba dang bcas skye ba de rnams gzigs gyur nas/_/sems ni khong du chud pa de yis tshig 'di smras/_/dran pa dang ni gzugs dang pha rol gnon pa yang /_/de ltar rgas pas rnam dpyod med par 
[\[12a\]]
[\[12a.1\]]'joms pas te/_/'di lta bur ni mngon sum nyid du song na yang /_/'jig rten pa dag skyo med nyer 'gro kho na'o/_/de ltar song la 'dren pa rta rnams phyir zlog la/_/khyod ni khyim nyid du ni skyes par rab tu song /_/rgas pa'i 'jigs pa'i sems su gnas pa kho bo 
[\[12a.2\]]la/_/skyed mos tshal gyi sa la bde ba ga la yod/_/de nas rgyal po'i sras po de yi bka' lung gis/_/kha lo sgyur bas shing rta phyir la bzlog par gyur/_/de nas gzhon nus pho brang de nyid kho nar ni/_/bsam pa'i dbang gis stong pa bzhin du rab tu thob/_/gang gi tshe na de yang bde ba ma yin te/_/
[\[12a.3\]]rgas pa rgas pa zhes ni rab tu yongs su rtog_/de nas mi dbang gis ni rjes gnang de ni slar/_/rim gyis de nyid kho nas phyi rol gshegs par gyur/_/de nas gzhan yang lus la nad dang yongs ldan pa/_/lha mchog de rnams nyid kyis bskyed cing sprul pa'i mi/_/zas gtsang rgyal po'i sras 
[\[12a.4\]]po can der song nyid kyis/_/gzigs nas kha lo sgyur ba de nyid la smras pa/_/gsus pa sbom zhing dbugs dag skyod pa'i lus po can/_/lag pa cha shas zha zhing rid la skya bseng lus/_/a ma zhes ni snying rje'i ngag dag smra ba po/_/gzhan la brten nas gnas pa'i mi de su zhig yin/_/
[\[12a.5\]]de nas kha lo sgyur bas smras pa zhi ba kye/_/'di yi khams ni rab 'khrugs rab stobs rab tu 'phel/_/nad ces bya ba mngon brjod shin tu don med chen po ste/_/nus kyang gang gis de ni rab dbang med par byas/_/rjes su brtse bcas mi de la ni gzigs bzhin du/_/rgyal po'i 
[\[12a.6\]]sras des slar yang 'di skad ces smras pa/_/skyes gyur skyon de so sor 'di nyid kho na'i 'am/_/nad kyi 'jigs la skye dgu rnams kyi spyi la yin/_/de nas shing rta rab tu 'dren pa des smras pa/_/gzhon nu nyes pa de ni thun mong gyur pa ste/_/de ltar nad rnams dag ni yongs su 
[\[12a.7\]]gtser ba ni/_/nad mthar phyin na 'jig rten dga' bar nye bar gshegs/_/de ltar don thos sems ni khong du chud pa de/_/chu rlabs nang song ri bong can bzhin rab 'dar zhing /_/brtse ba thugs rje mnga' bas gsung ngag tshig 'di ni/_/cung zhig dngar ba'i dbyangs kyis rab tu smras 
[\[12b\]]
[\[12b.1\]]pa'o/_/skye dgu rnams la rnam par zhen pa'i nad 'di ni/_/'jig rten pa dag rang bzhin gnas shing blo gtad byed/_/mi shes rnam par rgya chen e ma mi rnams kyis/_/gang zhig nad las rnam par grol rnams bzhad gad byed/_/'dren pa phyi rol rab tu 'gro las phyir log la/_/
[\[12b.2\]]mi dbang pho brang nyid du shing rta rab tu song /_/bdag gi nad kyi 'jigs pa thos nas dga' ba las/_/sems ni 'khums pa bzhin du rab tu bcom par gyur/_/de ltar lan gnyis phyir log de ni rab gzigs nas/_/sa yi bdag po yis kyang yongs su gros byas so/_/de yi phyir log nges 
[\[12b.3\]]pa'i mtshan ma thos gyur nas/_/bdag cag rnams ni 'di yis yang dag 'dor snyam ste/_/lam rnams gtsang mar lhag par byed pa po rnams la/_/khros shing 'khrugs kyang chad pa drag pos gcod pa med/_/slar yang sras po de la khyad par 'phags pa dang /_/ldan pa'i yul 
[\[12b.4\]]la rab tu dpyod pa rnams mdzad cing /_/dbang po g.yo ba nyid kyi phyir yang chags snyam ste/_/bdag cag rnam par mi 'dor zhes ni mgon blangs so/_/gang gi tshe na sgra sgrogs dbang po'i don rnams kyis/_/sras po 'di la pho brang 'khor na dga' ba med/_/de nas ro gzhan ro la gyur cig 
[\[12b.5\]]zhes sems shing /_/phyi rol ltad mo la ni rnam par bka' stsal to/_/brtse ba las ni sras po'i dgongs ba rtogs nas su/_/de la chags pa'i skyon rnams 'ga' zhig ma bsams nas/_/sgyu rtsal rnams la mngon par shes zhes res ma'i gtso/_/mdzangs ma rnams la des ni yang dag bka' 
[\[12b.6\]]stsal to/_/de nas khyad par du yang mi yi dbang po'i lam/_/legs par brgyan byas nyid dang yongs su brtags pa la/_/'dren pa dang ni shing rta brjes nas rgyal po yis/_/gzhon nu phyi rol du ni rab tu gshegs su gzhug_/de nas de ltar rgyal po'i bu ni gshegs pa na/_/lha 
[\[12b.7\]]mchog de rnams nyid kyis rnam par sprul pa yi/_/srog song shi ba'i ro de lam du bteg pa ni/_/'dren pa dang ni gzhon nus mthong ste gzhan gyis min/_/de nas 'dren pa rgyal po'i sras po des smras pa/_/zhan zhing stug pa'i mi rnams dag ni rjes 'brang zhing /_/rgyan gyis brgyan pa yin yang kun nas ngu ba 
[\[13a\]]
[\[13a.1\]]ste/_/mi bzhi rnams kyis bteg pa pha gi su zhig yin/_/de nas gtsang ba'i bdag nyid gtsang mar gnas pa yi/_/lha rnams dag gis sems ni zil non 'dren pa po/_/des ni brjod par bya ba min kyang don 'di ni/_/don ldan dbang phyug la ni rab tu rnam par gsol/_/
[\[13a.2\]]blo dang dbang po srog gi yon tan dang bral zhing /_/gnyid log ming bral rtswa dang shing du gyur pa ste/_/brtson 'grus ldan pa rnams kyis bskyed nas bsrungs nas kyang /_/sdug pa sdug pa rnams kyis spangs pa de ni su yin nam/_/de ltar tshig ni rab tu 'dren pa las thos nas/_/cung zhig 
[\[13a.3\]]kun nas bskyod cing de nyid la smras pa/_/chos 'di nyid ni skye ba 'di nyid kyang gi 'am/_/mthar gyur 'di 'dra skye dgu kun gyi yin nam ci/_/de nas rab tu 'dren pas 'di nyid la gsol pa/_/mthar gyur las 'di skye dgu rnams ni kun gyi ste/_/dman dang 'bring dang bdag nyid chen po 
[\[13a.4\]]thams cad kyi/_/'jig rten dag ni rnam par nges par rnam par nyams par 'gyur/_/de nas mi dbang sras po de ni bstan na yang /_/'chi ba thos nas de ma thag tu yi mug cing /_/dpung pas shing rta'i shing gi rtse la rab 'khyud nas/_/gdangs snyan dang ldan dbyangs kyis rab tu smras 
[\[13a.5\]]pa'o/_/'di ni nges par skye dgu rnams kyi mthar thug ste/_/'jig rten pa dag 'jigs pa spangs shing bag med la/_/mi rnams dag gi sems rnams sra ba snyam pa ste/_/lam la gnas rnams de ltar rang bzhin nyid du gnas/_/de phyir 'dren pa shing rta'i kha ni phyir bzlog dang /_/
[\[13a.6\]]bdag cag sa la rnam par rgyu ba'i yul dus min/_/nyam thag dus su rnam par nyams pa shes nas ni/_/sems dang bcas pa gang 'dra 'di na bag med yin/_/mi bdag bdag skyes kyis ni de skad smras na yang /_/de yis shing rta de ni phyir la ma bzlog ste/_/khyab par ldan pa yin 
[\[13a.7\]]yang mi dbang brten pa la/_/pad+ma'i tshogs bcas nags nyid du ni gshegs par gyur/_/de nas me tog rgyas ldan gzhon nu'i rkang 'thung zhing /_/yongs su bskor zhing rab dga' myos pa'i khu byug can/_/gzhal med khang ldan pad+ma dang bcas mdzes pa'i rdzing /_/dga' ba'i tshal 
[\[13b\]]
[\[13b.1\]]'dra dge ba'i nags de gzigs par gyur/_/lha mchog mas bskor grong khyer brgyan byed bdag po'i gzhir/_/brtul zhugs gsal ldan thub pa bgegs kyis 'jigs pa bzhin/_/yan lag mchog ma'i tshogs khyab mi skyong bdag skyes sras/_/de nas stobs las nags 
[\[13b.2\]]der spyan drangs so/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_thugs skyo ba skyes pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o//_//de nas skyed mos tshal de nas/_/ya mtshan gyis ni mig g.yos rnams/_/bud med rnams ni khyo thob bzhin/_/mi skyong 
[\[13b.3\]]sras la mdun du 'ongs/_/'ongs nas de la de rnams kyis/_/ya mtshan rab rgyas spyan ma drangs/_/pad+ma'i mdzod 'dra'i phyag rnams kyis/_/phrin las yang dag spyod pa byas/_/g.yo med dga' ba rnam gsal ba'i/_/spyan mig rnams kyis 'thung ba bzhin/_/yid dkrugs 'dod 
[\[13b.4\]]pas sems g.yengs rnams/_/de la yongs su bskor nas gnas/_/lhan skyes rgyan rnams kyis bzhin du/_/mtshan ni mdzes rnams kyis brgyan pa/_/'dod lha lus dang ldan no zhes/_/de la mi mo de rnams snyam/_/zhi ba nyid phyir brtan nyid phyir/_/gsang ba'i 'od zer zla ba ni/_/sa la 
[\[13b.5\]]dngos su babs pa bzhin/_/de la kha cig rnams kyis shes/_/de yi lus kyis g.yengs de rnams/_/nges bzung lus ni rnam 'gyur zhing /_/phan tshun mig rnams kyis bsnun nas/_/dal bus dbugs ni rnam par brgyus/_/de ltar mi mo de rnams kyis/_/de ni mig tsam gyis mthong nyid/_/
[\[13b.6\]]smra ba med cing dga' med par/_/'di yi mthu yis non par gyur/_/de ltar de rnams rtsom bral zhing /_/snying rjes rnam par 'khrugs mthong nas/_/bram ze mdun na 'don gyi sras/_/blo ldan 'char kas tshig smras so/_/kun gyi sgyu rtsal kun shes shing /_/dgongs pa len la mkhas ma 
[\[13b.7\]]rnams/_/gzugs dang mdzangs dang ldan pa ste/_/rang gi yon tan gtsor phyin no/_/lus ngan gyi yang skyed tshal dang /_/byang gi sgra mi snyan na yang /_/yon tan 'di rnams kyis mdzes te/_/nor 'dzin 'di na sngon nyid do/_/chags bral rnams dang drang srong yang /_/khyed cag rnams kyis g.yo 
[\[14a\]]
[\[14a.1\]]nus shing /_/lha mo rnams kyis shes pa yis/_/lha rnams la yang len par ro/_/dgongs pa shes dang rtsed 'jo dang /_/mdzangs dang gzugs ni phun tshogs pas/_/bud med rnams kyis chags nus na/_/skyes pa rnams kyis smos ci dgos/_/rnam pa de ltar rab sbyar ba/_/de yi rang gi spyod yul las/_/rnam pa de 
[\[14a.2\]]ltar spyod pa 'di/_/khyod kyi drang po'i tshig ma gyur/_/khyod kyi 'di ni ngo tsha bas/_/mig 'khums bag ma gsar pa yi/_/spyod pa dang mtshungs yang na ni/_/ba glang skyong gi chung ma'i 'os/_/gang yang 'di ni dpa' ba ste/_/dpal gyi mthu las chen po yin/_/bud med kyi yang gzi che ste/_/de phyir 
[\[14a.3\]]'di yang nges par bya/_/sngon yang ka shi'i mdzes ma ni/_/smad 'tshong ma yi drang srong che/_/lha yis thub par dka' ba yang /_/drang srong rgyas pas rkang pas brdungs/_/mAn thal gau tam dge slong ni/_/smad 'tshong gtso mo byin ma yin/_/dga' 'dod de yi don ched du/_/sngon gyi tshe na ro rnams 
[\[14a.4\]]bton/_/gau ta ma mun [{]rings [,]ring [}]ming /_/drang srong chen po tshe rings po/_/kha dog dbyibs mchog ma yin pa'i/_/bud med kyis ni tshim par byas/_/drang srong rwa skyes thub pa mchog_/bud med yul la mi mkhas pa/_/rnam pa sna tshogs thabs rnams kyis/_/zhi mas de ltar blangs shing pham/_/
[\[14a.5\]]sna tshogs bshes gnyen drang srong che/_/brtul zhugs chen po brtan pa yang /_/lha mo mar srung mas phrogs te/_/lo bcu rnams la nyi mar shes/_/drang srong de rnams de rnams sogs/_/bud med kyis sgyur blangs gyur na/_/rol zhing lang tsho snga ma yis/_/mi bdag sras po smos ci dgos/_/
[\[14a.6\]]de ltar gyur na yid ches shing /_/'di ni mi skyong rigs kyi dpal/_/'di las phyir phyogs ma yin pa/_/ji ltar de ltar nan tan mdzod/_/lang tsho ldan ma 'ga' zhig rnams/_/mtshungs pa'i skye bo 'phrog pa ste/_/mchog dang mchog min dag gi sems/_/len pa gang de bud med rnams/_/
[\[14a.7\]]de ltar 'char ka'i tshig thos nas/_/bud med de rnams phug pa bzhin/_/gzhon nu len par rab mtshon te/_/bdag nyid yang dag kun nas 'dzegs/_/'jigs las 'jigs bzhin yan lag ma/_/de rnams smin dang lta dang sgeg_/dgod dang rol dang 'gro ba yis/_/kun nas 
[\[14b\]]
[\[14b.1\]]bskyod pa'i spyod pa byas/_/rgyal po'i bka' lung sbyar phyir dang /_/gzhon nu nyid kyang 'jam phyir dang /_/rgyags pa yis ni myos pa'i phyir/_/yid mi ches pa myur bar dor/_/glang mo'i tshogs dang thabs cig tu/_/glang po gangs can nags na ltar/_/de nas mi mo'i skye bo 
[\[14b.2\]]yis/_/gzhon nu bskor zhing nags na spyad/_/rnam par mdzes pa'i skyed tshal na/_/lha mos bskor ba'i nyi ma bzhin/_/yid 'ong nags der gzhon nu de/_/bud med rnams ni mdun 'gro bar/_/rnam par sgeg bcas gzhon nu ma/_/rgyags pas ma spangs de rnams dga'/_/sra zhing 
[\[14b.3\]]zlum rgyas lhan cig pa'i/_/nu ma rnams kyis de la reg_/dpung pa las brul 'jam ring mnyen/_/lag pa'i ljon shing stobs med ma/_/kha cig bden min 'khrul pa ru/_/byas nas de la stobs las 'khyud/_/kha cig zangs mchu ma mchu can/_/kha cig chang gi dri ngag gis/_/gcig pu 
[\[14b.4\]]dben par sdod cig ces/_/'di yi rna bar shab shib byed/_/kha cig bka' lung stsol ba bzhin/_/byug pa gsher mas rab smras te/_/'dir ni gus par gyis shig ces/_/dgod dang 'khyud dang 'dod pas so/_/kha cig gser gyi ska rags kyi/_/cal col rnams kyis tsam tsom dang /_/
[\[14b.5\]]phra mo'i dar gyi gos gyon zhing /_/'di la ro smad ston cing bskor/_/yang dang yang du myos ma'i rgyus/_/gzhan ma dar sngon nyil ba ste/_/ska rags phan tshun sdud pa ni/_/mtshan mo glog phreng bzhin du mdzes/_/tsU ta'i me tog yal ga rgyas/_/bzung nas gzhan ni 'phyang gyur la/_/gser 
[\[14b.6\]]gyi bum par mtshungs pa yi/_/lo ma 'dzin pa ston par byed/_/kha cig pad+ma'i nags tshal nas/_/'ongs nas pad bcas pad+ma'i spyan/_/pad+ma'i zhal gyi logs gnas shing /_/pad+ma'i dpal bzhin bsdad par gyur/_/rang gnas de la bskul ba bzhin/_/bslus par gyur zhes mig rnams 
[\[14b.7\]]kyis/_/kha cig mngon par 'dren bcas lag_/don ldan dngar ba'i glu len no/_/dge ba'i gdong gis gzhan mas ni/_/smin pa'i gzhu ni rnam bkang ste/_/bskor gnas brtan dang rol pa yis/_/'di yi spyod la rjes zlos byas/_/rgyas zlum nu ldan kha cig ni/_/dgod pas rna lung 
[\[15a\]]
[\[15a.1\]]g.yos gyur cing /_/khyod ni thob par gyur cig ces/_/mthon po rnams kyis bzhad cing smras/_/gzhan ni de bzhin 'gro 'dod pa/_/me tog phreng ba'i tshal gyis bcings/_/kha cig cod bcas dngar mo yis/_/ngag gi zhags pa rnams kyis bzung /_/so sor 
[\[15a.2\]]'bral ba don gnyer 'ga'/_/tsU ta'i snye ma bzung nas ni/_/me tog 'di ni gang gi zhes/_/rgyags pas rnam par 'khrugs shing dris/_/kha cig 'gros dang cha lugs ni/_/skyes pa bzhin du byas nas ni/_/sa 'dir rgyal bar gyur cig kye/_/bud med kyis 'pham de la smras/_/de nas 
[\[15a.3\]]g.yo ba'i mig can 'gas/_/ut+pal sngon po snom byed cing /_/cung zhig rgyags skad tshig rnams kyis/_/mi skyong bdag skyes la smras so/_/gzigs shing jo bo tsU ta'i shing /_/me tog sbrang rtsi'i dris khyab la/_/khu byug 'dir ni skad 'don pa/_/gser gyi bu na bkag pa 'dra/_/mya ngan 
[\[15a.4\]]med pa'i shing 'di gzigs/_/'dod pa can gyi mya ngan 'phel/_/bung ba 'dir ni sgra bsgrags pa/_/me yis tshig pa yin pa 'dra/_/tsU ta'i shing gis yang dag 'khyud/_/gzigs shing ti la ka yi shing /_/bud med lus ser chags pa yis/_/skyes pa gos dkar gon pa 'dra/_/
[\[15a.5\]]rab rgyas ku ra ba ka gzigs/_/ma zos pa yi smug rtsi'i 'od/_/bud med rnams kyi sen 'od kyis/_/sma phab gyur te dud pa 'dra/_/gzhon nu mya ngan med 'di gzigs/_/'dab ma rnams kyis spras pa gang /_/kho mo'i lag pa mdzes pa yis/_/ngo mtshar gyur te gnas pa 'dra/_/rdzing 
[\[15a.6\]]bu gzigs shing 'gram skyes pa'i/_/lcang ma rnams kyis rab tu bskor/_/dkar po'i dar gyi gos gon pa'i/_/rab myos ma rnams nyal ba 'dra/_/bud med rnams la bdag nyid che/_/gzigs shig ngang pa chu 'di la/_/rgyab na bsgo bya'i bran bzhin du/_/ngang mo rjes 'jug rjes 
[\[15a.7\]]su 'gro/_/rab tu myos pa'i khu byug ni/_/skad grag sgra ni gson mdzod cig_/khu byug gzhan ni mngon sum du/_/kus 'debs brag ca'i sgra brnyan 'dra/_/mkha' la rgyu ba rnams kyi yang /_/rgyags pa dpyid kyis khyer te 'ongs/_/bsam bya min sems skye bo ni/_/shes rab can du 
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]rlom pa med/_/de ltar dar la bab pa rnams/_/yid dkrugs 'dod pas sems dregs shing /_/sna tshogs tshul ni de dang des/_/gzhon nu la ni nye bar brtsams/_/de ltar kun nas g.yengs na yang /_/brten pas dbang po dkris pa de/_/'chi bar bya zhes skyo byas shing /_/
[\[15b.2\]]dga' dang 'jigs pa med par 'gyur/_/de rnams kyi ni gnas skabs de/_/gzigs nas skyes mchog dam pa des/_/mnyam la skyo dang brtan pa yi/_/thugs kyis bsams par gyur pa'o/_/ci zhig bud med 'di rnams ni/_/lang tsho g.yo bar rtogs pa min/_/gang 
[\[15b.3\]]phyir gzugs kyi myos pa ni/_/gang zhig dga' bas nyams par 'gyur/_/gang gi nang du bying ba ru/_/nges par de rnams kyis mi mthong /_/'gro ba nad kyi chos can la/_/'jigs pa spangs te de ltar dga'/_/thams cad 'phrog byed 'chi ba yi/_/nges 
[\[15b.4\]]par mngon par shes min zhing /_/de ltar rang gnas skyo med par/_/rtsed mo dang ni bzhad gad byed/_/rga dang na dang 'chi ba yang /_/shes ldan yin na sems ldan su/_/rang gnas langs dang 'dug pa 'am/_/nyal byed rgod pa smos ci dgos/_/nad dang 'chi ba nyid dang ni/_/mchog tu 
[\[15b.5\]]'khogs gang de mthong nas/_/rang gnas gyur zhing skyo med pa/_/ji ltar sems med de ltar de/_/me tog rnams dang 'bras bu yis/_/rnam par spangs par gyur pa'i shing /_/'gyel lam yang na bcad pa ste/_/shing gzhan mdzes par byed ma yin/_/de ltar bsam gtan mchog mthong 
[\[15b.6\]]nas/_/yul rnams la ni 'dod pa thal/_/'char ka tshul lugs bstan bcos shes/_/mdza' bo nyid kyis de la smras/_/kho bo mi skyong gis byin te/_/khyod la grogs su nus pa lo/_/gang phyir khyed la smra 'dod pa/_/rab dga' ldan pa de phyir ro/_/phan pa min las rab 'gegs shing /_/phan 
[\[15b.7\]]pa la yang rjes rab 'jug_/sdug pa na yang yongs mi gtong /_/yongs rdzogs shes kyi mtshan nyid do/_/bdag de byams par dam bcas nas/_/skyes bu'i don la phyir phyogs pa/_/gal te khyod la btang snyoms byed/_/grogs po nyid kyang ma yin no/_/de phyir mdza' bor gyur nas smras/_/
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]gzhon nu'i lus dang ldan pa ni/_/khyod kyis 'di 'dra bud med kyis/_/yul la mkhas pa'i gzugs brnyan min/_/de yi ngo tsha'i rgod don dang /_/bdag nyid dga' ba'i don nyid du/_/bden min gyis kyang mi mo yi/_/rjes su 'jug pa rigs pa yin/_/yang dag 'dud dang 
[\[16a.2\]]rjes 'jug gi/_/bud med rnams kyis sems 'ching ste/_/brtse ba'i yon tan skye gnas phyir/_/yid la 'dod pa bud med yin/_/de phyir rnam par spangs pa'i spyan/_/snying la gzhan du phyir bltas kyang /_/'di yi yul dang rjes mthun par/_/mkhas pa rnam par dpyad par 'os/_/mkhas pa 
[\[16a.3\]]sman yin bud med la/_/mkhas pa rab mchog rgyan yin no/_/mkhas pa dang bral gzugs dag ni/_/me tog med pa'i nags dang 'dra/_/yang na mkhas pa'i rang bzhin gyi/_/bsam pa rang bzhin khas len mdzod/_/yul rnams nyes par dka' thob nas/_/phyi shol byed pa 'os ma yin/_/
[\[16a.4\]]sngon tshe lha yi dbang pos kyang /_/'dod pa mchog ces shes byas nas/_/gau tam thub pa'i dga' ldan ma/_/bshol med ma la 'dod pa byas/_/zla ba'i chung ma snar ma ni/_/drang srong ri byis don du gnyer/_/de nas de dang 'dra ba yi/_/brkam bya ma ni rnyed ces thos/_/u b+ha th+ya yi chung ma ni/_/rlung 
[\[16a.5\]]gi bu mo mthun ma la/_/dka' thub chen po'i phur bu yis/_/ba ra dwa dza bskyed par gyur/_/drang srong phur bu'i chung ma ni/_/sbyin sreg ma la sbyin sreg mchog_/zla ba yis ni lag pa yi/_/lha yi las byed bskyed par gyur/_/sngon tshe bu mo nag mo ni/_/chur skyes ma las skyes ma la/_/rtsib 
[\[16a.6\]]logs skyes ni chags skyes shing /_/ya nu ni yi gling du phyin/_/gnod sbyin phreng ma rigs ngan ma/_/snod ma la ni dga' 'dod pas/_/gong ma sreg ni za ba'i bu/_/thub pa gnas mchog gis bskyed do/_/'gro mgyogs rgyal po drang srong nyid/_/na tshod rnam par song ba yi/_/lha mo sna tshogs 
[\[16a.7\]]mchod mar bcas/_/sna tshogs shing rta'i nags su dga'/_/bud med 'grogs min zhi ba ni/_/skya bseng gis ni shes nas kyang /_/dga' ma'i gzugs yon gyis bkug ste/_/'dod las skyes pa'i bde ba bsten/_/skyed mdzad rgyal po dmar ser yang /_/bram ze'i bu mo phrogs nas ni/_/
[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]na babs thob pa nyid yin kyang /_/yid dkrugs 'dod pa ma bsten min/_/de la sogs pa bdag nyid ches/_/yul rnams smad pa yin na yang /_/dga' ba'i ched du longs spyad de/_/yon tan ldan la sngon nyid do/_/khyod kyang rigs pa las 'thob cing /_/stobs ldan gzugs ldan dar 
[\[16b.2\]]la bab/_/gang du skye 'gro 'di 'chags na/_/yul rnams la ni phyi shol byed/_/de ltar 'jam zhing lung dang bcas/_/de yis tshig ni gsan gyur nas/_/'brug gi sgra skad dbyangs kyis ni/_/gzhon nu yis ni lan smras so/_/ngag tshig 'di ni 'thad pa ste/_/khyod la mdza' bo nyid du gsal/_/
[\[16b.3\]]gang du bdag la ngan par sems/_/'dir ni khyod la rjes zhi bgyi/_/yul rnams la ni phyi shol byed/_/'jig rten de'i bdag nyid du shes/_/'gro ba mi rtag par shes nas/_/'dir ni bdag yid dga' ba min/_/rga dang na dang 'chi ba ste/_/gal te 'di gsum ma yin na/_/yid du 'ong ba'i yul 
[\[16b.4\]]rnams la/_/kho bo yang ni dga' bar 'gyur/_/gal te bud med rnams kyi lus/_/de nyid rtag par 'gyur na ni/_/skyon ldan 'dod pa rnams la yang /_/kho bo'i sems ni 'dod la phyogs/_/gang tshe 'di dag rnams kyi gzugs/_/rgas pas 'thung ba 'byung 'gyur zhing /_/bdag nyid kyi yang 
[\[16b.5\]]'dod med na/_/rmongs las der ni dga' bar 'gyur/_/rga dang na dang 'chi ba'i tshes/_/rga na 'chi ba'i bdag nyid kyis/_/skyo ba med par rol pa ni/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dang ni bya dang mtshungs/_/bdag nyid chen po 'di rnams kyang /_/'dod pa'i bdag nyid ces smras gang /_/'dir ni skyo bar bya ba ste/_/gang tshe de 
[\[16b.6\]]rnams kyi yang tshad/_/gang du phyi las zad pa ste/_/bdag nyid chen por mi sems te/_/yul rnams la ni chags pa 'am/_/sbyor min bdag nyid ldan nyid kyis/_/mkhas pa yis kyang dpe brjod cing /_/bden min gyis kyang bud med kyi/_/skye la zhugs zhes smras pa gang /_/bden min bdag gis 
[\[16b.7\]]shes ma yin/_/gang du drang min rjes 'jug pa/_/de la bdag gis sred pa med/_/dngos po kun gyi tshogs ba de/_/gal te smon na med par shog/_/mi brtan pa yi dad pa dang /_/skyon du mthong ba'i nus pa yis/_/chags pa skyes par gyur pa'i sems/_/slu bar bya ba ci zhig yin/_/bud 
[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]med skyes pa'i chags skyes shing /_/bud med skyes pa phan tshun du/_/gal te de ltar slu na ni/_/lta bar nus pa nyid ma yin/_/de ltar gyur na sdug bsngal gyis/_/nyam thag rga 'chi'i skal ba can/_/bdag ni 'phags min 'dod pa la/_/slu bar 'os pa ma yin no/_/e 
[\[17a.2\]]ma shin tu brtan la stobs ldan khyod kyi sems/_/'dod pa g.yo ba rnams su snying po mthong gyur la/_/shin tu rno zhing 'jigs pa'i yul la zon mdzad cing /_/rga dang rgud pa nad kyi 'jigs pa rnam sems shing /_/'gro ba rnams ni me yis tshig pa bzhin thos na/_/zhi ba 'thob min brtan min dga' 
[\[17a.3\]]ba ga la zhig_/the tshom med par 'chi zhes rab tu shes pa yi/_/mi rnams gang gi snying la chags pa rab skyed pa/_/de yi sems ni lcags las gyur par shes pa ste/_/'jigs pa che la chags pa gang zhig du ba med/_/de nas gzhon nu yis ni 'dod pa rnams kyis bsten/_/'joms byed 
[\[17a.4\]]gtam ni rnam par nges pa'i bdag nyid mdzad/_/skye bo rnams kyi mig gi 'gro bya gsal byed kyi/_/dkyil 'khor sa 'dzin ri bo la ni nub par gyur/_/de nas rgyan dang me tog phreng ba 'bras med 'dzin/_/sgyu rtsal yon tan gyis dang rab dgas 'bras med rnams/_/rang gi sems la yid dkrugs 
[\[17a.5\]]'dod pa rnams smad nas/_/yid la re nyams bud med rnams ni grong du thal/_/de nas grong khyer skyed mos tshal song skye bo'i dpal/_/legs par brtags nas spyod la so sor brtul te yang /_/thams cad mi rtag par gyur rnam par sems bzhin du/_/slar yang mi skyong bdag skyes grong du rnam par 
[\[17a.6\]]zhugs/_/de nas de yi yid ni yul la rnam par phyir phyogs rgyal pos thos gyur nas/_/snying la song ba'i zug la glang chen bzhin du mtshan mo de la gnyid ma log_/de nas sngags mang rnam pa sna tshogs lam rnams dag gis thang chad blon por bcas/_/des ni 'dod pa las gzhan sras kyi blo yi 
[\[17a.7\]]nges pa gzigs ma gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_bud med kyis gegs byas pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o//_//de ltar yul rnams dam pa'i mchod pa rnams kyis ni/_/sred du bcug kyang shAkya'i rgyal po'i sras po de/_/dug 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]bsgos mda' yis snying la phug pa'i seng ge bzhin/_/bde ba rnyed pa ma yin brtan par gshegs ma gyur/_/de nas res 'ga' zhig na blon po'i bu mkhas shing /_/grogs gyur sna tshogs gtam gyis rjes su 'brangs byas pa/_/nags kyi sa la 'gro 'dod lta bar 'dod pa yis/_/mi yi 
[\[17b.2\]]lha yis rjes gnang phyi rol rab tu gshegs/_/gsar ba'i gser gyi srab dang dril bu chung ngu dang /_/rab tu g.yo ba'i rnga yab mdzes pa'i gser sga dang /_/stan bcas dam pa'i rta la mngon par zhon nas ni/_/tog la ljon dang chu skyes tog can bzhin du gshegs/_/de ni mchog tu rnam par 
[\[17b.3\]]rgyang ring nags mtha'i sa/_/nags tshal 'dod dang sa yi yon tan las gshegs te/_/chu gnyer bzhin du rnam par bkram pa'i gshol lam can/_/nor 'dzin sa la zhing dag rmo ba gzigs par gyur/_/gshol gyi phya las rnam bkram rtswa myug ku sha dang /_/shi gyur 'bu srin srog chags 
[\[17b.4\]]phra mos khyab pa ste/_/rnam pa de 'dra'i sa de yang dag mngon gzigs nas/_/rang gi skye bo bsad pa la bzhin mya ngan gyur/_/zhing dag rmo ba'i skyes bu rnams ni rnam gzigs pa/_/rlung gis gtor ba'i rdul gyis rnam 'gyur kha dog can/_/khur bas thang chad rnam par 'khrugs pa'i glang rnams 
[\[17b.5\]]la/_/'phags pa mchog gis mchog tu brtse ba'i snying rje mdzad/_/de nas mgyogs 'gro'i rgyab nas dal bus babs nas ni/_/sa la rnam par rgyu zhing mya ngan dang ldan pa/_/skye bo dag gi skye dang 'god pa rnam sems zhing /_/nges par bkren pa 'di zhes smras shing nyam thag gyur/_/bsam pa yis 
[\[17b.6\]]kyang rnam par dben pa mngon bzhed cing /_/rjes su 'ongs pa'i mdza' bshes de rnams phyir bzlog nas/_/kun nas mngon par g.yo zhing mdzes pa'i lo 'dab can/_/'dzam bu'i rtsa ba skye bo med par de gshegs so/_/rin chen bai DUr+ya dang mtshungs pa'i rtswa 'jam can/_/gtsang mar ldan pa'i sa gzhi der ni 
[\[17b.7\]]de zhugs te/_/'gro ba'i skye ba dang ni 'god pa rnams sems shing /_/yid ni brtan pa'i lam la dmigs pa'o/_/yul la 'dod sogs sems kyi nad las rnam grol ba'i/_/sems gnas pa ni de ma thag tu thob gyur la/_/bsam gtan dang po zag pa med pa'i rab spyod pa/_/rtog pa dpyod pa dang 
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]bcas zhi ba thob par gyur/_/rnam par dben pa las skyes thob nas de nas ni/_/mchog tu dga' ba'i bde ba sems kyi ting nge 'dzin/_/yid kyis 'jig rten 'gro ba yang dag rab brtags nas/_/'di nyid de nas gzhan ni rab tu rtogs par gyur/_/kye ma skye bo bkren pa 
[\[18a.2\]]gang zhig rang dbang ni/_/yod min rga dang na dang rnam par nyams pa'i chos/_/nad pa dang ni shi dang rga bas nyam thag gam/_/gzhan la rgyags pas long zhing shes pa med pas smod/_/'dir ni gal te bdag gis rang dang mtshungs gyur pa/_/gzhan gyi rang bzhin dag la de ltar smod ba ni/_/mchog gi chos 
[\[18a.3\]]'di rnam par rtog pa kho bo yi/_/mtshungs par gyur pa ma yin nus pa'ang gzhan med do/_/'gro ba'i rga dang na dang rgud pa'i nyes pa rnams/_/de ltar ji lta ba bzhin rnam par gzigs de yi/_/stobs dang lang tsho dang ni srog la rab zhugs pa'i/_/bdag tu gyur pa'i rgyags pa skad cig gis med gyur/_/
[\[18a.4\]]de ni dga' ba med cing rjes su gdung med la/_/the tshom som nyir ma gyur gnyid dang rmugs pa med/_/'dod pa'i yon tan rnams la yang dag ma chags shing /_/gzhan la rnam par sdang bar ma gyur phyi shol med/_/de ltar bdag nyid chen po de yi rdul med cing /_/rnam par dag pa'i blo gros 'di ni 'phel 
[\[18a.5\]]gyur la/_/skyes bu gzhan dag rnams kyis mthong bar ma gyur pa'i/_/dge slong cha lugs ldan pa'i skyes bu nye bar 'ongs/_/su zhig yin pa smros zhes mi yi lha sras kyis/_/de la mngon par dris gyur de la des smras pa/_/mi yi skyes mchog skye dang 'chi bas 'jigs pa yi/_/thar pa'i phyir ni rab tu 
[\[18a.6\]]zhugs pa'i dge sbyong yin/_/bdag ni 'gro ba zad pa'i chos ni thar 'dod cing /_/dge zhing 'gyur ba med pa'i gnas de tshol ba ste/_/rang gi skye bo dang ni skye bor mnyam pa'i blo/_/yul rnams la ni chags pa'i nyes pa phyir log pa'o/_/skye bo med pa'i gnas khang dang ni ri 'am nags/_/shing gi rtsa 
[\[18a.7\]]ba 'ga' zhig tu ni gnas pa yi/_/don dam phyir ni ji ltar nyer ldan slong mo pa/_/sred bral yongs su 'dzin pa med par rnam par rgyu/_/de ltar gzigs pa'i rgyal po'i sras po de nyid la/_/'di skad smras nas nam mkha' la ni gshegs par gyur/_/de ni de yi lus kyis sangs rgyas gzhan gzigs 
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]shing /_/lha na gnas pa de yi dran pa'i ched du 'ongs/_/mkha' la mkha' 'gro bzhin du gshegs pa de la ni/_/mi mchog yang dag dga' bar gyur zhing ya mtshan gyur/_/de nas chos kyi 'du shes nye bar thob gyur nas/_/mngon par nges 'byung cho ga la ni blo gros mdzad/_/de nas dbang po 
[\[18b.2\]]dang mnyam dbang po'i rta thul ba/_/grong la rab tu 'jug 'dod rta la rab 'dzegs shing /_/'khor gyi skye bo dag kyang mngon par 'dod pa ste/_/de nyid phyir na mngon 'dod nags ni ma bsnyen to/_/de ni rga dang 'chi ba zad par byed 'dod pa/_/nags su gnas phyir blo gros dran pa la bzung nas/_
[\[18b.3\]]gnyis 'thung dbang po nags kyi sa nas grong du ltar/_/'dod pa las min grong la slar yang rab tu zhugs/_/de ni yang dag mthong nas shAkya'i bu mo zhig_/rab zhugs lam la thal mo sbyar ba dang bcas shing /_/kye ma spyan dbang gang gi bdag po 'di 'dra 'dir/_/mo de bde ma mya ngan 'das ma zhes 
[\[18b.4\]]smras so/_/de nas dbyangs 'di sprin chen dbyangs dang ldan pa yis/_/yongs su thos nas mchog tu zhi ba rnyed gyur la/_/mya ngan 'das zhes sgra skad thos pa dang ldan des/_/yongs su mya ngan 'da' ba'i chos gar blo gros mdzad/_/de nas gser gyi ri bo'i rtse mo'i sku mnga' zhing /_/glang chen phyag dang 
[\[18b.5\]]'brug gi sgra dbyangs khyu mchog spyan/_/seng ge'i stobs dang ri bong can gyi zhal mnga' bas/_/mi zad chos la chags skyes pho brang rab tu 'thob/_/de nas mtho ris dag na lha yi 'dun sa ru/_/lha dbang 'bar ba la ni bkug 'gyed gzhon nu bzhin/_/blon po'i tshogs kyis nye bar bsnyen pa'i mi 
[\[18b.6\]]bdag la/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dbang po'i 'gros dang ldan pa mngon par gshegs/_/rab tu btud nas thal mo dang bcas smras gyur pa/_/mi dbang bdag la legs par rjes su gnang ba byin/_/nges par skye bo 'di ni rnam par 'bral ba ste/_/thar pa'i ched du yongs su rnam par gshegs par bgyi/_/de ltar 
[\[18b.7\]]de'i tshig nges par thob nas rgyal po ni/_/lag ldan gyis ni mngon snun ljon pa ltar g.yos shing /_/ka ma la dang rab mtshungs thal mo dag bzung nas/_/mchi ma dang ni gre ba kha zhing tshig 'di smras/_/brtse ba'i blo gros 'di ni rab tu thong mdzod cig_/khyed kyi chos la bsnyen pa'i dus ni ma yin te/_/na tshod dang 
[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]po dang ni blo gros g.yo ba na/_/chos kyi spyod pa gsungs pa rnams ni skyon mang ngo /_/yul rnams su ni dbang po ya mtshan glo bur zhing /_/brtul zhugs g.yo ba rnams su nges par nus med la/_/khyad par du ni dben par mngon par mi shes pa/_/dar la bab pa'i yid 
[\[19a.2\]]ni dgon pa nas g.yo'o/_/mtshon gyur khyod la phun sum tshogs pa gtad nas ni/_/kho bo yi yang chos dga' chos kyi dus yin te/_/rnam par gnon pa bstan pa rnam par gnon pa yis/_/bla ma spangs nas khyod kyi chos kyis chos ma yin/_/de phyir khyod kyis nan tan nges pa 'di dor la/_/
[\[19a.3\]]khyod ni re zhig khyim gnas chos la dga' bar gyis/_/dar la bab pas bde ba rnams ni longs spyad nas/_/skyes bu'i dka' thub nags su 'jug pa nyams dga'o/_/de ltar rgyal po'i tshig 'di rnam par gsan nas ni/_/ka la ping ka'i dbyangs kyis lan ni smras pa ste/_/rgyal po gal te bdag 
[\[19a.4\]]gi khas len bzhi rnams su/_/khyed kyis 'gyur na dka' thub nags ni mi bsnyen to/_/bdag gi srog ni 'chi ba nyid du mi 'gyur zhing /_/bdag gi rang gnas gyur 'di nad kyis mi 'phrogs la/_/bdag gi lang tsho rgas pa yis ni mi g.yeng zhing /_/phun sum tshogs pa 'di ni rgud pas 
[\[19a.5\]]ma gtor na'o/_/de ltar rnyed par dka' ba'i don smras gyur pa yi/_/sras po la ni shAkya'i rgyal pos tshig smras pa/_/shin tu rab tu zhugs pa'i blo 'di thong mdzod cig_/shin tu yid la re ba 'phya zhing sha thang 'chad/_/de nas lhun po ltar lcis bla ma la smras pa/_/gal te rim pa de dag med 
[\[19a.6\]]de shol mi thebs/_/'bar ba'i me yis tshig pa'i khang pa nas/_/phyi rol 'byung bar 'dod pa 'dzin par nus ma yin/_/gang gi tshe na 'gro ba nges par 'bral ba ste/_/chos phyir ma yin 'di ni rnam par 'bral ba mchog_/'o na bdag la 'chi bas dbang med 'bral byed cing /_/rang gi don 
[\[19a.7\]]ma byas shing tshim pa ma yin nyid/_/de ltar mthar bzhed sras po de yis nges pa ni/_/sa yi bdag po yis ni nges par thos nas ni/_/'gro bar mi bya zhes ni mngon par brjod nas su/_/slar yang bsrung ba dag ni 'dod pa mchog rnams mdzad/_/dga' zhi zhi ba sngon 'gro bkur sti mang po las/_/
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]blon po rnams kyis ji lta ba bzhin dpe bstan zhing /_/bla mas mchi ma lhung ba rnams kyis shol btab ste/_/de nas de ni mya ngan bzhin du khyim la zhugs/_/rna lung 'khyil ba g.yo bas 'o byas gdong ldan ma/_/dbugs rlung stug pos rnam par bskyod pa'i nu ma can/_/bstan pa min pa'i mig ldan 
[\[19b.2\]]bud med rnams kyis ni/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]mo rnams kyis bzhin mngon par gyen du lta/_/gser gyi ri bo dang 'dra dkar ser mdog de ni/_/yan lag mchog ma rnams kyi snying ni myos mdzad cing /_/ngag dang reg bya dang ni lus dang yon tan gyis/_/thos byed lus dang lta byed bdag gi dngos rnams 
[\[19b.3\]]phrogs/_/nyin mo rnam par thal tshe de nas khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la/_/lus kyis nyi ma bzhin du mngon par gsal ba ni/_/rab rib rnam par 'joms 'dod bdag nyid snang ba yis/_/nyi ma shar ba lhun po la bzhin kun nas 'dzegs/_/gser gyis 'bar zhing gsal ba'i sgron ma'i shing dang ni/_/a ga ru 
[\[19b.4\]]mchog nag po'i bdug pas gang ba'i ltor/_/mngon par 'dzegs nas rdo rjes spras shing sna tshogs pa/_/rab mchog gser gyi bzhugs gdan la ni bsnyen pa'o/_/zla ba ltar dkar gangs dang ldan pa'i rtse mo na/_/nor dbang bdag nyid skyes la lha mo'i tshogs 'dus bzhin/_/de nas lus mchog ma 
[\[19b.5\]]rnams dbang po dang mtshungs mchog_/de la mtshan mor sil snyan rnams kyis nye bar gnas/_/mchog rab lha yi sil snyan 'dra ba rnams kyis kyang /_/de yang de yis bskyed par ma gyur dga' ba med/_/dam pa'i don gyi bde phyir legs pa de yi ni/_/mngon par nges par 'byung 'dod gang la dga' 
[\[19b.6\]]ma gyur/_/de nas der ni brtul zhugs mchog gyur 'og min gyi/_/lha rnams kyis ni 'di yi nges pa rtogs nas ni/_/lus kyi rnam 'gyur rnam par 'gyur byas dus gcig la/_/myos ma'i skye bo rnams ni gnyid du 'gro bar byas/_/de la kha cig dga' ba yin yang khros pa bzhin/_/pang du son pa'i 
[\[19b.7\]]gser gyi snod ni sna tshogs pa'i/_/pi wang rnam par spangs nas lag pa g.yo ba la/_/'gram pa rnam par bkod nas nyal bar gyur pa'o/_/'khyog min bung ba'i phreng ba sgra sgrogs pad+ma dang /_/chu bo'i ngogs na chu sbur rab tu rgod pa bzhin/_/gzhan rnams nu mar rnam nyil dkar po'i dar gon zhing /_/
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]gling bu lag par sbyar zhing gnyid log rnam par mdzes/_/de bzhin gzhan rnams pad+ma gsar pa'i snying po ltar/_/'jam zhing gdung bya gser gyis 'bar ba'i [(]da pubargyan [,]dpung rgyan [)]ni/_/'dus pa'i lag pa dag gis dga' ba bzhin du ni/_/rdza rnga nyid ni yongs su 'khyud 
[\[20a.2\]]nas gnyid log gyur/_/de bzhin gzhan ni gsar ba'i gser gyis rnam brgyan zhing /_/bgo ba'i gos ni ser po mchog dag gon pa ste/_/kar ni ka ra'i yal ga glang pos bsgyel ba bzhin/_/dbang med par ni sdug ma gnyid kyis 'gyel bar gyur/_/de bzhin gzhan ni skar khung 
[\[20a.3\]]logs la sngas nas ni/_/nyal bar gyur pa'i lus kyi mchod sdong rnams 'khyog pa/_/rta babs la ni sha la b+hany+dza ka bzhin du/_/mdzes pa'i do shal rnam par 'phyang ba rnam par mdzes/_/de bzhin gzhan gyi zhal gyi pad+ma rnam rgyas shing /_/'gram pa'i logs la nor bu'i rna lung gis 
[\[20a.4\]]dmugs pa/_/'dab ma brgya pa'i sdong bu'i phyed ni 'khyog pa la/_/gnas pa'i kar Ni Da bas bskyod bzhin g.yos par gyur/_/gzhan ni ji ltar rnam par zhugs bzhin gnyid log cing /_/nu ma'i khur gyis mngon par dud pa'i lus po rnams/_/lag pa'i zhags pa gser gyi gdu bu can rnams kyis/_/
[\[20a.5\]]phan tshun nye bar 'khyud nas rnam par gnas te mdzes pa'o/_/rnam par g.yo zhing bskyod pa'i gser gyi skud pa can/_/rna ba'i shog dril kun nas 'khrugs pa'i gdong gis ni/_/yongs su smra ldan pi wang chen po 'khyud nas ni/_/bud med kha cig grogs mo bzhin du gnyid log go/_/lang tsho ma 
[\[20a.6\]]gzhan lag pa'i dpung pa'i yul las ni/_/kun nas rnam 'phyang mdzes pa'i logs can pa Na wa/_/sgyeg bcas dga' ba'i mthar ni thang chad sdug pa bzhin/_/brla gnyis dag ni dbus su blangs nas nyal ba'o/_/rab tu yang dag 'khums gyur pad+ma dmar po'i mdzod/_/nyi ma nub par 
[\[20a.7\]]gyur tshe rdzing bu rnams ji ltar/_/rnam rgyas mig dang mdzes pa'i smin ma yod na yang /_/gzhan rnams mig rnams btsums shing mdzes par ma gyur to/_/de bzhin gzhan ni mgo skyes lhod pa las 'khrugs shing /_/rgyan dang dar rnams dpyi la rnam nyil de rnams ni/_/yan lag ma yi gzugs 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]brnyan glang pos bcom pa bzhin/_/mgrin pa'i skud pa'i do shal rnam par bkram zhing nyal/_/brtan ldan lus ni yon tan rnams dang ldan na yang /_/gzhan rnams kyang ni dbang med ngo tshas rnam spangs shing /_/rnam par 'gyur zhing bskyod pa'i yan lag gya gyu dang /_/dbugs ni rnam par rgyu

[\[20b.3\]]lus po rab brkyang zhing /_/kha yi chu ni rab 'dzags gsang ba rnam rab gsal/_/gzhan ma dag ni chang gis 'khrul par gyur pa nyid/_/lus rgyas rnam par 'gyur bar gnyid log mdzes ma gyur/_/de ltar sems pa dang ni rigs kyi rjes 'gro'i gzugs/_/rnam pa sna tshogs rab myos skye bo de 
[\[20b.4\]]nyal ba/_/rlung gis kun nas bskyod gyur gser gyi pad+ma yi/_/rdzing bu dang ni mtshungs pa'i gzugs ni mdzes par gyur/_/de ltar rnam par 'gyur la brtan min spyod pa can/_/de ltar nyal ba'i lang tsho ldan ma de mthong nas/_/lus kyi yon tan ldan zhing smra ba'i yid 'ong yang /_/mi skyong sras po des ni rnam 
[\[20b.5\]]par smad par gyur/_/mi gtsang rnam par 'gyur ba gson pa'i 'jig rten te/_/bud med rnams kyi rang gi ngo bo 'di 'dra ba/_/bgo ba rnams dang rgyan rnams kyis kyang slu ba ste/_/bud med rnams kyi yul la skyes pa chags par 'gro/_/gal te skyes pas bud med rnams kyi rang bzhin ni/_/rmi lam rnam 
[\[20b.6\]]par 'gyur ba bzhin du rnam dpyad na/_/nges par 'dir ni bag med 'phel ba ma yin la/_/'on kyang yon tan rtog pa bcom ste chags par 'gro/_/de ltar de yi go skabs rnam par rig nas ni/_/mtshan mor phyi ru 'gro 'dod yang dag skyes par gyur/_/'di yi sems ni kun nas rtog nas de nas ni/_/
[\[20b.7\]]lha rnams dag gis khang pa'i sgo ni phye bar gyur/_/de nas lang tsho ldan ma nyal ba de rnams ni/_/smad cing de ni lding khang logs nas babs pa ste/_/mngon par babs nas de nas som nyi rnam bral bar/_/khang pa'i rim pa dang por phyi ru rnam par gshegs/_/de ni mgyogs 'gro bsrungs la go bar 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]byas nas ni/_/skyen par 'dun pa la ni 'di skad ces smras so/_/bsngags ldan rta ni khyer shog myur ba dang ldan pas/_/bdud rtsi thob phyir 'di las [{]di ring [,]de ring [}]bdag skom mo/_/bdag gi snying la de ring tshim pa skyes gyur zhing /_/nges pa

[\[21a.3\]]par bdag ni 'di nas [{]di ring [,]de ring [}]'gro ba'i dus/_/mi dbang brtan pa'i don ni rnam par rig na yang /_/jo bo bka' lung des ni de nas rab bzung nas/_/yid la pha rol po yis yang dag bskul ba bzhin/_/mgyogs 'gro khyer te 'ong ba la ni blo gros byas/_/de nas gdong gi gser gyi srab kyis gang ba ste/_/
[\[21a.4\]]rgyab na yang ba'i stan g.yogs dag gis nye bar g.yogs/_/stobs dang sems pa mgyogs dang rjes 'gro nyer ldan pa/_/de yis rta mchog de la der ni nye bar 'ongs/_/snga ma'i rtsa ba rtsib logs gsum ga rab rgyas shing /_/rgyab dang rna ba lus skyes spu ni thung zhing dul/_/rgyab kyi rtsib ma dag ni dud cing 
[\[21a.5\]]rnam rgyal la/_/mchu dang dpral ba brang dang dpyi ni rab rgyas pa'o/_/zhi zhing ka ma la dang mtshungs pa'i phyag gis kyang /_/sku stong rnam par spangs des de la nyer 'khyud nas/_/rgyal mtshan sde la dmag dpon rab tu 'jug pa bzhin/_/mngar mo'i yi ge'i tshig gis zhal ta mdzad pa'o/_/g.yul du khyod la 
[\[21a.6\]]mngon par zhon nas sa skyong gis/_/dgra bo rnams ni lan grangs mang por nges par gtor/_/bdag kyang 'chi med go 'phang ji lta ba bzhin du/_/mgyogs 'gro'i gtso bo nye bar thob pa de ltar mdzod/_/yul thob bde ba la dang nor bsgrub pa la rung /_/g.yul 'gyed pa na 'khor ni nges par rnyed bla ste/_/brgyud par

[\[21b.1\]]'di ni yongs shes nas/_/bdag ni 'di las nges 'byung 'gro la phan pa'i phyir/_/mgyogs 'gro mchog skye shugs dang rnam par gnon pa yis/_/bdag phan la dang 'gro phan la ni 'bad par gyis/_/de ltar mdza' bshes bzhin du rjes su bslabs nas ni/_/mgyogs 'gro mchog la mi mchog

[\[21b.3\]]'jigs pa'i sgra ni dor ba'i gom pa'i 'gros kyis gshegs/_/gser gyi gdu bu'i rgyan ni lag pa'i mkhrig ma la/_/ka ma la 'dras ka ma la rnams rnam byas nas/_/lus 'dud gnod sbyin rnams ni de nas 'di yis ni/_/tshim gyur lag pa'i rtse mos rmig pa rnams bzung ngo /_/lci mo'i sgo

[\[21b.7\]]gzhan rnams ni/_/'di yis nges pa shin tu dka' ba rnams rig nas/_/sprin stug gseb nas zla ba'i 'od zer 'thon pa bzhin/_/mtshan mo'i mun pa'i lam ni rab tu gsal bar byas/_/nyi ma'i mgyogs 'gro lta bu'i mgyogs 'gro de/_/yid kyis sprul pa bzhin du rnam par rgyu bzhin du/_/shing 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]rta 'dren pas gzi nyams skar ldan bar snang la/_/de ni shin tu mang po'i dpag tshad rnams su gshegs/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_phyi rol du mngon par byung ba'i le'u ste lnga pa'o//_//de nas 'gro mig nyi ma

[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]ku sha'i myu gu rno ba can/_/dka' thub nags kyi sa gang na/_/khyod kyi 'bad pa thos nas kyang /_/rta gang bdag gis blangs pa 'di/_/mgon po de ni stobs kyis ni/_/lha yis byed du bcug pa 'dra/_/khyed kyi nan tan nges pa 'di/_/rang dbang shes na gang gi phyir/_/nye 
[\[23a.2\]]bar len te mgyogs 'gro'i rta/_/ser skya'i gzhi yi mya ngan gnas/_/med pa po yis dam chos bzhin/_/rgyal po brtse ba bgres pa dang /_/sras po sdug go 'dor ba ni/_/phyag chen de ni 'os ma yin/_/dam pa'i bya ba byed bcom yin/_/yang dag bskyed pas yongs su ni/_/thang chad gnyis 
[\[23a.3\]]pa'i ma de dang /_/lha mo brjod par 'os ma yin/_/ma ning gis ni dpal thob bzhin/_/sras po byis pa yon tan ldan/_/rigs btsun bdag po'i brtul zhugs ma/_/lha mo 'dor bar 'os ma yin/_/bsngags 'os sras po grags pa 'dzin/_/snyan grags chos ni 'dzin pa'i mchog/_/zhen
