[\[23a.7\]]pa 'am/_/rtag tu rjes su brtse bcas dang /_/rtag par snying rje shes pa yi/_brtse ba spong ba tshul min te/_/phyir log bdag la bka' drin mdzod/_/de ltar mya ngan gyis non pa'i/_/'dun pa'i tshig 'di gsan gyur nas/_/rang gnas mchog tu brtan pa yis/_/smra ba'i mchog gis bka' 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]stsal to/_/'dun pa bdag dang bral ba la/_/kun nas gdung ba 'di thong cig_/lus can 'gro ba tha dad la/_/dgongs pa sna tshogs nges pa yin/_/gang yang brtse las rang gi skye/_/bdag gis rang ni mi 'dor te/_/'chi bas phan tshun dbang med par/_/bdag cag rnams ni 
[\[23b.2\]]'dor bar 'gyur/_/sred pa chen po'i sdug bsngal gyi/_/mngal gang gis ni bdag bzung ste/_/'bras med 'bad pa med de yis/_/bdag su bdag gi de su yin/_/ji ltar sgong skyes gnas shing la/_/yang dag 'dus nas rnam par bral/_/de ltar 'byung po 'dus pa ni/_/
[\[23b.3\]]nges par 'bral ba'i mtha' yin no/_/ji ltar sprin rnams 'dus nas ni/_/slar yang rnam par 'byed pa'o/_/de ltar 'du dang 'bral ba ni/_/bdag dang srog chags rnams kyi lugs/_/gang gi phyir na 'jig rten 'di/_/slus nas rnam par 'bral ba de/_/de phyir rmi lam 'dus gyur la/_/
[\[23b.4\]]bdag gi ba nyid bzod ma yin/_/shing rnams 'dab ma dmar po dang /_/lhan cig skyes te rnam par 'bral/_/gzhan dang gzhan gyi 'bral ba ni/_/'gyur ba ma yin smos ci dgos/_/de phyir de ltar gyur na ni/_/zhi ba gdung ba ma byed song /_/gal te yang ni brtse ba 'bebs/_/song nas kyang ni 
[\[23b.5\]]slar yang shog_/kho bo cag la ltos byas pa'i/_/ser skya'i gzhi na skye bo la/_/der song brtse ba thong cig dang /_/'di yi nges la nyon cig smos/_/rga dang 'chi ba zad byas nas/_/yang na myur du 'ong ba'i gtam/_/rtsom pa nyams shing don ma byas/_/yang na nyams par 'gyur ba smros/_/

[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]nas/_/khung bu dag nas sbrul bzhin zhugs nas rab tu drungs/_/ut+pa la yi 'dab ma sngon po de drangs nas/_/sna tshogs dbu rgyan dbu skrar bcas pa rab bcad cing /_/rdzing bu dag na ngang pa bzhin du rnam bkram pa/_/'od zer dang ldan bar snang dag la de gtor ro/_/
[\[24a.2\]]mchod pa mngon par 'dod phyir rnam mang bkur sti'i phyir/_/lha ni gnas pas rab tu bkram pa de bzung ste/_/ji lta ba bzhin de ni lha ni lha tshogs rnams/_/lha yi khyad par 'phags pa rnams kyis mchod pa byas/_/rgyan ni bud med ldan pa rnam par spangs nas kyang /_/dbu las dpal ni rnam par rgyang ring 
[\[24a.3\]]byas nas ni/_/nags su gser gyi ngang pa'i mtshan ma gos gzigs nas/_/brtan pa des ni gos dag mngon par 'dod par gyur/_/de nas lha ni gnas par [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]sngon po'i lus/_/dgongs pa rnam dag 'di yi dgongs pa rnams rig nas/_/kha bsgyur ngur smrig gos can nye logs mngon par 
[\[24a.4\]]'ongs/_/de la shAkya'i rgyal po skyes kyis mngon par smras/_/khyod kyi dge yi ngur smrig drang srong rgyal mtshan dang /_/'tshe ba byed pa'i gzhu 'di rigs pa ma yin te/_/de phyir zhi ba gal te 'dir ni chags med na/_/bdag la rab tu byin la 'di dang 'di snoms mdzod/_/rngon pas smras pa 'dod sbyin 
[\[24a.5\]]'dod pa snying po las/_/'di yis blo ni phab nas [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rnams gsod do/_/brgya byin mtshungs pa gang de 'dis ni don 'gyur na/_/e ma rab tu bzhes la dkar po de gsol mdzod/_/de nas de ni mchog tu rab tu dga' ba yis/_/nags su byung ba'i na bza' blangs shing gos dor la/_/
[\[24a.6\]]rngon pa nyid kyang lha yi lus nyid rnam bzung ste/_/dkar po de nyid blangs nas lha ni gshegs par gyur/_/de nas gzhon nu dang ni rta mchog skyong ba des/_/de ltar gshegs pa de la ya mtshan skyes gyur te/_/nags nas byung ba'i na bza' nyid dang de la ni/_/slar yang ri mor bya ba mang 
[\[24a.7\]]po skyen par mdzad/_/gdong ni mchi ma dang bcas 'dun pa rnams bzlog nas/_/bdag nyid chen po brtan dang grags pa 'chang ba ste/_/mtshams kyi sprin gyis g.yogs pa'i skar ma'i rgyal po bzhin/_/ngur smrig gyon zhing gdung sems gang yin de nas gshegs/_/de nas de ltar jo bo 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]rgyal srid mi bzhed par/_/dka' thub nags su gshegs pa mdog nyams snang ba na/_/mgyogs 'gro 'chang de phyag rgyas gyen du bskyod byas nas/_/shin tu rnam par ngu zhing sa la 'gyel gyur to/_/rnam par bltas nas slar yang sgra bcas par ngu zhing /_/bsngags ldan rta la lag 
[\[24b.2\]]pa dag gis nyer 'khyud nas/_/de nas kho chad yang dang yang du zlos bzhin du/_/lus kyis song bar gyur kyang sems kyis ma yin no/_/la lar rab tu sems zhing la lar rnam par du/_/la lar sa la rab tu 'brad cing la lar 'gyel/_/de nas 'gro bzhin gus pa'i dbang gis sdug bsngal 
[\[24b.3\]]zhing /_/dbang med lam na bya ba mang po byas pa'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_bsngags ldan phyir la bzlog pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o//_//de nas 'dun pa ngu zhing gdong ni mchi ma can/_/bzlog nas nags su 'dun pa'i dka' 
[\[24b.4\]]thub chags med pa/_/don kun grub pa'i lus kyis mngon par zil mnan nas/_/dka' thub gnas de grub pa bzhin du rab tu thob/_/de nas [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rgyal po'i 'gros can rgyal po'i sras po de/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]gnas der [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]bzhin du rab zhugs pa/_/phun tshogs rnams dang bral yang 
[\[24b.5\]]lus kyi phun tshogs kyis/_/dka' thub gnas ldan kun gyi mig dag phrogs par gyur/_/khur ldan rnams bzhin mgo bo rnams ni phyed dud pas/_/lag na gnya' shing bcas par 'gro gyur ya mtshan la/_/de bzhin 'khor los sgyur ba chung ma dang bcas pa/_/dbang po dang mtshungs de 
[\[24b.6\]]mthong song bar ma gyur to/_/sbyin sreg bud shing phyir ni bram ze rnams phyin nas/_/me tog lo 'dab rgyas pa'i lag pa gtsang ma rnams/_/dka' thub gtso bo rnams ni blo rnams byas na yang /_/gshegs pa mthong ste klog glar song bar ma gyur to/_/chu gter sngon po bzhin du mthong nas rma bya 
[\[24b.7\]]rnams/_/dga' zhing gyen du dud cing skad dag 'don gyur la/_/rgya skyegs [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]spyod ldan rnams dang [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rnams/_/'bru rnams bor nas mngon par gdong phyogs gnas par gyur/_/'od zer ldan pa shar ba lta bur 'bar ba yis/_/bu ram shing pa'i rigs kyi sgron ma de mthong nas/_/
[\[25a\]]
[\[25a.1\]]sbyin sreg 'jo rnams bzhos pa yin na yang /_/rab tu dga' ba skyes te rab tu 'jo gyur to/_/'di ni nor sbyin rnams kyi brgyad pa yin nam ci/_/tha skar dag gi nang nas gcig ni 'phos sam zhes/_/de mthong ba la ya mtshan skyes par gyur pa yi/_/thub pa rnams kyi 
[\[25a.2\]]tshig der mthon por sgrogs par gyur/_/lha yi khyu mchog gnyis pa'i lus po lta bu dang /_/'jig rten rgyu dang mi rgyu'i snying po lta bu dang /_/gang 'dod pa yis nyi ma mngon par babs 'dra ba/_/des ni nags tshal mtha' dag rab tu snang bar mdzod/_/de nas gdung gnas pa de rnams kyi ji lta 
[\[25a.3\]]bzhin/_/nye bar snyan gsan phab cing mngon par mchod gyur la/_/de ni chu bcas chu 'dzin lta bu'i dbyangs kyis ni/_/chos 'dzin rnams slang yang rab tu mchod par gyur/_/de nas rnam par thar bzhed brtan pa de yis ni/_/dka' thub sna tshogs rnams la nges par rnam gzigs shing /_/mtho 
[\[25a.4\]]ris mngon 'dod bsod nams byas pa'i skye bo yis/_/khyab pa'i dka' thub gnas der rjes su dpyad par gyur/_/dka' thub nor can rnams kyi dka' thub nags tshal der/_/dka' thub rnam par 'gyur ba rnams ni nges gnas nas/_/rjes su gshegs pa'i dka' thub ldan pa kha cig la/_/zhi bas 
[\[25a.5\]]de nyid rnam par shes bzhed 'di gsungs so/_/gang phyir chos kyi cho ga 'di ni mi shes pa/_/bdag gis dka' thub gnas mthong de ring snga ma'o/_/de phyir khyod kyis bdag la smra bar 'os min te/_/nges pa gang zhig gang la rab tu 'jug pa'o/_/de nas dka' thub rnam par rgyu ba'i gnyis 
[\[25a.6\]]skyes des/_/shAkya'i khyu mchog khyu mchog rnam par gnon pa po/_/de la rim gyis dka' thub khyad par rnams dang ni/_/dka' thub 'bras bu dang bcas gtam dag byas pa'o/_/grong byung bza' ba ma yin chu la rab skyes pa'i/_/'dab ma rnams dang chu dang 'bras bu rtsa ba nyid/_/
[\[25a.7\]]thub pa rnams kyi spyod pa 'di ni lung ji ltar/_/dka' thub rnams kyi rnam rtog tha dad de de'o/_/kha cig [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]bzhin du rtswa rnams la spyod la/_/gzhan rnams bor ba yis ni mkha' la rgyu ba'i bya ltar 'tsho/_/kha cig lag 'gro rnams dang lhan cig 'tsho ba ste/_/grog mkhar gyur pa 
[\[25b\]]
[\[25b.1\]]rnams ni nags na rlung gis so/_/rdo yis rab 'bad bsgrub pa'i spyod pa gzhan rnams te/_/kha cig rang gi so yis nye bar blangs shing za/_/de bzhin gzhan ni gzhan gyi don du bcos byas nas/_/gal te lhag ma yod na bya ba byed pa'o/_/chu yis gsher ba'i ral pa'i tshogs can kha 
[\[25b.2\]]cig ni/_/gsang sngags sngon 'gro lan gnyis me la sbyin sreg cing /_/kha cig nya dang mnyam par chu la rnams zhugs nas/_/rus sbal bzhin du lus rnams rab tu bskum pa'o/_/de ltar dus su bsags pa'i dka' thub nags tshogs kyi/_/mchog gis lhar 'gro gzhan gyis mi yi 'jig 
[\[25b.3\]]rten te/_/sdug bsngal lam gyis bde ba nye bar thob phyir dang /_/chos kyi rtsa ba bde ba nyid du gsungs phyir ro/_/de skad ces sogs rkang gnyis dbang po'i sras po yis/_/dka' thub nor dang ldan pa de yi tshig gsan nas/_/de nyid gzigs pa min yang ngoms par ma gyur la/_/
[\[25b.4\]]bdag tu song ba 'di ni dal bus gsungs par gyur/_/sdug bsngal bdag nyid dka' thub rnam pa du ma ste/_/dka' thub bdag gi 'bras bu mtho ris gtso bo'o/_/'jig rten thams cad yongs su 'gyur ba dang ldan la/_/nyung ngu la ngal 'di ni gdung gnas rnams kyi'o/_/phangs pa rnams 
[\[25b.5\]]dang gnyen dang yul rnams bor nas ni/_/gang zhig mtho ris phyir ni rnam par spyod byed la/_/de rnams rnam par bral te nges par 'gro 'dod la/_/slar yang shin tu 'ching ba chen po nyid yin no/_/lus kyi ngal dub gdung ba'i bya ba rnams kyis ni/_/'dod pa'i rgyu las rab tu 'jug par 'dod 
[\[25b.6\]]pa'o/_/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho'i skyon rnams yongs su mi rtog par/_/sdug bsngal gyis de sdug bsngal kho na rjes su tshol/_/'chi bas skye dgu rnams kyis rtag par 'jigs pa ste/_/'bad pa yis ni slar yang rab tu 'jug par 'dod/_/rab tu zhugs par gyur na nges par 'chi ba ste/_/'di nyid du ni 
[\[25b.7\]]gang las 'jigs pa rnams lhung ngo /_/'di yi don du gcig rnams ngal bar rab 'jug cing /_/gzhan rnams mtho ris don du ngal ba 'thob pa ste/_/bde ba'i don du sred pa'i bkren pa don ma byas/_/gson pa'i 'jig rten nges par don med nyid du lhung /_/gang zhig dman pa dor nas khyad 'phags 
[\[26a\]]
[\[26a.1\]]'gro ba can/_/'bad pa 'di ni nges par smad pa ma yin la/_/shes ldan rnams dang mnyam pa'i yongs su gdung ba yis/_/bya ba ma yin de 'dir slar yang byed pa'o/_/lus kyi gtser bas gal te 'dir ni chos yin na/_/lus kyi bde ba chos ma yin par 'gyur ba ste/_/pha rol 
[\[26a.2\]]du ni bde ba chos kyis kyang 'thob la/_/de phyir 'di yis chos kyi chos min 'bras bu'o/_/gang gi phyir na bsams pa'i dbang gis lus yin la/_/rab tu 'jug pa dang ni zlog pa dag kyang ngo /_/de phyir sems kyi dul ba nyid ni rig pa ste/_/sems las gzhan pa'i lus ni shing dang [(]mnyas [,]mnyam [)]ba'o/_/bza' ba gtsang 
[\[26a.3\]]bas gal te bsod nams 'dod na ni/_/de phyir [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rnams kyang bsod nams yod pa ste/_/phyi rol skyes bu gang dag rnams kyang 'bras bu las/_/bskal pa'i nyes pas don rnams gzhan du kha bltas so/_/bsod nams phyir ni sdug bsngal mngon par mtshams sbyor na/_/'o na sdug bsngal mngon par 
[\[26a.4\]]mtshams sbyor tshad ma min/_/de ltar gang dag las ni rnam par dag pa'i phyir/_/stegs zhes rab tu zhugs te chu la reg par byed/_/de yang de yi snying la tshim pa tsam yin te/_/chu yis sdig pa dag par byed pa ma yin phyir/_/yon tan ldan pas rig pa'i chu ni gang dang gang /_/de dang de ni sa la gal 
[\[26a.5\]]te stegs 'dod na/_/chu yang the tshom med par chu nyid kho na ste/_/de phyir yon tan ldan rnams kho na stegs su go/_/de ltar rigs pa mang po ldan pa de dang de/_/smras te rnam par mdzes pa'i nyi ma nub par gyur/_/de nas sbyin sreg du bas kha dog 'gyur ba'i shing /_/dka' thub rab tu zhi ba'i khrus sar 
[\[26a.6\]]rnam par zhugs/_/mngon par bton pa'i rab tu 'bar ba'i me yis thal ba ni/_/dbang bskur byas pa'i drang srong skye bos khyab pa ste/_/bzlas bya'i dbyangs kyis kun nas skad sgrogs lha yi tshogs/_/chos kyi lam gyi las kyi mtha' bzhin rab tu zhugs pa'o/_/de ni mtshan mo thang 'ga' mtshan mor 
[\[26a.7\]]byed 'od kyis/_/dka' thub rnams la yongs su rtog cing zhugs gyur la/_/dka' thub thams cad yongs su brtags shing mkhyen gyur na/_/dka' thub zhing gi thang de las ni gshegs par gyur/_/de'i gzugs bdag nyid chen por song ba'i yid rnams kyis/_/dka' thub gnas ldan rnams ni de nas de 
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]rjes gshegs/_/'phags min sdig pa rnams kyis zil non yul las ni/_/drang srong chen po rnams ni chos bzhin pha rol gshegs/_/de nas ral pa shing shun zur phud kyis rol ba'i/_/dka' thub nor dang ldan ba de rnams des gzigs shing /_/de dag rnams kyis dka' thub rnams 
[\[26b.2\]]la rjes brten te/_/dpal dang ldan la dge ba'i shing gi rtsa bar gnas/_/de nas dka' thub gnas ldan rnams ni nyer 'ongs nas/_/mi mchog de la yongs su bskor nas gnas gyur la/_/de dag rnams kyi rgyan pos mchod ma sngon btang ste/_/dus kyi zhi bas tshig 'di rab tu 'di smras so/_/khyod 
[\[26b.3\]]kyis byon tshe gdung gnas gang ba bzhin gyur la/_/rab tu gshegs tshe stong pa bzhin du rab tu thob/_/de phyir brtse ba 'di ni 'bor bar 'os min te/_/'tsho bar 'dod pa'i phangs pa'i tshe yis lus bzhin no/_/lha yi drang srong tshangs pa'i drang srong rgyal po yis/_/drang srong gis bsnyen bsod nams nye bar gang 
[\[26b.4\]]ldan ri/_/dka' thub nor ldan rnams kyi dka' thub de rnams ni/_/gang gi nye logs dag nas mang por 'gyur ba'o/_/de bzhin kun nas bsod nams dag gi stegs gyur la/_/nam mkha'i 'og gi them skas dag tu gyur pa ste/_/lha yi drang srong rnams dang drang srong chen po ni/_/chos kyi bdag nyid bdag nyid 
[\[26b.5\]]chen po rnams kyis bsnyen/_/'di nas slar yang khyad 'phags chos kyi ched las ni/_/byang gi phyogs nyid kho nar bsnyen par nus pa ste/_/blo dang ldan pas gom pa gcig tu gyur pa yang /_/lho yi phyogs su 'gro bar nus pa ma yin no/_/dka' thub nags 'dir ji ste bya ba ngan pa 'am/_/bsre ma'i 
[\[26b.6\]]chos sam ltung ba min nam mi gtsang bar/_/khyed kyis mthong ste gang gis khyed 'di bzhugs 'dod min/_/de ni gsung la re zhig sred par bzhugs mdzod cig_/dka' thub gter dang rab tu mtshungs par gyur pa la/_/'di rnams kyis ni dka' thub grogs su 'dod pa ste/_/dbang po dang mnyam khyed dang 
[\[26b.7\]]lhan cig gnas pa ni/_/phur bu yi yang mngon par mtho ba 'dren pa yin/_/dka' thub ldan pa'i gtso bos de skad ces smras te/_/srid pa rab tu nyams phyir dam bca' byas pa yis/_/mkhas pa'i gtso bo des ni dka' thub ldan dbus su/_/nang du song ba'i rang bzhin smras par gyur pa'o/_/'gron 
[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]la sdug cing rang gi skye bo dang mtshungs pa'i/_/drang po'i bdag nyid chos 'dzin thub pa rnams kyis ni/_/bdag la rnam pa de ltar bsam pa skyes pa yis/_/bdag gi dga' ba mchog dang bkur sti skyes par gyur/_/mdor bsdus gyur na brtse zhing bsnyen pa 'di rnams dag_/snying la 
[\[27a.2\]]song ba'i ngag rnams ngag gis khrus byas bzhin/_/kun nas 'dzin med chos can bdag gi dga' ba ni/_/de ring slar yang cung zhig g.yos par gyur pa nyid/_/de ltar rab tu zhugs pa'i khyed cag skyabs 'os rnams/_/shin tu phyogs su lhung ba yang dag bstan pa rnams/_/bor nas 'gro zhes kho 
[\[27a.3\]]bo yi yang sdug bsngal te/_/ji ltar gnyen 'dun rnams ni spangs pa de ltar ro/_/khyed cag rnams kyi chos 'di mtho ris ched du ste/_/bdag gi mngon par mtho bar yang srid ma yin phyir/_/nags der gang gi bdag ni sdod 'dod ma yin te/_/chos las ldog pa rab tu 'jug pa tha dad phyir/_/
[\[27a.4\]]de phyir nags 'di dag na gang gis yongs 'gro ba/_/bdag gi ma dga' ma yin pha rol spyod log min/_/sngon gyi dus na rjes mthun chos la gnas pa yi/_/drang srong chen po thams cad rnams dang mtshungs par gyur/_/de nas don dang ldan la bde zhing yid du 'ong /_/legs mthun mdangs dang ldan 
[\[27a.5\]]la snying po dang ldan pa/_/gzhon nu'i tshig ni dka' thub ldan pa de rnams kyis/_/thos nas khyad 'phags dang ldan bkur sti mang pos gshegs/_/de nas gnyis skyes thal ba la nyal kha cig ni/_/lus ring gtsug tor dang ldan shing shun gos gyon zhing /_/mig ni cung zad dmar ser sna ni phra la 
[\[27a.6\]{]rings[,]ring[}]/_/lag gcig spyi blugs ldan pas tshig 'di smras pa'o/_/khyod kyis nges pa nyid du rgya chen blo ldan zhing /_/gzhon nu khyed kyi skye ba la ni nyes pa gzigs/_/mtho ris dang ni nyes pa yang dag rnam spyod phyir/_/thar pa gang gi blo gros yod pa de yin no/_/mchod sbyin dka' thub nges pa de dang de 
[\[27a.7\]]rnams kyis/_/chags pa dang ldan mtho ris la ni 'gro bar 'dod/_/dgra bo bzhin du chags dang lhan cig g.yul sprad nas/_/sems pa rang ldan thar pa ru ni 'gro bar 'dod/_/de phyir gal te khyod kyi blo gros 'di nges na/_/skyen par khyed nyid 'bigs byed phug par gshegs mdzod 
[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]cig/_/gang zhig mthar thug nges par legs pa thob ba'i mig/_/thub pa 'di ni de ru mtshan mo min par gnas/_/de phyir khyod kyis de nyid lam ni thob 'gyur la/_/sred par gyur na yang dag rab tu thob par 'gyur/_/bdag gi mthong ba ji ltar blo gros de de ltar/_/de nyid kyi yang blo gros bor 
[\[27b.2\]]nas 'gro bar 'gyur/_/gsal zhing mtho ba'i shangs dang rnam rgyas yangs pa'i spyan/_/zangs mdog ya mchu ma mchu rno dkar mche ba can/_/ljags ni dmar zhing phra ba'i zhal dang ldan pa 'di/_/shes bya'i rgya mtsho mtha' dag la ni 'gro ba nyid/_/khyod kyi zab pa dang ni gting dpag dka' gang 
[\[27b.3\]]dang /_/gang zhig sgron ma nyid dang mtshan rnams gang yin pas/_/sngon gyi bskal par drang srong gis kyang ma mthong ba'i/_/slob dpon nyid de sa la mngon par dga' nas gshegs/_/dka' thub ldan pa de rnams kyis kyang de la yang /_/cho ga bzhin rjes byas nas dka' thub nags der 
[\[27b.4\]]gshegs/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_/dka' thub kyi nags su zhugs pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o//_//de nas mgyogs 'gro spyod de yid mi bde gyur te/_/de ltar jo bo nags su nga med par gshegs la/_/lam du mya ngan med pa 'bad 
[\[27b.5\]]pa byas gyur te/_/de lta na yang de yi mchi ma khogs ma gyur/_/gang la jo bo'i bka' la mtshan mo gcig gis ni/_/mgyogs 'gro de dang gshegs par gyur pa'i lam/_/lam de nyid la 'gro zhag dag ni brgyad rnams kyis/_/jo bo nyid dang bral ba de yis sems shing 'ongs/_/'thon pa med pas rgyags 
[\[27b.6\]]pa med par gyur pa yis/_/bsngags ldan mgyogs 'gro nyid kyang mdangs med rgyu ba ste/_/de ltar rgyan de rnams kyis brgyan par byas na yang /_/de yis dor bas dpal ni song ba bzhin du gyur/_/phyir log nas kyang dka' thub nags su mngon phyogs te/_/snying rje bar ni yang dang yang du drag tu 'tsher/_/
[\[27b.7\]]lam na ltogs pa ldan pa yin yang 'bru dang chu/_/ji ltar sngon bzhin mngon par ma dga' ma bslangs so/_/de nas 'gro la phan pa'i bdag nyid bdag nyid chen po des/_/ser skya zhes bya'i grong khyer rnam par dman pa la/_/nyin mor byed pas bor ba'i nam mkha' 'bras med bzhin/_/rim gyis de gnyis 
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]stong pa bzhin du nye bar song /_/pad+ma dkar po dang bcas chu yis mdzes gyur zhing /_/me tog 'dzin pa'i shing rnams kyis ni brgyan pa yang /_/grong khyer pa rnams rab tu dga' ba song gyur te/_/de yi nye ba'i nags de nags ltar mdzes ma gyur/_/de ltar lta byed mchi mas bcom pa'i 
[\[28a.2\]]mi rnams ni/_/rab tu 'bar min zhan pa'i yid kyis phyogs su skor/_/'ong du mi ster ba bzhin gnyis po de grong du/_/dal bus log pa'i khrus bzhin mngon par song bar gyur/_/shAkya'i rigs kyi drang srong dang bral 'ongs pa yis/_/lus zags 'gros dang ldan pa de gnyis mthong nas kyang /_/
[\[28a.3\]]sngon tshe shing rta bcu pa'i shing rta 'ongs pa bzhin/_/grong khyer skye bo rnams ni lam du mchi ma phyung /_/de nas mya ngan nye bar ldan ba'i skye bo dag_/mchi ma byung ba rnams kyis 'dun pa la smras pa/_/grong khyer yul 'khor dga' mdzad rgyal po'i sras po de/_/khyed kyis gang du bor zhes rgyab nas rjes su 
[\[28a.4\]]'gro/_/de nas gus ldan skye bo de la des smras pa/_/mi dbang sras po bdag gis yongs su bor ba min/_/ngu bzhin skye bo med pa'i nags su kho bo dang /_/khyim bdag cha lugs dag kyang de yis rnam par spangs/_/de yi tshig ni skye bo de rnams kyis thos nas/_/nges par shin tu dka' ba nyid 
[\[28a.5\]]ces nges par song gyur la/_/'gyel zhing mig skyes chu ni rnam par 'thor ba ste/_/yid la smod cing bdag nyid kyis ni 'bras bu long /_/de nas smras pa gnyis skyes rgyal po'i shugs mnga' ste/_/gang du gshegs pa'i nags der [{]di ring [,]de ring [}]zhugs par bgyi/_/lus can dbang po ma tshang rnams 
[\[28a.6\]]kyi ji lta bar/_/de dag bral bas bdag cag 'tshe bar 'dod pa med/_/de dang bral ba'i grong khyer 'di ni nags yin la/_/de dang yang dag ldan pa'i nags de grong khyer yin/_/lha ni lha min gsod tshe brgya byin gyis ji ltar/_/de dag bral bas bdag gi grong khyer mdzes ma gyur/_/de 
[\[28a.7\]]nas slar yang gzhon nu phyir la log ces te/_/yan lag ma rnams skar khung phreng rnams rab thob ste/_/mgyogs 'gro'i rgyab ni rnam par dben pa mthong gyur nas/_/slar yang skar khung rnams ni rnam par bkag nas ngu /_/brtul zhugs dang ni mya ngan dag gis yid dub cing /_/brtul zhugs la rab zhugs 
[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]pas sras po nyer thob phyir/_/bzlas byas shing gi lha yi khang par mi bdag gis/_/bsam pa ji ltar bya ba de dang de rnams byas/_/de nas mig dag mchi mas rab tu gang ba des/_/mgyogs 'gro blangs nas mgyogs 'gro rjes su 'gro ba ste/_/g.yul 'gyed pa na dgra bos jo bo khyer ba ltar/_/mya ngan dag gis 
[\[28b.2\]]mngon bcom mi skyong khang par zhugs/_/mi dbang rgyal po'i khab tu rnam par zhugs pa na/_/rnam par blta zhing mig dag mchi ma 'bab pa yis/_/skye bo la ni sdug bsngal rab tu rtogs 'jug bzhin/_/bsngags ldan mgyogs 'gro gsal ba'i dbyangs kyis ngus par gyur/_/de nas khang pa'i dbus na spyod pa'i bya 
[\[28b.3\]]rnams dang /_/nye bar brtags pa'i mgyogs 'gro dam par byed rnams kyis/_/mi dbang sras po log par gshegs la the tshom ste/_/mgyogs 'gro de yi dbyangs la so sor ram 'degs byas/_/ji ltar bsngags ldan mgyogs 'gro 'tsher bar byed pa ste/_/nges par gzhon nu khab tu rnam par zhugs snyam 
[\[28b.4\]]ste/_/dga' ba phul du byung bas bslus pa'i skye bo rnams/_/skye bo'i bdag po'i btsun mo'i 'khor gyi nye logs song /_/shin tu dga' ba las ni mya ngan gyis brgyal rnams/_/gzhon nu blta ba la ni g.yo ba'i spyan ma rnams/_/ston ka'i chu gter sprin las glog ni g.yo ba bzhin/_/bud med rnams ni sred 
[\[28b.5\]]pas khyim nas phyir la 'thon/_/lan bu rnam par 'phyang zhing dar gos dri ma can/_/mig rtsi dang bral mchi mas mig bcom gdong rnams kyis/_/mtshan mo zad tshe skya rengs kyis mkhar skar ma bzhin/_/byi dor dang bral bud med rid rnams mdzes ma gyur/_/rkang pa rkang gdub dang bral zangs mdog ma 
[\[28b.6\]]chags rnams/_/gdong pa rna lung dang bral mgrin pa drang po rnams/_/rang bzhin gyis rgyas brla ni 'og phab dang bral rnams/_/nu ma do shal dang bral stod g.yogs tshad rnams so/_/rten dang bral ba'i 'dun pa dang ni mgyogs 'gro nyid/_/mig ni mchi ma dang ldan de rnams kyis mthong nas/_/nags mthar khyu 
[\[28b.7\]]mchog dang bral ba glang rnams bzhin du/_/sems khral gdong ldan yan lag mchog ma rnams ni ngus/_/de nas rgyal po'i btsun mo mchi ma dang bcas par/_/be'u nyams pa'i ma he mo ltar mnyes gshin ma/_/gser gyi chu shing lo 'dab rnam par g.yo ba bzhin/_/gau ta ma mas ni lag pa bzung nas 'gyel/_/
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]dpung pa lag pa mnyam zhing gzi brjid bcom pa yis/_/bud med gzhan rnams yid mi bde bas sems med bzhin/_/mchi ma ma bton 'o dod 'bod min dbugs med par/_/g.yo bral ri mo bris pa bzhin du gnas par gyur/_/brtan min bdag po'i mya ngan gyis brgyal 
[\[29a.2\]]bud med gzhan/_/rnam par lta byed nas 'bab rgyun gyi gdong rnams kyis/_/sa 'dzin ri bo nas 'bab rgyun gyi rdo ba ltar/_/tsan+dan rab bskus nu ma rnams la rnam par 'thor/_/de rnams kyis ni mig chus btab pa'i gdon rnams kyis/_/rgyal po'i khab de de tshe mdzes par gyur pa ni/_/
[\[29a.3\]]chu gsar dus na chu gter char gyis btab pa yi/_/chu 'dzin pad+ma rnams kyis rdzing bu ji ltar ro/_/rgyas shing bar med legs par zlum pa'i sor mo can/_/rgyan bral rtsa mi mngon pa'i yan lag mchog ma rnams/_/rlung gis g.yos pa'i ljon shing rang gi 'dab mas ltar/_/pad+ma lta bu'i lag 
[\[29a.4\]]pa rnams kyis brang rnams brdungs/_/nags rlung gis sgul pad+ma lta bur g.yos pa yis/_/ngang pa rnams kyi 'khrigs pa rnams kyis chu klung ltar/_/brang brdungs rab bskyod 'brel mtho'i nu ma rnams kyis ni/_/de lta na yang mi mo de rnams mdzes par gyur/_/ji ltar brang rnams la ni lag 
[\[29a.5\]]pas zug byas pa/_/de ltar lag pa rnams la brang ni zug byed cing /_/le lo can ma brtse med stobs med ma yis der/_/lag pa'i rtse dang brang rnams phan tshun zug byas so/_/de nas khros pas rab tu dmar ba'i spyan ldan ma/_/sems khral gyis sbrel mgrin pa kha zhing ldab ldib dang /_/shugs ring phyung 
[\[29a.6\]]bas 'o ma 'dzin g.yo brtan pa yi/_/mya ngan mchi ma 'dzin pa'i grags 'dzin mas smras pa/_/mtshan mo gnyid log dbang med bdag ni rnam spangs nas/_/'dun ba 'dod pa'i shing rta de ni gang du thal/_/gsum po mnyam du song la khyod dang bsngags ldan ni/_/nye bar 'ongs pas kho mo'i yid ni g.yos par gyur/_/kho 
[\[29a.7\]]mo 'phags min brtse min bshes ma yin pa'i las/_/phul byung de 'dir byas nas da ni ci la ngu /_/mchi ma ma 'don khyod kyi sems ni sim par 'gyur/_/khyod kyi mchi ma dang ni las de gtam ma yin/_/dga' dang dbang du song dang phan dang legs pa dang /_/ci lta ba bzhin don byed khyod nyid dang 'grogs shing /_/
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]slar mi ldog pa'i ched du jo bo sras po thal/_/khyod kyis nga la 'bras bcas sems ni dga' bar gyis/_/mi yi dbang po'i dgra bo dag ni mchog yin la/_/sbyor min la mkhas shes rab med pa'i bshes mchog min/_/nges par mdza' bshes rnam par mi mkhas khyod kyis ni/_/'di yi rigs kyi nye bar nub pa 
[\[29b.2\]]chen po byas/_/rab chags dri mas 'dres shing dmar ba'i mig can ma/_/kun nas rgyan dang bral ba 'di rnams mya ngan byed/_/gangs ldan ri bo dang mnyam bdag po gsan na yang /_/bud med rnams ni yugs sa mo bzhin mdzes pa nyams/_/'di dag rnams kyang bya 'dab lag pa rnam bskyod cing /_/
[\[29b.3\]]rtag par phug ron rnams kyi ring ba'i skad rnams ni/_/de dang bral bas 'bras med pho brang 'khor dang bcas/_/gzhal med khang pa'i gtsang rnams shin tu ngu ba 'dra/_/skye bo 'di ni kun nas don med 'dod pa ste/_/bsngags ldan mgyogs 'gro de yang nges pa nyid du'o/_/mtshan mo skye bo gnyid che rkun pos rin chen 
[\[29b.4\]]bzhin/_/bdag gi 'di ltar 'di nas nor rdzas thams cad stor/_/mgyogs 'gro 'dis ni mda' yi rma srol byung rnams kyang /_/sron par nus zhes grags na lcags rnams smos ci dgos/_/bdag gi snying dang 'dra ba'i dpal ni blangs gyur nas/_/'dis ni lcags lhung 'jigs las song ba yin nam 
[\[29b.5\]]ci/_/mi dbang pho brang yongs su gang ba bzhin du ni/_/'phags pa min las da lta drag tu 'tsher bar byed/_/gang gi tshe na bdag gi dga' ba phyi rol gshegs/_/de yi tshe na mgyogs 'gro lkugs pa thang chad gyur/_/gal te skye bo dag la go bar 'tsher byed dam/_/yang na rmig pas sa 
[\[29b.6\]]la rmig pa'i sgra byed dam/_/yang na 'gram pa'i sgra ni mchog tu bskyod na ni/_/bdag la sdug bsngal 'di 'dra 'byung ba ma yin no/_/de ltar 'dir ni lha mo'i smre sngags la brten zhing /_/mchi mas 'dud pa yi ge'i tshig dag thos gyur nas/_/'og tu gdong phyogs mchi ma dang bcas thal sbyar te/_/
[\[29b.7\]]'dun pas lan 'di dal bus smras par gyur pa'o/_/lha mo bsngags ldan la ni smod par 'os min zhing /_/kho bo la yang khro bar mdzad pa 'os ma yin/_/kho bo cag gi skyon min lha mo mkhyen par mdzod/_/mi yi lha de lha bzhin bkra shis kun gyis gshegs/_/bdag gis rgyal po'i brtan pa 
[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]shes pa yin na yang /_/lha nyid gang dag gis ni stobs las byas pa bzhin/_/bdag gis mgyogs 'gro 'di ni nye bar 'ongs pa de/_/lam na ngal ba med par de ltar rjes su song /_/mgyogs 'gro mchog 'di nyid kyang gshegs tshe sa la ni/_/bar snang dag la 
[\[30a.2\]]bzung bzhin rmig pas ma reg cing /_/de bzhin lha yi ched du gdong ni sngogs byas bzhin/_/'gram pa'i sgra ni byas min 'tsher ba dag kyang med/_/gang las sa skyong bdag nyid skyes pa gshegs pa na/_/de tshe sgo ni rang nyid bye bar gyur pa ste/_/mtshan mo'i mun pa nyi mas bzhin du bsal gyur te/_/
[\[30a.3\]]de yi phyir yang lha yi cho ga de zung shig_/gang phyir mi dbang bka' las skye bo stong phrag dag_/khang pa dang ni grong khyer nyid du bag yod kyang /_/de tshe de ni gnyid kyis phrogs shing ma sad de/_/de yi phyir yang lha yi cho ga de zung shig/_/gang phyir nags na gnas la mthun pa'i gos dag ni/_/lha na gnas 
[\[30a.4\]]pas dus su 'di la byin gyur la/_/rgyan dag mkha' la rab tu gtor ba de khyer te/_/de yi phyir yang lha yi cho ga de zung shig/_/de phyir de ltar lha mo nged gnyis la skyon du/_/ji ltar song ba de la rtog par 'os ma yin/_/mgyogs 'gro 'di dang bdag gi 'dod pa mi mdzad par/_/de ni lha rnams dag gis rjes 
[\[30a.5\]]'gro byas shing gshegs/_/de ltar bdag nyid chen po de yis rab gshegs pa/_/lha yi mtshar mang bud med de rnams kyis thos nas/_/rab tu gshegs las yid la 'bar ba yang 'thob ste/_/mya ngan rab tu nyams bzhin ngo mtshar ldan par thal/_/de nas yid mi bde bas yongs lding mig can ma/_/phrug 
[\[30a.6\]]gu rab tu nyams pa'i 'ol ba sdug bsngal bzhin/_/rmongs gyur nyid kyang mchi ma dang ldan gdong gis smras/_/rlabs chen dang ldan 'jam zhing dkar min dge ba ste/_/so so so sor rtsa ba nas 'khrungs gyen du 'khyil/_/de yid bu skyes sa la 'thor ba de rnams ni/_/mi dbang dbu rgyan la ni yongs su dkri bar 'os/_/
[\[30a.7\]]phyag ni rab 'phyang [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rgyal po'i stobs mnga' zhing /_/khyu mchog chen po'i spyan ldan gser 'bar 'od mnga' ba/_/'brug dang rnga yi sgra ldan sku stod rnam par yangs/_/de ltar gyur kyang dka' thub gnas su gnas par 'os/_/sor mo legs skyes 'jam zhing dra bas 'brel pa dang /_/long bu mi mngon pad+ma'i 
[\[30b\]]
[\[30b.1\]]rtsa ba ltar 'jam la/_/dbus na 'khor lo dang bcas zhabs ni de dag gis/_/drang srong rnams kyi nags mtha'i sa la 'gro 'am ci/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]logs na khri dang gdan la 'os pa ste/_/rin chen na bza' lci min tsan+dan gyis spras shing /_/de yi lus ni mdangs dang ldan pa nags dag tu/_/grang dang dro dang 
[\[30b.2\]]chu 'od rnams su bskor ram ci/_/rigs dang gzugs dang stobs dang sems pa dag dang ni/_/thos dang phun sum tshogs dang na tshod kyis khengs shing /_/slong bar ma yin ster ba nyid du mngon 'ongs pa/_/de ni pha rol dag la slong mo rgyu 'am ci/_/gser khri dang zhing gzims mal dag la gzims nas ni/_/
[\[30b.3\]]mtshan mo sil snyan sgra rnams dag gis rab slong ba/_/gos kyi phyogs gcig bar na sa yi logs la ni/_/bdag gi brtul zhugs ldan de de ring ji ltar gzims/_/snying rje'i smre sngags rab tu zlos pa 'di thos nas/_/bud med de rnams phan tshun lag pas yongs 'khyud de/_/ljon shing g.yos las me 
[\[30b.4\]]tog las ni sbrang rtsi bzhin/_/rnam par lta byed dag nas chu dag 'thor ba'o/_/de nas ngang pa med pa'i ngang mo bzhin du ni/_/grags 'dzin ma ni nor 'dzin sa la 'gyel gyur la/_/rnam par 'khrugs shing yang dang yang du ldab ldib kyi/_/skad kyis de dang de dag rnam par smras pa'o/_/chos dang lhan 
[\[30b.5\]]cig spyod pa mgon med kho mo ni/_/de yis dor nas gal te chos ni byed 'dod cing /_/chos dang lhan cig spyod ma dang bral dka' thub gang /_/yongs su longs spyod 'dod 'di'i chos ni ga la zhig/_/sngon gyi sa skyong rnams des nges par ma mthong ste/_/legs mthong chen po la sogs mes po chen 
[\[30b.6\]]po rnams/_/bdag po ldan ma dang bcas nags su gshegs gyur la/_/kho mo dang bral chos byed 'dod pa de ltar ltos/_/mchod sbyin du 'am rig byed cho gas 'dus byas shing /_/pho mo gnyis la dbang bskur de yi 'bras bu ni/_/gzhan du mnyam por longs spyod 'dod pa ma mthong ste/_/de nas 
[\[30b.7\]]bdag la 'di yi chos la ser sna skyes/_/bdag nyid la ni sdud dang phan tshun phrag dog cing /_/yang yang rtsod par chos 'ga' des ni nges par shes/_/khro ba kho mo spangs nas dbang chen 'jig rten du/_/'jigs med lha mo las ni dga' ba len par 'dod/_/de la lus kyi yon tan bzung ba'i 
[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]btsun mo de/_/ci 'dra zhig gam 'di ni bdag gis bsam mno ste/_/bdag gi gus pa nyid dang bud med bor nas ni/_/nags su gang phyir des ni dka' thub rnams la gdung /_/nges par bdag gi 'dod 'di mtho ris bde phyir min/_/sems ldan skye bo de ni rnyed par dka' ba min/_/
[\[31a.2\]]sdug pa des kyang bdag la 'di 'am gzhan du rung /_/gang gis ma spangs zhes ni bdag gi yid la re/_/gal te bdag ni jo bo'i gzigs byed yangs pa'i zhal/_/gtsang zhing bzhad pa gyen du blta ba'i skal med na/_/skal ba dman par 'os min sgra gcan 'dzin de yang /_/pha yi pang na 
[\[31a.3\]]yongs su gnas par nam zhig 'gyur/_/e ma yid mnga' 'di yi lus ni shin tu gzhon/_/yid ni mi rnams kyis bsngags shin tu sra ba ste/_/ngag gi skad grag rab zos dgra yang dga' ba yi/_/bu ni byis pa 'di 'dra kye ma spong bar byed/_/bdag gi 'dod pa'i sems kyang shin tu sra ba 
[\[31a.4\]]ste/_/rdo las gyur ram yang na lcags las byas pa 'am/_/bder 'os jo bo dpal dor nags su song ba la/_/mgon med ldan pa gang zhig dum bur ma song ngo /_/de lhar lha mo bdag po'i mya ngan gyis brgyal te/_sems shing rnam zlos lan cig ma yin 'di dus la/_/rang bzhin brtan yang dam 
[\[31a.5\]]pa mo de mya ngan gyis/_/brtan pa dran pa ma yin ngo tsha ma byas so/_/de nas de ltar mya ngan gyis smra rnam 'khrugs pa'i/_/grags 'dzin ma ni sa la 'gyel ba mthong nas ni/_/char gyis btang bas pad+ma chen po bzhin du ni/_/mchi bcas gdong gis bud med rnams ni cho nges 'debs/_/bzlas brjod 
[\[31a.6\]]rdzogs shing sbyin sreg bkra shis byas gyur te/_/mi skyong nyid kyang lta gang dag nas phyir gshegs pa/_/rdo rje'i sgra yis glang po ji bzhin skye ba yi/_/nyam thag sgra des kun nas bcom zhing g.yos par gyur/_/'dun pa dang ni bsngags ldan gnyis po mthong nas kyang /_/sras kyi nges par brten pa 
[\[31a.7\]]yang dag gsan nas ni/_/bde sogs bdag po'i ltad mo zin pa'i rgyal mtshan bzhin/_/mya ngan gyis bcom sa yi bdag po 'gyel bar gyur/_/de nas sras po'i mya ngan gyis rmongs yud tsam zhig/_/skye bo mtshungs mnyam mngon pa'i skye bos bzung gyur la/_/chab kyis gang ba'i spyan gyis mgyogs 
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]bgro la gzigs nas/_/sa yi logs nas sa skyong gis ni sme sngags bton/_/g.yul 'gyed pa na dga' chen mang po byas nas ni/_/bsngags ldan khyod kyis bdag gi dga' ba ma yin byas/_/bdag gi dga' ba yon tan dga' de gang gis ni/_/dga' ba yin yang dga' ba med bzhin nags 
[\[31b.2\]]su bor/_/de phyir de nyid gang du gshegs pa der bdag khyer/_/yang na de ni slar yang skyen par khyer la shog_/tshabs cher na ba'i sman dang bral bas de bzhin du/_/de dang bral bas bdag gi 'tsho ba med pa'o/_/rgyal bu gser 'bab 'chi bas phrogs par gyur pa na/_/dka' thub gang 
[\[31b.3\]]las yang dag rgyal ba shi ma gyur/_/bdag kyang chos 'dzin sras po gshegs par gyur pa na/_/bdag nyid thar 'dod bdag nyid med pa lta bur gyur/_/rgyal rigs bcu byas khyab bdag skye dgu'i bdag po ni/_/mchog dang mchog min shes pa nyi ma'i sras po yi/_/bu ni phangs shing sdug bsngal dam 
[\[31b.4\]]pa dang bral la/_/yid mi bde byas yid kyi rmongs pa gang las min/_/rgyal po a dza'i sras po blo dang ldan pa ni/_/mi bdag dbang po'i grogs la bdag gi 'dod pa ste/_/sras po nags su song la gang zhig lhar song la/_/mchi ma don yod ma yin bkren pas 'chos so kye/_/
[\[31b.5\]]khyod kyis bdag gi chu yi snyim pa de gang du/_/phrogs pa'i dka' thub gnas der bzang po bdag la smros/_/de la bdag gi skom pa 'dis ni yongs 'dod de/_/bdag gi srog ni [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]'gro bar 'dod/_/de ltar sras dang bral ba'i sdug bsngal skyes gyur te/_/sa dang 'dra ba'i 
[\[31b.6\]]lhan skyes brtan pa rnam spangs nas/_/rA ma'i mya ngan dag gis shing rta bcu pa bzhin/_/mi skyong 'du shes bral bzhin mang po rnam par labs/_/de nas de la thos dang dul dang yon tan ldan/_/rab bgres mdun na 'don dang blon po blo gros kyis/_/mnyam par gzhag 'di ji lta ba bzhin 
[\[31b.7\]]smas gyur te/_/yongs su gdung ba'i gdong min mya ngan med pa'ang min/_/mi mchog brtan pa mya ngan thong la brtan par gshegs/_/mya ngan bzhin du mchi ma 'don par 'os ma yin/_/me tog phreng ba rnying bzhin phun tshogs bor nas ni/_/sa na mi skyong mang po nags su shin tu song /_/gzhan yang 
[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]de yi dgongs pa nges pa 'di sngon tshe/_/drang srong dkar ming de yi tshig ni dran par mdzod/_/mtho ris su yang 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal srid la/_/skad cig kyang de gnas par bde bas nus ma yin/_/gal te yang de mi mchog 'bad pas byas pa ste/_/myur bar bka' lung stsol 
[\[32a.2\]]mdzod ji snyed 'dir 'gro bgyi/_/sna tshogs rnams kyi khyed kyi sras po de yi ni/_/sna tshogs mang po'i 'thab mo de rnyed der byed shog_/de nas de gnyis la ni mi bdag gis bslabs la/_/de phyir nges par 'di nas khyed gnyis mngon par song /_/bu la lhag par chags pa'i nags kyi bya yi bzhin/_/bdag 
[\[32a.3\]]gi sems ni dga' bar rab tu song ma yin/_/dam pa zhes ni mi dbang bstan las blon po dang /_/mdun na 'don pa de gnyis nags der song bar gyur/_/byas zhes gna' ma'i skye bo dang bcas btsun mor bcas/_/mi skyong gis kyang bya ba lhag ma rab tu mdzad/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya 
[\[32a.4\]]ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_btsun mo'i 'khor smre sngags bton pa'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o//_//de nas de'i tshe mdun na 'don dang blon po de/_/mi skyong mchi ma'i lcag gis mngon par bsnun pa dag_/phug pa'i dam pa'i rta bzhin 'bad pa thams cad las/_/
[\[32a.5\]]bsam pa bzang pos skyen par nags der song bar gyur/_/'dra ba'i rjes 'brangs ngal ba skyes pa de gnyis ni/_/dka' thub nags der dus su nye bar phyin nas ni/_/rgyal po'i rdzu 'phrul dor de dul ba'i spyod pa yis/_/drang srong shes ldan bu yi khang par nye bar song /_/de dag gis ni tshul 
[\[32a.6\]]bzhin bram ze de mchod nas/_/de ni de dag la yang rjes mthun mchod pa ste/_/gdan byas pa dag bdag tu lta ba bcad nas ni/_/shes ldan sras po gdan gnas pa la gtam smras so/_/mdangs gtsang gtsang zhing rnam par grags pa'i yangs pa mnga'/_/bu ram 
[\[32a.7\]]shing pa'i rigs su rab 'khrungs rgyal po yis/_/shru ka rab tu 'dzin dang gsang sngags yongs 'dzin la/_/nag por skye bo 'di ni khyod kyis rig par mdzod/_/dbang po dang mtshungs de yi rgyal ba dang mtshungs sras/_/rga dang 'chi ba'i 'jigs pa dag ni sgrol 'dod de/_/
[\[32b\]]
[\[32b.1\]]'di na mngon par nye bar 'ongs grag de yi phyir/_/bdag cag nye bar 'ongs par bcom ldan mkhyen par mdzod/_/de dag la des smras pa phyag ring gyur pa de/_/rab tu thob gyur gzhon nu shes pa min pa min/_/chos 'di 'khor bar byed ces mngon par mkhyen nas ni/_/
[\[32b.2\]]mngon par gdong phyogs ma yin thar bzhed skyen par gshegs/_/de nas de las de nyid de dag nyer thob nas/_/bram ze de la snyan dngags phab nas de ma thag_/dub kyang ma dub pa bzhin rgyal po la gus pas/_/de nyid gang nas gshegs pa de nas rab tu song /_/song ste de nas de dag 
[\[32b.3\]]gi ni rngon pa dang /_/rnam par bral ba'i sku lus rab tu 'bar ba de/_/nyi ma stug po'i longs spyod dag la zhugs pa ltar/_/lam ni shing gi rtsa bar nye bar bzhugs pa mthong /_/de nas de la mdun na 'don dang sngags 'dzin bcas/_/theg pa rnam par spangs nas nye bar gshegs pa ste/_/
[\[32b.4\]]slar gnas thub pa'i bu ni g.yon gyi lhar bcas pas/_/nags gnas lhag spyod lta bar 'dod pa ji ltar ro/_/lha na pa ba sangs dang phur bus dbang chen bzhin/_/de dag gis ni de nyid bskyed cing mchod pa'o/_/lha na pa sangs phur bu dag la dbang chen bzhin/_/mchod 'os des ni de gnyis 
[\[32b.5\]]dag la slar mchod do/_/sbyor bar song ba'i zla ba nye sar nab so bzhin/_/de nas kun nas mngon par rjes gnang byas pa ni/_/de dag shA kya'i rigs kyi rgyal mtshan de yi ni/_/nye logs dag tu song ba rnam par mdzes par gyur/_/shing gi rtsa bar gnas pa mngon par 'bar ba yi/_/rgyal po'i 
[\[32b.6\]]sras po de la mdun na 'don gyis smras/_/lha na yongs 'dus dag la brgya byin sras po ni/_/rgyal ba nye bar gnas la phur bu ji ltar ro/_/khyod kyi mya ngan zug rngu snying la btags pa na/_/sa yi logs la rmongs shing brgyal bar gyur pa ste/_/gzhon nu mig gi chu yi char ltar rgyal po yis/_/
[\[32b.7\]]gang zhig khyod la smras pa 'di ni mkhyen par mdzod/_/khyod kyis chos la rab dmigs nges par shes pa ste/_/khyod kyi 'byung bar 'gyur pa'i don ni shes pa'o/_/khyod kyi dus ma yin par nags su bsnyen las bdag_/me dang rab mtshungs mya ngan me yis tshig pa'o/_/de phyir tshur 
[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]spyon chos dga' dag gi dga' ston du/_/chos kyi don du blo gros de nyid thong mdzod dang /_/chu bo'i shugs kyis 'gram ngogs mngon par bcom pa bzhin/_/bdag la mya ngan shugs 'dir rab tu 'phel ba'o/_/rlung dang nyi ma me dang thog chen rnams kyi ni/_/'jug 
[\[33a.2\]]pa gang zhig sprin dang chu me ri rnams su/_/rnam par 'byer dang skems dang srog dang 'byed rnams kyis/_/bdag cag rnams la mya ngan gyis 'jug pa de byed/_/de phyir re zhig nor 'dzin bdag nyid longs spyod la/_/dus su bstan bcos mthong ste nags su gshegs gyur te/_/'byung po 
[\[33a.3\]]thams cad la ni brtse ba chos yin phyir/_/mi 'dod gnyen 'dun kho bo la ni ltos par mdzod/_/chos de nags nyid kho nar 'grub pa ma yin te/_/sdom brtson rnams kyis nges par grong khyer na yang 'grub/_/'dir ni blo dang 'bad pa dag ni mtshan ma ste/_/nags dang stag ni 'jigs pa dag gi 
[\[33a.4\]]mtshan ma'o/_/dbu rgyan 'dzin zhing dpung par rna cha do shal dang /_/dpung rgyan rnam par rengs pa'i phyag mnga' mi dbang rnams/_/phun tshogs yan lag dbus na yongs su gnas rnams kyis/_/khyim na gnas kyang thar pa'i lam ni rab tu thob/_/bstan pa'i nu bo 
[\[33a.5\]]stobs dang rdo rje'i lag pa dang /_/rnam mdzes bu dang chu stod de nas mthar gnas lha/_/lus 'phags rgyal po skyes byed dang ni de bzhin du/_/grong dang ljon shing can dang sde rgyal rgyal po rnams/_/nyes legs chos kyi cho ga la ni rnam dul ba'i/_/mi bdag khyim na gnas pa de rnams 
[\[33a.6\]]mkhyen mdzod la/_/nor gyi bdag po dang ni mi skyong dpal dag ste/_/gnyis po dag kyang de phyir dus gcig mkhyen par mdzod/_/mngon par dbang bskur byas pa'i chu yis brlan pa nyid/_/gdung ba skyob pa'i gdugs bzung khyed la drag tu ni/_/nye bar 'khyud nas kun nas gyen du lta ba ste/_/dga' 
[\[33a.7\]]ba des ni nags su rab tu 'jug par 'dod/_/de ltar mchi mas 'dud pa'i yi ge'i tshig gis ni/_/khyed nyid la ni sa yi bdag pos smras pa ste/_/thos nas de la brtse zhing de la dga' don du/_/khyed kyi brtse bas rjes su 'gro bar 'os pa'o/_/khyod las byung ba'i mya ngan chu ni gting med pa'i/_/
[\[33b\]]
[\[33b.1\]]sdug bsngal rgya mtshor shAkya'i rgyal po nub gyur te/_/de phyir mgon po rgya mtshor bying la gru bzhin du/_/dman zhing rten dang bral ba de ni sgrol mdzod cig_/chu bo gang gA'i lto nas byung ba 'jigs byed dang /_/lhag spyod dang ni ngan spong sras po lhag spyod kyis/_/pha yi dga' ba'i don du 
[\[33b.2\]]las byas thos nas ni/_/khyed kyang yab kyi bzhed pa mdzad par 'os pa lags/_/gnyen bshes be'u nyams par gyur pa'i ba bzhin du/_/rgyun mi 'chad par nyam thag snying rjer ngu ba mo/_/drang srong ri byi bsnyen zhing phyogs su ma song ba/_/yang dag skyed par byed pa'i lha mo mkhyen par mdzod/_/
[\[33b.3\]]ngang pa rnam par bral ba'i ngang mo lta bu dang /_/nags na glang po dang bral glang mo lta bu ni/_/mgon dang bcas kyang mgon med dman zhing nyam thag pa'o/_/btsun mo gzigs pa yis ni skyob par 'os pa yin/_/sras gcig byis pa sdug bsngal 'os pa ma yin pa/_/kun nas gdung 
[\[33b.4\]]ba mthar gyur pa ni 'dren pa ste/_/gang ba'i zla ba sgra gcan nye bar bsgyur las bzhin/_/sgra gcan 'dzin de gnyen gyi mya ngan las thar mdzod/_/pho brang btsun mo'i 'khor dang grong khyer mtha' dag ni/_/khyed dang bral ba'i bud shing can gyi mya ngan me/_/phyir 'byung dbugs ni du ba mun pa'i 
[\[33b.5\]]rtse mo yis/_/tshig pa dag ni khyed la lta ba'i chu 'dod do/_/mdun na 'don pa de yi tshig dag gsan gyur nas/_/byang chub sems dpa' yongs su gang ba'i sems dpa' des/_/yon tan ldan zhing yon tan shes pas yud tsam zhig_/bsam gtan mdzad nas rigs pa'i lan 'di 
[\[33b.6\]]smras pa'o/_/pha rnams kyi ni bu la dgongs pa shes pa ste/_/gang zhig bdag la sa skyong gi ni khyad par du/_/shes pa yin yang na dang rga dang rgud pa las/_/'jigs te nags su phyin nas rang gi skye bo dor/_/mthar ni gal te dga' ba dang bral ma yin na/_/rang gi skye bo dga' ba lta 'dod cis 
[\[33b.7\]]ma yin/_/gang tshe yun ring gyur kyang rnam par 'bral ba ste/_/de phyir bla ma brtse ba yin yang 'dor bar bgyi/_/gang yang bdag gi ched du mi yi bdag po yi/_/mya ngan khyod kyis smras des bdag la dga' ba med/_/gang phyir rmi lam yang dag 'dus par gyur rnams su/_/rnam par 'bral 
[\[34a\]]
[\[34a.1\]]bar 'gyur la kun nas gdung bar byed/_/'gro ba'i spyod pa rnam pa sna tshogs mthong nas ni/_/de bzhin du yang khyod kyi blo gros nges par song /_/bu min gnyen min kun nas gdung ba'i rgyu yin bzhin/_/'ching ba de ni shes pa med pa'i mtshan ma nyid/_/lam du 'gro rnams 'dir ni 
[\[34a.2\]]'dus pa ji lta ba'i/_/skye dgu rnams ni dus su nges par 'bral ba ste/_/gnyen 'dun dam 'cha' skye bos rnam par dman pa yi/_/shes rab ldan pa'i skye bos mya ngan bsnyen nam ci/_/gzhan du rang gi skye bo dor nas 'dir 'ongs la/_/'dir yang rab tu bslus nas slar yang 'gro ba ste/_/der song nas 
[\[34a.3\]]kyang pha rol gzhan du 'gro 'gyur la/_/de ltar 'dor ldan skyo la rjes su brten nam ci/_/gang tshe mngal nas bzung ste gnas skabs thams cad du/_/gsod pa'i phyir ni 'chi ba rab tu gnas pa ste/_/bu la dga' ba der ni khyed kyis smras pa yi/_/bdag gi nags su brten pa gang phyir dus ma yin/_/
[\[34a.4\]]yul la mngon par lhung tshe dus ma yin pa de/_/de bzhin nor gyi cho gar zhugs pa dus yin no/_/dus rnams kun du dus kyi 'gro ba khyab pa ste/_/dge legs nges par thob pa dus yod ma yin no/_/gang yang rgyal pos bdag la rgyal srid 'dir bzhad la/_/de yang yab kyi rgya 
[\[34a.5\]]chen mtshungs shing 'dra ba ste/_/sred pa las ni nad pa'i mi 'phrod zas bzhin du/_/bdag gis rab tu 'dzin par nus pa ma yin no/_/gti mug rmongs pa'i gzhi ma mi skyong rgyal po nyid/_/rab tu 'thob par mi ni mkhas pas gang phyir nus/_/gang du skyo ba dang bcas rgyags dang 
[\[34a.6\]]ngal dub dang /_/pha rol log par spyod pas ches la gnod pa'o/_/'dzam bu chu gser bzhin du gsal ba'i lding khang ni/_/dug dang 'dres pa'i kha zas bzang po lta bu dang /_/gza' yis 'khrugs pa'i pad+ma dang bcas chu lta bu/_/rgyal srid dang ni gser dag sdug bsngal dag gi 
[\[34a.7\]]rten/_/de ltar rgyal srid chos min bde ba ma yin pa/_/ji ltar mi gtsang skyes te mi dbang snga ma rnams/_/sdug bsngal yongs su spang bya na tshod rab mchog tshe/_/rgyal srid nges par dor nas nags su song bar gyur/_/rin chen bzhin du tshim pa mchog ni nyer mkhyud nas/_/'brog 
[\[34b\]]
[\[34b.1\]]dgon dag na rtswa dag zos pa mchog yin la/_/sbrul nag bzhin du mthong ba ma yin nyes pa ni/_/rnyed bla'i dpal dang lhan cig nyal ba mchog ma yin/_/rgyal srid rnam par spangs pa rgyal po rnams kyi chos/_/mngon par 'dod pas nags su rab 'jug bsngags pa ste/_/nags 
[\[34b.2\]]tshal yongs su dor nas khyim du rab 'jug pa/_/dam bca' nye bar bcom pa'i 'thad pa ma yin no/_/rigs su skyes shing sems pa dang bcas mi gang gis/_/chos ni mngon par 'dod pas nags su rab zhugs la/_/ngur smrig rab tu dor nas ngo tsha rnam spangs nas/_/grong khyer 'joms pa yi 
[\[34b.3\]]yang grong la bsnyen ma yin/_/sred dang rnam par rmongs dang yang na 'jigs pa yis/_/gang zhig skyugs pa'i bza' ba slar yang len byed la/_/sred pa de ni rmongs dang yang na 'jigs pa yis/_/'dod pa rnams ni dor nas slar yang len par byed/_/gang zhig rab 'bar khang pa dag nas ci zhig 
[\[34b.4\]]ltar/_/byung nas slar yang de nyid kho nar rab 'jug la/_/nyes pa mthong de khyim na gnas pa dor nas ni/_/rmongs pas slar yang len par mngon par 'dod pa'o/_/gang yang mi skyong khyim na gnas pas thar pa ni/_/'thob pa dang ldan zhes thos de ni med pa ste/_/zhi ba gtso bo'i thar pa'i chos ni gang la yang /_/
[\[34b.5\]]chad pa gtso bo'i rgyal po'i chos ni gang du'o/_/zhi ba dga' ba gal te rgyal srid dman pa ste/_/rgyal srid la blo gal te zhi ba rnam par nyams/_/grang dang dro dang me dang chu yi gcig nyid bzhin/_/zhi ba dang ni rno nyid 'thad pa ma yin no/_/me dang chu yi 'du ba yod pa ma yin la/_/khram dang drang 
[\[34b.6\]]po'i 'du ba yod pa ma yin zhing /_/'phags dang sdig pa'i 'du ba yod pa ma yin te/_/zhi dang chad pa'i 'du ba yod pa ma yin no/_/de phyir sa 'dzin bdag po de rnams nges pas sam/_/rgyal po'i srid rnams spangs te zhi ba thob pa can/_/rgyal srid dag la bsnyen nam dbang po sbas nyid las/_/
[\[34b.7\]]mthar ma thug par thar par mngon pa'i nga rgyal byas/_/de dag rnams kyi rgyal srid la zhi ji lta bzhin/_/bdag ni nags tshal rab thob nges pas ma yin zhing /_/khyim dang gnyen gyi 'du shes zhags pa bcad nas ni/_/thar gyur slar yang rab tu 'jug par 'dod ma yin/_/de ltar bdag 
[\[35a\]]
[\[35a.1\]]nyid rnam shes yon tan rjes mthun pa/_/re 'dod spangs shing gtan tshigs ldan pa sgrogs pa ste/_/mi dbang bdag nyid skyes kyi smra ldan thos gyur nas/_/gsang sngags 'dzin pa yis kyang lan ni smras pa'o/_/gang zhig chos kyi cho gar nges pa khyod kyi 'di/_/
[\[35a.2\]]rig pa min pa ma yin 'di ni dus rig min/_/na tshod la gnas yab ma mya ngan phyir byin nas/_/chos 'dod khyod kyi chos ma yin par gyur pa'o/_/nges par khyed kyi blo yang shin tu phra min pa/_/yang na chos dang don dang 'dod par mkhas ma min/_/gang zhig ma mthong 'bras bu'i don du khyed nyid 
[\[35a.3\]]kyis/_/mngon sum don ni yongs su brnyas par mdzad nas gshegs/_/yang srid yod ces kha cig dag ni smra ba ste/_/med ces kha cig dag ni nges par dam 'cha'o/_/de ltar gang tshe don 'di the tshom gyur pa ste/_/de phyir longs spyod par nus dpal ni nye bar gnas/_/gal te 
[\[35a.4\]]slar yang rab tu 'jug pa gang yang yod/_/der ni ji ltar 'bad pa dag la dga' bar bgyi/_/ci ste gzhan du rab tu 'jug pa gang yang med/_/'gro ba 'di yi 'bad rtsol med las thar pa 'grub/_/kha cig 'jig rten pha rol yod ces smra ba ste/_/thar pa'i sbyor ba la ni bsngags pa byed 
[\[35a.5\]]ma yin/_/ji ltar me yi dro dang chu yi gsher ba nyid/_/de ltar rab tu 'jug par rang bzhin nyid du smra/_/dge dang mi dge nyid dang srid dang srid min la/_/kha cig rang bzhin las zhes bsngags pa byed pa ste/_/gang phyir 'di dag thams cad rang bzhin las yin te/_/de yi phyir 
[\[35a.6\]]yang rab tu 'bad pa don med 'gyur/_/gang zhig dbang po rnams kyis nges par rab spyod pa/_/yul rnams la ni dga' ba dang ni mi dga' nyid/_/gang zhig dga' dang nad rnams dag gis sbyor byed de/_/de la gang gis 'bad dam de ni rang bzhin no/_/chu rnams kyis ni me rnams 
[\[35a.7\]]zhi bar nye 'gro zhing /_/'od zer rnams kyis chu ni skams par byed pa ste/_/lus la yang dag gnas pa'i 'byung ba tha dad rnams/_/gcig nyid gyur nas 'gro ba dag ni 'dren par byed/_/gang zhig lag pa rkang pa lto rgyab mgo rnams kyis/_/mngal du song ba'i ngo bo nges par sgrub byed la/_/
[\[35b\]]
[\[35b.1\]]gang tshe bdag nyid de yi de yis sbyor ba ste/_/rang bzhin las byung de shes rnams kyis de smra'o/_/bya dang [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rnams kyi sna tshogs ngo bo dang /_/tsher ma'i rnon po nyid du rab tu byed pa su/_/rab tu 'jug pa 'di kun rang bzhin las yin la/_/'dod pa byed pa yod min 'bad pa gang las 
[\[35b.2\]]so/_/de bzhin gzhan rnams dbang phyug las skyes smra ba ste/_/der ni skyes bu'i rab tu 'jug pa la don ci/_/'gro ba'i rab tu 'jug la gang nyid rgyu yin zhing /_/ldog pa la ni nges par de nyid rgyu yin no/_/kha cig bdag gi mtshan ma nyid du smra ba ste/_/rab tu skye ba nyid dang srid pa zad 
[\[35b.3\]]pa yang /_/'bad pa med las rab tu skye bar smra ba ste/_/'bad pa yis ni thar pa 'thob par smra ba'o/_/lha yi mchod sbyin drang srong rnams kyi rig byed dang /_/pha yi bu lon med pa bu rnams kyis te mi/_/gang gi grol ba yod pa de yi thar pa zhes/_/bu lon gsum po de dang lhan cig skyes 
[\[35b.4\]]pa'o/_/de ltar cho ga'i bya ba'i rim pa 'di yis ni/_/'bad rtsol bcas pa'i thar pa de shes rnam smra la/_/'bad rtsol ldan pa yin yang rnam par gnon pa yis/_/thar pa 'dod pa rnams ni ngal ba thob par byed/_/de phyir zhi ba gal te thar la gus yod na/_/cho gar brjod pa ji ltar 
[\[35b.5\]]rigs pas bsnyen mdzod dang /_/'di yi nye bar 'thad pa de ltar gyur pa ste/_/mi yi bdag po'i kun nas gdung ba nyams par 'gyur/_/dka' thub nags tshal dag nas khang par 'jug pa la/_/khyod kyi skyon gyi blo gros gang zhig rab zhugs te/_/yab gcig der yang khyod kyi bsam par ma gyur 
[\[35b.6\]]dang /_/snga ma rnams kyang nags nas rang gi khang par gshegs/_/dka' thub nags na gnas pa bu rnams kyis bskor te/_/rgyal po a ma ri Sha grong du zhugs gyur la/_/de bzhin 'phags min rnams kyis sa ni rnam bcom pa/_/dka' thub nags nas 'ongs nas rangs byed kyis gsungs 
[\[35b.7\]]so/_/de bzhin rdo ba dang bcas bdag po'i ljon shing ming /_/nags gnas bu dang bcas pa grong du bzhugs pa ste/_/tshangs pa'i drang srong gyur dang thub pa gnas 'jog las/_/sbyin sreg dang bcas mtha' can lha yis dpal bzung ngo /_/rnam pa de ltar chos dang grags par rab bzhugs pa/_/
[\[36a\]]
[\[36a.1\]]nags rnams bor nas khang khyim rnams su song ba ste/_/de phyir dka' thub nags nas chos kyi ched du nyid/_/khyim na rab tu 'gro ba skyon yod ma yin no/_/de nas mi skyong rgyal po'i dga' dang phan pa nyid/_/byas pa'i blon po de yi tshig dag gsan 
[\[36a.2\]]nas ni/_/dman min ma 'khrugs chags min brtan dang gnas pa la/_/myur min tshig ni rgyal po sras kyis smras pa'o/_/'dir ni yod dang med ces the tshom gang de ni/_/gzhan gyi tshig gis bdag la 'di na nges pa med/_/dka' thub dang ni zhi bas de nyid rtogs gyur nas/_/
[\[36a.3\]]gang 'dir nges par bdag nyid kyis ni gzung bar bgyi/_/phan tshun gnod cing mi gsal the tshom skye ba yi/_/lta ba dag ni len par bdag gis nus ma yin/_/mun par long bas long ba'i lam mkhan byed pa bzhin/_/mkhas pa'i skye bo su zhig gzhan gyi rkyen gyis 'gro/_/de nyid 
[\[36a.4\]]ma mthong gyur kyang 'on kyang kho bo yi/_/dge dang mi dger the tshom na dge blo gros gyur/_/'bras bu med phyir la yang dge ba'i bdag nyid mchog_/smod pa'i bdag nyid de nyid la yang bde ba med/_/rnam gzhag med pa'i lung 'di yang ni mthong gyur 
[\[36a.5\]]nas/_/tshad ldan rnams kyis gang smras de ni legs rtogs mdzod/_/nyes la rab dman tshad ldan nyid ni rtogs mdzod dang /_/nyes pa rab tu dman pas bden min smra ba yin/_/khyim la 'jug par dmigs gang bdag la khyod kyis ni/_/smras shing rangs byed la sogs rnams ni dpe 
[\[36a.6\]]bstan te/_/de rnams tshad min chos nges rnams su nus med cing /_/tshad ma'i ched du brtul zhugs rnams ni yongs su zad/_/de lta na yang nyi ma sa la lhung gyur la/_/gangs dang ldan pa'i ri bos brtan nyid 'dor na yang /_/de nyid ma mthong yul la kha bltas dbang po ni/_/khyim rnams 
[\[36a.7\]]dag la so so skye bo mi bsnyen to/_/bdag ni 'bar bar gyur pa'i me la 'jug pa ste/_/don ma byas par khyim gyi gzhi la rab mi 'jug_/de ltar thugs rgyal ldan des dam bca' mdzad gyur cing /_/ji ltar 'dod bzhin bzhengs nas ngar 'dzin med par gshegs/_/de nas mchi ma dang bcas blon 
[\[36b\]]
[\[36b.1\]]dang gnyis skyes gnyis/_/de yi bstan pa nyid dang nges pa thos gyur nas/_/gti mug bzhin ldan rjes su song nas sdug bsngal ba/_/dal bus song nas grong khyer nyid du song bar gyur/_/de nas de la brtse dang mi skyong la gus las/_/de dag ltos byas rab tu song zhing gnas gyur 
[\[36b.2\]]la/_/gsal zhing snang bas nyi ma blta bar dka' ba ltar/_/de ni lam du lta bar ma nus 'dor bar min/_/mchog tu gshegs te gshegs pa shes phyir de dag gis/_/so pa'i skyes bu gtsang ma sbas pa rnams byas nas/_/dga' zhing sras po 'dod pa'i rgyal po la song nas/_/ji ltar 
[\[36b.3\]]blta'am zhes ni ji ltar yang song ngo /_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_gzhon nu rjes su tshol ba'i le'u ste dgu pa'o//_//sku stod rgyas shing rgya chen rgyal po'i sras po ste/_/bsreg bya dang bsngags bdag po de dag rnams spangs te/_/dba' 
[\[36b.4\]]rlabs g.yo ba'i chu bo gang gA rgal nas ni/_/dpal dang ldan pa'i rgyal po'i khab tu gshegs par gyur/_/ri rnams dag gis legs par sbas dang brgyan pa dang /_/dge dang dka' thub gter bas gzung dang gtsang ba ste/_/zhi ba rang byung tshangs pa mtho ris logs su bzhin/_/ri 
[\[36b.5\]]lngas mtshan pa'i grong khyer rab tu thob par gyur/_/zab mor gyur pa'i mdangs dar de yi lugs gsal ba/_/skyes bu rnams ni shin tu gnon pa mthong gyur nas/_/khyu mchog rgyal mtshan de yi brtul zhugs brtan pa bzhin/_/der ni de tshe skye bo rnams ni ya mtshan gyur/_/de rab 
[\[36b.6\]]mthong nas gzhan gang ma song de gnas shing /_/der ni gang zhig lam na gnas de rjes 'gro zhing /_/gang zhig skyen por song ste brtan por song gyur la/_/kha cig bsdad pa gang yin de ni langs pa'o/_/skye bo kha cig de la mchod pa'i lag pa yis/_/bsti stang byas nas kha cig 
[\[36b.7\]]mgo bos phyag 'tshal zhing /_/kha cig gnyen po'i tshig gis mngon par stod byed cing /_/kha cig de la rab tu mchod nas song bar gyur/_/de rab mthong nas sna tshogs cha lugs rnams ngo tsha/_/rab bkram tshig ni lam du smra ba med par song /_/dngos su gzhan du chos rnams 
[\[37a\]]
[\[37a.1\]]dag gi nye logs su/_/kha cig tshul ma yin pa'i blo gros ma gyur to/_/lha dang mtshungs pa'i mi dbang sras po de mthong nas/_/bya ba gzhan ma rnams kyang rgyal po'i lam du ni/_/mchod mang bkur sti sngon 'gro bud med rnams kyi 'am/_/skyes pa rnams kyis mig 
[\[37a.2\]]dang ngoms par ma gyur to/_/dpral ba 'am smin ma dag dang zhal lam spyan dag dang /_/sku 'am phyag dag dang ni 'gros sam zhabs dag ni/_/gang gis de yi gang nyid der ni mthong gyur pa/_/de nyid kyis ni de yi mig dag bcings par gyur/_/smin ma mdzod spu dang bcas spyan dag 
[\[37a.3\]]yangs pa dang /_/sku lus mdzes shing dge ba'i dra ba dang ldan phyag_/sa skyong nyid du 'os pa'i dge slong cha lugs de/_/mthong nas rgyal po'i khab kyi phun tshogs g.yos par gyur/_/de nas khang pa'i phyi nas rnam rgyas skye bo'i tshogs/_/ma ga d+hA yi khyim gyi ngo bo bzo sbyangs kyis/_/
[\[37a.4\]]mthong bar gyur te de yi rgyu mtshan dris pa ste/_/de nas 'di la skyes bu rnams kyis de smras so/_/ye shes mchog gam yang na sa yi dpal dag ni/_/'thob par 'gyur zhes bram ze rnams kyis gang smras pa/_/rab tu gshegs par skye bos nges par mthong ba yi/_/shAkya'i bdag po'i sras po de ni 
[\[37a.5\]]'di yin no/_/de nas don thos yid kyis chags shing zhen gyur pa'i/_/rgyal po yis ni skyes bu de nyid la smras pa/_/de ni gang du song zhes rnam par shes par mdzod/_/de nas de ltar bgyi zhes skyes bu rjes su song /_/g.yo mi spyan ni gnya' shing gang tsam gzigs pa ste/_/gsung ni kun nas snyoms 
[\[37a.6\]]shing zab mo'i 'gros mnga' ba/_/g.yo ba'i sems dang lus rnams dag ni nges bsdams nas/_/dge slong mchog nyid de yang bsod snyoms la gshegs so/_/ji ltar nye bar 'thad pa'i bsod snyoms bsnams nas ni/_/rnam par dben zhing char rab 'bab par rab gshegs la/_/der ni 
[\[37a.7\]]tshul bzhin rigs pas mngon par gsol gyur nas/_/de ni sa 'dzin ri bo skya bseng la 'dzegs so/_/ro d+ha'i nags kyis nye bar sbas pa'i ri bo der/_/rma bya'i skad kyis rab gang lo ma'i spyil bo na/_/'char ri'i steng na nyi ma gzhon nu ji lta bar/_/ngur smrig gos kyis nyi ma'i nyi ma de mdzes 
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]so/_/der ni de nyid rgyal po'i 'khor de mthong gyur nas/_/bzo sbyangs rgyal po la ni gtam dag byas pa ste/_/thos nas rgyal po des kyang mchod pa mang po las/_/der ni sbas pa'i rjes su 'brang bas rab tu song /_/skya bseng ri bo mchog la skya bseng na dang ni/_/brtson 'grus mnyam 
[\[37b.2\]]zhing ri bo dang mnyam sku lus can/_/mi yi seng ge'i 'gros can cod pan 'dzin pa ste/_/ral pa g.yo ba'i seng ge bzhin du 'dzegs par gyur/_/de nas de yi steng na rtse mor gyur pa yi/_/byang chub sems dpa' dbang po nye bar zhi ba ni/_/ri bong gis mtshan sprin gyi sbubs nas byung ba bzhin/_/
[\[37b.3\]]skyil mo krung gis bzhugs nas rnam par mdzes pa mthong /_/gzugs dang phun sum tshogs dang nye bar zhi ba yi/_/chos kyi sprul pa bzhin du nye bar bzhugs de la/_/ya mtshan dang bcas rab tu bsnyen ldan mi dbang po/_/rang byung la ni brgya byin gyis bzhin nye bar gnas/_/rigs pa 
[\[37b.4\]]yis ni rigs pa rig pa rnams kyi mchog_/khams mnyam gyur pa de la song nas dris pa ste/_/de nyid kyis kyang mtshungs shing zhi bas mi skyong la/_/yid ni rang bzhin gnas dang nad med smras par gyur/_/de nas gtsang la glang po'i rna ba ltar sngo ba'i/_/rdo ba'i logs la rgyal po yang 
[\[37b.5\]]dag nges gnas shing /_/nye sar nye bar bsdad nas rjes su mthun pa yis/_/de yi dgongs pa rnam par shes 'dod 'di smras so/_/rim pas 'ongs pa nyid dang yongs su brtags pa ste/_/khyod kyi rigs kyis bdag gi dga' ba mchog yin la/_/gang phyir bdag la rang gi na tshod smra 
[\[37b.6\]]'dod pa/_/skyes te de phyir brtse ba'i tshig 'di mkhyen par mdzod/_/khyod kyi rigs ni rnam rgyas sngon du nyi ma ste/_/lus kyang na tshod gsar pa gsal la mdzes pa 'di/_/gang phyir 'di ni khyod kyi blo gros rim med pas/_/slong mo nyid la mngon dga' rgyal srid la ma yin/_/khyod kyi 
[\[37b.7\]]lus 'di tsan+dan dmar po 'os pa ste/_/ngur smrig gis dkris de ni 'os pa ma yin no/_/lag pa 'di ni skye dgu skyong bar 'os pa ste/_/gzhan gyis byin pa'i bza' ba za bar 'os ma yin/_/de phyir zhi ba gal te yab kyi rgyal srid la/_/khyod kyi yab kyi brtse las stobs kyis mi 'dod cing /_/
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]khyod kyi blo gros rim par bzod par ma gyur na/_/skyen par kho bo'i yul gyi phyed la longs spyod mdzod/_/de lta na ni rang gi skye la gnod min zhing /_/dus kyi rim pa yis kyang dpal gang bsngags pa ste/_/de phyir khyod kyis bdag la brtse ba mdzod cig dang /_/
[\[38a.2\]]dam pa rnams dang bcas pas dam pa'i dpal 'phel lo/_/ci ste da ni rigs kyis khengs pa nyid las ni/_/bdag cag rnams la khyod la blo gtad yon tan med/_/sde rgyas rnams ni mda' yis rnam par dkrugs byas nas/_/bdag dang bcas pas pha rol rnams ni pham par mdzod/_/
[\[38a.3\]]de phyir 'dir ni gnyis dbus gcig blo tshol mdzod la/_/chos don 'dod pa rnams la cho ga bzhin bsnyen mdzod/_/'di na sde tshan gsum po chags las rnam bzlog nas/_/'di dang gzhan du phyin nas lhung ba kun nas 'thob/_/gang zhig don dang chos la gnod nas 'dod pa dang /_/chos dang 'dod pa 
[\[38a.4\]]yongs su brnyas nas don dang ni/_/'dod dang don dag nye bar zhi bas chos de ni/_/don byas gal te mtha' dag 'dod na don yin no/_/de phyir sde tshan gsum la nges par bsnyen pa yis/_/khyod kyis gzugs 'di 'bras bu bcas pa nyid du mdzod/_/chos dang don dang 'dod pa dman min thob pa la/_/
[\[38a.5\]]mi rnams dag gis dman min skyes bu'i don du smra/_/de phyir gzhu ni rnam par 'gengs 'os rgyas pa'i phyag_/'di dag 'bras bu med par byed par 'os min te/_/nga las nu bzhin 'dis ni 'jig rten gsum rnams kyang /_/pham par 'os pa yin na sa 'dir smos ci dgos/_/brtse bas nges par de ni 
[\[38a.6\]]bdag gis smra ba ste/_/ya mtshan gyis min dbang phyug nyid kyi chags pas min/_/khyod kyis dge slong nyid kyi cha lugs 'di mthong nas/_/rjes su brtse ba skyes nas mchi ma rang byung ngo /_/de phyir rang rigs rab mtshungs khyod kyi gzugs ji srid/_/rga bas zil gyis mnan nas slar yang ma 'ongs par/_/
[\[38a.7\]]slong mo'i gnas 'dod 'dod pa rnams la longs spyod la/_/dus su chos la dga' ba chos ni gyis mdzod cig_/'khogs pas nges par chos ni 'thob par nus pa ste/_/'dod pa nye bar spyod la rgas pa'i 'gro ba med/_/de phyir dar la bab pa'i 'dod pa rnams smra ste/_/bar ma'i nor yin rgan po rnams 
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]kyi chos yin no/_/gson pa'i 'jig rten dag na chos dang don dag ni/_/rnam par 'gal bar gyur pa yin phyir lang tsho rnams/_/yang dag srung ba yin yang thub par dka' ba ste/_/gang phyir 'dod pas lam de las ni 'phrog par byed/_/na tshod 'khogs pa rnams ni rnam spyod ldan pa ste/_/shin tu 
[\[38b.2\]]brtan pa'i gnas skabs mchog tu song ba rnams/_/ngo tsha ba nyid phyir dang song ba ma yin phyir/_/'bad pa nyung ngus zhi ba'i bdag nyid gyur pa'o/_/de nyid phyir na g.yo zhing yul ni gtso bo dang /_/rab myos bzod pa ma yin ring por mthong min la/_/'brog dgon dag la des par thar nas dbugs 
[\[38b.3\]]phyung bzhin/_/g.yo sgyu mang po'i lang tsho shin tu 'das nas so/_/de phyir mi brtan g.yo la bag med ldan pa yi/_/na tshod gsar pa 'di ni re zhig rnam par 'ong /_/'dod pa'i lha yi sngon du na tshod dben yin la/_/dbang po rnams las bsrung bar nus pa ma yin no/_/ci ste khyod kyi chos nyid 
[\[38b.4\]]mdzad par 'dod pa ni/_/mchod sbyin gyis lha mchod mdzod 'di ni rigs kyi chos/_/mchod sbyin rnams kyis mtho ris logs ni byin brlabs nas/_/lha dbang nyid kyang mtho ris logs su gshegs par 'gyur/_/gser gyi dpung rgyan rnam par bcings pa'i phyag rnams dang /_/nor bu'i sgron mas 'bar 
[\[38b.5\]]ba'i dpung rgyan sna tshogs can/_/mi skyod drang srong rnams dang drang srong chen po rnams/_/ngal bas gar gshegs 'gros der mchod sbyin rnams kyis gshegs/_/de ltar ma ga d+hA yi bdag pos tshig smras pa/_/gang zhig dbang po'i bzhin du smra ba yang dag mdzes/_/sna tshogs du ma'i sa 
[\[38b.6\]]phyogs ti se'i gangs ri ltar/_/rgyal po'i sras des de thos gyur nas rnam ma g.yos/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_bzo sbyangs sngon du song ba'i le'u ste bcu pa'o//_//de nas bshes kyi sgo nas phan pa min pa'i don/_/de ltar ma ga d+hA yi 
[\[38b.7\]]bdag pos smas pa yis/_/rang gnas rnam par 'gyur min rigs gtsang gtsang pa po/_/zas gtsang rgyal po'i sras kyis tshig 'di smras pa'o/_/seng ges mtshan pa'i yangs pa'i rigs su rab skyes pa/_/khyed kyi cho ga de ni ngo mtshar che ma yin/_/gang phyir bshes gnyen phyogs su 
[\[39a\]]
[\[39a.1\]]gshegs 'dod khyod kyi ni/_/yongs su dag pa'i spyod pas spyod pa de yin no/_/rang rigs rjes su spyod pas dam pa min la byams/_/dbang po ma tshang ba la dpal bzhin gnas min la/_/snga mas byas pa'i dga' ba rim rnams kyis/_/de ni dam pa rnams 
[\[39a.2\]]kyis rnam par 'phel bar byed/_/don ni dka' ba rnams su mi rnams gang zhig gis/_/mdza' bo rnams kyis mnyam pa'i don gyur 'jig rten na/_/bshes gnyen de rnams blo yis yongs su shes bgyi ste/_/rang gnas dag ni 'phel bar gang gi phyir ma yin/_/de ltar gang gis rdzas dag thob nas 
[\[39a.3\]]'jig rten na/_/bshes rnams dang ni chos la nges par sbyor byed pa/_/de rnams kyis ni nor gyi snying po thob pa ste/_/mthar ni nyams las gdung ba skyed par byed ma yin/_/rgyal po mdza' bo nyid dang 'phags pa nyid kyis kyang /_/khyod kyi nges pa gang zhig bdag la dmigs pa 'di/_/
[\[39a.4\]]mdza' bo nyid kyis 'dir ni rjes su bsnyad bgyi ste/_/rgol ba gzhan min 'dir ni bdag gis smra bar bgyi/_/bdag gis rga dang 'chi ba'i 'jigs pa rnam rig nas/_/thar pa 'dod pas chos 'di rab tu thob pa ste/_/gnyen 'dun dga' ba mchi ma'i gdong ldan rnams spangs nas/_/mi dge'i rgyur 
[\[39a.5\]]gyur 'dod pa rnams ni sngon nyid do/_/bdag ni yul rnams dag la ji ltar 'jigs pa nyid/_/de ltar sbrul gyi dug rnams la ni mi 'jigs shing /_/nam mkha' dag las 'phos pa'i thog rnams las min la/_/rlung dang bcas pa'i me rnams dag las ma yin no/_/mi rtag 'dod pa rnams kyis 
[\[39a.6\]]dge ba'i nor rku zhing /_/stong pa rmi lam sgyu ma dang mtshungs 'jig rten na/_/yid la smos pa yis kyang rmongs par byed pa ste/_/mi yi sems ni bdag nyid gnas pa smos ci 'tshal/_/kye ma 'dod pas zil gyis non na mtho ris su/_/bde ba thob min 'chi ba'i 'jig rten na smos ci/_/rlung grogs 
[\[39a.7\]]ldan pa'i me yis bud shing gis ji ltar/_/sred dang bcas pa'i 'dod pa rnams kyis tshim pa med/_/'gro na 'dod pa rnams dang mnyam pa'i don med med/_/rmongs las de rnams kho nar skye bo rab tu chags/_/de nyid shes nas de ltar don med 'jigs pa ste/_/shes ldan su zhig rang gis don 
[\[39b\]]
[\[39b.1\]]med mngon par 'dod/_/rgya mtsho'i gos can sa ni kun nas thob nas kyang /_/rgya mtsho chen po'i pha rol rgal bar 'dod pa ste/_/rgya mtsho'i chu bo rnams dang chu bran rnams kyis ltar/_/'jig rten pa yi 'dod pa rnams kyis ngoms pa med/_/lha yis gser gyi char pa dag kyang phab gyur la/_/
[\[39b.2\]]gling rnams bzhi po mtha' dag la yang longs spyad de/_/brgya byin gyi yang gdan phyed dag kyang rab thob nas/_/nga las nu ni yul rnams dag la ngoms pa med/_/lha na lha rnams kyi yang rgyal srid longs spyad nas/_/mchod sbyin brgya pa lha min 'jigs las rab nyams tshe/_/rgyags 
[\[39b.3\]]las drang srong chen po rnams la'ang khur bcom nas/_/'dod pa rnams la ngoms med sgra med lhung bar 'gyur/_/e la'i rgyal pos mtho ris rnams ni rnam dkrugs te/_/brla mdzes lha mo de nyid dbang du blangs nas kyang /_/sred phyir drang srong rnams las gser dag phrogs pa ste/_/yul 
[\[39b.4\]]rnams la ni ngoms med nyams par song bar gyur/_/lha min las ni dbang chen dbang chen las dgra med/_/dgra med las slar dbang chen gang du nyer song ste/_/mtho ris dang ni sa 'am yul ni de rnams su/_/bskal pa'i tshogs 'khrugs rnams la su zhig blo gtad byed/_/shing shun gos can 
[\[39b.5\]]rtsa ba 'bras bu chu za zhing /_/lag 'gro'i sbrul ltar ring ba'i ral ba'i khur rnams kyang /_/gang gis don gzhan ma yin thub pa yang nyams te/_/'dod pa zhes bya'i dgra bo rnams ni su yis tshol/_/rno ba'i mtshon cha dang ni rno 'dzin mtshon cha yang /_/gang gi don du 'jigs sde las ni 'chi thob la/_/
[\[39b.6\]]de dag rnams kyi dge min sems kyang spyod rnams kyis/_/'joms phyir de phyir brtul zhugs rnams kyis smos ci dgos/_/yul rnams su ni mya ngan nyung ngur shes gyur nas/_/kun nas sbyor zhing mchog tu ngoms pa med pa nyid/_/dam pa rnams kyis smod cing nges par sdig pa ste/_/'dod 
[\[39b.7\]]pa'i mig can dug ni su yis len par byed/_/zhing gi las la sogs pa rnams kyis nyam thag cing /_/'dod pa rnams la mi tshar rnams kyi rang gnas dang /_/'dod pa'i bdag nyid rnams kyi rang gnas thob nas ni/_/'dod pa rnams ni spong bar sems ldan rnams kyis nus/_/grub 'gyur

[\[40a.1\]]'dod pa rnams su rgyags pa nyer 'thob phyir/_/'dod pa can la 'dod 'byor rgud pa shes bya ste/_/rgyags las bya ba min byed bya ba ma yin la/_/gang gis zad cing ngan 'gror nye bar 'gro bar 'gyur/_/gang la 'bad pas thob cing yongs su bskyangs pa rnams/_/

[\[40b.1\]]ldan su yi dga' ba yin/_/gang rnams bsgrubs nas dang ni rnam par spel nas dang /_/yongs su bskyangs nas dang ni bde bar 'gro min la/_/lci ba'i me ma mur dang rab mtshungs de rnams su/_/'dod pa rnams la sems ldan su yi dga' ba yin/_/gtum po long byed

[\[40b.4\]]'dod pa rnams la sems ldan su yi dga' ba yin/_/bshes med 'dod pa'i don du bkren por byed pa ste/_/gsod dang 'ching ba la sogs sdug bsngal rab thob la/_/'dod pa'i don du sems ni bkren pa'i dka' thub can/_/gson pa'i 'jig rten 'ching ba dang ni ngal bar 'gro/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rnams

[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]ba'i ched du gyur pa ste/_/de bzhin skye dgu rnams kyi yul nas longs spyod min/_/rab tu zhi ba'i cho gar rab zhugs shes rab can/_/longs spyod rnams ni bza' zhes su yis khas len byed/_/gang zhig 'gro ba'i gdung bas rnam par gdungs pa ni/_/

[\[41a.3\]]dngos po gang nyid kyis ni bde ba ster byed pa/_/de nyid kyis ni slar yang sdug bsngal 'dren par byed/_/bgo ba lci mo dang ni lci ba ma yin nyid/_/grang dang dro ba'i tshe na bde dang bde min la/_/zla 'od rnams dang tsan+dan nyid kyang dro ba na/_/bde ba'i phyir dang sdug bsngal phyir yin grang
