[\[41a.5\]]'dres mthong nas/_/rgyal srid dang ni bran nyid mnyam pa bdag gi lugs/_/rgyal po nyid ni rtag par rgod pa ma yin zhing /_/bran ni kun nas gdung ba nyid kyang ma yin no/_/gang phyir mi skyong nyid du bka' lung lhag par gyur/_/de phyir rgyal po'i sdug bsngal dag ni chen po 
[\[41a.6\]]nyid/_/kun nas brten pa'i shing dang rab mtshungs rgyal po ni/_/srid pa'i rgyu las yongs su ngal bar 'gro ba nyid/_/rgyal srid 'dor ldan bshes min mang por mi skyong gis/_/gal te blo gtad 'cha' bar gyur na rgud pa ste/_/ci ste yang na blo gtad nye bar byed min na/_/'dir 
[\[41a.7\]]ni 'jigs ldan rgyal po'i bde ba zhes bya ci/_/gang tshe sa ni mtha' dag rgyal bar gyur nas kyang /_/gnas pa'i ched du grong khyer gcig pu nyid mthong zhing /_/de nyid la yang pho brang gcig pu bsnyen bya ste/_/rgyal por gyur pa pha rol don du ngal lam ci/_/rgyal po sdod pa'i gnas kyang 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]gnya' shing gcig nyid do/_/de bzhin bkres pa 'gegs phyir bza' ba tsam yin la/_/de bzhin nyal lam gcig dang stan ni gcig nyid de/_/lhag dang lhag min mi skyong gis ni rgyags phyir ro/_/gal te tshim pa'i don du 'bras bu de 'dod na/_/rgyal srid rnams dang bral yang bdag ni 
[\[41b.2\]]tshim pa yod/_/tshim pa yod par gyur na skyes bu'i 'jig rten na/_/khyad par med pa ma yin thams cad khyad par ro/_/de phyir dge dang zhi ba'i lam ni rab thob pa/_/kho bo 'dod pa rnams la slu bya ma yin la/_/mdza' bshes nyid du dran par byas nas yang dang yang /_/dam bca' 
[\[41b.3\]]nges par skyongs zhes bdag la smras pa'o/_/bdag ni nags su zhugs pa mi bzod pas min la/_/dgra bo'i mda' rnams kyis ni mgo bo 'dar ba min/_/'bras bu lhag pa rnams las re ba byas min te/_/de phyir khyed kyi tshig de bzung ba ma yin no/_/gang zhig yang yang shin tu sos 
[\[41b.4\]]'debs khros pa'i sbrul/_/spangs nas slar yang len pa la ni nan tan byed/_/yang na sreg pa'i rang bzhin 'bar ba'i rtswa yi me/_/spangs nas 'dod pa rnams ni slar yang de yis brten/_/gang zhig ma long long ba'i phyir ni 'dod pa ste/_/grol ba 'ching ba'i phyir dang phyug po nor med phyir/_/
[\[41b.5\]]rang bzhin gnas pa'i sems ni bsnyon pa'i sems phyir la/_/yul gyi bdag nyid phyir ni 'dod par de yis byed/_/gang zhig 'dir ni mchog tu zhi ba'i bde ba dang /_/gzhan du sdug bsngal rnams ni kun nas sgrib pa ste/_/byed ldan rga dang 'chi ba'i 'jigs las sgrol 'dod pa/_/slong mo nye 
[\[41b.6\]]bar rgyu zhes rjes su brtse bya min/_/gang zhig 'dir ni zhi ba'i bde ba 'thob min la/_/gzhan du sdug bsngal rnams kyis rab tu 'dzin byed cing /_/phun tshogs chen po'i bdag nyid la yang rnam gnas pa'i/_/sred pas zil gyis gnon la rjes su brtse bar byas/_/sems pa yi dang spyod pa yi 
[\[41b.7\]]dang rigs nyid kyi/_khyod kyis brjod pa la ni rjes su mthun pa ste/_/sems pa yi dang spyod pa yi dang rigs nyid kyi/_/kho bo yi yang dam bca' 'dren par mtshungs pa'o/_/bdag kyang 'khor ba'i rgya mtsho'i mda' yis rnam phug pa/_/zhi ba thob par 'dod pas rnam par nges 'byung ste/_/nad med mtho ris na 
[\[42a\]]
[\[42a.1\]]yang rgyal srid 'thob pa ru/_/'dod pa med na kye ma mi rnams su smos ci/_/mi skyong gang yang sde gsum mtha' dag brten pa ni/_/rab mchog mi yi don zhes khyed kyis bdag la smras/_/de ltar don med ces ni bdag gi bstan pa 'dir/_/mdzad ldan sde tshan gsum 
[\[42a.2\]]ni tshim byed kyang ma yin/_/gang du dga' bar 'gro min 'jigs min nad ma yin/_/skyong min nye bar 'da' min sems kyi nad rnams min/_/gang na yang dang yang du bya ba yod min pa/_/de nyid skyes bu'i don ni mchog tu shes pa'o/_/ji srid na tshod so ma rnam par 'gyur bar 'gro/_/de srid dga' ba 
[\[42a.3\]]yongs su skyongs mdzod zhes smras pa/_/'di ni nges pa ma yin g.yo bar mthong ba ste/_/rga ba brtan pa min dang lang tsho brtan ldan mthong /_/rang gi las mkhan mthar byed gang gi 'gro ba ni/_/na tshod kun la dbang med rnam par 'gugs pa ste/_/rnam gzhag ma yin rnam par nyams tshe gang ltar 
[\[42a.4\]]ni/_/zhi 'dod mkhas pa dag gis dga' ba rtogs par byed/_/gang tshe dga' ba'i gzhu dang nad rnams bkram pa'i mda'/_/mthar byed dge ba ma yin rngon pa bzhin gnas pas/_/skye dgu'i ri dwags bskal pa'i nags brten rnams gtor te/_/na tshod rab mchog la dmigs yid la re ba yi/_/
[\[42a.5\]]de phyir gzhon nam rgan dang yang na byis pa ni/_/rab tu 'jug par 'dod dam rnam par ldog pa nyid/_/chos ldan bdag nyid byas pa ji lta bar gyur pa/_/de ltar myur ba ldan pas 'dir ni bya bar 'os/_/yang na 'dod pa'i 'bras bu rigs su 'os pa rnams/_/chos phyir mchod sbyin bya ba 
[\[42a.6\]]gyis zhes gang smras pa/_/gang zhig gzhan gyi sdug bsngal bya bas 'dod pa ste/_/mchod sbyin rnams la phyag 'tshal bde ba 'dod pa med/_/'bras bu 'dod pas dbang med pha rol gsod pa la/_/snying rje'i bdag nyid dam pa rnams kyi rigs tshul min/_/gang yang mchod sbyin dag gi 'bras 
[\[42a.7\]]bu rtag gyur pa/_/snying rje'i bdag nyid gang gis de ltar byas nas ci/_/sems ni zhi ba yis sam tshul khrims brtul zhugs kyis/_/gal te chos tshul gzhan gyi cho ga ma yin la/_/de lta na yang mchod sbyin bsnyen par 'os min te/_/gang du gzhan la 'tshes nas 'bras bur brjod pa'o/_/'di 
[\[42b\]]
[\[42b.1\]]nyid du yang re zhig skyes bu gnas pa yi/_/bde ba gang zhig pha rol 'tshe bas rab zhugs pa/_/de yang brtse bcas blo dang ldan pa'i 'dod min te/_/gang zhig sred pa'i mtha' mar mi mthong kye ma ci/_/rgyal po 'khor bar bdag gi yid ni dga' med cing /_/'bras bur rab tu 'jug phyir bdag 
[\[42b.2\]]ni bslu bya min/_/chu 'dzin char gyis brgyab pa'i ljon shing bzhin du ni/_/kun du song ba'i rab tu 'jug pa rnams ni g.yo/_/rnam par thar pa smra ldan rtsibs phur thub pa ni/_/blta bar 'dod pas bdag ni 'dir yongs de yi phyir/_/mi skyong khong la dge bar gyur cig de ni 'gro/_/
[\[42b.3\]]bdag gi de nyid ltos med dag ni bzod par mdzod/_/lha na dbang po bzhin srungs rtag par nyi bzhin srungs/_/yon tan gyis srungs sa srungs 'dir ni dge legs srungs/_/tshe srungs 'phags pa yis srungs dam pa'i bu rnams dang /_/dpal srungs rgyal po'i bdag nyid kyis ni 
[\[42b.4\]]chos srungs shig_/kha ba'i dgra yi rdeg las skyes pa las skyes nas/_/ji ltar gnyis skyes gang zhig lus ni rnam thar 'gro/_/kha ba'i dgra yi dgra yis khyim gyi dgra 'joms nang /_/nang du de ltar gang zhig yid ni rnam par thar gshegs mdzod/_/thal mo dang bcas re thob mi skyong gis smras pa/_/
[\[42b.5\]]khyed kyis ji ltar 'dod pa bgegs med thob par shog_/bya ba byas pa kun nas thob pa dus su 'dir/_/kho bo yi yang rjes su 'dzin pa khyed kyis mdzad pa'o/_/de ltar gyur zhes brtan pa'i dam bca' mdzad nas sa skyong la/_/de nas de ni thams cad sgrol gyi dka' thub gnas su gshegs/_/yongs 
[\[42b.6\]]su gshegs pa de mthong nas kyang ya mtshan gyur pa yi/_/mi skyong nyid kyang ri yi tshogs kyi grong khyer nyid du song /_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_'dod pa la rnam par smod pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o//_//de nas lus kyis gang ba bzhin/_/
[\[42b.7\]]bu ram shing ba'i zla ba yis/_/zhi bar gnas pa'i thub pa yis/_/rtsibs 'phur gyis ni gnas su bsnyen/_/sgyu rtsal shes kyi rigs de yis/_/rgyang nas gzigs nas mthon po yis/_/legs par 'ongs zhes smras pa ste/_/drung du nye bar gshegs pa'o/_/de gnyis phan tshun rigs par ni/_/
[\[43a\]]
[\[43a.1\]]khams ni snyoms par dris nas su/_/shing gtsang brtul zhugs gdan dag la/_/sa phyogs gtsang bar bzhugs pa'o/_/bkur mang rnam par yangs pa yis/_/lta byed dag gis 'thung ba bzhin/_/mi skyong sras po bzhugs de la/_/thub pa mchog des smras pa'o/_/
[\[43a.2\]]zhi ba bdag gi shes pa ltar/_/khyim nas nges par 'byung bar 'gyur/_/gnyis 'thung rgyags pas zhags pa ltar/_/brtse ba'i zhags pa bcad nas 'ongs/_/khyod kyi yid ni shes rab dang /_/kun nas brtan pa ldan pa nyid/_/dug gi ljon pa'i 'bras bu bzhin/_/gang khyod dpal dor nas 
[\[43a.3\]]'ongs so/_/longs spyad lhag ma'i phreng ba bzhin/_/bu rnams dag la dpal byin nas/_/sa skyong 'khogs pa'i na tshod rnams/_/gang zhig nags 'ongs ngo mtshar min/_/bdag gi lugs ni ngo mtshar te/_/so ma'i na tshod la khyod kyis/_/yul gyi spyod yul la gnas pa/_/dpal 
[\[43a.4\]]nyid longs ma spyad nas 'ongs/_/de phyir chos 'di shes pa la/_/khyod ni dam pa'i snod yin la/_/ye shes gru bo byin brlabs nas/_/skyen par sdug bsngal rgya mtsho sgrol/_/gang yang slob mar shes pa na/_/dus kyis bstan bcos la 'jug ste/_/zab pa las dang nan tan las/_/bdag gi yongs 
[\[43a.5\]]rtog khyod ma yin/_/de ltar rtsibs 'phur nyid kyi tshig_/mi yi drang srong des gsan nas/_/mchog tu dga' bar gyur pa yis/_/lan gnyis rab tu smras pa'o/_/don ma byas kyang 'di yi ni/_/don byas bzhin du da lta nyid/_/chags dang bral ba gang 'di ni/_/dam pa khyed kyi mngon du gyur/_/blta 
[\[43a.6\]]bar 'dod pas 'od bzhin dang /_/sgrol bar 'dod pas gru bzhin dang /_/'gro bar 'dod pas lam mkhan bzhin/_/khyed mthong ba la bdag gis shes/_/rga dang 'chi ba'i 'jigs rnams las/_/gal te gsung byar dgongs nas ni/_/de phyir gsung bar 'os pa'o/_/de ltar rtsibs 'phur gyis gzhon 
[\[43a.7\]]nu'i/_/bdag nyid che las bskul ba nyid/_/rang nyid kyi ni bstan bcos kyi/_/nges par mdor bsdus gsungs pa'o/_/ji ltar 'khor ba yin pa la/_/ji ltar nges par ldog pa nyid/_/nyan pa rnams kyi mchog grub mtha'/_/kho bo'i 'di ni gson mdzod cig_/rang bzhin dang ni rnam 'gyur dang /_/
[\[43b\]]
[\[43b.1\]]skye dang 'chi dang rga ba ste/_/re zhig sems pa zhes brjod pa/_/brtan pa'i sems pa de shes mdzod/_/de la rang bzhin shes bya ba/_/rang bzhin shes pa shes par mdzod/_/'byung ba lnga yi bdag nyid dang /_/blo dang mi gsal ba nyid do/_/rnam 'gyur zhes bya mkhyen par mdzod/_/yul 
[\[43b.2\]]rnams dang ni dbang po rnams/_/lag pa rkang pa dag dang ni/_/'phongs dang mdoms dang de bzhin yid/_/'di yi zhing ni shes pa la/_/zhing shes pa zhes bya ba ste/_/zhing shes pa zhes bdag nyid du/_/bdag tu bsam byed rnams kyis smras/_/slob ma dang bcas ser skya yis/_/'di ni 
[\[43b.3\]]rab tu rtogs zhes dran/_/bu dang bcas pa'i rtogs pa yang /_/skye dgu'i bdag por 'di brjod do/_/skye ba nyid dang rga ba dang /_/na ba dang ni 'chi ba dang /_/de ni gsal zhes rtogs bya ste/_/bzlog las mi gsal ba yang ngo /_/mi shes pa dang las dang srid/_/'khor ba'i rgyu rnams 
[\[43b.4\]]shes bya ste/_/gsum po 'dir gnas 'gro ba ni/_/snying stobs de ni 'da' ba med/_/log pa las dang ngar 'dzin las/_/the tshom las dang mngon 'phyo las/_/khyad par med las thabs min las/_/chags pa las dang mngon lhung las/_/de la log pa zhes bya ba/_/phyin ci log tu 'jug 
[\[43b.5\]]pa ste/_/bsam par bya ba gzhan sems shing /_/bya ba gzhan ni byed pa'o/_/smra ba nga dang rig pa dang /_/'gro ba nga dang gnas pa nga /_/de ltar 'di ni ngar 'dzin pa/_/ngar 'dzin med kyi 'jug pa'o/_/gang zhig the tshom med dngos rnams/_/gcig tu gyur pas mthong ba ste/_/the tshom med 
[\[43b.6\]]pas 'jim gong ltar/_/de 'dir the tshom du brjod do/_/gang zhig nga nyid de nyid 'di/_/yin dang blo dang las yin la/_/gang zhig drangs te de nyid nga /_/zhes gang de ni mngon par 'phyo/_/rab rgyas rab rgyas ma yin pa'i/_/khyad par med pa'i khyad shes kyi/_/rang bzhin rnams kyis gang shes 
[\[43b.7\]]pa/_/de ni khyad par med ces dran/_/phyag 'tshal ba dang gzhi mi 'grib/_/bsang gtor dang ni mngon 'thor sogs/_/thabs min zhes ni shes ldan gyis/_/rab tu rtogs bya thabs shes kyi/_/chags pa mi skye yul rnams la/_/yid ngag blo dang las rnams kyis/_/blo ngan gang gis 
[\[44a\]]
[\[44a.1\]]chags byed pa/_/de ni mngon par chags zhes dran/_/bdag gi 'di dang 'di bdag ces/_/gang zhig sdug bsngal mngon snyam ste/_/gang zhig 'khor bar lhung shes bya/_/de ltar mkhas skye ma rig pa/_/gnas skabs lnga ni rab thob ste/_/mun pa rmongs pa 
[\[44a.2\]]rmongs pa che/_/mun las gyur pa gnyis nyid do/_/de la mun pa le lor dang /_/rmongs pa skye 'chir rtogs par mdzod/_/rmongs pa chen po 'dod pa zhes/_/kun nas rmongs med mkhyen par mdzod/_/gang phyir 'di na 'byung rnams kyis/_/chen por yang ni rab rmongs byed/_/de phyir phyag chen 
[\[44a.3\]]'di dag ni/_/rmongs pa chen po zhes dran no/_/mun las gyur pa khro nyid du/_/lhag par byed pa'o khro med kyis/_/mi shes mun las gyur pa ni/_/skyo bar rab bshad skyo med kye/_/ma rig pa 'dis byis pa 'di/_/gnas skabs lnga dang ldan pa ste/_/'khor bar sdug bsngal gyur pa yi/_/skye ba 
[\[44a.4\]]rnams su 'bab par byed/_/lta bo nyan po shes pa po/_/bya ba dang ni rgyu nyid dag_/da zhes de ltar 'ongs nas ni/_/'khor bar yongs su 'khor bar byed/_/blo ldan rgyu 'di rnams kyis 'di/_/skye ba'i rgyun la rab tu 'jug_/rgyu med pa las 'bras bu med/_/de ltar rnam par shes par 
[\[44a.5\]]'os/_/de la rgyun rab rab rgyas mig_/gsal ba dang ni mi gsal nyid/_/thar pa 'dod pa bzhin rnams te/_/yang dag 'gro ba rigs par mdzod/_/bzhi po de dag ji lta bzhin/_/'gyur med go 'phang 'thob par byed/_/de phyir 'jig rten na tshangs pa/_/mchog tu tshangs par smra 
[\[44a.6\]]ba rnams/_/tshangs pa'i spyod pa spyod byed 'dir/_/tshangs pa rnams kyis gnas par byed/_/mi skyod sras kyis thub pa de'i/_/tshig 'di de ltar gsan nas ni/_/mthar thug go 'phang nyid dang ni/_/thabs mngon pa dag dran pa'o/_/ji ltar ji srid gang du ni/_/tshangs pa'i spyod pa 'di 
[\[44a.7\]]spyad cing /_/chos 'di yis ni mthar thug pa/_/khyod kyis rnam par bshad par 'os/_/de ltar thub pas bstan bcos ltar/_/rnam gsal don ni mdor bsdus pas/_/cho ga'i rtog pa gzhan nyid kyis/_/chos de de la smras pa'o/_/'dis ni dang por khyim spangs nas/_/slong mo'i 
[\[44b\]]
[\[44b.1\]]rtags ni bsten pa ste/_/phrin las rnam par rgyas pa yi/_/tshul khrims blangs nas 'jug par byed/_/byed ldan rtsod med bstan bcos rig_/mchog tu tshim pa la gnas nas/_/gang dang de yis gang dang der/_/rnam dben gnas ni bsten pa'o/_/de nas chags las 'jigs 
[\[44b.2\]]mthong nas/_/chags pa med las mchog zhi yang /_/dbang po'i grong ni nges bzung ste/_/yid la zhi ni 'bad pa byed/_/de nas 'dod pa rnams las dang /_/gnod sems sogs las rnam dben nyid/_/bsam gtan dang po rnam dpyod ldan/_/rnam par dben pa las skyes thob/_/
[\[44b.3\]]bsam gtan bde ba de thob nas/_/de dang de nyid rnam dpyod cing /_/sngar med bde ba thob pa yis/_/byis pa'i skye bo 'phrog par byed/_/'dod dang sdang ba rnam smad pa'i/_/zhi ba'i cho ga de 'dra 'dis/_/tshangs pa'i 'jig rten thob pa ste/_/yongs su tshim pas gnas pa'o/_/rnam dpyod 
[\[44b.4\]]yid ni skyong byed rnams/_/mkhas par rnam par shes nas kyang /_/de las rnam par grol ba ni/_/bsam gtan dga' ba'i bde ldan thob/_/gang zhig khyad par ma mthong zhing /_/dga' ba de yis phrogs pa de/_/'od gsal ba yi lha rnams su/_/'od gsal ba yi gnas 'thob bo/_/gang 
[\[44b.5\]]yang dga' ba'i bde de las/_/yid ni rnam par dben byed pa/_/dga' ba'i bde ba dang bral ba/_/bsam gtan gsum pa thob par byed/_/de 'dra'i bde ba 'dir thob nas/_/gang zhig chags min btang snyoms shing /_/bde dang sdug bsngal rnam spangs pa'i/_/bsam gtan bzhi pa thob par 
[\[44b.6\]]byed/_/bde dang sdug bsngal zhi phyir dang /_/sems kyi bya ba med pa'i phyir/_/thar zhes mngon pa'i nga rgyal gyis/_/der yang kha cig 'bad pa byed/_/bsam gtan de yi 'bras bu ni/_/'bras bu che bas lhas mnyam par/_/longs spyod shes rab che rnams kyis/_/chen po'i dus su smra ba'o/_/
[\[44b.7\]]ting 'dzin de las rnam langs shing /_/lus skyes skyon rnams mthong nas ni/_/lus las rnam par ldog pa'i phyir/_/shes ldan bsam gtan la 'jog go/_/de nas bsam gtan de bor nas/_/khyad par la ni nges byas pa'i/_/shes rab ldan de 'dod las bzhin/_/gzugs rnams las 
[\[45a\]]
[\[45a.1\]]kyang chags pa med/_/lus 'di la ni kha gang du/_/de la yongs su rtog pa ste/_/de nas rdzas sdug rnams la yang /_/nam mkhar lhag par mos par byed/_/nam mkhar song ba'i bdag nyid du/_/bsdus nas gzhan du blo ldan gyis/_/de nyid mtha' med par mthong 
[\[45a.2\]]zhing /_/khyad par 'phags pa lhag par 'thob/_/nang na mkhas pa gzhan dag ni/_/bdag nyid bdag nyid kyis bzlog nas/_/ci yang med ces mthong ba ni/_/ci yang med pa zhes dran no/_/de nas gur nas bya bzhin dang /_/gong bu dag nas srin bal bzhin/_/zhing shes lus las 'thon pa las/_/
[\[45a.3\]]thar pa zhes ni mngon par brjod/_/de nyid shes pa'i mkhas pa yis/_/gang zhig thar pa zhes brjod pa/_/'di de tshangs pa dam pa ste/_/rtags med rtag cing 'gyur ba med/_/de ltar thabs dang thar pa dag_/bdag gis khyod la bstan pa ste/_/gal te shes shing gal te sred/_/ji lta ba 
[\[45a.4\]]bzhin rtogs par mdzod/_/rgyal 'dod bu dang skyed byed dang /_/gzhan mi 'bral ba rgan po dang /_/thar pa 'dod pa gzhan rnams kyang /_/lam 'di thob nas grol bar 'gyur/_/de yi dam pa'i tshig 'di dang /_/rnam par dpyod pa yang bzung nas/_/sngon du rgyu yi stobs thob pas/_/lan ni smras par 
[\[45a.5\]]gyur pa'o/_/gong nas gong du zhi ba yi/_/ye shes phra mo 'di thos shing /_/zhing shes yongs su ma btang phyir/_/de ni mthar thug min par go/_/rnam gyur dang ni rang bzhin las/_/zhing shes grol bar kho bo yis/_/rab tu skye ba'i chos su dang /_/sa bon chos su snyam pa'o/_/gang yang rnam dag 
[\[45a.6\]]bdag nyid ni/_/nges par thar zhes rtog pa ste/_/slar yang rkyen dag yod pa las/_/de ni grol bar mi 'gyur ro/_/ji ltar dus sbyor sa dang chu/_/me las sa bon skye ma yin/_/de bzhin rkyen ni de rnams kyis/_/skye ba de yang bdag gi lugs/_/gang zhig mi shes las sred rnams/_/spangs las 
[\[45a.7\]]thar pa rtogs byed pa/_/de ni mchog tu yongs spong ba/_/bdag yod gyur na yod ma yin/_/spangs nas spangs nas gsum po 'di/_/khyad par du yang nyer 'thob cing /_/gang du bdag ni gnas gyur pa/_/der ni gsum po 'di phra'o/_/skyon rnams phra ba nyid las dang /_/sems kyi bya ba med las 
[\[45b\]]
[\[45b.1\]]dang /_/tshe ni ring ba nyid las kyang /_/thar par kyang ni yongs su rtog_/ngar 'dzin par ni yongs spong bar/_/gang de yongs su rtog byed la/_/bdag spyod gyur nas ngar 'dzin pa/_/yongs su spong ba yod ma yin/_/drang srong rnams kyis ma spangs pa/_/'di ni yon tan med ma yin/_/de phyir yon tan 
[\[45b.2\]]med gyur na/_/'di yi thar par mi brjod do/_/yon tan can gyis yon tan gyi/_/ldog pa yod pa ma yin te/_/gzugs dang 'gro ba dang bral ba'i/_/me ni nye bar 'thob ma yin/_/lus las sngon du lus can dang /_/yon tan las sngar yon tan can/_/ma yin de phyir dang por grol/_/lus bzhin slar 
[\[45b.3\]]yang 'ching bar byed/_/zhing shes lus dang ma bral ba/_/shes pa yin nam shes min nyid/_/gal te shes 'di shes bya yod/_/shes bya yod na mi grol lo/_/ci ste khyod la shes mi 'grub/_/bdag tu rtog pas ci zhig bya/_/bdag dang bral yang shes pa ni/_/rab grub shing dang rtsig pa bzhin/_/gang 
[\[45b.4\]]phyir gong nas gong du ni/_/de ltar rtsibs 'phur gyi ni chos/_/mkhyen nas de ni ma tshim zhing /_/mtha' dag min zhes shes nas su/_/de nas so sor gshegs pa'o/_/de nas khyad par chos 'dod pa/_/lhag spyod kyi ni gnas su gshegs/_/de yi bdag tu 'dzin las kyang /_/des ni lta bar sred 
[\[45b.5\]]ma gyur/_/'du shes 'du shes med pa'i skyon/_/thub pa lhag spyod kyis shes nas/_/'du shes 'du shes med bdag min/_/cung zad med las gzhan 'gro thob/_/gang phyir dmigs pa phra mo ni/_/'du shes 'du shes med las gzhan/_/'du shes med min 'du shes min/_/de phyir der ni re 
[\[45b.6\]]'dod min/_/gang phyir der ni blo gnas shing /_/gzhan du rab tu rgyu pa med/_/phra zhing rno ldan de phyir der/_/'du shes med min 'du shes med/_/de phyir de yang rab thob nas/_/slar yang 'gro ba 'khor ba ste/_/byang chub sems dpa' mchog 'dod pas/_/de phyir lhag spyod dor ba'o/_/de nas de yi 
[\[45b.7\]]gnas thob nas/_/dge legs don gnyer nges byas nas/_/gang yang rgyal ba'i drang srong gis/_/grong khyer zhes bya'i gnas bsnyen no/_/de nas nai rany+dza na'i 'gram/_/gtsang mar gtsang zhing pha rol gnon/_/dben pa'i gnas ni mngon 'dod ba'i/_/thub pa yis ni gnas mdzad do/_/de nas de ni sngar brten 
[\[46a\]]
[\[46a.1\]]zhing /_/dbang po lnga yi dbang las khengs/_/mun pas bsnyen pa'i brtul zhugs can/_/dge slong lnga rnams des gzigs so/_/bsod nams kyis bsgrubs nor nad med/_/dbang don rnams kyis dbang phyug bzhin/_/dge slong de rnams kyis kyang 'dir/_/de mthong gyur 
[\[46a.2\]]nas nye bar gnas/_/de'i dbang gis gnas slob ma rnams/_/dbang po g.yos pas yid bzhin du/_/rab tu de rnams kyis mchod cing /_/rnam par dul las rjes su 'jug_/'chi dang skye ba mthar byed la/_/de nas thabs ni 'di yis zhes/_/bza' ba med par shes pa yis/_/dka' thub 
[\[46a.3\]]gdung ba rnams brtsams so/_/basmyung gnas cho ga du ma rnams/_/mi yis spyod dka' rnams mdzad cing /_/las ni thob bzhed lo drug tu/_/bdag gi sku lus rig par mdzad/_/pha mtha' med pa'i 'khor ba yi/_/pha rol thob par 'dod pa nges/_/bza' ba'i dus su til dang 'bru/_/
[\[46a.4\]]rab sha rnams ni re re gsol/_/dka' thub gdung ba des de yi/_/sku las bri bar byas pa gang /_/slar yang de yi gzi brjid kyis/_/sku lus nye bar 'phel byas gyur/_/ston ka'i dkar phyogs dang po yi/_/zla bas ku mu ta rnams bzhin/_/ring kyang dpal grags ring min zhing /_/gzhan gyis 
[\[46a.5\]]mig rnams tshim par mdzad/_/rus lpags lhag ma lhag med par/_/tshil dang sha dang khrag rnams kyis/_/zad kyang zab mo ma zad par/_/rgya mtsho bzhin du de mdzes so/_/de nas sangs rgyas nyid 'dod pas/_/ngal dub gdung bas don med bsal/_/nyon mongs lus can thub pa 
[\[46a.6\]]ni/_/srid las 'jigs pa blo mdzad do/_/chos 'di chags bral phyir min zhing /_/rtogs phyir ma yin thar phyin min/_/de tshe 'dzam bu'i rtsa bar gang /_/bdag gis thos te nges pa yin/_/'di ni stobs chung gis thob par/_/nus pa min phyir gus 'ongs shing /_/sku yi stobs ni 'phel don 
[\[46a.7\]]du/_/slar yang 'di ni rjes su bsams/_/bkres dang skom pas yongs gtser zhing /_/ngal las rang gnas med pa'i sems/_/yid kyis thob bya'i 'bras bu ni/_/ldog pa med pa gang zhig 'thob/_/rtag par dbang po tshim pa las/_/yang dag gnas pa 'thob pa ste/_/dbang po yang 
[\[46b\]]
[\[46b.1\]]dag tshim nyid kyis/_/yid ni rang bzhin gnas pa 'thob/_/dang zhing rab dang yid kyis ni/_/ting 'dzin nye bar skye ba ste/_/ting 'dzin gyis sbyar sems kyi ni/_/bsam gtan sbyor ba rab tu 'jug_/bsam gtan rab tu zhugs pa las/_/rnyed dka' zhi zhing dga' med mchog_/'chi med go 'phang de 
[\[46b.2\]]chos rnams/_/gang gis thob pa rab thob po/_/de phyir bza' ba rtsa ba ste/_/'di ni thabs zhes nges pa yin/_/bza' ba byed la brtan pa ni/_/dpag med blo yis blo mdzad nas/_/khrus byas nai rany+dza 'gram nas/_/lag byin 'gram gyi ljon shing ni/_/gus pa yis dud yal ga'i rtses/_/sku lus rid 
[\[46b.3\]]pa dal bus drangs/_/de nas lha rnams kyis bskul ba'i/_/ba lang skyong bdag bu mo ni/_/snying la dga' skyes 'gyur ba yi/_/dga' stobs ma ni der 'ongs so/_/dung dkar gyis 'bar lag pa can/_/la ba sngon po gon pa mo/_/dbu ba dang bcas chu sngon phreng /_/chu mchog ya mu na bzhin no/_/
[\[46b.4\]]spyan mig ut+pal rab rgyas shing /_/dad pas 'phel ba'i dga' ma des/_/de la mgo yis rab btud nas/_/'o ma drangs par gyur pa'o/_/des de gsol nas de la ni/_/skye ba'i 'bras bu thob byas nas/_/yang dag dbang po drug tshim ste/_/byang chub 'thob par nus par gyur/_/phul 
[\[46b.5\]]byung rgyas pa'i sku mnga' zhing /_/rang gi grags bcas thub pa ni/_/nus gyur rgya mtsho gnyis dag gis/_/mdzes dang bstan pa gcig pus bzung /_/bdag ni nges par grol ba la/_/blo ldan khams lnga rnams kyis ltar/_/kun nas 'jug ces rnam shes nas/_/dge slong lnga rnams kyis de dor/_/
[\[46b.6\]]de nas 'bad rtsol gnyis pa ni/_/byang chub ched du nges mdzad de/_/rtswa 'jam bkram pa'i sa gzhi la/_/a shwad tha yi rtsa bar gshegs/_/de tshe glang po'i 'gros mnga' ba'i/_/dpe med zhabs kyi sgra yis sad par byas pa ni/_/thub pa chen po'i byang chub nges par rtogs pa yi/_/grub mchog nag po zhes 
[\[46b.7\]]byas bstod pa smras par gyur/_/ji ltar thub pa khyod kyis zhabs kyis brdzis pa ni/_/yang nas yang du sa ni sgra skad sgrogs pa 'dra/_/ji ltar khyod kyi 'od ni nyi mar snang ba ste/_/khyod kyis nges par 'dod pa'i go 'phang mkhyen pa nyid/_/ji ltar mkha' la khug rta'i phreng ba rnams bskor zhing /_/pad+ma'i 
[\[47a\]]
[\[47a.1\]]spyan mnga' khyed la skor ba byed pa nyid/_/ji ltar mkha' la rlung rnams zhi bar 'gro ba ste/_/khyod kyis nges par de ring sangs rgyas 'gyur ba nyid/_/de nas glu mchog gis ni yang dag bstod pa ni/_/lco ga las ni gtsang ma'i rtswa rnams nyer blangs nas/_/byang chub shing 
[\[47a.2\]]chen rtsa ba dge la rab brtan cing /_/byang chub ched du dam bca' mdzad cing bzhugs pa'o/_/de nas gnyid log lto 'phye'i gdengs kas bsdus pa la/_/des ni rdo rje'i skyil krung g.yo med mchog bcings te/_/ji srid bya ba byas pa nyid ni ma thob par/_/de srid sa las gdan de 'byed par mi 
[\[47a.3\]]bgyi'o/_/de nas nges pa'i bdag nyid bcom ldan gdan mdzad tshe/_/lha na gnas rnams mnyam med dga' ba thob gyur la/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]tshogs rnams gnas par ma gyur bya rnams dang /_/rlung gis bsnun pa'i nags kyi ljon shing sgra med gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i 
[\[47a.4\]]snyan dngags chen po las/_ring 'phur dang lhag spyod kyi drung du gshegs pa'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o//_//der ni rnam par thar phyir dam bca' mdzad pa na/_/rgyal po'i rigs las rab 'khrungs drang srong chen po ni/_/der ni nye bar zhugs tshe 'jig rten rab 
[\[47a.5\]]dga' zhing /_/der ni bdud kyang dam pa'i chos kyi dgrar gyur to/_/gang la 'dod pa'i lhar ni 'jig rten na smra ba/_/sna tshogs mtshon cha me tog mda' can de bzhin du/_/'dod pa rab tu spyod pa'i bdag po de nyid la/_/thar ba'i dgra bo bdud du dpe rjod byed pa'o/_/de yi bu ni rol pa dga' ba 
[\[47a.6\]]rgyags pa dang /_/bu mo dga' ma dges ma sred ma gsum rnams kyis/_/de la sems kyi rnam par 'gyur ba dris pa ste/_/des ni de rnams la tshig mngon par smras/_/thub pa 'dis ni nges pa'i go cha rnam bzung zhing /_/sems pa'i mtshon cha blo yi mda' ni bsdogs byas nas/_/bdag cag 
[\[47a.7\]]yul rnams la ni rgyal 'dod bsdad pa ste/_/gang phyir 'dis ni bdag gi yid ni rnam par 'gyur/_/gal te 'dis bdag zil gyis mnan nas 'gro ba ste/_/'jigs pa la yang thar ba'i lam dag 'chad byed cing /_/lus 'phags jo bo spyod las lhung ba'i bzhin du ni/_/de phyir 'di 
[\[47b\]]
[\[47b.1\]]yis bdag gi yul ni de ring stongs/_/de phyir ji srid 'dis ni spyan mig ma thob cing /_/ji srid bdag gi spyod yul dag na gnas pa nyid/_/chu bo shin tu 'phel ba'i shugs kyis zam pa bzhin/_/de srid 'di yi brtul zhugs 'joms su 'gro ba'o/_/de nas me tog las byas gzhu 
[\[47b.2\]]ni bzung nas dang /_/kun du rmongs par byed pa'i mda' lnga rnams bzung nas/_/skye dgu rnams kyi yid kyi rang gnas mi byed pa/_/de ni bur bcas a shwad tha yi rtsa bar song /_/de nas srid pa 'khor ba'i pha rol rgal 'dod pa'i/_/thub pa rab tu zhi bar gdan la bzhugs pa la/_/lag pa 
[\[47b.3\]]g.yas pa mtshon cha'i rtse mor bdungs nas ni/_/mda' yis rol zhing bdud kyis 'di ni smras pa'o/_/kye ho rgyal rigs 'chi bas 'jigs pa longs mdzod cig_/thar pa'i chos ni spongs la 'jig rten mdzod/_/mda' dang mchod sbyin rnams kyis 'jig rten pham byas nas/_/'jig 
[\[47b.4\]]rten dag nas dbang po'i go 'phang 'thob par mdzod/_/gang zhig mi dbang snga ma rnams kyis 'drims pa ste/_/nges par 'byung la 'di ni grags pa'i lam yin zhing /_/rgyal po'i drang srong rnams kyang rigs su skyes pa yis/_/slong mo rab thob la ni 'di ni bsngags ma yin/_/ji srid nges 
[\[47b.5\]]pa'i bdag nyid de ring mi ldang ba/_/dam bca' rnam par ma dor brtan par gyur cig dang /_/gang zhig nya dgra sU rwa ka la rnam spangs pa'i/_/de nyid bdag gis mda' 'di 'bar bar byas pa'o/_/'dis ni cung zhig reg pa tsam gyis e la de/_/zla ba'i tsha bo yin yang sems med gyur pa 
[\[47b.6\]]ste/_/zhi ba'i lus zhes bya ba de yang der 'gyur nas/_/zad pa'i dus su kye ma mi gzhan smos ci dgos/_/de phyir skyen par longs la 'du shes thob gyis dang /_/yang yang shin tu ro myang mda' ni mnan pa ste/_/dga' ma'i byar 'os dga' zhing dges pa rnams la ni/_/gang zhig ngang pa 
[\[47b.7\]]rnams la bzhin du mi 'phen no/_/gang tshe de skad brjod kyang chags pa dang bral ba'i/_/shAkya thub pas gdan ni bshig pa ma yin nyid/_/de nas 'di la bdud kyis mda' ni 'phangs pa ste/_/bu mo rnams dang bu rnams bdun nas byas nas so/_/der ni de yis mda' ni rab tu 'phangs na yang /_/
[\[48a\]]
[\[48a.1\]]chags par byas pa med cing brtan las g.yos pa med/_/de ltar de mthong gyur nas bdud ni yi mug cing /_/bsam mno yongs su ldan pa dal bus smras par 'gyur/_/ri dbang bu mo la dmigs gang gis phug pa yi/_/bde 'byung lha yang yongs su g.yos par gyur 
[\[48a.2\]]pa ste/_/'dis ni mda' de nyid la sems par mi byed pa/_/sems med yin nam mda' 'di de ma yin nam ci/_/de phyir me tog mda' 'di 'os pa ma yin zhing /_/dga' ba ma yin dga' bar sbyor ba 'os ma yin/_/'di ni zhi ba ma yin 'byung po'i tshogs rnams kyis/_/'jigs dang 
[\[48a.3\]]bsdigs dang rdeg pa rnams ni 'os pa'o/_/de nas bdud kyis rang gi sde ni dran pa ste/_/shAkya thub pa'i bgegs ni mnyam por byed 'dod pa/_/ral gri be con shing rtse ljon shing rtse gsum mdung lag rnams/_/sna tshogs bsam pa dang ldan rjes su 'brangs rnams 'ongs/_/phag 
[\[48a.4\]]dang nya dang bong bu rnga mo'i gdong can rnams/_/stag dang dom dang seng ge gnyis 'thung gdong can rnams/_/mig gcig kha ni du ma mgo bo gsum ldan rnams/_/gsus pa 'phyang ba rnams dang gsus pa che ba rnams/_/brla rnams pus mo'i bar du bum pa'i pus mo rnams/_/mche 
[\[48a.5\]]ba'i mtshon cha rnams dang sen mo'i mtshon cha rnams/_/keng rus gdong can rnams dang gzugs ni mang po rnams/_/gdong phyed gzhom pa rnams dang gdong ni chen po rnams/_/thal skya rnams dang thig le dmar po sna tshogs rnams/_/kha T+wAM ga rnams dang ni spre'u ltar ngud sgra rnams/_/
[\[48a.6\]]'phongs 'phyang rnams dang glang po'i rna ba'i rna ba rnams/_/pags pa'i gos can rnams dang gos dang bral ba rnams/_/gdong phyed dkar po rnams dang lus phyed ljang gu rnams/_/zangs mdog dung mdog rnams dang ljang gu dkar po rnams/_/sbrul gyi sham thabs rnams dang de bzhin 
[\[48a.7\]]lag pa rnams/_/rab tu sgra sgrogs dril bu 'khrugs pa'i ske rags rnams/_/ta la'i tshad mnyam rnams dang mdung rnams bzung ba rnams/_/byis pa'i tshad mnyam rnams dang mche ba gtsigs pa rnams/_/lug gi gdong can rnams dang bya yi mig can rnams/_/byi la'i gdong can 
[\[48b\]]
[\[48b.1\]]rnams dang mi yi lus can rnams/_/skra bkram rnams dang ral pa can dang byi bo rnams/_/gos dmar rnams dang rnam par 'khrugs pas dkris pa rnams/_/rab dga'i gdong can rnams dang gnyer ma'i gdong can rnams/_/gzi brjid 'phrog pa rnams dang yid ni 'phrog pa rnams/_/kha cig 
[\[48b.2\]]dag ni 'pho zhing drag tu phan tshun du/_/mchan khung 'khyud cing de bzhin gzhan rnams mchongs/_/mkha' la 'gro ba rnams ni kha cig rtse ba ste/_/kha cig shing gi rtse mo rnams la zhon pa'o/_/kha cig rtse gsum bskor zhing gar ni byed pa ste/_/kha cig dbyug pa gdengs shing thog sgra sgrogs pa'o/_/kha 
[\[48b.3\]]cig dga' bas khyu mchog bzhin du skad 'don zhing /_/kha cig lus 'phos rnams las rab tu 'bar bar gyur/_/'dzin par 'dod pa rnams dang 'joms par 'dod pa rnams/_/jo bo'i bka' ni rab tu skyong bar byed pa ste/_/rnam pa de ltar 'byung po'i tshogs rnams kun nas ni/_/byang chub sems 
[\[48b.4\]]dpa' de la yongs su bskor nas gnas/_/mtshan mo snga ma la ni shAkya'i khyu mchog dang /_/bdud kyi 'thab mo dus de rab tu mthong nas ni/_/nam mkha' gsal ba ma yin sa ni g.yos gyur la/_/phyogs rnams sgra dang bcas pa rab tu 'bar bar gyur/_/drag po'i shugs can rlung ni kun du 
[\[48b.5\]]g.yos gyur la/_/skar ma rnams ni mi snang zla ba gsal ma gyur/_/slar yang mtshan mor mun pa rnam par rgyas gyur la/_/rgya mtsho rnams ni thams cad kun du g.yos par gyur/_/sa gzhi 'dzin cing chos dag 'dzin pa'i klu rnams ni/_/thub pa chen po'i bgegs la bzod pa ma yin zhing /_/bdud 
[\[48b.6\]]las khros pas rnam par log pa'i mig can rnams/_/rngam pa bton zhing lus ni rnam par 'gyur ba'o/_/gtsang mar lhag par gnas pa'i lha yi drang srong ni/_/dam chos bsgrub pa'i don du mngon par zhugs pa rnams/_/bdud las yid kyis rjes su brtse ba byas pa ste/_/chags dang bral ba 
[\[48b.7\]]las kyang khros par ma gyur to/_/'tshe ba'i bdag nyid bdud kyi dpung ni de yis ni/_/byang chub rtsa ba de ni khyab pa mthong nas ni/_/chos kyi bdag nyid 'jig rten thar pa 'dod rnams kyis/_/nam mkha' dag la hA hA'i sgra dag byas par gyur/_/chos kyi cho ga de ni nye bar nyams byed pa/_/bdud dpung 
[\[49a\]]
[\[49a.1\]]gnas pa drang srong chen pos gzigs nas ni/_/ba lang rnams kyi dbus su seng ge gnas pa bzhin/_/g.yo 'gul med cing rnam par 'gyur bar yang ma gyur/_/de nas de yi 'jigs pa'i ched du bdud kyis ni/_/'byung po'i sde ni drag po rnams la bka' stsal zhing /_/
[\[49a.2\]]de nas 'di yi sde des rang rang mthu rnams kyis/_/de yi bstan pa dbye phyir blo gros byas pa'o/_/kha cig lce rnams g.yo zhing du mar rnam 'phyang zhing /_/mche ba'i rtse mo rnams rno mig rnams ljang ser zlum/_/kha rnams gdangs shing phur bu'i rna ba rnams bstan zhing /_/kun nas skrag par byed 
[\[49a.3\]]pa snyam du gnas zhes grag_/des ni gzugs dang bsam pa dag gis mi bzad pa/_/rnam pa de dag nyid du gnas pa de rnams las/_/rtsed mo byed pa'i byis pa rgyags pa rnams las ni/_/drang srong chen po zug med skyo bar ma gyur to/_/de nas kha cig khros pas lta byed rnam 'gyur zhing /_/'di 
[\[49a.4\]]la dbyug pa rdeg par 'bad pa bya ba ste/_/sngon tshe grong khyer 'joms pa'i rdo rje dang bcas ltar/_/de nas 'di yi dbyug pa dang bcas lag pa rengs/_/kha cig rdo dang shing rnams gyen du 'phyar ba ni/_/thub pa la ni rnam par 'phen par ma nus te/_/'bigs byed ri brags rnams ni rdo rjes bcom pa 
[\[49a.5\]]bzhin/_/shing rnams dang bcas rdo rnams bcas pa de bzhin lhung /_/kha cig mkha' la gyen du 'phur bas rdo rnams dang /_/dgra sta rnams dang shing rnams rnam par 'phangs pa rnams/_/kha dog gcig min mtshams sprin rkang pa rnams bzhin du/_/mngon par lhung ba ma yin nam mkhar gnas par 
[\[49a.6\]]gyur/_/gzhan ni de yi steng du 'bar ba'i khyu mchog gis/_/glang bu ri bo tsam pa'i rwa can 'phangs pa ste/_/de ni 'phangs pa tsam du nam mkhar gnas pa nyid/_/de yi mthu las rnam pa brgyar ni gas par gyur/_/'jig rten 'jig tshe lhun po rab tu 'bar ba yis/_/gser gyi bya skyibs 
[\[49a.7\]]rnams kyi phye ma rnam bzhin du/_/kha cig nyi ma shar ba bzhin du 'bar ba yi/_/me mdag char pa chen po mkha' las phab par gyur/_/byang chub shing gi rtsa bar rab tu rnam 'phangs pa'i/_/me stag 'phro ba dang bcas me mdag char pa de/_/drang srong dam pa mchog gis byams pa'i gnas pa las/_/
[\[49b\]]
[\[49b.1\]]ut+pal dmar po'i 'dab ma'i char par gyur pa'o/_/lus dang sems la rnam par gnod par bya ba'i phyir/_/rnam pa de ltar phab pa de dang de rnams kyis/_/rang gi nges pa'i gnyen 'dun nye bar 'khyud nas bzhin/_/shAkya thub pa gdan las g.yos par ma gyur to/_/de nas gzhan 
[\[49b.2\]]rnams 'khogs pa'i ljon shing rnams nas ltar/_/kha rnams las ni sbrul rnams rnam par skyugs pa ste/_/sngags kyis bcings bzhin de rnams de yi drung du ni/_/song ba ma yin dbugs min g.yos par ma gyur ro/_/gzhan rnams glog dang bcas shing thog dang bcas pa yis/_/chu 'dzin chen 
[\[49b.3\]]po gtum pa'i dbyangs ldan rnams gyur nas/_/ljon shing de la rdo ba'i char pa phab pa ste/_/de ni me tog char pa mdzes par gyur pa'o/_/dbang phyug ma yin sron par dkar ba'i mi yi ni/_/snying la yongs su g.yos pa'i khro ba bzhin du ni/_/de nas gzhan gyis gzhu la mda' dag bkang ba ni/_/de nyid 
[\[49b.4\]]du ni 'bar zhing lhung bar ma gyur to/_/yul la rab tu 'jug la 'khor ba la 'jigs pa/_/yongs su rtog pa po yi dbang po lnga rnams bzhin/_/gzhan rnams dag gis mda' lnga rab tu 'phangs pa rnams/_/nam mkhar gnas bzhin thub pa la ni ma lhung ngo /_/gzhan ni brdeg par 'dod pas khros 
[\[49b.5\]]shing rab bskor zhing /_/dbyug pa bzung nas drang srong chen po'i mngon du phyogs/_/don med byed pa'i nyes pa rnams la 'jig rten bzhin/_/des ni 'dod pa ma thob dbang med 'gyel bar gyur/_/bud med sprin pa nag mo lag na thod pa can/_/drang srong chen po'i thugs ni rmongs par byed ces grag_/g.yo 
[\[49b.6\]]ba'i bdag nyid can gyi blo ni lung la bzhin/_/der ni nges pa med par bskor zhing gnas ma yin/_/kha cig mig ni rab tu 'bar bar byas nas ni/_/mig gi me yis sbrul gyi dug bzhin bsreg 'dod pas/_/'dod pa'i bdag nyid can gyis nyer gnas dpal bzhin du/_/der ni drang srong bzhugs 
[\[49b.7\]]pa mthong bar ma gyur to/_/ye shes ting 'dzin dag gis 'gro bya nyes legs chos/_/lus kyi ngal ba rnams kyis 'thob par 'dod pa bzhin/_/de bzhin gzhan ni rdo ba lci mo bteg pa ni/_/rab tu 'bad pa rnam bcom don med ngal bar gyur/_/de bzhin gzhan rnams dred dang seng ge'i rnam pa yis/_/
[\[50a\]]
[\[50a.1\]]chen po'i sgra rnams mthon po rab tu sgrogs pa ste/_/rdo rjes bsnun pa'i nam mkha' 'gas pa zhes snyam nas/_/sems can rnams ni gang gis kun nas 'khums par gyur/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]glang po rnams ni nyam thag skad 'don zhing /_/rnam par 'bros shing thim pa nyid 
[\[50a.2\]]du gyur pa ste/_/de yi mtshan mor phyogs rnams las ni nyin mo bzhin/_/nyam thag bya rnams skad 'don yongs su babs par gyur/_/rnam pa de ltar de rnams kyi sgra de rnams kyis/_/'byung po thams cad rnams su g.yos pa yin na yang /_/khab rnams dag gi sgra rnams dag gis mkha' 
[\[50a.3\]]lding ltar/_/thub pa 'jigs pa ma yin 'khums par ma gyur to/_/'jigs pa 'dron pa yongs su bskor ba'i tshogs rnams las/_/ji lta ji ltar thub pa'i 'jigs pa ma yin pa/_/de lta de ltar chos 'dzin rnams kyi dgra bo yi/_/bdud rnams mya ngan las dang khros nas byer ba'o/_/de 
[\[50a.4\]]nas 'byung po cung zhig gzugs ni mtho ba ma yin zhing /_/khyad par 'phags par gyur pa nam mkhar gnas nyid kyis/_/drang srong la gnod dgra ma yin la khros mthong nas/_/bdud lha chen po'i dbyangs kyis smras par gyur pa'o/_/don med ngal bar byed par 'os pa ma yin bdud/_/
[\[50a.5\]]'tshe ba'i bdag nyid spongs la bde bar song mdzod dang /_/ri bo chen po lhun po rnams kyi sa bzhin du/_/khyod kyis bskyod cing bsgul bar nus pa ma yin no/_/sa yi brtan pa nyid dang chu yi gsher ba nyid/_/me yi dro ba nyid ni rab tu 'dor ba yang /_/bskal pa du mar bsgrub pa'i bsod nams las 
[\[50a.6\]]mnga' ba/_/'dis ni nan tan nges par 'dor ba ma yin no/_/'dis ni nges pa gang dang pha rol gnon pa dang /_/gzi brjid gang dang brtse ba skye dgu rnams la brten/_/'od zer stong gis mun rnams ma bcom gnas bzhin du/_/'di ni de nyid ma thob nas ni ldang ma yin/_/shing la bsrubs nas me ni 
[\[50a.7\]]rnyed par 'gyur ba ste/_/sa la brkos na chu yang 'thob par 'gyur ba nyid/_/rigs pa dang ni ldan pa'i thams cad byas pa ste/_/brtson 'grus ldan pa'i bsgrub bya ma yin ci yang med/_/de phyir chags sogs nad rnams dag la gnas pa yi/_/'jig rten nyam thag pa la thugs rje mnga' ba ni/_/
[\[50b\]]
[\[50b.1\]]ye shes dman gyi don du yongs su ngal ba po/_/sman pa chen po 'di la bgegs 'os ma yin no/_/lam ngan mang po rnams kyis 'jig rten stor ba la/_/gang zhig zhi bas dam pa'i lam ni rjes tshol ba/_/chud zos pa la legs par ston byed ded dpon bzhin/_/ston mdzad de ni bskyod par 
[\[50b.2\]]rigs pa ma yin no/_/mun pa chen po sems can chud zos rnams la ni/_/ye shes sgron ma rab tu gsal bar mdzad pa 'di/_/mun pa la ni rab tu 'bar ba'i sgron ma yin/_/'phags pa'i mya ngan 'da' bar byed par 'os ma yin/_/'khor ba'i rnam 'gyur chu bo chen por 'gro ba ni/_/pha rol 
[\[50b.3\]]ma thob par ni bying bar gzigs nas ni/_/gang gis de ni sgrol bar byed la rab zhugs te/_/de la sdig pa 'phags pa su yis sems par byed/_/bzod pa'i chu rgyus bstan pa'i rtsa ba brtan pa ste/_/spyod pa'i me tog blo dang dran pa'i yal ga can/_/ye shes ljon shing chos kyi 'bras bu ster ba 
[\[50b.4\]]po/_/'phel bzhin pa ni zlog par 'os pa ma yin no/_/gang gi snying la rmongs pa'i zhags pa sra rnams kyis/_/bcings pa'i skye dgu rnams kyi grol phyir blo mnga' zhing /_/'gro ba bcings pa thar pa'i ched du 'o brgyal ba/_/de la brdegs pa khyod kyis 'thad pa ma yin no/_/byang chub ched du 
[\[50b.5\]]las gang dag na mdzad pa rnams/_/de rnams kyis ni nges par de ring dus yin la/_/ji ltar thub pa snga ma rnams ni de bzhin du/_/gnas der de bzhin 'di ni nye bar bzhugs pa'o/_/dbyig 'dzin sa gzhi yi ni lte ba 'di yin zhing /_/snying po dam pa mtha' dag dang ni ldan pa nyid/_/gang zhig 'di yis 
[\[50b.6\]]ting nge 'dzin gyi shugs bzod pa'i/_/sa yi phyogs de 'di las gzhan pa yod ma yin/_/de phyir khro bar ma byed zhi bar nyer song la/_/bdud ni khyod kyi drags pas nga rgyal ma gyur cig_/dpal ni mi brtan blo gtad par ni nus min zhing /_/go 'phang g.yo la rgyags par mngon par nyer 'gro 
[\[50b.7\]]zhing /_/de nas de yi tshig de yang dag thos nas dang /_/thub pa chen po'i g.yo ba med pa nyid mthong nas/_/gang gis mda' yis 'gro ba'i snying la rdeg byed pa'i/_/bdud ni 'bad pa bcom zhing yid mi bde bar song /_/dga' ba med cing 'bras med ngal ba byas pa rnams/_/pha bong rnams ni khyu 
[\[51a\]]
[\[51a.1\]]mchog ljon shing rnams gtor te/_/dgrar gyur dgra bo'i dpung ni gtso bo bsad pa ltar/_/de nas 'di yi dpung ni phyogs phyogs bros pa'o/_/me tog tog can 'khor dang bcas pa bros shing nges par 'pham pa ni/_/drang srong chen po mun pa 'pham zhing rdul dang

[\[51a.3\]]rnams sa la bab pa ste/_/lang tsho ma bzhin ngal ba med par mtshan mo gsal/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_bdud las rnam par rgyal ba'i le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o//_//de nas brtan dang zhi ba yis/_/bdud kyi stobs ni pham mdzad nas/_/

[\[51b\]]
[\[51b.1\]]nyid 'phel bar gyur/_/'di rnams sdig pa'i las can rnams/_/srog chags rnams ni ngan 'gror 'gro/_/'di gzhan dge ba'i las can rnams/_/mtho ris su ni rab gnas so/_/shin tu mi bzad dmyal ba ru/_/rab tu 'jigs par gyur pa rnams/_/'di rnams sdug bsngal sna tshogs

[\[52a\]]

[\[52b.1\]]sred dang yongs ldan byis rnams kyis/_/rgyun chad med par rjes su myong /_/'di gzhan bsod nams las rnams kyis/_/mtho ris su ni skyes pa rnams/_/'dod chags me yis drag tu ni/_/me ltar rab tu sreg par byed/_/yul rnams kyis ni ngoms med rnams/_/gang

[\[53a.1\]]ste/_/tsam tsom du ni bskor ba yi/_/'gro bas gnas ni thob ma yin/_/de ltar des ni 'gro ba lnga/_/lha yi spyan gyis gzigs pa ste/_/chu shing gshags pa bzhin du ni/_/srid par snying po rnyed ma yin/_/de nas de yi mtshan mo'i thun/_/gsum

[\[53b.5\]]yod na/_/skye mched drug ni 'byung ba'o/_/ming dang gzugs ni rgyu gang zhes/_/de yi de nas gyur de nas/_/de yi skye ba rnam shes su/_/ye shes pha rol son pas mkhyen/_/rnam par shes pa 'das pa na/_/ming dang gzugs ni rab gnas te/_/sa bon bskrun pa rdzogs pa na/_/myu gu

[\[54a\]]
[\[54a.1\]]'bar bar byed na yang /_/'bar ba de yis de gdung ste/_/de bzhin phan tshun rgyu nyid do/_/de ltar rnam par shes pa las/_/ming gzugs 'byung ba mkhyen gyur pa/_/de nas dbang po 'byung 'gyur zhing /_/dbang po las byung reg pa'o/_/reg pa las skyes

[\[54a.3\]]blo gros nges par song ba ni/_/skye ba zad las 'chi dang rga ba 'gags pa ste/_/srid pa nyams pa las kyang skye ba nyams pa nyid/_/len pa 'gags pa las ni srid pa rnam ldog cing /_/de nas srid 'gags pa las de ni 'gag pa ste/_/tshor ba yod pa min na sred pa yod ma yin/_/

[\[54a.5\]]pa ste/_/'du byed 'gags pa las kyang de ni 'gag pa'o/_/ma rig pa ni mtha' dag med las de bzhin du/_/'du byed 'gag par drang srong chen pos mkhyen pa ste/_/'di las mkhyen bya 'di ni yang dag mkhyen mdzad nas/_/sangs rgyas zhes ni 'jig rten na rab 
[\[54a.6\]]gnas par gyur/_/yan lag brgyad dang ldan pa rab tu shar ba yi/_/rtse mor myur 'gro dam pa'i lta ba'i lam gyis ni/_/srid rtse'i bar las mchog gyur bdag med rnam gzigs nas/_/bud shing tshig pa'i me bzhin zhi bar gshegs par gyur/_/de nas bdag nyid mdzad pa de yi 
[\[54a.7\]]gyur pa ni/_/mchog gi drang srong mchog dang mchog min mkhyen pa'i tshogs/_/gna' ma rnams kyis don dam rgyu las gshegs pa yi/_/snying po'i lam 'di bdag gis lhag par thob par gyur/_/thun ni bzhi pa'i skya rengs shar ba'i yud tsam na/_/rgyu dang mi rgyu'i 'byung po rnams ni zhi 
[\[54b\]]
[\[54b.1\]]ba na/_/drang srong mchog gis 'gyur ba med pa'i go 'phang dang /_/kun gyi mgon gyis thams cad mkhyen pa nyid brnyes so/_/de nyid 'di ni sangs rgyas nyid kyis mkhyen pa na/_/bud med chang gis myos bzhin sa ni g.yos gyur la/_/grub pa rnams kyis mngon gang phyogs ni rab

[\[54b.3\]]gyur/_/man dA ra ba'i me tog rnams dang pad+ma dang /_/gser dang bai DUr+ya yi rang bzhin pad+ma ni/_/mkha' las bab pa rnams kyis shAkya thub pa'i sa/_/de tshe mtho ris bzhin du kun nas bkram par gyur/_/yud tsam de ni kha cig khro ba med gyur la/_/nad med gyur zhing mi 
[\[54b.4\]]dga' bar yang ma song ste/_/sdig pa bsnyen pa ma yin rgyags par ma byas shing /_/grub pa brnyes pa bzhin du 'jig rten zhi bar gyur/_/thar pa'i 'dod pa'i lha yi tshogs rnams dga' ba ste/_/sems can 'og tu song ba rnams kyang dga' bar gyur/_/yon tan phyogs kyis 
[\[54b.5\]]'phel bas chos ni rgyas pa ste/_/rdul dang mun pa dag las 'gro ba gyen du byung /_/de yi las kyis ya mtshan dga' bas gang ba yi/_/bu ram shing pa'i rigs kyi mi skyong drang srong rnams/_/rgyal po'i drang srong rnams dang drang srong che rnams kyis/_/yang dag mchod rnams 
[\[54b.6\]]mkha' la gzhal med rnams su gnas/_/mthong min 'byung po'i tshogs kyis drang srong chen po yi/_/ston pa rnams ni mthon por 'thon zhing sgrogs pa ste/_/gson pa'i 'jig rten 'phel ba bzhin du dga' gyur zhing /_/ltung ba mchog la bzhin du bdud ni yi mug cing /_/de nas 
[\[54b.7\]]nad mi mnga' ba zhag bdun bdag nyid kyis/_/sems la gzigs shing spyan mi 'dzums par bzhugs pa ste/_/kho bos 'dir ni rnam par thar pa brnyes so zhes/_/thub pas yid la re ba yongs su rdzogs par gyur/_/de nas rkyen nyid thob pa 'di ni thub pa nyid/_/bdag med 
[\[55a\]]
[\[55a.1\]]cho gar rnam par gzhag pas gsan nas ni/_/zhi phyir 'jig rten dag la mchog tu brtse bas so/_/sangs rgyas spyan gyis rnam par gzigs par gyur pa'o/_/lta ba ngan pas chud zos 'bad la 'khrugs pa yi/_/'gro ba rdul ni rgya chen dang ldan

[\[55a.3\]]chung ngu'i rdul nyid dang /_/ldan pa'i skye bor yid ni rnam par dpyad pa'o/_/de nas zhi ba'i don du gdams pa'i ched du nges par ni/_/gzung ba'i bde bar gshegs pa'i thugs ni shes gyur nas/_/mngon par snang zhing 'gro la phan par 'dod pa can/_/mtho ris 
[\[55a.4\]]gnas pa'i gtso bo nyid ni nye bar 'ongs/_/sdig pa spangs shing don mdzad gdan la bzhugs pa po/_/mchog gi chos ni gzigs pa de la mchog gnyis so/_/mchog tu zhi bas rab tu bstod nas de gnyis kyis/_/'jig rten phan pa'i don du tshig 'di gsol ba'o/_/e 
[\[55a.5\]]ma 'gro ba 'di gang nor 'os 'di lta bu/_/skye dgu rnams la rjes su brtse mdzad khyed kyi thugs/_/'jig rten na yod skye bo rnam par bsre ma'i rgyu/_/chen po'i rdul dang nyung ngu'i rdul nyid ldan pa'o/_/thub pa srid pa'i mtsho las rang nyid rab rgal nas/_/'gro ba sdug bsngal 
[\[55a.6\]]bying ba rab tu drongs mdzod cig_/legs pa'i dbang phyug gis ni phun sum tshogs pa thob nas bzhin/_/gzhan rnams la yang rang gi yon tan gyis sbyor mdzod/_/don mkhyen 'di na'i don dang pha rol du 'am gang /_/skye bo kha cig 'di na bdag gi phan pa byed/_/gang 
[\[55a.7\]]zhig 'jig rten dge ba'i ched du rab zhugs de/_/mi yi 'jig rten dang ni mtho ris su rnyed dka'/_/de ltar drang srong che la tshig 'di gsol nas ni/_/lha na gnas pa de gnyis ji ltar 'ongs par gshegs/_/thub pa yis kyang de yi tshig de rab mnod nas/_/'gro ba rnam par thar 
[\[55b\]]
[\[55b.1\]]phyir nges par rgyas par mdzad/_/de nas bsod snyoms dus su phyogs bzhir drang srong la/_/lha na gnas pas lhung bzed rnams ni phul ba ste/_/gau ta mas bzhi po de rnams rab bzhes nas/_/de yi chos kyi ched du gcig nyid mdzad pa'o/_/de nas de tshe don bcas rab song 
[\[55b.2\]]tshong dpon gnyis/_/mdza' bshes nyid kyis lha yis rab tu bskul ba dag_/rgya chen thugs mnga' drang srong che la thog ma ru/_/dga' ba dang ldan phyag byas nas ni bsod snyoms phul/_/rtsibs 'phur dang ni rangs byed bu ste de gnyis ni/_/chos 'dzin nus pa'i blo gros 
[\[55b.3\]]thub pas gzigs pa ste/_/de gnyis lha na gshegs par thugs kyis mngon gzigs nas/_/dge slong lnga po rnams la thugs kyis gshegs pa'o/_/de nas zhi ba'i gdams pa las ni gau ta mas/_/nyi ma shar bas mun bzhin mun pa rnam 'joms phyir/_/bA rA Na sIs 
[\[55b.4\]]brgyan pa'i sna tshogs nags tshal can/_/'jigs byed shing rtas bsnyen pa'i grong khyer dge bar gshegs/_/de nas khyu mchog 'dra ba'i spyan mnga' glang chen myos pa'i 'gros ldan pa/_/'gro ba mngon par rnam par 'dus phyir kA shi sa la gshegs bzhed pa/_/glang chen bzhin du 
[\[55b.5\]]rang gi sku lus ma lus phyir la rab log nas/_/thub pas spyan mig mi 'dzums par ni byang chub shing gi rtsa bar gzigs/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'i le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o//_//zhi 
[\[55b.6\]]zhing mdangs ldan mdzad pa kun nas brnyes pa de/_/gcig pur rab gshegs mang po dang ni thabs cig bzhin/_/lam na dge slong chos ldan kha cig gis mthong nas/_/ya mtshan dang bcas thal mor bcas pas 'di skad gsol/_/dbang po'i rta ni g.yo bar dbang po'i rta thul zhing /_/
[\[55b.7\]]sems can chags rnams ji ltar chags ma gyur/_/shin tu ro ldan gsar pa'i shes rab ro yis ni/_/ri bong gis mtshon dang mtshungs tshim par gyur pa rnams/_/brtan pa khyod kyi zhal ni ji ltar rab rgyas shing /_/'dir ni dbang po rnams kyi dbang phyug mdzad pa ste/_/khyu mchog chen 
[\[56a\]]
[\[56a.1\]]po'i spyan mnga' nges par don mdzad cing /_/khyod kyi bla ma su lags gang las grub pa gsungs/_/de rnams la des smras pa bdag la ston pa med/_/bdag la mchod bya med cing smad bya su yang med/_/mya ngan 'das pa thob cing gzhan rnams dang mi 
[\[56a.2\]]mnyam/_/bdag ni chos la rang byung du ni shes par mdzod/_/gang phyir gzhan rnams kyis ma rtogs pa'i rtogs bya ni/_/bdag gis mtha' dag rtogs pa de phyir sangs rgyas te/_/gang phyir bdag gi nyon mongs rnams ni dgra bzhin 'pham/_/de phyir zhi ba dag ni kun nas 
[\[56a.3\]]shes bar mdzod/_/sdug bsngal rnams kyis nyam thag grogs la phan don du/_/zhi ba kho bo 'di yang bA rA Na sIr 'gro/_/der ni 'chi med chos kyi rnga dag rdung ba ste/_/snyan grags bde ba'i phyir min nga rgyal phyir ma yin/_/rgal bas 'gro ba rnams ni rgal bar 'gyur bgyi zhing /_/
[\[56a.4\]]grol bas sems can rnams ni yongs su thar bar bgyi/_/de ltar gson pa'i 'jig rten nyam thag par mthong nas/_/sngon du bdag gi dam bca' 'di ni gyur pa'o/_/'di na kha cig nor ni thob nas bdag nyid ni/_/gcig tu nyid kyang 'dzin par byed pa ngo mtshar cing /_/skye bo chen po ma 
[\[56a.5\]]btsums mig la gang gis kyang /_/khyad 'phags thob nas rnam par 'dzin pa de don yin/_/gang zhig skam la gnas pas mi ni chus khyer ba/_/'dren par 'dod min de ni legs pa'i gzugs ma yin/_/gang zhig gter ni thob nas skye bo dbul po la/_/nor gyi sbyor

[\[56b\]]
[\[56b.1\]]gang yang rnams su thub pa rnams kyis ni/_/byang chub nges shing kA shi rnams dang chos smra des/_/de nas nye bar 'ongs nas a la la zhes pa/_/rnam par brjod nas ji ltar 'dod bzhin rab spangs nas/_/yang dang yang du ya mtshan mig ni g.yo ba yis/_/re 'dod skyes shing

[\[57a\]]
[\[57a.1\]]bcom ldan gyis smras pa/_/mchod 'os dgra bcom la ni gus pa ma yin pas/_/sngar gyi spyod pas dge slong rnams kye ma smra shig_/bstod dang smad pa nges par bdag gis mnyam pa ste/_/khyed rnams bsod nams min las rnam par bzlog par bgyi/_/sangs

[\[57b.1\]]ba med thob ngal bas min/_/de ltar don med 'dod pa rnams la chags pa ni/_/rdul dang mun pa dag gis zil non sems ldan rnams/_/bstan bcos don la nus kyang grub pa thob min na/_/chags bral 'gog pa'i cho ga la ni sngon nyid do/_/ji ltar nad kyis mngon par bcom pa'i

[\[57b.5\]]'tsho ba lo legs chen po ste/_/yang dag rtsol bas stobs rab rjes su 'gro ba can/_/kun du yang dag dran pa'i sbas pas sbas pa ste/_/ting 'dzin gzims mal dang ni gdan dang khyab pa yis/_/de ltar mchog gi yan lag brgyad 'dir 'di lam ste/_/'chi dang rga dang na ba

[\[58a\]]
[\[58a.1\]]can gyi 'am yang na lus can ma yin pa'i/_/gang zhig yon tan med pa gang la rab zhugs kyang /_/mdor bsdus nas de sdug bsngal du ni shes par mdzod/_/de ltar 'bar ba zhi ba'i me ni chung na yang /_/lhan cig skyes pa dro ba'i ngo bo spong min nyid/_/de bzhin bdag tu

[\[59b.1\]]yang chos la spyod/_/gang zhig lus ni 'thon pa ste/_/sems kyis 'thon pa ma yin pa/_/nags na gnas kyang chags pa can/_/khyim na gnas shes de shes bya/_/gang zhig sems ni 'thon pa ste/_/lus kyis 'thon pa ma yin pa/_/khyim na gnas kyang nga med

[\[60a\]]
[\[60a.1\]]pa bzhin/_/der ni 'od srungs gzigs pa'o/_/ri rnams dang ni nags rnams su/_/gnas rnams yod pa na yang de/_/rnam par 'dul bzhed stobs bcu bas/_/rab tu brten nas bslangs pa'o/_/de nas grub pa nyams tshad phyir/_/me khang lto 'phye ches 
[\[60a.2\]]bsnyen par/_/ma rungs gnas des 'di la byin/_/der gzims mtshan mor rang gnas shing /_/ltos bral thub ba chen po la/_/mthong ba'i dug can khros pa yi/_/sprul des mthong nas dbugs phyung ngo /_/de yi khro bas 'bar ba yi/_/me yi khang par gyur pa des/_/drang srong 
[\[60a.3\]]chen po'i sku lus la/_/'jigs pa bzhin du mes ma reg_/dus mtha'i me nyer zhi ba na/_/tshangs pa bzhugs pa mdzes pa ltar/_/me yi khang par bsregs na yang /_/gau ta ma rnam 'gyur med/_/gang tshe sangs rgyas der bzhugs tshe/_/gnod med g.yo ba med pa ste/_/de tshe 
[\[60a.4\]]klu de ya mtshan zhing /_/drang srong mchog la phyag 'tshal lo/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]nags der skye bo yis/_/thub pa der bzhugs shes gyur nas/_/srugs pa 'o brgyal 'di 'dra ba'i/_/dge slong tshig ces rjes su brtse/_/de nas mtshan mo 'das pa na/_/rnam par 'dren pas 'od srungs la/_/sbrul

[\[60a.7\]]kyi tshogs rnams kyang /_/lnga brgyas de yi chos la bsnyen/_/der ni spun zla slob mar bcas/_/mthar phyin shun lpags spangs pa na/_/ga ya chu klung zhes byas kyang /_/der song lam la bsnyen pa'o/_/de nas thub pas slob mar bcas/_/'od srungs gsum po de rnams la/_/sa 'dzin ga ya'i rtse mor 
[\[60b\]]
[\[60b.1\]]ni/_/nges par mthar phyin gtam mdzad do/_/rnam par rtog pa'i skye gnas dang /_/gti mug rnam bkram du ba can/_/chags sdang me yis 'gro ba ni/_/thams cad dbang med par bsreg go/_/de ltar nyon mongs mes tshig cing /_/rab tu ma zhi mgon med can/_/rga na 'chi ba'i me rnams 
[\[60b.2\]]kyis/_/slar yang rgyun mi chad par sreg_/rnam pa sna tshogs mes tshig pa'i/_/'gro ba skyabs med 'di mthong nas/_/yid bcas dbang po dang bcas pa'i/_/gzugs la blo ldan skyo ba ste/_/skyo ba las ni chags bral ro/_/chags bral las ni rnam grol zhing /_/rnam grol de nas thams cad 
[\[60b.3\]]na/_/rnam par grol zhes shes pa'o/_/skye ba'i rgyun ni yongs 'dzad cing /_/tshangs pa'i spyod pa 'dir gnas la/_/byed pa thams cad byas pa ste/_/sred pa gzhan ni yod ma yin/_/bcom ldan gyi gsung 'di thos nas/_/dge slong stong phrag dag gi sems/_/len pa med nas zag pa las/_/
[\[60b.4\]]de ma thag tu rnam par grol/_/sbyin pa tshul khrims dul rnams kyis/_/chos ni yongs su yongs bskor bzhin/_/de nas shes rab rgya che ba'i/_/'od srungs gsum gyis sangs rgyas mdzes/_/chos don 'dod pa dang bral ba'i/_/sems can ngan pa'i srog bzhin du/_/bgres mchog de rnams kyis 
[\[60b.5\]]bor ba'i/_/dka' thub nags de mdzes ma gyur/_/de nas de rnams kyis yongs bskor/_/thub pa rgyal po'i khab tu gshegs/_/ma ga d+hA yi mi dbang phyir/_/sngon gyi dam bca' dran pa'o/_/de nas 'od ma'i tshal yul ni/_/de bzhin gshegs pas rab tu thob/_/blon po rnams ni rjes 'gro bas/_/

[\[61a\]]
[\[61a.1\]]bsdad pa'o/_/der mi rnams kyis bsams par gyur/_/e ma shAkya thub pa'i stobs/_/'od srungs bcom ldan drang srong ni/_/'di yi slob ma nyid du song /_/de nas de'i sems sangs rgyas kyis/_/mkhyen nas 'od srungs la smras pa/_/'od srungs yon tan gang mthong nas/_/

[\[61b\]]
[\[61b.1\]]rab 'bab chu gter sprin bzhin du/_/'bar ba dang ni 'bab pa yis/_/rgya cher rnam par gnon te mdzes/_/der ni mig rnams chags byas nas/_/ya mtshan gyen phyogs skye bo rnams/_/mchod mang po las phyag 'tshal zhing /_/seng ge'i sgra ni sgrogs pa'o/_/de nas rdzu 'phrul bsdus nas des/_/bya ba

[\[61b.3\]]legs la gnas pas/_/chos thos par ni 'dod pa yi/_/bzo sbyangs la ni bka' stsal pa/_/gzi chen ldan pa yid bcas shing /_/dbang po thul ba dbang por bcas/_/sa yi bdag po gzugs nyid ni/_/skye ba dang ni rnam par nyams/_/yon tan 'phel phyir 'di yi ni/_/skye dang 'gag pa shes bya

[\[61b.6\]]par mthong na/_/chags pa rnams las rnam par grol/_/mi bden par mthong 'ching ba ste/_/bden par mthong ba rnam par grol/_/bdag yod ces ni 'jig rten 'dir/_/rab tu 'jug 'di bden min 'dzin/_/gal te bdag yod gyur na de/_/rtag gam yang na mi rtag nyid/_/phyogs ni

[\[62a.1\]]ni/_/'god pa med cing skye ba med/_/gal te bdag 'di khyab bdag 'gyur/_/gang du mi gnas pa ma yin/_/'di dang song nas dang thar par/_/thams cad du ni cig car 'gyur/_/khyab bdag nyid phyir bya med 'di'i/_/las kyi bya ba med par 'gyur/_/las rnams ma

[\[62b\]]
[\[62b.1\]]gnas pa yi/_/mi ni du ma rnams dang lha na gnas pa rnams/_/thub pa'i gsung thob pa las rnam dag bsam ldan rnams/_/'gyur med 'chi ba med pa'i go 'phang thob par gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_slob ma mang po dang ldan pa'i le'u ste

[\[62b.3\]]'gro la phan pa'i phyir/_/ye shes las gyur dge ba'i sgron ma bsnams pa ste/_/tshangs dang lha dang de bzhin 'phags pa rnams kyi ni/_/gnas pa de dang de yis sangs rgyas rnam par gnas/_/de nas rta thul dbang po'i rta ni kun bsdams shing /_/bsod snyoms 'dod pas rgyal po'i khab

[\[62b.5\]]nas tshig ni mngon par smras/_/khyod kyi gsar pa'i rtags mtshan dang ni zhi ba nyid/_/mthong nas kho bo'i blo gros ya mtshan mchog gyur te/_/de phyir re zhig gal te de nyid rig pa dang /_/khyod kyi ston pa'i mtshan ci smra ba gang yin smros/_/de ltar mchod mang sngon 'gro bram ze de yis ni/_/de skad

[\[63a\]]
[\[63a.1\]]pa'o/_/de ltar rta thul gyi ni tshig 'di thos nas ni/_/gnyis skyes nyer rgyal zhes bya'i ming can de yi ni/_/skyen par chos la mig ni rdul dang bral ba ni/_/dge zhing rnam par dag par nges par gyur pa'o/_/rgyu med pa dang bya ba med dang dbang phyug ste/_/zhing shes pa ni

[\[63a.3\]]pa ni/_/sangs rgyas pa yi bstan las 'gyur zhes snyam par gyur/_/bdag tu 'du shes skyes pa nye bar mkhyud nas ni/_/ngar 'dzin spong ba ma yin de nas nga yi nyid/_/sgron ma dang ni nyin mor byed pa yod pa na/_/'od kyi rnam par nyams par gtan tshigs shes bya ci/_/ji ltar pad+ma'i chu ba che bar gyur pa

[\[63a.5\]]rab thob nas/_/rang bzhin gnas shing mkhas pa 'od ma'i tshal du song /_/de bzhin rab tu dang bas mchog dang ldan pa yi/_/nye rgyal kyang ni mngon du zhugs pa mthong nas ni/_/thos pa dang ni bsam pas bya ba mnyam pa yi/_/maud gal rigs su rab tu skyes pas smras pa'o/_/dge slong de nyid gang

[\[63a.7\]]nas tshig de nyid ni de yis smras pa ste/_/thos nas nyid kyi yang dag mig ni skyes par gyur/_/las dang bsam pa rnam dag blo ldan de gnyis ni/_/lag nas sgron ma bzhin du de nyid mthong ba ste/_/shes pas ston la rnam par g.yo min bsam ldan dag_/de ni lta ba'i don du da lta nyid du gshegs/_/slob

[\[63b.1\]]ma'i sder bcas de gnyis rgyang ma nas gzigs nas/_/bcom ldan drang srong chen pos dge slong rnams la gsungs/_/shes rab can dang rdzu 'phrul ldan pa rnams kyi gtso/_/kho bo'i mchog gi slob ma 'di gnyis 'dir 'ongs so/_/de nas zab dang brtan pa'i gsung ni zhi ba yis/_/thub pas de

[\[63b.3\]]yis ngur smrig gos dang ldan pa yi/_/dge slong nyid du skad cig gis ni gyur pa'o/_/slob ma'i sder bcas cha lugs de yis de gnyis kyis/_/mgo bo dag gis thams cad mkhyen la mchod pa ste/_/de nas sangs rgyas kyis chos de gnyis la gsungs shing /_/dus kyis de gnyis kyis ni nges 
[\[63b.4\]]legs thob par gyur/_/de nas 'od srungs rigs kyi sgron ma gnyis skyes kyis/_/kha dog dang ni gzugs dang nor dang nyer ldan las/_/rdzu 'phrul dang ni bdag ldan bzang mo rnams spangs nas/_/thar pa'i ched du ngur smrig bzung zhing gshegs pa'o/_/mdog [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]gser gyi mchod sdong dang 
[\[63b.5\]]mtshungs 'bar ba ni/_/bu [{]mangs [,]mang [}]ming bcas mchod rten du ni nges mthong nas/_/dngos po thams cad spangs shing ya mtshan dang bcas pas/_/thams cad mkhyen la thal mo rab sbyar mngon par song /_/thub pa la ni rgyang nas mgo bos phyag 'tshal zhing /_/phyag 'tshal nas ni ji lta ba bzhin mthon 
[\[63b.6\]]por gsol/_/bdag ni slob ma bcom ldan bdag gi ston pa lags/_/mun pa rnams su bstan pa bdag gi sgron mar gyur/_/gnyis skyes skom pa skyes shing nye bar 'ongs par ni/_/mkhyen nas bsam pa rnam dag thar 'dod de la ni/_/sems la gsung gi chab kyis rab tu tshim mdzad pa/_/de bzhin

[\[64a\]]
[\[64a.1\]]pas chos ni phra mor gsungs tsam na/_/de yis don ni mtha' dag yang dag thob gyur te/_/de phyir yang dag rig dang na tshod che gnyis las/_/dgra bcom 'od srungs chen po nyid du mtshan gyur to/_/gang gis lus las gzhan dang yang na lus su ni/_/bdag dang bdag gi

[\[64a.3\]]khrims dang ni brtul zhugs mchog tu ma bzung ngo /_/phyin ci log tu gyur par song ba gang gis ni/_/mngon du bskor zhing mchog ni rab tu thob ma yin/_/bden pa bzhi po'i go 'phang rnams ni mthong gyur nas/_/the tshom som nyir gyur te yang dag mchod par gyur/_/'dod pa gang rnams su ni yang dag 
[\[64a.4\]]rgyar nyid dang /_/brgyal bar gyur dang brgyal bar 'gyur nyid 'jig rten pa/_/mi gtsang bden pa ma yin de rnams des shes nas/_/'dod chags shes bya'i yul de spangs par gyur pa'o/_/de bzhin du ni byams par gyur ba'i sems kyis des/_/bshes dang bshes min rnams su khyad par med pa ste/_/
