[\[64a.6\]]'jug pa'i nyes pa rnams ni rtogs nas so/_/rmongs las snyoms 'jug rnams su thar pa'i 'du shes dang /_/gzugs med lha rnams mdzad pa can du rtogs nas ni/_/zhi zhing mtshan ma med pa'i sems ni gzung nas su/_/gzugs med sred pa las ni chags dang bral bar song /_/chu bo sin d+hu'i shugs 
[\[64a.7\]]chen rab tu 'jug pa bzhin/_/sems kyis g.yo zhing skyod par byed pa shes nas ni/_/brtan pa'i brten las rgod pa rnam par spangs pa ste/_/zhi zhing rdzing bu gang ba bzhin du gnas par gyur/_/snying po med pa'i phyir dang 'god ba'i chos nyid phyir/_/bdag med nyid phyir dngos rnams yang dag rab mthong nas/_/
[\[64b\]]
[\[64b.1\]]khyad 'phags nyid dang mnyam dang mchog nyid mthong ba ste/_/nga rgyal rnam par spangs nas yod ma yin zhes rtogs/_/ye shes me yis mi shes mun pa rnams bcom nas/_/zad pa can dang zad pa med par sna tshogs mthong /_/rnal 'byor gyis ni rig pa nyid ni yongs bsgoms nas/_/ma rig pa ni 
[\[64b.2\]]mtha' dag ji lta ba bzhin spangs/_/mthong dang bsgom pa dag gis kun du sbyor ba ni/_/bcu po 'di dag mtha' dag rnams kyis rnam par grol gyur cing /_/thal mo sbyar byas sangs rgyas nyid la lta bzhin du/_/bya ba kun byas rang bzhin gnas te gnas par gyur/_/skar ma gsum gyis gnas skabs 
[\[64b.3\]]gsum skyong rjes skyes lha dang ni/_/ldan pa'i lnga gsum tshes kyi dus su skar ma'i rgyal po bzhin/_/gsum ni chud zos gyur cing gsum ni lhag par thob pa yi/_/gsum rig slob ma gsum gyis bde bar gshegs pa rnam par mdzes/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_
[\[64b.4\]]nyan thos chen po rab tu gshegs par mdzad pa'i le'u ste bcu bdun pa'o//_//de nas 'ga' zhig tshe na nor ldan khyim bdag ni/_/skal ba med pa rnams la nor ni rab sbyin pa/_/bzang sbyin zhes ni rab tu grags pa'i ming ldan pa/_/byang phyogs yul ko sa la rnams nas der song 
[\[64b.5\]]gyur/_/de yis der ni thub pa bzhugs pa thos pa ste/_/thos nas blta bar 'dod pas mtshan mo rab tu song /_/rnam dag sems pa des ni rab sleb rab mkhyen nas/_/rab tu phyag byas pa la bde bar gshegs pas bstan/_/gang phyir chos la bsgoms pas nyid ni rnam spangs nas/_/blo ldan 
[\[64b.6\]]mtshan mor bdag la lta 'dod rab sleb ste/_/da lta nyid du 'ongs pa de yi de ma thag_/nges par legs pa'i mgron 'di rab tu long mdzod cig_/gang zhig khyod kyis lhag bsam gang zhig bstan pa dang /_/bdag gis grags pa nyid las gang zhig dang ba dang /_/gang zhig sngon gyi rgyu las blo gros rab 
[\[64b.7\]]zhugs te/_/des ni yon tan chen po rnams kyi snod yin no/_/de phyir 'jig rten dag na grags dang gzhan du ni/_/'bras bu sbyin pa mchog las byung bar rig nas ni/_/bkur sti byas nas dus su snyoms pa'i bdag nyid kyis/_/chos las thob pa'i nor ni sbyin par bya ba'o/_/tshul khrims yang dag 
[\[65a\]]
[\[65a.1\]]blangs nas spyad par bya ba ste/_/tshul khrims nyid ni bsrung ba dang ni rgyan yin zhing /_/'og tu song ba rnams kyi rnam par ldog byed do/_/nam mkha'i steng gi nges par rdzogs pa po nyid yin/_/'dod pa rnams su gcig min zhes pas rjes bcings pa'i/_/yongs su tshol ba la 
[\[65a.2\]]sogs skyon rnams thos nas ni/_/nges par 'byung ba'i cho gar yon tan rnam shes nas/_/rnam dben las skyes shi ba'i bden pa bsnyen par mdzod/_/'chi bas nye bar gzir zhing rga bas zil non pa'i/_/'jig rten rab tu bskor ba yang dag yongs shes nas/_/zhi ba skye ba med phyir 'bad pa mdzod cig dang /_/
[\[65a.3\]]skye ba bden pa med na rga med 'chi ba med/_/ji ltar mi rnams la ni mi rtag bdag nyid kyis/_/rab chags sdug bsngal mngon sum du ni rig mdzod la/_/de bzhin lha rnams la yang sdug bsngal rig mdzod dang /_/'jug pa rtag pa nyid ni su yang yod ma yin/_/gang du mi rtag pa nyid der ni sdug bsngal zhing /_/
[\[65a.4\]]gang du rang nyid med pa der yang sdug bsngal te/_/gang du bdag ces pa dang bdag gi ba zhes pa/_/de tshe mi rtag sdug bsngal bdag med gang la yin/_/de phyir sdug bsngal las ni sdug bsngal 'di dang ni/_/mtshan ma las ni 'di yi mtshan ma ltos mdzod la/_/rnam par nye bar zhi las sdug bsngal zhi ba 
[\[65a.5\]]dang /_/lam las khyod kyi lam ni dge ba shes par gyis/_/'gro ba g.yo ba sdug bsngal 'di ni shes nas su/_/me dang rab mtshungs dus kyi me yis 'jig rten 'di/_/mtha' dag rab tu tshig par yongs su rtogs bzhin du/_/srid dang srid min rnams su smon pa med par gyis/_/stong pa nyid dang bdag 
[\[65a.6\]]med bdag gi ba med pa/_/sgyu ma dang mtshangs 'jig rten 'di ni shes byas nas/_/'du byed tsam zhig rab tu yongs su rtogs bzhin du/_/lus 'di la ni khams kyi 'du shes nyid du gyis/_/sems ni srid pa g.yos las yang dag g.yos nas dang /_/'khor ba'i 'gro ba rnam pa sna tshogs mthong nas 
[\[65a.7\]]ni/_/rtog pa med cing zhi la bden zhing chags med par/_/sems ni snyoms shing mtshan ma med pa bsnyen par gyis/_/de nas drang srong chen po'i chos de thos pa las/_/chos kyi cho ga'i sngon gyi 'bras bu thob gyur zhing /_/gang zhig thob nas 'di yi sdug bsngal las lhag ma/_/rgya mtsho chen po las ni thigs pa 
[\[65b\]]
[\[65b.1\]]gcig bzhin gyur/_/chags bral rnams kyi dgon par yang dag gnas kyang ngam/_/lus med rnams kyis srid rtser yang dag gnas na yang /_/nags sam lha na de nyid mthong min ma yin la/_/khyim na gnas pa yin yang lta bas dge legs gyur/_/rnam pa sna tshogs lta ba'i dra ba rnams kyis 
[\[65b.2\]]dang /_/'khor ba'i sdug bsngal rnams kyis de rnams rnam mi 'grol/_/de phyir rab tu nyams shing de nyid ma mthong bar/_/chags pa rnam par bsal nas khyad par 'phags par gshegs/_/lta ba legs skyes des ni lta ba ngan de rnams/_/ston ka'i sprin gyis rdo yi char bzhin spangs pa ste/_/dbang phyug la 
[\[65b.3\]]sogs mi mthun rgyu las ma yin zhing /_/des ni 'jig rten rgyu med par min zhes rtogs so/_/de phyir mi mnyam rgyur ni 'thad pa ma yin zhing /_/rgyu med par ni 'gal ba chen po yod pa nyid/_/'dir yang rigs pa shes pas so sor mthong ba ste/_/the tshom med pas de nyid mthong ba kye ma ci/_/gal 
[\[65b.4\]]te dbang phyug gis ni 'gro ba skyed gyur na/_/'gro ba rim gyis rab tu 'jug pa ma yin la/_/skye dgu rnams kyi 'khor ba'i 'khor lo ma yin zhing /_/gang du gang zhig bskyed nas de dang de nyid 'gyur/_/lus can rnams kyis mi 'dod thob pa ma yin zhing /_/de yi bdag nyid rnams kyis 'dod pa'i spyod pa 
[\[65b.5\]]med/_/gang zhig lus can rnams kyi dge dang mi dger gyur/_/de ni dbang phyug nyid phyir dbang phyug la yin no/_/de yi dbang phyug nyid la the tshom 'gyur min zhing /_/der ni mi rnams dag gis byams pa pha bzhin 'gyur/_/de la nyam thag pas ni smod pa smra min zhing /_/lha rnams de dang de la 'jig rten mchod ma yin/_/
[\[65b.6\]]gal te 'di mi bskyed la dgongs par gyur na ni/_/bya ldan nyid phyir deng 'dir dbang phyug ma yin zhing /_/gal te 'di yis 'jug la 'dod par gyur na ni/_/de nyid dbang phyug yod pa nyid la byed par 'gyur/_/ci ste 'di yi skye la nus pa cung zhig med/_/byis pa bzhin du 'di yis spyod rnams rgyu med do/_/gal 
[\[65b.7\]]te dbang phyug gi ni bdag la mnga' nyid med/_/'jig rten bskyed la 'di yi stobs de ci zhig yod/_/ji ltar 'dod la gal te bde sdug la bsnyen na/_/'byung po rnams ni 'jig rten na des byas pa nyid/_/gang phyir 'dod la chags pa khong khro la 'jug ste/_/de phyir de yi dbang nyid yod pa ma yin no/_/
[\[66a\]]
[\[66a.1\]]de'i rten nyid la sems med rnams kyis gnas bya ste/_/skyes bu'i 'bad rtsol tshad mar gyur pa nyid yin no/_/las 'bras med par 'gyur zhing lung ni byas min la/_/las kyi sbyor ba rnams ni de yi rten yin na/_/gal te dbang phyug rang gi las kyis 'gyur na ni/_/thun mong gyur pa'i las 
[\[66a.2\]]la dbang phyug gyur ma yin/_/ci ste de yi khyab bdag rgyu med gyur na ni/_/'gro ba kun gyi dbang phyug nyid ni rab tu grub/_/ci ste de yi dbang phyug nyid gzhan las yin zhing /_/de nyid kyi yang byed po'i dbang phyug gzhan yin la/_/de yis gzhan zhes de ltar 'dir ni rnam gzhag med/_/de phyir 'gro ba skyed pa yod 
[\[66a.3\]]pa ma yin nyid/_/dbang phyug skyed por gyur na rigs pas tha dad du/_/rnam par 'gal ba rnam pa du ma mthong ba ste/_/de yi phyir yang rang bzhin la ni nyes pa de/_/slar yang rab tu chags par 'gyur ba nyid yin no/_/rgyu shes rnams kyi rten ni cung zad rnam log pa'o/_/rgyu ni bya ba'i cho gar smra ba ma yin zhing /_/bya 
[\[66a.4\]]ba'i cho gar sa bon la sogs de dang de/_/ltos pa yin te de phyir rang bzhin rgyu ma yin/_/'bras mi rdzogs par byed pa po ni gcig pa nyid/_/'ga' zhig gi yang rgyu ni cung zad yod ma yin/_/gcig gi bdag nyid can gyi rang bzhin rab bstan te/_/de phyir rab tu 'jug la rang bzhin rgyu ma yin/_/gang gi rang 
[\[66a.5\]]bzhin gyis ni khyab pa nyid 'dod pa/_/de yi bya ba med pa nyid kyang rab chags shing /_/bya ba ma gtogs 'bras bu'i cho ga mthong min te/_/de yi phyir yang bskyed la rang bzhin rgyu ma yin/_/de yi khyab pa po nyid la ni rgyu nyid phyir/_/thams cad las ni thams cad rgyun mi 'chad par 'gyur/_/'bras bu'i rab tu 
[\[66a.6\]]'jug la nges pa mthong ba ste/_/de phyir skyed pa la ni rang bzhin rgyu ma yin/_/ji ltar 'di yi yon tan med nyid grub pa ste/_/de ltar 'bras bu la ni yon tan med nyid 'gyur/_/rnam 'gyur yon tan can rnams 'jig rten na mthong ste/_/de phyir rab tu 'jug la rang bzhin rgyu ma yin/_/rtag pa'i rgyu las 
[\[66a.7\]]khyad par 'gyur ba ma yin phyir/_/rnam 'gyur la ni tha dad yon tan nyid ma yin/_/rnam 'gyur la ni tha dad yon tan zhugs pa ste/_/de phyir rang bzhin las ni skyed pa rigs ma yin/_/rang bzhin gyis ni bskyed pa'i bdag nyid can nyid la/_/'bras bu rnams su nyams pa'i rgyu ni grub ma yin/_/gang 
[\[66b\]]
[\[66b.1\]]phyir rnam 'gyur gyi ni nyams pa mthong ba ste/_/de phyir rtogs par bya ba rgyu ni gzhan nyid do/_/nus par gyur pa'i sbyor la shin tu mthar thug pa'i/_/thar pa 'dod pa'i sdom brtson rnams kyi ma yin zhing /_/skye dgu rnams kyi rang bzhin rab tu 'jug pa'i phyir/_/gang las thar pa'i ched dang pha rol gnon 
[\[66b.2\]]par gyur/_/skyed pa'i bdag nyid rang bzhin gyis ni nges nyid phyir/_/de yi bdag nyid rnam 'gyur la ni nges nyid yin/_/'jig rten dag na rnam 'gyur la ni brtan nyid med/_/de phyir rang bzhin gyis ni skyed par rigs ma yin/_/sems kyi spyod pa mi gsal rang bzhin zhes bya zhing /_/rnam 'gyur 
[\[66b.3\]]gsal ba 'di ni de nas grags pa ste/_/de phyir rab tu 'jug la rang bzhin rgyu min zhing /_/gsal ba'i rgyu la 'dir ni 'bras bu grub pa nyid/_/sems med rang bzhin las ni sems dang bcas pa yi/_/rta glang bong bu la sogs 'bras bu rigs pa ma yin zhing /_/sems dang rnam par bral ba'i rgyu 
[\[66b.4\]]rnams dag las ni/_/sems dang ldan pa cung zad zhig ni 'jug ma yin/_/gal te 'gro ba skyed phyir dus ni nges pa ste/_/de phyir yongs su rtog byed rnams kyis thar pa med/_/gang phyir skye dgu rnams kyi mtha' ni byas min te/_/de phyir 'jig rten rgyun chad med par skye bar 'gyur/_/gang zhig 
[\[66b.5\]]yon tan rnams kyi gcig dang du mas byas/_/rjes rnams su ni rang nyid nges pa mthong ba ste/_/gal te rgyu gcig nyid du gnas par gyur pa na/_/de yang tha dad khyad par gyur pa ma yin no/_/gang zhig smin pa las skyes rdzas kyi cho gar sar/_/yon tan shes pas yon tan tha dad nyid du mthong /_/gang 
[\[66b.6\]]phyir rgyu ni khyad par min pa 'dod pa ste/_/stong pa'i rdzas rnams su yang nges par gyur pa nyid/_/gang gi mi gsal ba las rdzas ni 'char ba ste/_/de yi rjes su dpag pa nges par grub ma yin/_/'bras bu gsal ba yi ni yang dag rab 'jug pa/_/mi gsal ba las gang gis mngon du mthong ma 
[\[66b.7\]]yin/_/gsal ba gnyis pa dag las rab tu 'jug pa bzhin/_/mi gsal ba las gang gi 'bras bu dang por gyur/_/de yi 'dod pa las 'dir mi 'dod chen po'o/_/rang bzhin nyid kyis nyes pa gnyis ni rab tu chags/_/ji ltar gser gyi phreng ba khyad par 'phags pa ste/_/de ltar rang bzhin 
[\[67a\]]
[\[67a.1\]]gyi ni rnam 'gyur khyad par 'phags/_/'bras bu khyad par yin zhing rgyu yi ma yin te/_/de phyir skyed la rang bzhin rigs pa ma yin no/_/gal te bya bar skyes bu de ni rgyu yin na/_/gang gis nges par gang 'dod des de 'thob par 'gyur/_/'dod kyang kha cig don ni 
[\[67a.2\]]'thob pa ma yin la/_/mi 'dod na yang dbang med 'dir ni 'thob par 'gyur/_/gang zhig ba glang rta bong rnga mo'i skye gnas 'gro/_/mnga' bdag nyid du gyur na de ni bsnyen min zhing /_/de ru 'gro ba'i sdig pa'i las ni byed pa dang /_/sdug bsngal mi 'dod su yis sdug bsngal byed par 'gyur/_/gal te 'jig rten 
[\[67a.3\]]dag na skyes bu byed par gyur/_/sdug bsngal byed pa ma yin bde ba kho na byed/_/'dod pa byed par 'dod kyang mi 'dod byed pa ste/_/dbang phyug chen por 'gyur na mi 'dod su yis spyod/_/gang tshe mi ni chos min pa las rnam 'jigs shing /_/'bad pas chos ni rab tu son par byed pa nyid/_/sna tshogs 
[\[67a.4\]]nyon mongs rnams kyis dbang med 'phrog pa ste/_/de phyir 'di na mi ni pha rol rgyud du gyur/_/grang dang dro dang char dang thog dang glog sogs kyi/_/bya ba rnams ni mi la bzlog nas mthong ba ste/_/gang phyir rang dbang ma yin mi ni gzhan ldan te/_/de phyir bya ba rnams su skyes bu dbang phyug min/_/gang tshe 
[\[67a.5\]]sa bon las dang sa chu'i rten las ni/_/dus sbyor rab tu 'jug la lo thog phun tshogs shing /_/mar me dag las rab gsal shing las me yin te/_/rgyu med las yin zhes pa rgyu med ma yin no/_/rgyu med pa las gal te 'gro ba'i rab 'jug na/_/mi yi byed pa yis ni med par 'gyur ba ste/_/
[\[67a.6\]]thams cad kyis ni thams cad nges par 'thob byed cing /_/rtag na 'di ni thams cad du ni song bar grub/_/bde dang sdug bsngal gal te rgyu med las yin na/_/gang char kun gyi bde dang sdug bsngal skal min zhing /_/rgyu med las kyang bde dang sdug bsngal rtogs min te/_/de phyir rgyu med las 
[\[67a.7\]]shes 'di ni rgyu ma yin/_/de skad ces pa la sogs mi mnyam rgyu rnams des/_/'jig rten rab tu 'jug la rgyu rnams shes ma yin/_/rgyu med par ni 'jig rten mthong ba ma yin zhing /_/nyes pa 'di rnams rgyu med las ni yang dag rtogs/_/brtan pa dang ni g.yo ba'i 'byung po rnams su yang /_/
[\[67b\]]
[\[67b.1\]]de dang de la brten nas de dang de 'byung ste/_/'jig rten dag na rgyu med ci yang yod min zhing /_/kun gyi rgyu ni 'jig rten pa yis shes ma yin/_/de nas sbyin pa bzang por byin pa'i bzang byin des/_/rgya che'i chos kyis dam pa'i chos ni rnam shes nas/_/g.yo ba med pa'i blo gros nges par rab 
[\[67b.2\]]dang bas/_/drang srong chen po la ni tshig 'di gsal ba'o/_/mnyan yod rta ljang rta rid kyis bsnyen grong khyer ni/_/bdag gi gnas kyi sa ni bsod nams grags pa can/_/der ni bdag gis khyed kyi gnas ni byed 'dod de/_/sdig med gnas mchog dam pa de ni bsnyen par mdzod/_/gang lta na yang 
[\[67b.3\]]thub pa khyed kyi gnas su snyoms pa ni/_/gzhal yas khang gi logs sam rnam dben nags su ste/_/bdag la rjes su brtse phyir gnas kyi ched du ni/_/dgra bcom rab tu gzhes par mdzad par 'os pa lags/_/de nas gtong bar lhag par mos pa 'di yis ni/_/rnam par grol ba'i sems ni 
[\[67b.4\]]rnam par mkhyen nas ni/_/chags pa med pa'i blo mnga' blo gros mkhyen pa yis/_/mchog tu zhi bas blo gros yang dag bka' stsal to/_/sems dpa' brtan kye gang zhig rdzas glog g.yo rnams su/_/khyod kyi sems dpa' gtong la lhag par mos pa ste/_/rang bzhin gyis ni sbyin la dga' zhing chos 
[\[67b.5\]]la dgyes/_/de nyid mthong ba khyed la de ni ngo mtshar min/_/gang zhig khyim ni tshig las phyi rol 'thon byed pa/_/de yi don de 'tshig par byed pa ma yin te/_/de bzhin dus kyi me yis 'gro ba tshig pa na/_/gang gis gang zhig sbyin pa de yis de nyer 'thob/_/de phyir rnam spangs sems dpa' 
[\[67b.6\]]rnams kyis sbyin par dang /_/yul rnams yang dag longs spyod par ni shes pa nyid/_/bkren pa 'jig rten dag na zad pa'i skyon mthong bas/_/nye bar longs spyod ma yin 'jigs las sbyin ma yin/_/dus su smod la don ni rnam par gtong ba dang /_/dpal dang nga rgyal las ni de bzhin g.yul 
[\[67b.7\]]'gyed par/_/sems dpa' lhag pa nyid kyis shes te gzhan gyis min/_/sems dpa' yis ni sbyin pa nyid dang g.yul 'gyed do/_/sbyin byed dga' ba nyid du 'gro la 'gro ba ste/_/snyan pa dang ni dam par grags pa rab tu 'thob/_/sbyin pa po yod gang zhes dam pa rnams sbyin nas/_/yang 
[\[68a\]]
[\[68a.1\]]dag bkur ba dang ni mngon du 'gro bar byed/_/de bzhin 'jig rten dag na blo gtad nyid 'gyur zhing /_/mi bzad pa rnams las kyang nyes pa 'thob ma yin/_/bsod nams rnams ni bdag gis byas zhes tshim pa ste/_/'chi ba'i dus su skrag par mngon par 'gro ma yin/_/
[\[68a.2\]]sbyin pa'i 'bras bu 'dir ni me tog tsam zhig ste/_/sbyin pa po yi 'bras bu gzhan nyid rab tu 'thob/_/'khor ba'i 'khor lo la ni 'khor ba'i skye bo yi/_/gtong ba dang mnyam gnyen ni yod pa ma yin no/_/mi yi 'jig rten na dang lha na skyes pa rnams/_/gtong ba las dang mnyam pa rnams las lhag 
[\[68a.3\]]par 'gyur/_/rta dang glang po'i skye gnas su yang gnas pa rnams/_/'bras bu de ni gtso bo thob par 'gyur ba'o/_/sbyin pas longs spyod rnams kyis yongs su 'dzin pa ste/_/tshul khrims kyis bsrungs mtho ris su ni 'gro bar 'gyur/_/ye shes sngon du 'gro ba can gyi zhi ba yis kyang ni/_/
[\[68a.4\]]rten dang bral ba gang na grangs su 'gro ba 'gro ma yin/_/'chi med bdud rtsi de yis 'thob pa'i ched du yang /_/sdom par gyur pa rab tu sbyin pa yin pa nyid/_/sbyin pa dran nas rab tu dga' bar gyur pa ste/_/yid ni rab tu dga' las nges par ting 'dzin yin/_/mnyam par gzhag na yang 
[\[68a.5\]]dag mtho ba 'di ru ni/_/skye dang 'god pa shes par rim gyis 'gyur ba ste/_/gzhan la sbyin pa sbyin pa'i sbyin pa po yis ni/_/snying la gnas pa'i nyon mongs rnams ni bsrabs par 'gyur/_/gang zhig sbyin pa byin byed de yi don rnams su/_/chags par grags pa de ni dang por chod pa 
[\[68a.6\]]nyid/_/gang la rab tu byams pa'i sems kyis sbyin pa ste/_/de phyir zhe sdang dang ni nga rgyal spangs par gyur/_/rab tu len pa po ni bde ldan rnams mthong nas/_/gang zhig 'ga' ste des na ser sna yod ma yin/_/sbyin pa pos ni sbyin pa'i 'bras bu mngon rtogs nas/_/med pa nyid dang 
[\[68a.7\]]mi shes mun pa 'joms pa po/_/de phyir zhi ba 'phags min gyis bsnyen ser sna dang /_/sred pa gang las sbyin pa byed pas bcom pa ste/_/de nas thar pa'i yan lag gyur pa sbyin pa ni/_/yin zhing nyes pa rab tu thob phyir thar pa'o/_/ji ltar gzhan rnams grib ma'i don du shing 'dod 
[\[68b\]]
[\[68b.1\]]cing /_/kha cig 'bras bu'i don du gzhan rnams me tog phyir/_/de ltar sbyin pa yongs su sbyin par byed pa ste/_/kha cig zhi ba'i don du gzhan rnams nor gyi phyir/_/de phyir khyad par du ni khyim na gnas rnams kyis/_/sbyin pa ji ltar nus pa mchog ni gter yin zhing /_/sbyin pa snying po med pa'i nor 
[\[68b.2\]]gyi snying po ste/_/dam pa rnams kyi rjes su song ba lam 'di yin/_/bza' ba rab tu sbyin par stobs ni rab ster zhing /_/bgo ba sbyin byed la yang lus ni ster ba'o/_/gang gis dam pa rnams la gnas ni sbyin byed pa/_/des kyang 'jig rten dag na thams cad byin pa yin/_/theg pa sbyin byed 
[\[68b.3\]]la yang bde ba byin pa ste/_/mar me sbyin byed la ni spyan mig sbyin pa nyid/_/de nas dbrog bya ma yin 'chi med rab sbyin zhing /_/gang gis mthar thug gyur pa'i chos kyis ston pa nyid/_/kha cig 'dod pa'i ched du sbyin pa gtong ba ste/_/gzhan ni don gyi don du gcig ni grags pa'i phyir/_/
[\[68b.4\]]kha cig mtho ris don du gzhan ni brtse ba'i phyir/_/khyod kyi sbyin pa 'di ni chags pa med pa yin/_/de phyir legs so khyod ni sred pa de ltar gyur/_/gang zhig yang dag bsgrubs mdzod 'dod pa tshim par shog_/rdul bcas mun pa dang ldan gang khyed 'ongs nas ni/_/ye shes rnam dag blo ni rab 
[\[68b.5\]]tu 'gro bar 'gyur/_/de nyid de bzhin gshegs pas lam gyi yang dag par/_/dga' ba byas pa dang yang gzhug pa byas pa ni/_/gtsug lag khang de snying la rnam par byas nas ni/_/nye rgyal dang ni lhan cig dus su song bar gyur/_/song nas de na kau shAm rgyal po'i khab dang ni/_/gtsug 
[\[68b.6\]]lag khang gi sa la so sor yang dag rgyus/_/dpal ldan mnyam zhing mdzes la yid 'ong shing ldan pa/_/der ni de yis rgyal byed kyi ni tshal mthong ngo /_/de nas de yi don du rgyal byed la zhus te/_/de yis chags pa las ni de la ma byin no/_/khyod kyis gal te don gyis kun nas bkram na yang /_/
[\[68b.7\]]sa ni bdag gis sbyin pa ma yin zhes smras so/_/de nas der ni bzang sbyin gyis de la smras pa/_/tshal de la ni rin byas shes ni mngon 'dod do/_/de nas des ni nor gyis sa ni yongs g.yogs nas/_/chos kyi tha snyad mthong zhing nyos par gyur pa'o/_/de yi gtong ba de ni mthong nas rgyal byed kyis/_/
[\[69a\]]
[\[69a.1\]]sangs rgyas la ni mchog tu rab tu dang ba byas/_/nags tshal de yi lhag ma dag ni kun nas ni/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la ni phul bar gyur pa'o/_/de nas mgon med zas sbyin gyis ni drang srong chen po yis/_/nye rgyal gyis ni nges par gzigs par bya ba'i don 
[\[69a.2\]]ldan par/_/myur ba'i blo gros dang ldan ma chags grol ba'i sems kyis ni/_/mdzes shing dge ba mchog gis gtsug lag khang ni byed du bcug_/nor gyi dbang phyug nyid kyi gnas ni 'phos pa lta bu dang /_/byang phyogs ko sha la yi khang pa'i bde ba lta bu dang /_/de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[69a.3\]]nyid kyi gzhi ni mtshungs pa lta bu dang /_/rang gi nor dang stobs kyi dang ni lta ba yi yang ngo /_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_mgon med zas sbyin la rjes su ston pa'i le'u ste bco brgyad pa'o//_//de nas thub pa sna tshogs tha dad blo can 
[\[69a.4\]]gyi/_/ston pas thams cad ye shes kyis ni zil mnan nas/_/bcu phyed phrag ri nas dang grong khyer nas rim gyis/_/rgyal po bum pas bsnyen pa'i grong khyer du gshegs so/_/de nas slob ma stong phrag tsam ni dul ba na/_/gshegs so des ni yab kyi yul ni rab tu thob/_/de 
[\[69a.5\]]nas de la rjes su 'dzin pa mdzad bzhed pa/_/rang gi grong gi gnas la mtha' ru bsnyen pa'o/_/de nas mdun na 'don dang blon po blo gros can/_/dga' ba'i gtam la dam pa rgyu ba de rnams kyis/_/yongs su rdzogs pa'i rab brnyes 'phags pa ste/_/sa skyong rgyal po de la zhus shing 
[\[69a.6\]]gsol ba'o/_/de nas de ni 'ongs pa thos nas sa skyong ni/_/dga' ba yongs ldan sems kyis blta bar 'dod pa yis/_/grong khyer ba ni kun gyis rjes su 'gro byas pa/_/myur bas brtan pa spangs shing bsu ba la gshegs so/_/drang srong dbus su tshangs pa bzhugs pa bzhin du 
[\[69a.7\]]ni/_/slob mas bskor ba de ni rgyang ring nas gzigs nas/_/drang srong chen po'i chos la rjes su 'gro bas ni/_/shing rta spangs nas rkang pa rnams kyis nye bar gshegs/_/myur bar de yi nye logs su ni yongs phyin nas/_/thub pa mthong ba nyid de des ni ma smras so/_/de la bu zhes smra bar 
[\[69b\]]
[\[69b.1\]]nus pa ma yin zhing /_/de bzhin dge slong zhes ni nus pa ma yin no/_/de nas de yi dge slong cha lugs de mthong zhing /_/bdag tu song ba'i sna tshogs dpal la brtags nas ni/_/dbugs ring brgyus nas mchi ma rnam par bton pa de/_/drag tu mun par gyur cing rnam par zlos pa nyid/_/
[\[69b.2\]]lam 'gro skom par gyur pa mngon par song ba yis/_/rgyang ring dag nas rdzing bu skam po mthong nas ltar/_/rang gnas rnam pa dang bral nye bar nyer bzhugs pa/_/mthong nas bdag gi nyam thag stobs ldan mchog tu gyur/_/'jig rten mtha' ru gnas pa'i sems gyur thim pa na/_/sdug pa'i 
[\[69b.3\]]rnam 'gyur ri mor song ba ji lta bar/_/gzugs de nyid ni mthong ba na ni bdag la ni/_/dga' ba yod pa ma yin 'di la dga' ba thal/_/gang gis mtha' dag ri yis zlums pas sa gzhi ni/_/dang po'i dus su nga las nu ni gnas pa bzhin/_/gang zhig dbang po la yang slong bar 'os min pa/_/
[\[69b.4\]]bdag gi de ni pha rol rnams la slong mos 'tsho/_/gangs 'di snying po lhun po las ni khyad 'phags shing /_/gsal bas nyi ma la dang mdzes pas zla ba la/_/'gros kyis glang po che la dbyangs kyis khyu mchog la/_/des ni slong mo za zhing sa ni rdzogs la min/_/de nas bu yi 'du shes kyis ni 'phyo 
[\[69b.5\]]ba nyid/_/rgyal po'i bsam pa 'di ni rnam par mkhyen nas dang /_/'jig rten lhag ma la yang [(]rjas [,]rjes [)]su brtse ba ste/_/de yi don du sangs rgyas mkha' la 'phur bar gyur/_/nyi ma'i shing rta phyag gis reg par mdzad nas ni/_/rlung gis bsnyen pa'i lam la zhabs dag gis gshegs shing /_/sku la 
[\[69b.6\]]gcig tu gyur zhing du mar gyur pa dang /_/mang po nyid du gyur nas gcig tu gyur pa'o/_/sa la chu bzhin du yang zhugs shing thogs med la/_/chu yi logs la sa la bzhin du spyod pa dang /_/ra ba dang ni ri la thad kar mdzad pa dang /_/thogs pa med cing mkha' la bzhin du rab tu zhugs/_/lus kyi 
[\[69b.7\]]phyed kyis chu yi char pa 'bebs pa dang /_/phyed kyis me bzhin du ni 'bar bar gyur pa ste/_/ri bos 'bar ba'i sman dang rab tu 'bar ba bzhin/_/mkha' la gang zhig 'bar dang 'bar bas mdzes par gyur/_/dbang phyug mngon par 'dod pa'i skye bo de yi ni/_/sems kyi rab tu dga' ba de ltar skyed 
[\[70a\]]
[\[70a.1\]]nas ni/_/nyi ma gnyis pa bzhin du mkha' la bzhugs nas su/_/mi skyong la ni chos dag bgro bar mdzad pa'o/_/mi skyong khyed kyis bdag la gang zhig ltos pa dang /_/mya ngan rjes su zhugs pa snying rje'i bdag nyid shes/_/bu la dga' ba de ni chod mdzod zhi bar gshegs/_/
[\[70a.2\]]bdag las bu yi 'bras bu gsar pa snoms mdzod cig_/gang zhig sngon chad bu yis pha la ma byin zhing /_/sngon ni pha yis bu las rab tu ma thob pa/_/rgyal srid las khyad 'phags shing mtho ris las khyad 'phags/_/rgyal po mchog gi bdud rtsi de ni bzhes mdzod cig_/sa skyong las kyis bdag nyid las 
[\[70a.3\]]kyi skye gnas dang /_/las kyi rten dang las kyi rnam smin skal ba dang /_/las kyi dbang gis gnas par 'gro ba shes nas ni/_/phan pa'i ched du las gang gyur pa de bsnyen mdzod/_/'jig rten de nyid rtogs nas dang ni btsal nas su/_/dam pa'i las ni mi yi bshes min gzhan ma yin/_/
[\[70a.4\]]thams cad bor nas las dang lhan cig khyod kyi ni/_/rten dang bral zhing gcig pur 'gro bar bya ba'o/_/lha 'am dmyal ba dud 'gro mi yi 'jig rten du/_/las kyi rten las son pa'i 'jig rten 'gro ba ste/_/srid pa'i rgyu ni rnam pa gsum dang skye gnas gsum/_/sna tshogs las ni 'jig rten pa 
[\[70a.5\]]yis rab tu byed/_/de phyir sde tshan gnyis ni yang dag 'jug par yang /_/lus dang ngag gi las 'di rnam par dag pa dang /_/yid kyis rab tu zhi la 'bad par bya ba ste/_/de ni bdag gi don yin gzhan don ma yin no/_/'gro ba chu yi dba' rlabs g.yo bar shes nas ni/_/brtags nas khyod kyis 
[\[70a.6\]]srid pa rnams su dga' mi bya/_/las ni yongs su zad phyir dam pa'i las gang de/_/mchog tu bde ba'i don du gang gyur bsnyen par bya/_/rgyu skar 'khor lo mkha' la bzhin du rtag par ni/_/'jig rten yongs su 'khor bar shes par byas nas ni/_/rtse mo 'das nas lha nas lha yang lhung ba 
[\[70a.7\]]ste/_/kye ma mir gyur smos ci blo gtad byed pa su/_/bde ba rnams las mchog tu thar pa'i bde ba dang /_/dga' ba rnams las bdag tu dga' ba gtsor shes nas/_/lag 'gro rnams kyis brang la bzhin du bdag ldan ni/_/su zhig 'jigs pa du ma'i dpal gyis bde la dga'/_/de phyir 
[\[70b\]]
[\[70b.1\]]brten pa khyim la mes bzhin 'jig rten ni/_/'jigs pa chen po dang ni yongs ldan mthong nas ni/_/gang zhig skye 'chi ngal ba mya ngan dang bral ba/_/zhi zhing brtan pa'i go 'phang de ni slongs mdzod cig_/don gyis ma yin g.yul gyis ma yin mtshon gyis min/_/gang la rta dang glang pos don ni yod 
[\[70b.2\]]ma yin/_/skyon ni dgra bo rnams kyi sde de choms mdzod dang /_/de las rgyal na rnam par rgyal ba gzhal du med/_/sdug bsngal dang ni sdug bsngal gyi rgyu zhi ba dang /_/zhi ba yi yang mngon pa'i thabs ni shes par mdzod/_/bzhi po 'di yi kun nas khong du chud pa las/_/'jigs pa chen po dang 
[\[70b.3\]]ni ngan 'gro rnams 'gog go/_/de nas bde bar gshegs pas rdzu 'phrul gyi sngon du/_/zhing du byas pa'i sems la dga' ba skyes pa ste/_/thos nas mi skyong de yis chos ni rab thob cing /_/dga' bzhin thal mo sbyar nas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/blo ldan dang bcas 'bras bu dang bcas khyed 
[\[70b.4\]]dgyes shing /_/sdug bsngal chen po las ni rnam grol khyed dgyes so/_/mya ngan ched du don med sa sbyin bdag dga' la/_/bu yi 'bras bu dang ldan bdag ni dga' bar gyur/_/phun tshogs spangs nas gshegs pa khyed ni gnas su gyur/_/ngal ba chen pos dub las khyed ni gnas su gyur/_/
[\[70b.5\]]dga' ba'i gnyen 'dun dga' ba khyod kyis bor ba rnams/_/bdag cag rnams ni snying rje bar du gnas su gyur/_/lha yi drang srong rnams sam rgyal po'i drang srong rnams/_/de rnams sngon dus na yang gang la ma gshegs pa/_/nyam thag 'jig rten dag gi dge ba'i ched du lam/_/nges par 
[\[70b.6\]]legs pa 'di dang khyed kyis lhag par thob/_/khyod ni 'khor los sgyur bar gal te gyur na ni/_/bdag gi dga' ba de ltar 'gyur ba ma yin zhing /_/rnam pa de ltar rdzu 'phrul 'di dang khyod kyi chos/_/mthong nas ji ltar bdag gi bstan pa dga' bar 'gyur/_/'dir ni yang srid nyid du sbyor 
[\[70b.7\]]ba'i chos la ni/_/'khor los sgyur bas skye dgu rnams ni skyong ba ste/_/'khor ba'i sdug bsngal chen po rnams ni 'joms pa la/_/'jig rten ched du chos kyang thub pa khyod kyis gsungs/_/gang gi rdzu 'phrul 'di dang rnam rgyas blo gros dang /_/'khor ba'i 'jigs pa mtha' dag nges par 
[\[71a\]]
[\[71a.1\]]log pa ni/_/dpal dang bral yang 'jig rten na khyod dbang phyug ste/_/dpal ni 'phel yang dbang med sred bcas ma yin no/_/brtse ba'i chos kyis bstan pa'i 'os gyur pa/_/shAkya'i rgyal pos mang po de dag smras nas ni/_/rgyal po nyid dang yab nyid du ni gnas na yang /_/
[\[71a.2\]]sras la de nyid khong du chud phyir phyag byas so/_/thub pa'i mthu ni lhag par thob pa mthong nas dang /_/de nyid rab tu 'bigs pa'i bstan bcos mthong nas dang /_/bla ma rgyal pos sbyar ba'i mchod pa mthong nas ni/_/du ma rnams kyis rab tu 'byung phyir re 'dod byas/_/de nas gzhon nu mang po 'bras bu la gnas rnams/_/chos kyi 
[\[71a.3\]]cho ga de nyid rab tu thob gyur te/_/sngags dang 'dod pa chen po rnams la ltos med nas/_/byams pa'i gnyen 'dun ngu ba rnams ni spangs nas so/_/kun dga' mdzes dga' kra mi la dang ma 'gags pa/_/dga' bo nye dga' dag dang de nas 'khyil pa 'dzin/_/slob ma'i lkog tu ston pa yis ni lha sbyin te/_/thub pas rjes su 
[\[71a.4\]]bslabs pa de rnams byung bar gyur/_/de nas mdun na 'don gyi bu ni bdag nyid che/_/'char kas lam de nyid la rab tu song ba ste/_/de yis nges pa rab tu mthong nas de bzhin du/_/nye ba 'khor dang rgyun shes bu yi blo byas so/_/rgyal po yis kyang sras kyi mthu ni mthong nas dang /_/mchog gi bdud rtsi rgyun ni 
[\[71a.5\]]rab tu thob byas nas/_/chags pa med par rgyal srid spun zla la byin zhing /_/rgyal po'i drang srong spyod pas khyim rnams la bsnyen to/_/de skad ces pa la sogs legs pa'i gnyen rnams dang /_/'khor dang gnyen rnams rjes su brtul nas sangs rgyas ni/_/mchi ma dang bcas grong khyer pa yis mngon 
[\[71a.6\]]dga' ba/_/rang gnas grong khyer la ni dus su rab zhugs pa/_/don kun grub pa zhes pa rgyal po'i sras po ni/_/don mdzad grub pa grong khyer du ni rab zhugs pa/_/zhes ni don thos khang pa rnams su mi mo rnams/_/sgo rnams dang ni skar khung rnams la mnyam par brten/_/mtshams kyi sprin gyis phyed 
[\[71a.7\]]bkag 'od zer ldan pa bzhin/_/ngur smrig bsnams shing mdzes pa de ni rab mthong nas/_/bud med de rnams mig nas mchi ma phyung ba ste/_/pad+ma dang mtshungs thal mo rnams kyis phyag 'tshal lo/_/chos kyis dang ni gzugs kyis gsal bar gyur pa ni/_/'og tu zhal gzigs gshegs pa 
[\[71b\]]
[\[71b.1\]]de ni mthong gyur nas/_/brtse ba dang ni gus pa rnam par ston pa ste/_/mchi mas rnyogs pa'i mig can rab myos ma rnams zlos/_/legs skyes dbu ni bzhar bar rnam par mdzad nas dang /_/na bza' rnying pa yongs su gsol nas dang /_/sku la gser gyi kha dog rab tu g.yogs nas 
[\[71b.2\]]dang /_/des ni spyan mig rab tu bkod nas gshegs/_/gang zhig gdung ba skyob pa'i gdugs dkar bsnyen phyir dang /_/sras po rnam par dul ba blta ba'i phyir 'ongs shing /_/kun nas gnas phyir 'os shing rgyal ba'i phyir 'os pa/_/des ni lhung bzed yang dag yongs su bzung nas gshegs/_/
[\[71b.3\]]'gram par ta ma la yi lo ma rnam bkod pa'i/_/mdzes ma'i gdong dang gdung ba skyob pa'i gdugs dkar ni/_/gang gis bsnyen par 'os shing mgyogs 'gro 'os pa ste/_/des ni lhung bzed yongs su bzung nas rkang pas gshegs/_/sa skyong bdag skyes rnams kyi dgra rnams zad par dang /_/dga' 
[\[71b.4\]]ba rnams kyi chags dang 'gro ba'i rjes chags par/_/blta bar 'os shing sna tshogs dbu rgyan bsnams pa gang /_/des ni sa yi gnya' shing gang tsam gzigs shing gshegs/_/'di yi blta ba ci zhig mtshan nyid de ni ci/_/don gnyer ci zhig rol pas dgra bor gyur pa ci/_/gang 'di brtul zhugs

[\[71b.6\]]yi gzugs ni nges par rjes su mthun pa dang /_/kha dog ngan pa'i gzugs 'di snang ba mthong nas ni/_/sras 'dod mi dbang rgyal po ji lta bur gyur zhing /_/'tshe ba'i dgra bo la yang ji lta bur byed dam/_/mchi mas gang ba'i sras po sgra gcan 'dzin mthong nas/_/gal te de lta nyid du 'di ni 
[\[71b.7\]]chags ma yin/_/byams pa'i gnyen rnams las kyang gzhan du kha bltas pa/_/'di 'dra nges pa'i brtul zhugs la ni dge legs shog_/gzi mdangs dang ni lus kyi dbyibs dang 'gros sam ste/_/bde ba'i rab tu 'jug la nges par tshad ma yin/_/yon tan de rnams dag gis 
[\[72a\]]
[\[72a.1\]]'di ni mdzes pa ste/_/zhi bas ldan zhing yul rnams kyis ni rnam par bral/_/sna tshogs ston ni rang mtshungs bsam pa yang dag yongs bzung nas/_/de skad ces sogs rab myos ma rnams mang po rnam par labs/_/sangs rgyas ma chags thugs kyang rang gi grong khyer 
[\[72a.2\]]la gshegs nas/_/bsod snyoms brnyes shing n+ya gro d+ha yi mtshan ma'i nags su gshegs/_/nyung ngu nyung ngu'i bsod snyoms sbyin pa rnams kyis nor rdzas phra zhing dge ba ma byas skye bo sgrol bar mdzad bzhed pa/_/spyod pa 'di yis yang dag tshim pa ma yin dge sbyong skye bo bsam pa ma thul ba la 
[\[72a.3\]]brten par mdzad bzhed la/_/'jig rten dag na skyid de ba zhes rgol ba nub par mdzad bzhed de bzhin yongs su bris nyid ston par mdzad bzhed pa/_/brtag par snyam la bsod snyoms phyir ni yab kyi grong khyer la ni sred med thugs mnga' de bzhin gshegs pa zhugs par gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi 
[\[72a.4\]]spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_yab dang sras mjal ba'i le'u ste bcu dgu pa'o//_//nga rgyal nyid kyis skye bo dam pa ma smod cig_/bshes gnyen 'du shes dka' thub rnams ni ma 'joms shig_/nyon mongs dbang gis dam pa'i brtul zhugs ma byed cig_/
[\[72a.5\]]dam pa min pa'i lta ba cung zad ma bsnyen cig_/mtshar la ma 'gro mi dge'i lam la ma gnas shig_/rgyags par ma 'gro dga' min mi bzod ma nyan cig_/snyan grags mi bzad [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]la blo gros ma 'dzin cig_/bstan bcos mthong las lhag par gtor ma ma 'phrog cig_/blo ni mnyam 
[\[72a.6\]]par zhog cing chos ni gyis mdzod cig_/dam pa rnams dang sbrel mdzod ri mo khod mdzod cig_/ji ltar phul byung 'di ni thob nas slar yang ni/_/rgya cher mngon par 'gro ba de ltar gyis mdzod cig_/brtson 'grus brtsams nas dang ni bstan pa yongs bzung nas/_/rigs pa rnams ni bskyed 
[\[72a.7\]]nas skyon rnams zil mnan nas/_/'chi ba yang dag dran nas 'phags pa'i bya ba gyis/_/che ba'i bdag nyid thob nas zhi lam thob par gyis/_/gang gi 'bras bu 'di ni zhi ba skyong ba ni/_/las de nyid ni slar yang byed par 'os pa ste/_/yid gzhungs ldan pa 'di dang byed pa byas pa nyid/_/'bras bu 
[\[72b\]]
[\[72b.1\]]gang zhig mthong bas sa bon bskrun par byed/_/'di na mchog gyur gang zhig sdig pa byed pa ste/_/snang ba de ni mun par gnas pa bdag gi lugs/_/chos ni gtso bo skyes bu gtso bo ma yin pa/_/mun pa snang ba la ni gnas pa bdag gi lugs/_/khyad 'phags gang zhig chos kyi cho gar 'jug pa ste/_/
[\[72b.2\]]dkar po dkar por mchog tu 'gro ba bdag gi lugs/_/dman pa gang zhig sdig pa'i las ni byed pa ste/_/de yang mun pa mun pa'i mtha' ru 'gro ba lugs/_/de phyir phung po bzhi po de rnams shes byas nas/_/rgyal po 'bad pa gyis shig de ltar ji ltar 'dir/_/bde nas bde bar gal te 'gro bar 
[\[72b.3\]]'dod na ni/_/phyogs ni gsum pa bsnyen zhing snga ma ma yin no/_/gzhan gyis gzhan la dge ba byed pa ma yin zhing /_/ci ste byed na de la rjes su 'gro ma yin/_/byis pa'i nyams pa yod min rang la 'thob pa ste/_/ma byas pa yi 'ong ba 'grub pa yod ma yin/_/ma byas pa yang brgya 
[\[72b.4\]]la byed pa ma yin zhing /_/ma byas pa ni gzhan du dge legs 'gyur ma yin/_/der ni 'gro ba la yang nor ni yod min te/_/de phyir dge ba'i las kyi cho gar 'bad pa bgyis/_/gang zhig sdig pa lhag par spyod pa legs min zhing /_/gson pa'i 'jig rten na de'i bdag la dga' ma yin/_/bdag 
[\[72b.5\]]gi ched du bdag gis sdig pa byas nas ni/_/gzhan du 'bras bu bdag nyid kho nas longs spyod do/_/rgyal po chen po ri bo chen po bzhi rnams ni/_/'ongs pa rnams su 'gro ba kun nas srub pa ste/_/chos kyi bya ba rnam pa sna tshogs bsten las ni/_/ji ltar 'thad pas gzhan na bya ba ci zhig 
[\[72b.6\]]yod/_/rga dang rgud pa nad dang de nas 'chi ba ste/_/bzhi po 'di rnams de bzhin du ni 'ongs pa ste/_/ri bo rnams kyis kun nas bskor ba bzhin du ni/_/'jig rten mtha' dag dbang med 'khol bar byed pa'o/_/de nas 'ongs pa rnams su dbang med sdug bsngal te/_/rten ni yod min mngon du 
[\[72b.7\]]'thab min srung ba med/_/der ni byed pa'i ched du nor med chad pa med/_/chos las ma gtogs sman gzhan yod min na/_/de ltar mi rtag gyur na gson pa'i 'jig rten na/_/glog ni g.yo ba'i yul la rab tu zhugs pa na/_/'chi ba'i lag pa'i rtse la yongs su gnas pa ste/_/chos ma yin pas 
[\[73a\]]
[\[73a.1\]]'bras bu longs spyod par 'os min/_/dbang chen dang mtshungs mi dbang de dang de rnams ni/_/g.yul du lha yi mngon par 'thab la phyin nas kyang /_/rab tu rgyas pa rnams dang nges par zum pa rnams/_/dus kyi dus su bde dang sdug bsngal skal ba'o/_/'byung po 'dzin 
[\[73a.2\]]pa'i sa yang zad par 'gro ba ste/_/'jig pa'i mes ni lhun po de bzhin sreg par byed/_/rgya mtsho chen po yongs su bskams par 'gro ba ste/_/dbu ba dang mtshungs mi yi 'jig rten kye ma ci/_/shugs byas nas kyang rlung ni zhi bar 'gro ba ste/_/'jig rten gdungs nas kyang ni nyi ma 
[\[73a.3\]]nub par 'gro/_/bsregs nas kyang ni me nyid 'da' bar nyer 'gro ste/_/gang de de ltar gnas par 'gyur ba yin snyam mam/_/lus po 'di ni yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]dus su bsrungs kyang ngam/_/sna tshogs longs spyod rnams kyis nye bar bskyangs kyang ni/_/de yis 'dir ni nyin zhag 'ga' zhig bzhugs 
[\[73a.4\]]nas su/_/skye bo chen po'i rjes su brtse ba mdzad nas ni/_/bde bar 'pham byed kyis ni phyag gis mngon bsrungs pa'i/_/grong khyer la ni chen po'i tshogs kyis rab tu dpags/_/me tog rab rgyas mya ngan med pas gsal byas shing /_/yan lag dmar por gyur cing khu byug skad sgrogs 
[\[73a.5\]]pa/_/ti se'i gangs ltar mtho ba'i lding khang phreng ba can/_/dpal ldan rgyal byed tshal ni de nas rab tu thob/_/des ni gtsang ma'i chus gang phreng ba dkar po can/_/gser gyi bum pa dang bcas bum pa blangs nas ni/_/de nas dus su rgyal byed tshal ni bzang sbyin gyis/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la 
[\[73a.6\]]ni rab tu phul bar gyur/_/de nas shAkya thub pa lta 'dod rgyal po ni/_/sde rab 'pham byed rgyal byed tshal du song ba ste/_/song nas de nas bkur sti byas te thub pa la/_/rab tu btud cing gdan byas tshig 'di gsol ba'o/_/thub pa grong khyer 'dir ni khyed kyi gnas bzhed pa/_/nges par rang ni ko 
[\[73a.7\]]sa la rnams kyi dge ba'o/_/de nyid gzigs pa 'di 'dra brten pa med pa ni/_/nye bar 'tshe byas yul ni skal ba med pa'am/_/khyed mthong ba 'am yang na mngon par phyag 'tshal ba/_/yang dag thob po bdag cag rnams kyi rjes bzung phyir/_/dam pa rnams kyi 'dus pa thob nas 'jig rten na/_/tshim 
[\[73b\]]
[\[73b.1\]]pa med kyang ji ltar tshim pa nyid yin no/_/gang gis rten can rlung ni rab tu g.yos pa ste/_/de yi bdag nyid las ni nye ba las 'gro ste/_/mkha' 'gro'i bya rnams lhun po'i 'dus pa nyer thob nas/_/rang bzhin rnam par spangs nas gser du 'gyur ba'o/_/de phyir 'di dang che ge mor ni mgon gyur 
[\[73b.2\]]zhing /_/skye bo legs pa'i rten las gzhan min bdag gi nags/_/ga d+ha'i sras po thub pa chen po rab thob nas/_/phur bu gsum pa'i khyim ltar lta na mdzes par gyur/_/'jig rten dag na rnyed pa rab rnyed sna tshogs pa/_/tshad dang ldan pa dag ni mdzad pa can yin la/_/khyod kyi nye ba las ni don gang 
[\[73b.3\]]yin pa rnams/_/de dag rnams kyi grangs med nyams pa yod ma yin/_/legs pa khyod kyi nges pa'i lta ba'i lta ba las/_/'di las gzhan du rnyed pa shes pa ma yin zhing /_/khyab bdag bdag ni gnod pa rnyed bla sdug bsngal ba/_/chags pa dang ldan rgyal po'i chos la spyod par 'gyur/_/de ltar dbang 
[\[73b.4\]]po dang mtshungs rgyal po de yis ni/_/rnam pa de ltar mngon du gyur te gsan nas ni/_/don dang 'dod pa dag gi chags pa mkhyen nas su/_/de yi bsam pa yang dag bskul ba thub pas gsungs/_/mi dbang khyod kyi tshig 'di ngo mtshar che ma yin/_/khyod kyi spyod pa 'di ni legs pa rnams la min/_/'og 
[\[73b.5\]]nas 'phos pa 'gro bar 'dod pa rnams kyis ni/_/phan pa 'dod pa'i dam pa'i skyes bu rnams la sdang /_/gang phyir sa skyong khyed kyi dgongs pa de nyid ni/_/des na khyod la 'dir ni cung zad smra 'dod de/_/de ni shes par gyis shig bdag gi lta ba ni/_/'bras bcas ji ltar gyur pa khyed kyi de ltar gyis/_/mi skyong 
[\[73b.6\]]rgyal bo'i dus kyis bcings nas bkug pa na/_/mdza' bshes ma yin gnyen min phun tshogs rjes 'gro min/_/thams cad kyis ni dman zhing dbang med 'gro ba ni/_/grib ma bzhin du las gcig rjes su 'gro ba nyid/_/de phyir mtho ris dang ni grags pa dam pa ni/_/mngon par 'dod bzhin chos kyi rgyal 
[\[73b.7\]]srid rjes su skyongs/_/rmongs pa las ni chos la 'khrul pa'i rgyal po yi/_/rgyal srid de nyid mtho ris na yang ma yin no/_/'dir ni rgyal srid yang dag yongs su bskyangs nas ni/_/chos las ring min rta rid mtho ris rab 'thob nas/_/'dir ni rmongs las mi yi bdag po ni kum+b+ha/_/chos las 
[\[74a\]]
[\[74a.1\]]'phos shing ka shi rnams su sa la zhugs/_/'phags pa yi dang sdig pa yi ni las kyi ni/_/khyod la dper bstan zhi ba bdag gis de smras te/_/rang gi skye dgu rnams ni rtag tu legs bsrungs shing /_/dpyad nas de phyir brtan pa dge la 'bad pa gyis/_/ji ltar srog chags 
[\[74a.2\]]rnams ni gtse bar ma byed cig_/rtag par dbang po rnams ni rol par ma byed cig_/'phags min gyis bsnyen khro ba 'dzin par ma byed cig_/phyogs min bya bar sems ni g.yo bar ma byed cig_/nga rgyal dang ni rgyags pa khur nas dus su ni/_/rab tu 'thor zhing shin tu gyur pa gnyid log gyur/_/
[\[74a.3\]]rnam pa de ltar 'jig rten 'gro ba shes nas su/_/de la ma nyal dge ba la ni gnyid ma log_/'khor ba'i phyogs ni g.yo bar 'dzegs nas su/_/nges par ltung na sa gzhi bag med 'gyur ba'o/_/gang zhig bde ba 'brel min de ni bsnyen bya min/_/gang gis 'bras bu legs min gang de bya 
[\[74a.4\]]ba min/_/gang zhig dge la sbyor min de ni bshes ma yin/_/sdug bsngal rnam par 'joms min gang de ye shes min/_/gal te ye shes min na yang srid ma yin zhing /_/srid par gyur na lus med nyid la yin pa ste/_/lus su gyur na yul las thar pa ma yin zhing /_/nyi tshe ma yin 'dod 
[\[74a.5\]]pa'i srid pa don med do/_/gang phyir lha na gnas rnams dus kyi dbang gis ni/_/gzugs med rnams kyang mdzad pa can phyir mi rtag ste/_/de bzhin 'jug pa min la blo gros zhugs bya zhing /_/gal te 'jug pa med na sdug bsngal yod ma yin/_/gang phyir lus ni 'gro dang gnas pa la sogs pa'i/_/
[\[74a.6\]]sna tshogs rten gyi sdug bsngal dag gi rtsa ba ste/_/de phyir lus med du ni ye shes nus pa gang /_/de yod lus kyi bu lon yod pa ma yin no/_/gang las 'gro bas 'dod pa'i ched du bskyed pa yis/_/sdug bsngal chen po du ma rnams la nyer 'gro ste/_/'di las 'dod pa'i srid las rnam par 
[\[74a.7\]]dben nus shing /_/sdug bsngal rnams las chags med nyam thag yod ma yin/_/de phyir gzugs dang bral ba'i lha rnams na yang ngam/_/'dod pa rnams dang ldan pa'i gzugs ldan rnams na yang /_/skor ba nyid phyir 'jug pa zhi ba ma yin na/_/'dod pa spyod pa drug po rnams su sngon nyid do/_/
[\[74b\]]
[\[74b.1\]]rnam pa de ltar mi rtag sdug bsngal de med cing /_/rtag par 'bar ba'i 'jig rten gsum po nyid mthong nas/_/bya tshang shing la sgong skyes rnams kyis gdung ba ltar/_/gang du 'jug la smon lam rigs pa ma yin no/_/mchog de rig par bya zhing gzhan ni rig bya min/_/mchog 
[\[74b.2\]]de blo gros yin zhing gzhan ni blo gros min/_/mchog de bya ba yin zhing gzhan ni grub pa min/_/chos 'di khyim dang ldan pa'i skye bo rnams kyis te/_/yod pa min zhes de ltar nges par rig bya min/_/nags sam khyim na gnas pa dag gi zhi ba ni/_/gang gis sgrub pa yod pa de yis 
[\[74b.3\]]yod pa'o/_/tshad pas mngon par gdungs pa chu la rnam zhugs te/_/spyi las thams cad tshim pa nye bar 'gro ba nyid/_/sgron ma dang ldan mun pa ru ni mthong ba ste/_/rnal 'byor tshad ma'o na tshod ma yin rigs ma yin/_/kha cig nags na gnas kyang na tshod la mi sbyor/_/brtul zhugs zad 
[\[74b.4\]]cing ngan 'gro la ni bsnyen pa nyid/_/kha cig khyim na gnas kyang rnam dag las can ni/_/bag dang ldan pa nges legs la ni bsnyen pa'o/_/mun pa'i rgya mtsho la ni lta ngan dba' rlabs can/_/sred pa'i chu la srog chags rnam par 'phyo rnams su/_/shes rab gru bo dran dang brtson 'grus skye ba 
[\[74b.5\]]ni/_/gang gis yod pa de ni de la sgrol ba yod/_/de ltar yul la mchog tu song ba'i rgyal po de yis ni/_/thams cad mkhyen las chos kyi de nyid 'di ni nyer thob nas/_/rgyal srid ngan pa mi rtag g.yo zhes nyer skyes blo can ni/_/myos pa dang bral gnyis 'thung bzhin du mnyan yod nyid du zhugs/_/
[\[74b.6\]]sa dbang btud pa de ni shes nas mu stegs can gzhan rnams/_/der ni stobs bcu pa la rdzu 'phrul gyis ni 'gran btsugs gyur/_/sa skyong las ni rnam par gsol nas de nas bdag 'pham pa'i/_/drang srong gis ni rdzu 'phrul ched du sdug pas gnas pa byas/_/de nas gsal zhing 'od zer 'phro 
[\[74b.7\]]ba dang bcas dkyil 'khor can/_/skar ma rnams ni 'bar bar gyur pa'i nyi ma shar ba bzhin/_/yang dag dga' zhing rnam pa sna tshogs blo can ston pa rnams/_/der ni thub pas rdzu 'phrul rnam pa mang pos pham par mdzad/_/de nas mnyan yod kyi ni de las skyes pa'i grags pa dang /_/bkur 
[\[75a\]]
[\[75a.1\]]sti nges pa mchog gi dpal gyis yongs su spangs par gyur/_/de nyid kyis ni srid pa gsum las gyen du 'dzegs pa ste/_/de'i phyir chos dag gsung bar bzhed cing yul la phan phyir ro/_/de nas thub pa mtho ris gnas pa'i yul ni ye shes kyis/_/rnam par btul nas 
[\[75a.2\]]mtho ris gshegs pa lo ni rnam phul des/_/mkha' la lha yi rgyal po'i bsod snyoms cho ga bzhin bzhes nas/_/lha yi 'jig rten dag nas sAm ka par ni yang dag babs/_/de la gzhal med gnam sa'i logs la babs pa yi/_/zhi ba thob cing ltos pa dang bcas lha rnams rjes su 'ongs/_/sar gnas nam 
[\[75a.3\]]mkha'i logs la gyen du gdong phyogs rgyal po ni/_/de dang de rnams mgo bo rnams kyis btud cing rab tu bzung /_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_rgyal byed kyi tshal rab tu bzhes pa'i le'u ste nyi shu pa'o//_//thar 'dod lha na gnas rnams dang /_/skyed ma 
[\[75a.4\]]mtho ris su btul nas/_/thub pas gdul bya rnams 'dul zhing /_/de nas de ni 'jig rten rgyus/_/ri lnga'i dbus kyi grong khyer du/_/'od ldan 'tsho byed dbang po dang /_/gro bzhin skyes dang yan lag sbyin/_/rnam par 'dren pas de nas btul/_/rgyal po'i bu ni 'jigs med dang /_/dpal 
[\[75a.5\]]sbas dang ni nye ba 'khor/_/n+ya gro d+ha sogs mthar ldan rnams/_/snga ma'i lta ba las zlog go/_/chos kyi thos pa nyid las ni/_/mi skyong dpal ni yangs pa can/_/spos 'dzin gyi ni dbang phyug 'dir/_/pad+ma zhes bya rnam par btul/_/de nas gnod sbyin gangs ldan dang /_/rtse mo brgya pa de nas ni/_/rnam 
[\[75a.6\]]rgyas pha rol gnon pa yis/_/rnam rgyas ri bor btul ba'o/_/yon tan gzigs pas mi skyong ni/_/lta byed btsun mo'i 'khor bcas la/_/'tsho byed a mra'i nags tshal du/_/mtshan mo dad pas sbyar bar mdzad/_/de nas pha rol 'gro ba can/_/bram ze zhi bar 'gro rnams des/_/rdo ba'i 
[\[75a.7\]]ri bor phra mo yi/_/tshigs bcad don rnams klog tu bcug_/de nas dam pa dran mdzad pas/_/dngos su nor sbyin mthong ba mo/_/dga' ba'i skyed ma legs ldan ma/_/'od ma'i tsher ma can du btul/_/de nas brtan ldan grong khyer du/_/brtsegs sbyin gnyis skyes dam pa ni/_/mchod 
[\[75b\]]
[\[75b.1\]]sbyin rnam pa sna tshogs kyis/_/mchod 'dod thar pa'i chos la btsud/_/de nas lus 'phags ri bo ru/_/btsud phyed rtse mo nyid dang ni/_/lha min skyes ma dang lha rnams/_/nges par 'dzin du btsud par gyur/_/de nas ang+ga'i grong khyer du/_/gnod sbyin gang ba bzang po dang /_/mchog dang dbyug pa 
[\[75b.2\]]dkar dmar dang /_/gtum po lto 'phyo che rnams btul/_/a pa Na yi grong khyer du/_/bram ze ke bya she la dag_/dka' thub thob pa thar par sbyar/_/'tsho byed sor mo'i phreng ba can/_/bran [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang mtshungs gnyis skyes ni/_/bcom ldan rdzu 'phrul phun tshogs kyis/_/phra mo rnams su 
[\[75b.3\]]btul ba mdzad/_/gang ba bzang po dang mtshungs rdzas/_/lug ces bya ba bzang pos 'tsho/_/nor sbyin bzang dang bzang po'i bu/_/bzang por yang dag rtogs par mdzad/_/de nas tshangs pa'i tshe ldan ming /_/rnam rgyas tshangs pa'i tshe ldan pa/_/gang gi smra ba smra mchog gis/_/lus 'phags po 
[\[75b.4\]]yi grong du pham/_/yangs pa can gyi rdzing bu ru/_/srin bu sha za dang seng ge_/la sogs lits+tsha bI rnams dang /_/mchog dang bden byed nyid btul to/_/de nas las bzang pos gnod sbyin/_/bzang po bzang po'i sems ldan pa/_/rgyan byed dang mtshungs grong khyer du/_/chos kyi lam la btsud 
[\[75b.5\]]pa'o/_/de nas blo dang ldan pa yis/_/shin tu mi bzad 'brog tu ni/_/gnod sbyin 'brog gnas zhes bya dang /_/gzhon nu legs 'ongs la ston to/_/de nas gnod sbyin sdig med la/_/gnod sbyin bdag pos phyag byas pas/_/bde dga'i grong du thar pa ni/_/thar pa gzigs pas gdams pa 
[\[75b.6\]]mdzad/_/thub pa grags pa yangs rnams dang /_/gnod sbyin drag po'i las can dang /_/kha spu ldan pa gnyis la ni/_/ga yar drang srong gis bstan to/_/de nas drang srong dkar min gyi/_/tsha bo kA t+yA ya na ni/_/bA rA Na sI'i grong du sngon/_/gnyis skyes stobs bcu ldan pas btul/_/mtsho 'obs 
[\[75b.7\]]bde bar pha rol 'gro'i/_/grong la rdzu 'phrul gyis gshegs nas/_/dus su tshangs pa rna bstod la/_/de nas des ni rjes su bstan/_/rjes su bstan de rab dang bas/_/drang srong mchog gi gtsug lag khang /_/rtag par dri ldan kha la reg_/tsan+dan rang bzhin byed du bcug_/de nas gang du 
[\[76a\]]
[\[76a.1\]]thub pa'i zhabs/_/rdo las 'khor lo'i mtshan mthong bas/_/ser skya dka' thub ldan pa ni/_/'od zer ldan pa des btul lo/_/de nas gnod sbyin glang po la/_/glang po ldan par bstan pa ste/_/de bzhin drag po bong bu ni/_/bcom brlag tu ni btul ba'o/_/
[\[76a.2\]]yul 'khor skyong dang rdzas mnyam pa/_/yul 'khor skyong zhes dran pa ni/_/grong rdal rags pa'i mdzod ldan du/_/ston pas de nas rnam par btul/_/tshangs pa dang 'dra skye bo mchog_/dgra can du ni btul ba ste/_/mkhas pa rgya chen skyes bu can/_/nyon mongs dul bar de 
[\[76a.3\]]bzhin no/_/'jigs bcas kyi dang mtshams [{]bzangs kyi[,]bzang gi[}]/_/bu'i bu mos 'jug bu dag dang /_/mu stegs gzhan rnams kyi mun pa/_/mnyan yod nyid du thub pas bcom/_/'dir ni bram ze mchod sbyin can/_/dge sogs rnams dang rigs gtsang dang /_/ko sa la yi dbang phyug ni/_/
[\[76a.4\]]sangs rgyas nyid kyis dad pas sbyar/_/de nas bram ze mkhas pa ni/_/bram ze su ka sA ri dang /_/she ta bi ka'i 'brog zhi bar/_/bstan pa mchog gis bstan pa'o/_/de nas 'thab pa'i grong khyer du/_/grong khyer gtso dang dus byed dang /_/'jigs pa'i las can chu srin klu/_/rab 
[\[76a.5\]]tu ma zhi zhi bar mdzad/_/de nas b+har ga rnams su des/_/gnod sbyin 'jigs byed btul mdzad cing /_/rigs med kyi ni bla ma gnyis/_/rgan po de bzhin rjes su bzung /_/nor ldan gyi mchog dbyangs ldan dang /_/bud med sgur mchog sogs rnams dang /_/skyes chen de rnams de rnams 
[\[76a.6\]]ni/_/kau shAm bIr ni rjes su bzung /_/de nas spos 'dzin yul nyid dang /_/klu chen sog ma med pa ni/_/dul ba'i bdag nyid dul ba la/_/nang gi pha rol son par gshegs/_/'bar zhing 'jigs pa'i las can ni/_/mthar byed bzhin du sreg 'dod pa/_/rnam par btul nas mkhas 
[\[76a.7\]]pa des/_/dus su de nas gdams ngag bstan/_/de la sogs pa'i 'byung po rnams/_/sa pa rnams dang mkha' 'gro rnams/_/btul nas tshigs su rgya mtsho bzhin/_/sangs rgyas grags pa 'phel bar gyur/_/che ba'i bdag nyid de mthong nas/_/lha sbyin rlom sems byas pa ste/_/bsam 
[\[76b\]]
[\[76b.1\]]gtan rnams las 'khrul thob cing /_/'dra ba min pa mang po byas/_/nye bar bcom pa'i sems ldan des/_/thub pa dge 'dun dben byas shing /_/'phye las 'dod pa ma byas te/_/de la gnod pa'i phyir brtsams so/_/de nas bya rgod phung po'i rir/_/rdo ba'i 'khrul 'khor byas pa ste/_/
[\[76b.2\]]thub pa'i steng du gang 'phangs pa/_/ltung ba ma yin gnyis su gyur/_/'jig rten dus su chu 'dzin sngon po sgra sgrogs pa/_/zla ba 'gog pa la ni mkha' la rlung bzhin du/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la ni mngon du gdong phyogs par/_/glang po'i dbang po rgyal po'i lam la gtad pa'o/_/lus kyis snun 
[\[76b.3\]]pas yan lag mchog ni nyer blangs shing /_/mche bas phug pas rgyu ma'i dra ba rab bkram pa'i/_/lus po rnams kyis rgyal po'i khab kyi lam rnams ni/_/rgyu bar dka' bar byed la mngon par rab tu zhugs/_/sha ni 'dod pas mi yi brla la rmugs pa ste/_/mche ba'i rtse mos reg cing g.yo 
[\[76b.4\]]ba'i rgyu ma'i phreng /_/khrag gis gsher ba'i mgo bo sna ljags drag po yis/_/'phen pa'i don du rdo rnams bzhin du gyen du 'phen/_/gsum du rab dga'i chang gis 'thor ma'i dri ldan zhing /_/rul ba'i khrag dang zhag gi dri yis bsgos rnams kyis/_/de tshe bskor zhing mtha' med khro 
[\[76b.5\]]ba dang ldan pa/_/grong khyer skye bo de la skyo bas 'jigs par byed/_/gnyis 'thung myos pa dus kyi dbyug pa 'jigs 'dra ba/_/khengs las gdong ni bcos shing sgra skad 'thon pa ste/_/khros pas rnam par log pa'i mig can de mthong nas/_/hA hA byed pa rgyal po'i khab nas byung bar 
[\[76b.6\]]gyur/_/kha cig yid mi bde bas phyogs su bros pa ste/_/gzhan ni mtho ba min pa'i yul rnams la thim zhing /_/de bzhin 'jigs pa las gzhan 'jigs pa ma bzung nas/_/gzhan rnams gzhan rnams dag gi khyim rnams la zhugs so/_/kha cig rnam par 'dug pa'i seng ge'i 
[\[76b.7\]]sgra rnams ni/_/rang gi srog la the tshom yang dag ma bsams shing /_/sangs rgyas la ni mngon par 'tshe ba ma byed ces/_/rab tu bzung nas gnyis 'thung de la rgyab nas 'bod/_/de bzhin gzhan rnams glang po skyong la 'bod pa ste/_/gzhan rnams 'di la thal mo dag gis phyag 'tshal lo/_/de nas 
[\[77a\]]
[\[77a.1\]]de la gzhan rnams bsdigs par byed pa ste/_/kha cig nor gyis sred cing 'dod par byed pa'o/_/rlung 'gro skar khung dag nas rnam par lta bzhin du/_/lang tsho ma rnams lag pa g.yo zhing ngu ba ste/_/'jigs ma gzhan rnams gser gyi gdu bu dag dang ldan pa yis/_/
[\[77a.2\]]zangs mdog lag pa rnams kyis mig rnams 'gebs pa'o/_/yongs su ro myang glang pos 'joms par 'dod pa na/_/gyen du lag bteg skye bo ngu bar gyur na yang /_/gshegs pa 'byed pa med cing gnod pa mi mnga' bar/_/bde bar gshegs pa rang gnas rnam 'gyur med par gshegs/_/
[\[77a.3\]]byams pas 'byung po rnams la rjes su brtse ba ni/_/gus pas lha rnams kyis ni rjes su 'gro ba ste/_/de la dbang po'i glang po ches kyang reg pa ru/_/nus pa ma yin gang phyir thub pa brtan par gshegs/_/gang phyir sangs rgyas la ni rjes 'gro dge slong ste/_/des ni rgyang nas glang chen mthong nas 
[\[77a.4\]]bros pa ste/_/sna tshogs bdag nyid 'jig rten rang bzhin gyis bzhin du/_/kun dga' bo gcig sangs rgyas la ni rjes su song /_/de nas glang chen khros pa de ni nyer 'ongs nas/_/thub pa'i mthu las 'du shes nye bar thob pa ste/_/mig sman ri bo rdo rjes phye mar bcom pa bzhin/_/lus po 
[\[77a.5\]]rab tu zegs shing spyi bos 'gyel bar gyur/_/de la shin tu mdzes shing 'dam skyes 'jam po yis/_/legs skyes sor mo'i dra ba'i phyag gis mgo bo la/_/zla ba chu 'dzin la ni 'od zer gyis bzhin du/_/gnyis 'thung dbang po la ni thub pas reg par gyur/_/chu yi shin tu khur las 
[\[77a.6\]]dus kyi sprin bzhin du/_/zhabs kyi rtsa bar gnyis 'thung de ni bsdad pa ste/_/thub pas rna ba'i ta la g.yo med la gzigs shing /_/de la sems can ma 'os zhi bas bstan pa'o/_/sdig med dang bcas rnam par 'joms pa sdug bsngal te/_/sdig med nyid la glang chen gnod pa 
[\[77a.7\]]ma byed cig_/srid pa srid par sdig med bcom pa'i srog chags kyis/_/bde 'gro brgyad la glang chen 'gyur ba ma yin no/_/'dod chags dang ni zhe sdang de bzhin gti mug ste/_/glang chen gsum bo bzod par dka' ba'i chang yin la/_/chang gsum rnams dang thub pa rnams ni bral ba ste/_/
[\[77b\]]
[\[77b.1\]]rims nad med cing mya ngan 'das pa mngon par brnyes/_/de phyir mun par 'dod pa 'di ni spongs 'dod pa/_/chang ni spangs la rang [(]bzhan [,]bzhin [)]'du shes thob par gyis/_/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho'i 'dam ni rgyas par chags pa ste/_/slar yang de ltar gnyis 'thung dbang po ma ltung cig_/de nas 
[\[77b.2\]]glang po des ni gsung 'di thos nas su/_/chang ni spangs shing yang dag rig par song ba ste/_/bdud rtsi 'thungs nas nad rnams dag las grol ba bzhin/_/nang du song ba'i bde ba dam pa thob par gyur/_/slob ma bzhin du thub la rab btud de ma thag_/chang ni yongs 
[\[77b.3\]]spangs glang po'i dbang po de mthong nas/_/kha cig chos rnams bskor zhing g.yug par gyur pa ste/_/gzhan rnams lag pa rnams ni rdebs shing skad 'don to/_/gzhan rnams kyis ni mgo bos thub la phyag 'tshal zhing /_/de nas de la gzhan rnams bskor ba byas pa ste/_/gzhan 
[\[77b.4\]]rnams 'phags pa nyid las glang chen la bstod cing /_/ya mtshan dang bcas gzhan rnams reg par gyur pa'o/_/lding khang la gnas bud med gzhan rnams de la ni/_/gtsang zhing rin thang chen po'i gos rnams kyis mchod cing /_/gzhan rnams gsar ba'i phreng ba 
[\[77b.5\]]yon tan yid 'ong dang /_/rgyan ni de dang de dag rnams kyis mngon par 'thor/_/dus dang mtshungs pa'i glang po dul bar gnas pa na/_/skye bo dad pa med pa dbus nas song ba ste/_/dbus gnas khyad par las ni dang bar rab song zhing /_/dad pa rnams kyang gang de bstan par nye bar 
[\[77b.6\]]song /_/dad pa la gnas ma skyes dgra ni thub pa yis/_/glang chen dbang po de ni brtul ba mthong nas ni/_/ya mtshan gyur zhing de nas dga' ba skyes pa ste/_/sangs rgyas la ni mchog tu dad pa byas pa'o/_/nyon mongs rtsod ldan dus ni nges par log pa na/_/rdzogs ldan 
[\[77b.7\]]dus ni 'ongs nas slar yang chos don bzhin/_/de ltar rdzu 'phrul dang ldan snyan grags mchog rnams kyis/_/rab tu gnas pa'i dka' ldan thub pa mchog 'phel to/_/mi dge ba dang yongs su ldan pa'i lha sbyin no/_/nyon mongs sdig pa'i las ni mang po byas nas ni/_/mi skyong 
[\[78a\]]
[\[78a.1\]]ste na rgu ba gnyis skyes drang srong gi_/dmod pa las bzhin sa yi 'og tu bying bar gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_las kyi rgyun gyi le'u ste nyi shu rtsa gcig pa'o//_//de nas 'jig rten rjes bzung nas/_/
[\[78a.2\]]dus kyi smra ba rnams kyi mchog_/chos kyis sa ni khyab mdzad nas/_/mya ngan 'da' ba dang mtshungs dgongs/_/de nas dus su rgyal khab na/_/pa TA li yi grong du gshegs/_/mchod rten pa TA li mtshon pa/_/der ni 'phags pa de bzhugs so/_/dus ni de tshe grong khyer gyi/_/li ts+tsha 
[\[78a.3\]]bI ni zhi ba'i phyir/_/ma ga d+hA yi rgyal po yi/_/blon po char dbyibs kyis byas so/_/lha rnams kyis der rang gi nor/_/nye bar len pa rab gzigs nas/_/grong de 'gro na phul byung du/_/de bzhin gshegs pas rnam par gsungs/_/de tshe char pa'i rnam pa yis/_/bkur 
[\[78a.4\]]byas byed pa rnams kyi mchog_/rgya mtsho'i chung ma gtso bo la/_/mngon phyogs slob ma dang bcas gshegs/_/gang+gA'i 'gram du de gshegs nas/_/ji ltar rnyed gru de dang des/_/skye bo rnams ni sgrol ba gzigs/_/de rnams gzigs nas thugs mdzad do/_/'bad pas chu bo sgrol 
[\[78a.5\]]ba la/_/bdag gi de ltar 'dra ba yin/_/de phyir rdzu 'phrul mthu yis ni/_/bdag gi gru med rab tu 'gro/_/de ltar der ni slob mar bcas/_/lha mi rnams la mi snang zhing /_/rlung gis 'gros kyang phan mdzad nas/_/skad cig gis ni pha rol gyur/_/sdug bsngal mtsho yi pha rol 
[\[78a.6\]]sngon/_/ye shes gru bos de mkhyen nas/_/slar yang gru 'di ma dmigs nas/_/thub pas gang+gA bsgral ba'o/_/bcom ldan nyi ma mdzes pa ni/_/gang gi sgo las nges 'byung ba/_/de ni de tshe gau tam sgor/_/de la mchod don nyid du byas/_/'gram gang dag las rnam 'dren pa/_/gang+gA'i 
[\[78a.7\]]pha rol du gshegs pa/_/de ni stegs su 'gro la grags/_/de yi rigs kyis mngon par brjod/_/de nas sgrol 'dod mi rnams dang /_/rgal ba rnams dang sgrol ba rnams/_/ya mtshan rnam rgyas gdong rnams kyi/_/mig rnams de la btung bar gyur/_/de nas gang+gA'i 'gram nas ni/_/
[\[78b\]]
[\[78b.1\]]sangs rgyas ku Da'i grong du gshegs/_/der ni chos ldan gtam mdzad nas/_/nA dA'i kar ni mngon par gshegs/_/dus ni de tshe de na ni/_/skye bo chen po rgud pa ste/_/gang gis gang du gang 'gro ba/_/de rnams la der thub pas gsungs/_/der ni mtshan mo gcig phul nas/_/yangs pa 
[\[78b.2\]]can gyi grong du gshegs/_/de nas a mra skyong ba'i sa/_/dpal ldan tshal du dpal stug gshegs/_/a mra skyong ma res ma'i gtso/_/rnam par 'dren pa der thos nas/_/rnam dul theg pa la gnas nas/_/mchog tu dga' bas rab tu song /_/phra ba'i gos dkar 'dzin 
[\[78b.3\]]pa mo/_/phreng ba med cing dbyug bral ma/_/lha yi bya ba'i dus su ni/_/legs ma rigs kyi mna' ma 'dra/_/rang gi gzugs kyis khengs pa yi/_/dam pa lits+tsha bI rnams kyi/_/sems rnams dang ni nor rnams dang /_/yon tan mtha' dag rnams kyis phrogs/_/dpal 
[\[78b.4\]]dang mdzes pa brtan pa yis/_/nags kyi lha mo'i gzugs 'dra ba/_/de ni theg pa la babs nas/_/skyen par nags der rab tu zhugs/_/rigs kyi mo btsun gdung ba mo/_/mig rab g.yo ba de gzigs nas/_/bde bar gshegs pas rnga dbyangs kyis/_/dge slong rnams la 
[\[78b.5\]]bka' stsal pa/_/stobs chung rnams kyi yid kyi rim/_/a mra skyong ma 'di mngon 'ongs/_/dran ldan bdud rtsi'i sems ldan rnams/_/ye shes kyis ni khyed rnams gnas/_/nye sar sbrul ni mchog yin zhing /_/ral gyi gdengs pa'i dgra bo mchog_/dran dang ye shes rnams 
[\[78b.6\]]bral ba'i/_/nye sar bud med rnams ma yin/_/bsdad pa 'am ni nyal ba'am/_/'gro 'am yang na gnas pa 'am/_/bud med ri mor bris pas kyang /_/mi rnams kyi ni yid 'phrog go/_/'di rnams zhen pas nyam thag gam/_/gyen du lag bteg ngu na yang /_/skra bkram rnams 
[\[78b.7\]]kyis bsreg pa ru/_/nus na mo btsun rnams sngon nyid/_/pha rol po 'brel rnam mang po'i/_/dbrog bya yon tan gyis bslus rnams/_/de yi rang bzhin ma mthong ste/_/bud med rnams la byis rnams rmongs/_/mi rtag sdug bsngal bdag med dang /_/mi dge ba zhes mthong nas ni/_/
[\[79a\]]
[\[79a.1\]]lta zhing sgom pa po rnams kyi/_/sems ni zil gyis non ma yin/_/gnas de rnams su legs goms blo/_/spyod yul rnams su ba lang bzhin/_/lha yi yul rnams kyis 'joms par/_/nus min mi rnams kyis ci smros/_/de phyir shes rab mda' 
[\[79a.2\]]'dzin zhing /_/brtson 'grus gzhu ni lag pa na/_/dran pa'i go cha yongs bgos shing /_/yul gyi 'du shes can du mnos/_/lcags kyi thur ma tsha mo yis/_/mig la reg pa mchog yin zhing /_/g.yo ba'i mig can bud med la/_/ma rungs dran pas lta phyir min/_/gal 
[\[79a.3\]]te dus ni de yi tshe/_/chags pa'i sems kyis dus byed na/_/dud 'gro 'am ni 'og nyid du/_/dbang med bcings nas khrid par byed/_/de phyir 'jigs pa 'di shes nas/_/mtshan mar spros pa mi bya zhing /_/lus po la ni gzugs tsam du/_/gang gis mthong ba de mthong ngo /_/
[\[79a.4\]]dbang pos 'jig rten na yul rnams/_/'chi min dbang po rnams des min/_/gang zhig de rnams la chags gyur/_/de ni de rnams las 'ching ngo /_/ba lang gnyis kyi sbyor ba'i phyir/_/sbyor ba la gcig ji ltar gyur/_/yul dang dbang po de dag ni/_/chags pa 
[\[79a.5\]]phan tshun de ltar min/_/mig gis gzugs ni 'dzin pa ste/_/sems kyis rnam par rtog par byed/_/rnam par rtog las der skye zhing /_/chags dang chags bral nyid kyang ngo /_/rnam par ma brtags dbang po'i don/_/gal te don med ched gyur na/_/dbang po'i 
[\[79a.6\]]spyod yul la spyod pa/_/thams cad don med kyis sbyor to/_/de phyir ma dor dran pas dang /_/bag yod mchog tu 'gro bas dang /_/rang don rjes su mthong bas dang /_/gdung med sems kyis bsgom par bya/_/de ltar don mthar ma gyur pa'i/_/dge slong rnams la de rjes 
[\[79a.7\]]bstan/_/ltos byas a mra skyong ma ni/_/thal mo sbyar byas nye bar 'ongs/_/des ni thugs zhi drang srong ma/_/shing drung gnas pa mthong nas ni/_/nags kyi yongs su longs spyod kyis/_/mchog tu rjes su 'dzin pa snyam/_/de nas bkur sti mang po las/_/g.yo yang lta 
[\[79b\]]
[\[79b.1\]]byed rab bkod nas/_/me tog tsam pa ka rgyas ltar/_/mgo bos thub pa la phyag 'tshal/_/kun mkhyen gyis kyang rjes gnang ma/_/de nas sa la bsdad pa ste/_/de yis blo la bzod gsung gis/_/thub pas de la bka' stsal to/_/bsam pa 'di ni yon tan ldan/_/
[\[79b.2\]]rnam dag sems dpa' nyid bstan to/_/bud med byis pa phul gnas pa/_/chos la 'dod pa rnyed par dka'/_/gal te chos kyi bdag nyid gyur/_/zhen pas bcom pa'i sems ldan pa'i/_/na rab mi mo rnams kyis dang /_/blo ldan mi rnams kyis mtshar cing /_/rang bzhin 
[\[79b.3\]]gyis ni blo chung zhing /_/g.yo ba'i sems ldan lang tsho ma/_/yul gcig ro yis 'jig rten na/_/chos shes shes pa ya mtshan no/_/khyod kyi chos la yid gyur pa/_/de ni khyod kyis bdag nyid don/_/mi rtag gson pa'i 'jig rten na/_/chos las gzhan na nor yod min/_/nad kyis 
[\[79b.4\]]nad med 'joms pa ste/_/rga bas lang tsho gcod pa nyid/_/'chi bas srog ni len pa ste/_/chos kyis rgud pa yod ma yin/_/gang phyir dga' ba rnams dang bral/_/mi dga' ba rnams dang sbyor zhing /_/mngon par 'dod pa thob pa na/_/de phyir chos ni mchog tu 'gro/_/gzhan dbang 
[\[79b.5\]]sdug bsngal chen po ste/_/rang dbang mchog tu bde ba nyid/_/ma nu'i rigs su skyes na yang /_/bud med thams cad gzhan dbang ngo /_/gang phyir gzhan dbang nyid phyir dang /_/de bzhin phru gu 'dzin pa'i phyir/_/bud med rnams kyi sdug bsngal lhag_/de phyir yang dag nges par 'os/_/de 
[\[79b.6\]]ltar thub pa chen po'i gsung /_/rang bzhin gyis dang mkhas las dang /_/brtan las byis kyang byis min bzhin/_/thos shing mngon par dga' ba'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi smra ba yis/_/'dod pa'i bdag nyid can gyis sems la brabs pa ni/_/bud med nyid ni smad 
[\[79b.7\]]cing yul las log pa ste/_/gang gi skye bo'i rten la btsog par byas pa'o/_/de nas me tog khur las tsu ta'i mchod sdong bzhin/_/yang dag kun nas dud pa'i lus kyi mchod sdong can/_/thub pa chen po la ni gus pas mig zhugs shing /_/yang yang chos ni rnam par dag pa'i lta bas 
[\[80a\]]
[\[80a.1\]]gnas/_/rang bzhin gyis ni ngo tsha dang ldan bud med de/_/chos ni mngon par 'dod pas yang yang bskul ba ni/_/'dam skyes mdzod dang 'dra bas thal mo sbyar nas ni/_/dal bus smra ba'i skad kyi gdangs kyis gsol ba'o/_/bcom ldan don mdzad bdag la 
[\[80a.2\]]gsung don du/_/nang par bsod snyoms dus su dge slong sder bcas pa/_/sdug bsngal nye bar zhi ba'i mdzad po 'jig rten na/_/'bras bur gyur pa'i bdag la 'bras bur mdzad du gsol/_/de nas de yi gus pa de ltar gzigs nas dang /_/sems can rnams ni bza' ba'i dbang 
[\[80a.3\]]nyid mkhyen nas ni/_/gsung ma phye nas bde bar gshegs pas de ltar zhes/_/de la nges par rnam 'gyur brda yis mdzad pa'o/_/skad cig mdzad pa dang ldan spyan yangs mchog gi chos mnga' zhing /_/chos kyi snod la des ni dga' zhing mgu la rungs pa ste/_/gus pas rnyed pa 
[\[80a.4\]]mchog ni rnam par nges shing bstod par gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_a mra skyong ma'i nags gzigs pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa'o//_//de nas thub pa'i thugs rig nas/_/phyag 'tshal nas kyang grong du song /_/de 
[\[80a.5\]]ni lits+tsha bIs thos nas/_/lta 'dod sangs rgyas la song ngo /_/dkar po'i rta dang shing rta gdugs/_/phreng ba rgyan dang bgo ba rnams/_/kha cig kha cig de rnams dang /_/dmar ser rnams dang de bzhin gzhan/_/kha cig bai DUr+ya ljang dang /_/gzhan rnams rma bya'i mjug ma ste/_/de ltar cha 
[\[80a.6\]]lugs rgya chen po/_/bdag gis dga' byed rnams dang song /_/ri bo rnam rgyas ro stod can/_/gser gyi gnya' shing lag pa rnams/_/dpal ldan bong ring dngos su ni/_/de rnams mtho ris bzhin du mdzes/_/mtshams sprin rkang la sna tshogs rnams/_/glog ni rab tu 
[\[80a.7\]]snang ba bzhin/_/shing rta rnams las babs 'dod pas/_/de rnams gnas pa rnam par mdzes/_/dbu rgyan rnam g.yo rnam btud pa'i/_/brtan rnams thub la phyag 'tshal nas/_/nga rgyal ldan rnams rgyags med bzhin/_/chos la dga' bas de rnams gnas/_/nyi ma sprin 
[\[80b\]]
[\[80b.1\]]dang bral ba yi/_/logs su khyer 'jigs gzhu bzhin du/_/thub pa'i drung der de rnams kyi/_/chags bral dkyil 'khor mdzes pa'o/_/sa la seng ge gnas pa rnams/_/gser gyis spras pa'i seng ge'i gdan/_/seng ge la sogs de rnams la/_/mi yi seng ges de

[\[80b.3\]]sam rgyan/_/phreng ba rnams kyis ma yin no/_/gang du khyed cag bdag po rnams/_/chos shes 'dul ba 'dod pa rnams/_/spong byed pa rnams rjes bzung zhing /_/skal ba bcas rnams bdag gi lugs/_/'phags skyes rnyed par dka' dus su/_/chos la gnas rnams srung 
[\[80b.4\]]byed gyur/_/phyis 'byung ma yin yul rnams kyi/_/skal bas dman pa rnams kyis min/_/dpal ldan skal ba chen po rnams/_/chos shes srung bar byed rnams kyis/_/bsrung ba nyid kyis yul 'di ni/_/chos kyis kyang ni rjes su 'dzin/_/rlung rnams rgal 'dod pa la rnams/_/
[\[80b.5\]]khyu mchog rgal ba rnams kyi bzhin/_/rgyal po rnams kyis gang bzung ba'i/_/yul la de nas skye dgu 'gro/_/khyed cag rnams la tshul khrims de/_/rtag par 'gyur bar 'os pa ste/_/bdag gi don ni 'dod rnams kyi/_/'dir dang song nas dbrog bya min/_/yid ni tshim zhing 
[\[80b.6\]]bkur mang dang /_/rnyed dang grags dang yid ches nyid/_/dga' ba nyid dang song nas bde/_/tshul khrims kyi ni 'bras bu che/_/'byung po rgyu dang mi rgyu yi/_/rten ni ji ltar sa yin pa/_/de ltar yon tan thams cad kyi/_/rten ni tshul khrims dam pa'o/_/'phur 'dod gshogs 
[\[80b.7\]]pa med pa dang /_/rgal 'dod gru med ji lta bar/_/mi ni tshul khrims rnam spangs par/_/dge legs 'dod pa de ltar shes/_/me tog 'bras bus khyab pa'i shing /_/tsher ma can gyur ji lta bar/_/thos dang gzugs don phun tshogs pa/_/tshul khrims 'chal pa de ltar ro/_/
[\[81a\]]
[\[81a.1\]]sna tshogs gos dang rgyan ldan zhing /_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]rnams su gnas na yang /_/tshul khrims dang ldan skye bo gang /_/de yi drang srong dang mnyam 'gros/_/ngur smrig shing shun zur phud dang /_/gtsug phud spyi bo ral pa rnams/_/tshul khrims nyams pa kun rnams 
[\[81a.2\]]kyi/_/bcos ma nyid du shes par bya/_/stegs las dus gsum khrus byas nas/_/me la lan gnyis bsregs nas dang /_/gdung ba drag por gdungs nas kyang /_/tshul khrims med na bdag ma yin/_/sha za rnams la lus byin nas/_/ri yi logs nas bor nas sam/_/me 'am 
[\[81a.3\]]chur ni bdag mchongs na/_/tshul khrims med na bdag ma yin/_/'bras bu rtsa ba chu za zhing /_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]bzhin du rtswa za'am/_/rlung gi bza' ba 'dod pa yang /_/tshul khrims med na bdag mi 'gyur/_/mi gang tshul khrims kyis dman pa/_/de ni bya dang ri dwags mtshungs/_/rdol 
[\[81a.4\]]ba'i snod ni chu yis bzhin/_/de ni chos kyi snod ma yin/_/'jigs pa dang ni gtam ngan dang /_/yid mi ches dang mi dga' nyid/_/mngon sum 'dir ni thob pa ste/_/song nas don med za bar 'gyur/_/de phyir tshul khrims gzhom mi bya/_/'brog dgon dag na lam mkhan bzhin/_/rang 
[\[81a.5\]]dbang dag ni rnyed dka' ba'i/_/tshul khrims mtho ris dag na gru/_/skyon rnams kyis bcom sems kyis ni/_/thams cad bcom nas 'jug pa ste/_/tshul khrims rab tu gnas byas nas/_/nyon mongs 'joms la 'bad pa gyis/_/de phyir dang por nga snyam pa/_/brgyan par 'dod 
[\[81a.6\]]pas spang bar bya/_/bsreg bya za ba'i du ba bzhin/_/yon tan rnams kyis nga snyam nyid/_/skar ma nyi ma zla ba rnams/_/sprin gyi dra ba chen pos bzhin/_/nga rgyal gyis bcom yon tan rnams/_/yod par gyur kyang mdzes ma yin/_/dregs pa ngo tsha 'joms pa ste/_/mya ngan gyis 
[\[81a.7\]]gtan dga' bas lus/_/yon tan rnams kyi rtsa ba nas/_/'joms phyir nga'o snyam pa skye/_/'gran pa dang ldan nga rgyal las/_/lha min lha rnams kyis bcom zhing /_/mi skyong ta la'i byin pa dang /_/dong gsum pa rnams zad par gyur/_/gang zhig mi rtag dngos rnams su/_/mchog 
[\[81b\]]
[\[81b.1\]]ni nga yin snyam pa dang /_/mi dman pa ni nga zhes snyam/_/de ni mkhas pa min par rtogs/_/gzugs ni brtan pa ma yin zhing /_/'jig pa'i chos can g.yo ba yis/_/nga zhes gang zhig nga rgyal te/_/yongs su rtog pa min gzhan ci/_/'dod pa'i 'dod chags stobs ldan 
[\[81b.2\]]pa/_/rab gab lhan skyes dgra bo ste/_/bshes min nang na spyod pa bzhin/_/gang zhig bshes kyi sgo nas 'joms/_/'dod chags me dang me nyid ni/_/sreg pa'i bdag nyid kyis mnyam ste/_/'dod chags me ni 'bar ba na/_/mtshan mo de nyid ring bar 'gyur/_/'dod chags me'i 
[\[81b.3\]]stobs ji 'dra ba/_/me yi stobs ni de 'drar smin/_/me ni chu rnams kyis shes te/_/'dod chags me ni mtshos kyang min/_/dus su nags su mes tshig rnams/_/nags kyi shing rnams skye ba ste/_/'dod chags mes tshig blo med rnams/_/chos la skye ba ma yin no/_/chags las 
[\[81b.4\]]bde ba tshol ba ste/_/bde phyir mi dge ba byed cing /_/mi dge ba las dmyal bar 'gro/_/chags pa dang mnyam dgra bo med/_/chags las sred pa skye ba ste/_/sred las 'dod pa rnams la chags/_/'dod pa rnams las sdug bsngal 'thob/_/chags pa dang mnyam yul med do/_/chags pa zhes bya'i 
[\[81b.5\]]nad chen po/_/de ni byis pas rtogs ma yin/_/mi rtag nyid las mi gtsang las/_/sdug bsngal nyid las bdag med las/_/gal te mthong na mi chags te/_/log pa'i blo yis chags pa'o/_/de phyir dngos po la chags pa/_/gang du nye bar skye ba na/_/der ni ji ltar 'gyur brtag 
[\[81b.6\]]ste/_/gyur pa mthong ba zhes brjod do/_/yon tan yongs su rtogs pa na/_/ji ltar chags par nye bar 'gro/_/yon tan med na yongs rtogs na/_/de bzhin khro ba 'gugs par byed/_/de phyir khro ba spong 'dod pas/_/zhe sdang yongs su brtag mi bya/_/dkar min 'gros 
[\[81b.7\]]las ngu ba bzhin/_/khro ba las ni zhe sdang 'dzag_/khro ba gzugs ldan rnams kyi dga'/_/mig ldan rnams kyi mun pa yang /_/chos don 'dod pa rnams kyi gshed/_/mos pa'i mi mthun pa nyid do/_/khro ba blo yi mun pa mchog_/mdza' bshes gyur pa'i dgra bo mchog_/
[\[82a\]]
[\[82a.1\]]bkur sti mang po'i gcod pa po/_/zil gyis non pa'i byed pa po/_/de bzhin khro ba mi bya zhing /_/byas nas kyang ni dor bar bya/_/dmug pa'i rang bzhin lto 'phye bzhin/_/khro ba'i rjes su 'jug mi bya/_/skor ba'i shing rta bzhin gang gis/_/khro ba brtan pas thag 
[\[82a.2\]]pas bzung /_/de bdag kha lo sgyur snyam ste/_/cig shos skye bo thag pa 'dzin/_/gang zhig khro ba 'dod pa ste/_/skye ba 'gog pa 'dod ma yin/_/phyis de khro ba med pa na/_/me la reg pa bzhin du bsreg_/khro ba skyes pas sngon rol du/_/bdag nyid kyis ni sems bsreg ste/_/rnam par 
[\[82a.3\]]'phel zhing phyin pa ni/_/gzhan ni sreg gam mi sreg gam/_/nad sogs nye bar 'tshe rnams kyi/_/gang tshe 'jig rten brtse ba rnams/_/lus can bshes ma yin rnams la/_/gnod sems byas nas yon tan ci/_/de phyir 'jig rten sdug bsngal ba/_/shes nas khro ba zlog 
[\[82a.4\]]pa'i phyir/_/'byung po kun la byams pa dang /_/snying rje nyid ni bsgom par bya/_/de ltar nyon mongs lhag pa gang /_/de yi de tshe gzigs pa ste/_/de la brtse bas sangs rgyas kyis/_/de rnams la gsungs smad pa'o/_/sems can nyam thag rnams la ni/_/ji ltar rang bzhin gyis 
[\[82a.5\]]mkhas pa'i/_/sman pas sman ni gsungs pa ste/_/nad rnams nye par zhi phyir ro/_/de bzhin chags dang rga sogs kyi/_/nad kyis 'byung po nyam thag la/_/thub pas bsam pa mkhyen nas ni/_/de nyid shes pa'i sman byin to/_/de ltar thub pa'i gsung la ni/_/lits+tsha bI rnams 
[\[82a.6\]]mngon dga' ste/_/rab 'phyang gtsug gi nor bu can/_/mgo bo rnams kyis phyag 'tshal lo/_/de nas thal mo rnam sbyar nas/_/cung zhig dud pa'i lus rnams kyis/_/phur bu la ni lha rnams bzhin/_/sangs rgyas la ni byon par zhus/_/dang po a mra skyong ma'i rigs/_/thub pas 
[\[82a.7\]]de rnams la gsungs shing /_/dam pa rnams kyis thog mar ni/_/snyan mnan 'dor bya min par gsungs/_/de rnams bud med de yis ni/_/bdag la bslus par shes pa ste/_/de bzhin gshegs la mchod mang po/_/nyis 'gyur nyid du byas pa'o/_/legs brjod drang srong sngags 
[\[82b\]]
[\[82b.1\]]rnams kyis/_/sbrul rnams kyi ni dug bzhin du/_/kun mkhyen gyis bstan de rnams ni/_/rab tu zhi ba'i yid rnams song /_/de yi mtshan mo 'das nas ni/_/a mra skyong mas bkur byas te/_/'od ma ldan pa'i grong du ni/_/dbyar rnams rnam par 'das pa na/_/yangs pa can nyid 
[\[82b.2\]]du gshegs shing /_/spre'u rdzing gi 'gram du ni/_/thub pa chen po bzhugs pa'o/_/shing gi rtsa bar de bzhugs so/_/de bzhin du ni 'bar ba la/_/nags tshal de ni snang byas shing /_/nye bar 'ongs nas bdud kyis smras/_/nai rany+dza na'i 'gram du sngon/_/thub pa khyod la bdag gis 
[\[82b.3\]]smras/_/bya ba byas pa mya ngan zlos/_/zhes 'dir khyod kyis lan smras so/_/nyam thag pa rnams 'jigs med cing /_/nyan thos rnams ni don byas par/_/bdag gi ji srid ma gyur pa/_/de srid mya ngan mi 'da' zhes/_/de 'di mang po rnams grol zhing /_/de bzhin grol bar 
[\[82b.4\]]'dod rnams dang /_/grol bar gyur pa rnams nyid de/_/de phyir mya ngan 'da' bar 'os/_/de nas tshig 'di gsan gyur nas/_/zla ba gsum na mya ngan 'da'/_/mi brtan pa ni dor cig ces/_/dgra bcom rnams kyi mchog gis gsungs/_/de nas dam bca' 'di shes nas/_/yid la re 
[\[82b.5\]]ba gang ba ste/_/rab tu dga' zhing mgu ba ni/_/de nyid du ni mi snang gyur/_/de nas drang srong chen po'i thugs kyi ting nge 'dzin/_/rnam pa de ltar rnal 'byor stobs las mdzad pa nyid/_/sku tshe ji ltar gyur pa sngon du btang ba ste/_/rdzu 'phrul mthu las mtshungs pa med pa 'tsho bar gyur/_/
[\[82b.6\]]des ni sku tshe btang ba'i yud tsam de la ni/_/bud med myos ma bzhin du sa ni g.yos gyur la/_/ma yis tshos pa'i lhun po'i rdo ba rim bzhin du/_/phyogs rnams dag las sgron ma chen po rnams lhu dang /_/de bzhin kun nas dbang po'i rdo rje mi bzad rnams/_/glog dang bcas shing me yi snying po 
[\[82b.7\]]can g.yos shing /_/bskal pa 'jig che 'jig rten bsreg par 'dod pa bzhin/_/phyogs rnams thams cad rnams su me dag 'bar bar gyur/_/shing rnams kyis ni phung po 'khogs pa rnams bor zhing /_/ri rnams kyis ni rtse mo rnams ni dor ba ste/_/rlung gis mngon par gang ba'i rdo ba'i phug 
[\[83a\]]
[\[83a.1\]]bzhin du/_/mkha' la rnga rnams kyi ni ma rungs sgra rnams bsgrags/_/de nas de ltar kun du bskyod tshe yud tsam la/_/mi yi 'jig rten na dang lha dang nam mkha' la/_/ting 'dzin chen po de las rnam par bzhengs mdzad nas/_/thub pa chen pos gsung ni 'di nyid 
[\[83a.2\]]gsungs pa'o/_/shing rta srog shing rnam par nyams pa dang 'dra ba'i/_/tshe btang lus po nang gi stobs las 'dren bgyi zhing /_/sgo nga'i skye gnas pa rnams sgo nga phye nas bzhin/_/srid pa'i 'ching las tshe dang lhan cig 'grol bar bgyi/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags 
[\[83a.3\]]chen po las/_sku tshe'i 'du byed byin gyis brlabs pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa gsum pa'o//_//de nas bskyod pa de mthong nas/_/kun dga' bo ni spu langs shing /_/ci zhig gyur zhes shugs 'ongs te/_/'dar bar gyur cing dga' ma gyur/_/kun mkhyen gtan tshigs 
[\[83a.4\]]mkhas pa la/_/des der gtan tshigs dris pa ste/_/de nas de la thub pas ni/_/khyu mchog myos pa'i dbyangs kyis gsungs/_/rgyu mtshan gang gis g.yos pa 'di/_/bdag gi tshe 'di btang ba la/_/zla ba gsum ni yongs bgrangs par/_/byin gyis brlabs nas de ni 'tsho/_/de 
[\[83a.5\]]ltar thos nas kun dga' bo/_/tsan+dan ljon pa'i thang chu ni/_/glang po che yis brabs pa bzhin/_/mchi ma phyung zhing g.yos par gyur/_/des gnyen nyid phyir bla ma'i phyir/_/chags bral min phyir sdug bsngal zhing /_/snying rje bar ni rnam zlos te/_/yongs su dga' ba med par 
[\[83a.6\]]ngus/_/mgon gyi nges pa thos gyur nas/_/bdag gi lus ni lhung ba bzhin/_/bdag gi phyogs rnams spobs pa med/_/bdag gis chos rnams thos pa rmongs/_/gangs kyis gtser rnams la me bzhin/_/rga ba rnyings pa'i chos rnams su/_/kyi hud mis bsngags skyen par ni/_/
[\[83a.7\]]de bzhin gshegs pa mya ngan 'da'/_/nyon mongs 'brog dgon chen po ru/_/chud zos lus dang ldan rnams la/_/lam mkhyen shin tu skyen pa ru/_/lam mkhan nges par nyams par 'gyur/_/thag ring lam la rab zhugs pa'i/_/sred pa yongs ldan bdag rnams kyi/_/lam 
[\[83b\]]
[\[83b.1\]]na rdzing bu bsil ba'i chu/_/shin tu myur bar bskam par gyur/_/blo ni rdzi ma zhi ba smin/_/'das dang ma 'ongs da ltar gzigs/_/ye shes rnam spangs 'jig rten gyi/_/mig ni nges par zum par 'gyur/_/nges par lo thog skyed rnams su/_/chu ni don gnyer ngal rnams la/_/char 
[\[83b.2\]]bab gyur nas skyen pa ru/_/chu sbyin sprin ni nub par 'gyur/_/mi shes mun pas phrogs rnams su/_/sems can lam gol ba rnams la/_/rab tu gsal ba'i sgron ma ni/_/shin tu skyen par zhi bar 'gyur/_/de nas de ltar mya ngan gyis/_/sems gzir kun dga' bo gzigs nas/_/
[\[83b.3\]]zhi byed mgon pos de nyid ni/_/de nyid rig pa'i mchog gis gsungs/_/'gro ba'i rang bzhin shes nas ni/_/mya ngan ma byed kun dga' bo/_/'dus byas ngo bo mi rtag cing /_/'gro ba 'di kun 'dzad pa nyid/_/lus can rtsod pa'i skyed tshal du/_/sdug pa kun dang bral ba ste/_/brtse 
[\[83b.4\]]ba kun nas thang cig ces/_/sngon du bdag gis smras pa nyid/_/'dus byas g.yo ba skyes pa gang /_/rten nas 'byung ba dbang med de/_/bdag gis rtag par gyur cig ces/_/'di ni rnyed par mi nus so/_/sa stengs 'byung po rtag gyur na/_/'jug pa g.yo ba ma yin zhing /_/thar 
[\[83b.5\]]pas bya ba su yis 'gyur/_/de phyir mthar thug 'dod pa'o/_/ci ste khyod dam skye bo gzhan/_/bdag la khyod kyi sred pa ci/_/gang phyir ngal ba skyes pa 'di/_/khyod kyis bdag dang bral bas byas/_/lam ni mtha' dag khyed cag la/_/bdag gis bstan cing bshad pa ste/_/sangs 
[\[83b.6\]]rgyas rnams dang slob dpon gyi/_/dpe mkhyud med ces gzung mdzod cig_/bdag gi gnas sam nyer zhi na/_/don ni de tsam nyid yin te/_/de bzhin gshegs rnams chos kyi sku/_/lus kyis khyed cag la don ci/_/de phyir dang ngam bdag 'das tshe/_/skyo ba yis dang bag yod 
[\[83b.7\]]dang /_/bdag gi sgron mas gsal byas te/_/chos kyi sgron ma rtag par ro/_/der zhugs brtson 'grus brtan pa dang /_/rtsod med bdag gi don la mkhas/_/blo ni gzhan dbang ma yin pa/_/bdag gi sgron ma zhes shes bya/_/rig pa dang ldan mkhas pa ni/_/gang gi sgron mas 
[\[84a\]]
[\[84a.1\]]mun pa bzhin/_/shes rab sgron mas 'joms pa ste/_/chos kyi sgron mar shes par bya/_/dge legs nye bar thob bya'i phyir/_/de dag rnams kyi spyod yul zhi/_/lus dang tshor ba nyid dang ni/_/sems dang bdag med pa nyid do/_/rus lpags khrag dang rgyus 
[\[84a.2\]]pa dang /_/sha dang spu sogs rnams kyis dkris/_/mi gtsang lus su mthong bas ni/_/lus la chags pa ma yin no/_/de lta de ltar rkyen rnams kyis/_/'jug pa'i tshor ba rnams su ni/_/sdug bsngal zhes de ltar mthong ba'i/_/bde ba'i shes rab rab tu 'joms/_/skye ba dang ni 
[\[84a.3\]]gnas pa dang /_/chos kyi 'god pa nyid dag ni/_/zhi ba'i sems kyis mthong ba yin/_/rtag par 'dzin pa ldog pa'o/_/rkyen rnams las ni skyes pa rnams/_/phung po rnams ni mthong ba yi/_/ngar 'dzin gyis ni skyed pa po/_/bdag tu 'dzin pa 'jug ma yin/_/sdug bsngal nye bar 
[\[84a.4\]]zhi ba'i phyir/_/bgrod pa gcig pa'i lam 'di ni/_/lam ni bzhi po de rnams su/_/de ltar dran pa nye bar gzhag_/de ltar ram ni bdag 'das tshe/_/gang rnams 'di la gnas 'gyur ba/_/de rnams 'god med go 'phang mchog_/nges par legs pa thob par 'gyur/_/de ltar kun
