[\[84a.5\]]dga' bo la gtam/_/de rnams rnam par 'dren pas mdzad/_/lits+tsha bI rnams kyis thos nas/_/de la gus phyir skyen par song /_/thub la brtse dang gus pa'i phyir/_/gdung bas phrog pa'i sems can rnams/_/spyod mthas bkod pa'i don rnams kun/_/myur las rdzu 'phrul don 
[\[84a.6\]]rnams so/_/ston pa la ni smra 'dod pa/_/de rnams btud nas nye bar gnas/_/smra bar 'dod rnams ston pa yis/_/mkhyen nas thub pas tshig gsungs so/_/gang 'di bdag la song ba'i sems/_/khyed cag rnams kyis de kun shes/_/de nyid gzhan bzhin mya ngan gyis/_/de ring khyed 
[\[84a.7\]]kyis spobs par gyur/_/dpal gyi bde rnams la gnas kyang /_/khyed cag rnams kyis thams cad nas/_/mngon par zhugs pa 'di yis ni/_/gsal ba nyid dang chos mkhan nyid/_/gal te ye shes bdag las kyang /_/cung zhig thos shing shes pa na/_/bdag gi zhing ba rab dmigs te/_/
[\[84b\]]
[\[84b.1\]]gdung ba ma byed brtan par song /_/mi rtag 'dus byas dngos po rnams/_/g.yo zhing yongs su gyur pa can/_/snying po dang bral blo gtad min/_/cung zhig brtan par gnas pa med/_/gnas 'jog rgyun shes la sogs dang /_/gang gzhan thig le gzhan rnams dang /_/dus kyi dbang 
[\[84b.2\]]du song ba ste/_/srid pa 'di ni ma rungs nyid/_/sa 'dzin nga las nu dang ni/_/brgya byin dang mtshungs dbyig nor dang /_/skal ba che dang skal med min/_/'byung por mnyes gshin 'byung ba'o/_/'gro mgyogs de nas 'gro byar 'gro/_/skal ldan shing rta shing rta bzang /_/sgra ngan gyis smad sgra ngan 
[\[84b.3\]]rdzogs/_/rangs byed ri mo rdul ma skyes/_/mi skyong drang srong bdag nyid che/_/de rnams dang gzhan mang po rnams/_/dbang chen dang mtshungs nyams pa ste/_/mi nyams pa ni yod ma yin/_/nyi ma gnas las 'phos pa ste/_/nor lha rnams ni sa la song /_/dbang po brgya 
[\[84b.4\]]rnams 'das pa ste/_/rtag pa ci yang yod ma yin/_/gang gzhan yang dag sangs rgyas rnams/_/'gro ba'i snang ba mdzad nas su/_/mar me snum zad bzhin du ni/_/de rnams mya ngan 'das par gyur/_/gang gzhan bdag nyid chen po rnams/_/'byung 'gyur de bzhin gshegs rnams 
[\[84b.5\]]kyang /_/bud shing tshig pa'i me bzhin du/_/de rnams mya ngan 'da' bar 'gyur/_/thar 'dod dka' thub nags su bzhin/_/de phyir bdag kyang 'gro bar bgyi/_/bdag gi don med lus ming ni/_/'gran pa la ni rgyu mtshan med/_/gang du gdul bya 'ga' zhig yod/_/gang phyir 'di nas 
[\[84b.6\]]'gro bar blo/_/nyams dga' yangs pa can la ni/_/khyed cag blo gzhan ma gyur cig_/de phyir 'jig rten skyabs med cing /_/dbang med g.yo bar shes nas su/_/chags bral la ni 'gro bar dang /_/skyo ba thob par 'os pa'o/_/de ltar gtam bcad nas dus su/_/ltos med 
[\[84b.7\]]de bzhin gshegs pa ni/_/ston gyi zla ba snang byed bzhin/_/lus ngan phyogs su rab tu gshegs/_/de la mchi mas yongs gang mig_/li ts+tsha bI rnams rjes song zhing /_/rgyan bcas lag pa rgyas rnams kyis/_/thal mo sbyar nas rnam par smras/_/kyi hud 'o brgyal gser 
[\[85a\]]
[\[85a.1\]]mtshungs shing /_/sum cu rtsa gnyis mtshan mnga' ba/_/ston pa'i sku lus dbye 'gyur zhing /_/thugs rje mnga' ba mi rtag nyid/_/srid cing 'o ma med pa yi/_/sems ma thob pa'i be'u ni/_/bkren rnams ye shes 'jo ba'i bas/_/kye ma shin tu skyen par 'bor/_/
[\[85a.2\]]'gro na mig ldan mi rnams kyis/_/rmongs pa'i mun pa bcom mdzad nas/_/ye shes 'od zer skyen pa ru/_/thub pa nyi ma nub par 'gyur/_/mi shes rgyun gyis 'jig rten na/_/de las de las khyer ba la/_/rnam yangs chos kyi zam pa ni/_/shin tu myur bar chag par 'gyur/_/brtse ldan 
[\[85a.3\]]sman pa chen po ni/_/ye shes mchog gi sman mnga' bas/_/sems nad kyis na 'jig rten ni/_/bor nas nges par gshegs par 'gyur/_/sems kyis gzer bu'i yo byad dang /_/shes rab rgyan gyis brgyan pa ste/_/ltad mo la ni skom rnams kyis/_/dbang po'i tog ni 'gyel bar 'gyur/_/
[\[85a.4\]]'gro ba sdug bsngal skal ba can/_/'khor ba'i 'ching bas bcings pa la/_/gal te thar pa'i sgo 'di ni/_/dus kyis sdom par 'gyur ba'o/_/de ltar rnam zlog lits+tsha bI/_/mig rnams mchi mas rnams 'khrugs pa/_/rjes su 'ongs pa de rnams ni/_/thub pas slar la zlog 
[\[85a.5\]]par mdzad/_/de nas thub pas bcad shes nas/_/bstan pa rab tu bzung nas su/_/phyir la ldog phyir nges pa ni/_/sdug bsngal sdug bsngal gyis byas so/_/rlung gis bskyod pa'i me tog rnams/_/kar ni ka ra'i ljon shing bzhin/_/gser gyi ri ltar dkar de rnams/_/
[\[85a.6\]]thub pa'i zhabs la phyag 'tshal te/_/de la sems rnams kyis chags shing /_/rkang pa rnams kyang 'khyar ba ste/_/gnyen po'i rlung la chu gnyer bzhin/_/'gro ba med par de rnams log_/zhe sa yin zhing dga' ma yin/_/dga' ba yin zhing zhe sa min/_/thub pa la ni de dag 
[\[85a.7\]]gis/_/dga' dang zhe sa g.yo ba med/_/'brog mtha' nas 'thon khyu mchog che/_/khyu mchog chen po rnams kyis bzhin/_/gnas na gnas kyang de rnams kyis/_/yang yang stobs bcu ldan la lta/_/de bzhin gshegs par sems song ba/_/lus dang ldan yang 'od med 
[\[85b\]]
[\[85b.1\]]rnams/_/rkang pas de nas mya ngan 'das/_/log pa'i 'khrus bzhin rjes su 'ongs/_/rnam par ma 'khrugs mda' gzhu rnams kyi sgra rnams zil mnan nas/_/de rnams nga rgyal ldan yang stobs dang ldan yang 'gugs ldan yang /_/'gro na dpal 'dod li ts+tsha bI ni 'dod pa chen po rnams/_/mya ngan 
[\[85b.2\]]gyis 'khrugs gdong rnams rang gi khang pa rnams su 'ongs/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_li ts+tsha bI rnams la thugs brtse ba'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa bzhi pa'o//_//snang byed 'dzin par gyur pa na/_/mun bcas kyis 'khrugs nam mkha' bzhin/_/
[\[85b.3\]]thub pa 'da' phyir gshegs pa na/_/de phyir yangs pa can ma mdzes/_/dpal ldan dregs pa mi ldan zhing /_/thams cad du ni nyams dga' yang /_/bdag po nyams las bud med bzhin/_/kun nas gdungs pas mdzes ma gyur/_/thos pas dman pa'i gzugs bzhin dang /_/yon tan 
[\[85b.4\]]dang bral rig pa bzhin/_/ngag gis dman pa'i blo bzhin dang /_/'du byed dang bral ngag bzhin no/_/spyod pas dman pa'i dpa' bzhin dang /_/dang po dang bral byams pa bzhin/_/rnam gnon phun sum tshogs pa bzhin/_/chos kyis dman pa'i bya ba bzhin/_/ston kar char ni log pa na/_/
[\[85b.5\]]sha li bskams pas nor 'dzin bzhin/_/de tshe mya ngan las ma mdzes/_/gang du der ni ma btsos shing /_/zos pa ma yin mya ngan des/_/kun du ngu zhing thams cad kyis/_/grags ldan thub pa grags pa byas/_/gzhan ni ma gtams ma byas shing /_/sus kyang bsams pa ma yin la/_/mya 
[\[85b.6\]]ngan dang ni ngu ba ste/_/grong khyer pa kun bya ba gcig_/de nas dga' 'dun gtso bo la/_/brtan kyang mya ngan gyis nyam thag_/sde chen bdag po seng ge ni/_/bsams shing rnam par zlos pa'o/_/lam log mu stegs rnams 'pham nas/_/'di 'dra'i lam la gshegs nas su/_/gang 
[\[85b.7\]]gis dam pa'i lam bstan te/_/slar mi ldog phyir gshegs pa nyid/_/nyam thag nyams par gyur pa yi/_/snang med 'jig rten bor nas su/_/skye dgu mgon med mdzad nas ni/_/mgon po de ni zhi phyir gshegs/_/rnal 'byor gtso bo bla ma mchog_/mthar thug zhi la mngon phyogs nas/_/
[\[86a\]]
[\[86a.1\]]tshe zad pa las gzi mdangs bzhin/_/bdag gi brtan pa nyams par 'gyur/_/mi skyong sgra ngan mtho ris las/_/rdzu 'phrul dang ldan lhung pa bzhin/_/de dang bral ba rtse byar gyur/_/sa la mi shes bya ba ci/_/tsha bas dub pa chur bzhin dang /_/grang bas nyam thag me 
[\[86a.2\]]la bzhin/_/the tshom gcod la skye bo rnams/_/da ni su la brten par 'gyur/_/me ni 'phel phyir sbud pa bzhin/_/gang gis sbud ba dge legs phyir/_/'jig rten slob dpon thub pa der/_/nyams tshe chos ni nyams par 'gyur/_/tshul med tshul ngan gyis bcings pa'i/_/rga shi dbang bdag 
[\[86a.3\]]rnams kyi ni/_/sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khor chen po yi/_/bshig byed de 'dra su zhig yod/_/sin d+hur shing ni skams pa rnams/_/dpyid mthar chu gter gyis bzhin du/_/chags skyes rgod pa'i mi rnams ni/_/gsung gis tshim mdzad su zhig lags/_/gang du bla ma lhun po'i snying /_/kun mkhyen 
[\[86a.4\]]nyid kyang rgud 'gyur na/_/blo gros ldan pa 'jig rten na/_/blo gtad la 'gro 'os pa su/_/ji ltar gsang bya bskyangs byas nas/_/'chi ba'i ched du 'khrid par byed/_/de bzhin gson pa'i 'jig rten sdug_/'ching ba'i ched du skye bar byed/_/ji ltar shing ni rno ba yi/_/sog le yis ni 
[\[86a.5\]]gshegs par byed/_/de bzhin 'gro 'di nyams pa yi/_/sog le yis ni gshegs par byed/_/nyon mongs mtha' dag bsregs nas kyang /_/ye shes kyi ni stobs ldan kyang /_/'gro ba'i mchog gi slob dpon ni/_/rab tu nyams phyir gshegs par gyur/_/lta ngan srog chags ngan pa 
[\[86a.6\]]chu/_/srid pa'i dba' rlabs rdul ni nya/_/ye shes gru po chen po yis/_/gang gi srid pa'i mtsho las rgal/_/rga ba'i yal ga nad me tog_/'chi ba'i rtsa ba srid myu gu/_/ye shes mtshon cha chen po yis/_/gang gis srid pa'i ljon shing bcad/_/ma rig gtsub shing las skyes shing /_/
[\[86a.7\]]'dod chags me lce yul bud shing /_/gang gis ye shes bsil ba'i chus/_/skyon gyi me ni zhi bar mdzad/_/gang gis zhi ba'i lam la gshegs/_/gang gis mun pa chen po spangs/_/gang gis dge legs mchog mkhyen zhing /_/mkhyen nas byams pa yis bstan to/_/nges kyang nyon mongs kun 
[\[86b\]]
[\[86b.1\]]mthar phyir/_/kun du zhi ba gzigs pa ste/_/kun mkhyen kun la phan pa mdzad/_/kun spangs ched du gshegs pa nyid/_/mnyen zhing rnam yangs dbyangs mnga' ba'i/_/phyag ring thub pa chen po de'i/_/gal te mtha' na su yi mtha'/_/'byung 'gyur ma yin 'byung 'gyur min/_/'brog 
[\[86b.2\]]dgon dag na chu mthong nas/_/lam 'gro don bcas 'khrul ba bzhin/_/myur ba dang ldan mkhas pas ni/_/de phyir chos la bsnyen par 'os/_/nyams phyir khyad par med pa yi/_/sdig pa mi rtag nyid shes nas/_/gang zhig chos la gnyid ma log_/de ni nyal yang gnyid ma 
[\[86b.3\]]log_/de nas mi yi seng ge yi/_/ye shes za phod seng ge des/_/skye ba'i nyes pa rnams smad cing /_/srid pa zad pa'i bstod pa nyid/_/srid pa'i rtsa ba 'dor 'dod cing /_/brtul zhugs dam pa len 'dod la/_/g.yo ba'i sems ni thul 'dod de/_/dge ba'i lam la gnas par 
[\[86b.4\]]'dod/_/zhi ba'i lam la 'gro 'dod cing /_/srid pa'i mtsho las rgal 'dod la/_/rtag par sbyin pa gtong 'dod de/_/yang srid gcod par 'dod pa'o/_/sbyin pa byin zhing nga rgyal spangs/_/chos ni bsams shing zhi bar song /_/thub pa mya ngan 'da' bzhed tshe/_/de yis sa ni stong par 
[\[86b.5\]]go/_/de nas sku lus thams cad kyis/_/gnyis 'thung rgyal po bzhin bzlog nas/_/grong khyer la ni gzigs bzhin du/_/thub pas 'di skad bka' stsal to/_/yangs pa can gyi bdag gi 'di/_/khyod la lta ba'i phyi ma yin/_/gyad gnas kyi tshe tshong dus su/_/mya ngan 
[\[86b.6\]]'da' phyir 'gro par gyis/_/de nas gus ldan chos 'dod pa/_/nye bar 'ongs pa rnams gzigs nas/_/de yi rab spyod las rnams la/_/thub pas mngon par rjes gnang mdzad/_/de nas rim gyis rnam 'dren pa/_/longs spyod grong la bsnyen pa ste/_/der bzhugs 
[\[86b.7\]]thams cad mkhyen pa yis/_/dge slong rnams la mngon par gsungs/_/da ltar ram ni bdag 'das nas/_/chos la mchog tu gzhol bar bya/_/de ni khyod kyi don mchog ste/_/gang gzhan de ni ngal bar 'gyur/_/gang zhig mdo sder ma zhugs shing /_/'dul ba na yang mi snang 
[\[87a\]]
[\[87a.1\]]la/_/rigs par so sor gnod pa ste/_/de ni cis kyang gzung mi bya/_/de ni chos min 'dul ba min/_/de ni bdag gi tshig ma yin/_/mang po rnams kyi tshig yin kyang /_/nag po'i gdams pa de dor bya/_/dag pa'i gdams pa gzung bya zhing /_/gang 
[\[87a.2\]]la yod min zlog pa ste/_/de ni chos yin 'dul ba ste/_/de ni bdag gi tshig yin no/_/de phyir bdag gis mdor bsdus nas/_/tshad ma 'di rnams bshad pa ste/_/gang zhig byed de tshad ma yin/_/'di las gzhan du tshad ma min/_/bdag gi dgongs pa phra mo de/_/rnam par ma 
[\[87a.3\]]bzhag ma shes nas/_/rmongs las chos ma yin pa la/_/chos kyi blo rnams 'byung bar 'gyur/_/mun pa dang bcas lta bas sam/_/khyad par rnams ni shes min las/_/gser dang rab tu mtshungs pa yi/_/ji ltar zangs kyis slu bar byed/_/shes rab khyab pa med pas sam/_/
[\[87a.4\]]de nyid 'dzin pa ma yin pas/_/dam pa'i chos kyi gzugs brnyan gyi/_/de ltar chos ma yin pas slu/_/brdar las gcad las bsregs pa las/_/mkhas pa rnams kyis gser bzhin du/_/'dul ba mdo las rigs pa las/_/de phyir yongs su rtog par rigs/_/bstan 
[\[87a.5\]]bcos rnam par mi shes pas/_/mkhas pa rnams ni ma yin zhing /_/tshul min la tshul blo gros dang /_/tshul la tshul min mthong ba nyid/_/de phyir don dang tshig gis ni/_/yang dag thos pas gzung bya ste/_/bstan bcos log par gzung ba yis/_/mtshon cha bzhin du rnam par 'joms/_/
[\[87a.6\]]sngar med ldan pas 'khyog mang po'i/_/mtshan mo'i 'od byed khyim la bzhin/_/tshig ni log par brjod pa yi/_/don la sdug bsngal gyis 'jug go/_/don nyams pa las chos nyams shing /_/chos nyams pa las nus pa ste/_/de phyir ma log don la ni/_/gang gi blo de blo ldan no/_/dge 
[\[87a.7\]]mang de skad gsungs nas ni/_/dus su sdig pa'i grong la gshegs/_/der ni 'di la mkhyen rnams kyis/_/bsnyen bkur dam pa byas pa'o/_/de nas der ni gus ldan pa/_/skul byed legs pa'i khyim nyid du/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis zas mdzad de/_/de yi phyir yin rgyas phyir 
[\[87b\]]
[\[87b.1\]]min/_/de nas nyan thos tshogs bcas te/_/de bzhin gshegs pas zas mdzad nas/_/skul byed la ni chos gsungs te/_/ku sha ldan pa'i grong du gshegs/_/de bzhin nor ldan zhes pa'i klung /_/skul byed bcas pa de rgal nas/_/grong khyer der ni zhi ba yi/_/pad ma'i nags 
[\[87b.2\]]bcas nags su bsnyen/_/gser dang 'dra ba'i 'od mnga' des/_/gser ldan la ni khrus mdzad nas/_/kun dga' bo ni dga' med la/_/'jig rten dga' bas bka' stsal to/_/kun dga' gshin rje'i ra ba yi/_/dbus su bdag gi khri shoms shig_/de ring nam gyi cha smad la/_/
[\[87b.3\]]de bzhin gshegs pa mya ngan 'da'/_/zhes gsungs kun dga' bos thos nas/_/mchi mas zhag ngan mig ldan pas/_/thub pa'i gzims mal bshams pa ste/_/bshams nas bstod cing kyi hud do/_/de nas gzims mal bstabs pa la/_/rkang gnyis rnams kyi mchog byon te/_/
[\[87b.4\]]yang yang sad pa med phyir dang /_/sdug bsngal thams cad zad phyir ro/_/de ni rtsib logs g.yas kyi ni/_/phyag la dbu ni rnam gzhag nas/_/zhabs la zhabs ni bkod nas su/_/slob mas mdun bdar gzims pa'o/_/de nas yud tsam de na der/_/bya rnams skad ni mi sgrogs te/_/
[\[87b.5\]]zags shing snyoms pa'i lus rnams kyis/_/bsam gtan la ni gzhol bzhin gnas/_/de tshe 'dab ma g.yo rnams las/_/rlung rnams kyis kyang ma g.yos pa'i/_/ljon shing rnams kyi mchi ma bzhin/_/mdog ngan me tog rnams gtor to/_/nub ri'i rtse la nyin mor byed/_/gnas la 
[\[87b.6\]]lam 'gro rnam pa bzhin/_/khri la thub pa lta bzhin du/_/don dam mthong rnams skyen par gyur/_/de nas mthar thug gzims mal la/_/thams cad mkhyen pa gzims pa yis/_/mchi mas rnam 'khrug kun dga' la/_/rjes su brtse bas bka' stsal to/_/bdag ni mya ngan 'da' ba'i 
[\[87b.7\]]dus/_/gyad rnams la smros kun dga' bo/_/mya ngan 'da' ba ma mthong na/_/phyis nas bdag la gdung bar 'gyur/_/de nas gsung ni rab bzung nas/_/mchi mas brgyal ba'i kun dga' bos/_/thub pa'i gzims mal mthar thug pa/_/gyad rnams la ni smras pa'o/_/de nas de'i tshe kun 
[\[88a\]]
[\[88a.1\]]dga' bo yi tshig thos nas/_/mig nas chu yi char ldan de rnams ngu bzhin du/_/seng ge'i 'jigs las sa 'dzin dag nas khyu mchog ltar/_/brtan min rnam par gnon rnams grong khyer nas 'thon to/_/zhen pa med pas gos rnams g.yengs shing rnam bkram 
[\[88a.2\]]ste/_/'gros kyis kun nas bskyod pas cod pan g.yo ba rnams/_/lha na gnas rnams bsod nams yongs su zad las bzhin/_/de na nyam thag 'ongs pa rnams ni nags der song /_/de ltar 'ongs pa rnams kyis thub pa mthong ba ste/_/mthong nas mchi ma'i gdod ldan rnams 
[\[88a.3\]]kyis phyag 'tshal zhing /_/phyag 'tshal nas ni yongs gdung sems rnams gnas pa ste/_/gnas pa de rnams la ni thub pas bka' stsal to/_/dga' ba'i dus su mya ngan byed par rigs min zhing /_/yid mi bde bas don med bstan pa bsnyen par gyis/_/bskal pa du ma rnams su 
[\[88a.4\]]bdag gi 'dod pa gang /_/shin tu rnyed dka'i don de bdag la nye bar gnas/_/sa dang chu dang me dang rlung dang mkha' med pa'i/_/dge zhing 'gyur med dbang po'i dbang don las 'das pa/_/zhi zhing dbrog bya ma yin mchog de gang thob nas/_/skye ba med cing 'pho ba med la mya ngan med/_/
[\[88a.5\]]ga ya rnams su byang chub tshe na bdag gis sngon/_/dge min srid pa'i rgyu rnams sbrul bzhin dor ba ste/_/sngon tshe bsags pa'i lus kyis rnam par gnas pa 'di/_/lus 'di nyid ni de ring nye bar 'gro bar 'gyur/_/sdug bsngal gyi ni gter chen phung po song ba na/_/'jigs pa chen po 
[\[88a.6\]]yang srid nye bar zhi ba na/_/gang tshe sdug bsngal chen po las ni byung ba ste/_/gang gis ngu ba bdag la mya ngan 'os ci yod/_/rab tu zhi ba'i dus yin na yang 'brug gi dbyangs/_/shAkya thub pa'i gsung ni de rnams kyis thos nas/_/de nas kha rnams bye zhing smra 'dod gyur pa ste/_/de dag 
[\[88a.7\]]rnams kyi tshig de gnas brtan gyis gsol to/_/gang gis ngu ba khyod kyi mya ngan 'os ci yod/_/me ni khang pa 'bar las rnam par 'thon pa ltar/_/de ltar thub pa de ni lha gtso rnams kyis kyang /_/nges par bsnyen bya yin na mi rnams kyis smros ci/_/khyab bdag skye bo gang 'di de bzhin 
[\[88b\]]
[\[88b.1\]]gshegs pa ni/_/mya ngan 'das 'di mthon min des na mya ngan byed/_/'brog dgon dag na song ba'i lam mkhan bzang po ni/_/rnam par nyams tshe kun nas 'khrul bar cis ma 'gyur/_/bla ma drang srong chen po thams cad gzigs pa ni/_/dngos su mthong nas gal te khyad par ma thob na/_/
[\[88b.2\]]gser gyi 'byung gnas dag nas dbul po song rnams bzhin/_/de tshe 'di na bzhad gad nyid du 'gro ba nyid/_/de ltar don ldan mang po de de smra ba rnams/_/bu rnams la bzhin gus ldan thal mo sbyar ba rnams/_/dge zhing zhi la dam pa'i don ni bsngags pa yis/_/gyad rnams dag la 
[\[88b.3\]]mchog bdag rnams kyi mchog gis gsungs/_/de ltar nges pa de ni kho bo mthong ba las/_/rnal 'byor cho gar 'bad rtsol med par grol min te/_/bdag gi chos 'di gang zhig rjes su rtog byed pa/_/sdug bsngal dra ba las de ma mthong na yang grol/_/ji ltar sman ni bsnyen par mi byed 
[\[88b.4\]]skyes bu yis/_/sman pa mthong ba las kyang nad ni 'joms min te/_/de bzhin bdag gis ye shes 'di ni ma bsgoms na/_/bdag ni mthong ba las kyang sdug bsngal 'joms ma yin/_/'di ni bdag ldan gang zhig bdag gi chos mthong ba/_/rgyang ring dag na gnas kyang des ni bdag 
[\[88b.5\]]mthong ste/_/gang zhig dge legs la ni gzhol zhing mi 'jug pa/_/bdag gi nye bar gnas kyang de ni rgyang ring nyid/_/de phyir brtson 'grus ma spangs bsdams pa'i sems rnams kyis/_/dge ba'i las rnams la ni bag dang ldan par gyis/_/sdug bsngal du ma nye bar 'dus shing g.yo ba 
[\[88b.6\]]ste/_/srog ni tho rangs mar me dang ni mtshungs pa nyid/_/de ltar thub pa'i skyes mchog gis bstan de rnams ni/_/mig nas chu ni rnam byung nyam thag yid rnams kyis/_/chu bo'i gzhung ni so sor sgrol bar byed pa bzhin/_/ku sha zhes bya'i grong la dal bus dbang med zhugs/_/sangs rgyas 
[\[88b.7\]]kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_mya ngan las 'da' bar gshegs pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa lnga pa'o//_//de nas bzang pos yang dag nyer ldan legs bzang ni/_/dbyu gu gsum 'dzin 'byung po'i tshogs su mi mchod pa/_/bde bar gshegs pa lta 'dod 
[\[89a\]]
[\[89a.1\]]dge slong thar 'dod pas/_/kun dga' byed pa'i kun dga' bo la smras pa'o/_/thub pa mya ngan 'da' ba'i dus ni bdag gis thos/_/gang gi ched du bdag gis lta 'dod skyes pa ste/_/'jig rten dag na mchog gi chos kyi rtogs pa po/_/zla ba gnam stong la bzhin mthong 
[\[89a.2\]]bar zla ba min/_/sdug bsngal mtha' dag rnams kyi tha mar gshegs bzhed pa/_/khyod kyi 'dren pa de ni lta bar 'dod pa ste/_/sprin gyis mngon par bkag pa'i mkha' la nyi ma bzhin/_/bdag gis ma mthong bar de nub par 'gro ma yin/_/de nas yongs su 'gro ba po ni 'ongs pa ste/_/smra 
[\[89a.3\]]bar 'dod cing chos 'dod pa ni des shes nas/_/ngal ba dang ni ldan pa'i sems ldan kun dga' bos/_/dus ma yin zhes mchi ma'i gdong gis smras pa'o/_/de ni zla ba'i 'od mnga' bsam pa mkhyen pa po/_/'dab ma rgyas pa'i spyan yangs rnam par mkhyen nas su/_/gang phyir kho bo 'jig 
[\[89a.4\]]rten phan phyir skyes pa ste/_/kun dga' bo kye gnyis skyes ma 'gogs zhes gsungs so/_/de las legs bzang mchog tu dga' zhing yid ches shing /_/dpal stug dge legs mdzod la mngon du song ba ste/_/de nas dus kyi rjes su mthun pas zhi ba yi/_/gsung ni mngon par gsungs 
[\[89a.5\]]nas slar yang de gsol to/_/kho bo lta [(]ba'i [,]bu'i [)]yongs su rtog pa po rnams las/_/thar pa'i lam gzhan lhag par khyed kyis lhag par thob ces grag_/de phyir bdag la gsung mdzod bdag la 'dzin 'dod yod/_/bdag gis lta 'dod rtse bar 'dod pa'i phyir ma yin/_/de nas gnyis 
[\[89a.6\]]skyes mngon par 'ongs pa de la ni/_/yan lag brgyad kyi lam ni sangs rgyas kyis gsungs shing /_/rab tu chud zos lam ni yang dag dran pa bzhin/_/thos nas gang ni gang gis yang dag rtogs/_/des ni sngon du mthong bar gyur pa'i lam gzhan la/_/des ni mthar thug dam pa nye bar ma thob 
[\[89a.7\]]ste/_/sngon du ma mthong lam ni lhag par thob nas su/_/snying la mun pa dang bcas lam de dor bar gyur/_/rdul dang lhan cig mun pa rab tu 'phel ba ste/_/de dag rnams kyi mi dge'i las ni sogs zhes grags/_/snying stobs dang bcas rdul ni rab tu 'phel ba ste/_/de dag rnams 
[\[89b\]]
[\[89b.1\]]kyi dge ba'i las ni rgyas zhes grags/_/thos dang blo dang 'bad pas snying stobs 'phel ba na/_/rdul dang mun pa nag ni rnam pa med pa'i phyir/_/las nyams pa las las ni zad par 'gro ba ste/_/de dag rnams kyi las de 'thad pa yin zhes grags/_/yid ni rmongs par byed 
[\[89b.2\]]pa rdul dang mun pa dag_/rang bzhin par ni 'jig rten dag na smra ba ste/_/de phyir de gnyis zad pa yod min brtan nyid phyir/_/rang bzhin par ni brtan pa nyid du rab tu grub/_/snying stobs sbyor las de dag bde bar gyur na yang /_/dus kyi dbang gis slar yang 'byung bar 'gyur ba 
[\[89b.3\]]ste/_/bsil ba'i chu ni tshan mo bzhin du rim gyis ni/_/dus kyi rim las rang bzhin du ni 'gro bar 'gyur/_/rtag pa'i bdag nyid snying stobs de ltar 'phel ba ru/_/thos pa blo dang 'bad pa rnams kyis mi nus te/_/de ma 'phel na de dag nyams pa yod min zhing /_/de dag ma 
[\[89b.4\]]nyams pa las zhi ba yod ma yin/_/sngon du des ni rang bzhin las ni skyes snyam ste/_/des kyang der ni thar pa mthong ba ma yin zhing /_/me yi 'bar ba'i 'od kyi nyams pa ji lta bar/_/rang bzhin la ni gnas na mthar thug gang las yin/_/sangs rgyas pa yi lam gyis de nyid mthong ba 
[\[89b.5\]]ste/_/srid pa la ni brten nas 'jig rten des rtogs shing /_/de yi rnam par nyams pas zhi ba rigs pa ste/_/rgyu ni rab tu nyams phyir 'bras bu nyams pa'o/_/gsal ba 'di yin rnam par 'gyur ba med pa'i bdag_/lus las gzhan ni de yis sngon du rtogs pa ste/_/thub pa'i 
[\[89b.6\]]gsung ni yongs su thob nas 'jig rten ni/_/bdag med pa dang bdag tu bya ba med par shes/_/chos ni du ma so sor brten nas skyes pa ste/_/rang nyid dbang med yongs su shes nas ni/_/des ni rab tu 'jug pa sdug bsngal nyid du dang /_/nges par ldog ste sdug bsngal nyid du kye ma mthong /_/
[\[89b.7\]]ji ltar 'jig rten dag ni skye ba rtogs nas su/_/des ni chad pa'i lta ba rnam par spangs pa ste/_/'jig rten dag gi nub par 'gro ba shes nas su/_/rtag pa'i lta ba brtan po skyen par spangs pa'o/_/drang srong chen po'i gsung ni thos nas bzung ba ste/_/de ltar 'di na des ni sngon gyi lta ba dang /_/
[\[90a\]]
[\[90a.1\]]de yis sngon du yongs su las ni byas pas te/_/gang gis skyen par dam pa'i chos la rjes su song /_/yid ni rab tu dang bas mchog dang ldan pa des/_/zhi zhing 'gyur med go 'phang de ni rab thob nas/_/thub pa gzims pa la ni bsngags pa byas pa ste/_/lta bzhin 'di 
[\[90a.2\]]las blo 'di rnams ni byas pa'o/_/mchod 'os dam pa mya ngan 'da' bar 'os pa yi/_/mgon po mthong nas bdag ni gnas par 'os ma yin/_/ston pa thugs rje ldan pa mya ngan 'da' ba yi/_/sngon du re zhig bdag ni mthar thug la 'gro'o/_/de nas des ni thub pa la ni phyag 'tshal nas/_/
[\[90a.3\]]sbrul bzhin g.yo ba med pa'i gdan ni bcings nas su/_/sprin rlung gis ni phra mo'i phra mor byed pa bzhin/_/de ma thag tu zhi ba mya ngan 'da' bar gshegs/_/de nas de phyir gdung gi 'du byed cho ga ni/_/cho ga mkhyen pa'i thub pas rnam par bka' stsal pa/_/mthar gyur de ni skye 
[\[90a.4\]]ba mthar mdzad drang srong che/_/rgya chen slob ma mnga' ba'i slob mar gyur pa'o/_/de nas nam gyi thun ni 'das pa na/_/zla bas skar ma rnams kyi 'od ni phrogs pa na/_/nags rnams nyal ba bzhin du sgra med gyur pa na/_/dge slong rnams la thugs rje chen po mnga' bas bstan/_/slob dpon 
[\[90a.5\]]bzhin du sgron ma bzhin du don bzhin du/_/bdag 'das tshe na so sor thar pa bstan bya ste/_/khyed kyi ston pa de yin de'i dbang gis 'gyur bya/_/kho bo gnas pa na yang de tsam nyid smra'o/_/lus dang ngag gi las ni rnam par dag 'dod pas/_/de bzhin 'jig rten tha snyad thams cad 'dor 
[\[90a.6\]]ba dang /_/sa dang srog chags sa bon rdzas la sogs rnams kyis/_/rab tu 'dzin pa las ni me bzhin ldog pa'o/_/sa la skye ba gcod dang 'byed pa dag las dang /_/sa yi logs la rko dang rmo ba dag las dang /_/gso ba dang ni skar rtsis mtshan nyid rnams las ni/_/nges par 
[\[90a.7\]]ldog pa bza' ba'i cho ga dus yin no/_/pho nya'i rigs pa sman dang sngags ni bsnyen rnams la/_/sbyor min nyid dang 'chab pa nyid ni med pa nyid/_/chos las ma bkag pa yi sgrub pa rnams la ni/_/tshod shes nyid dang tshim dang sogs pa ma yin no/_/rnam pa de ltar tshul khrims 
[\[90b\]]
[\[90b.1\]]yang dag bsdus pa 'di/_/thar pa'i rtsa ba zhes pa so sor thar pa ste/_/'di las ting nge 'dzin rnams rab tu skye ba ste/_/ye shes thams cad rnams dang mthar thug rnam 'da'o/_/de phyir ma ral ma nyams dbrog bya ma yin pa'i/_/gang gis tshul khrims gtsang ma yod pa de yi 
[\[90b.2\]]chos/_/de med gal te 'di yi thams cad med pa ste/_/tshul khrims yon tan dam pa rnams kyi rten yin no/_/tshul khrims ma nyams rnam par dag pa gnas pa na/_/dbang po spyod yul rnams la 'jug pa ma yin zhing /_/dbyug pas ba lang tshogs rnams lo thog la 
[\[90b.3\]]bzhin du/_/brtan las dbang po drug rnams bsgrib par bya ba'o/_/dbang po'i rta rnams yul rnams la grol gang zhig gzhan/_/len zhing de rnams las ni de tsam nyid ma yin/_/g.yo ba'i rta rnams kyis phrogs lam ngan la bzhin du/_/des ni de yi ched du don med thob par 
[\[90b.4\]]'gyur/_/dgra bo chen po rnams kyi dbang du song ba rnams/_/'di na kha cig sdug bsngal 'thob par 'gyur ba ste/_/rmongs pas yul rnams dag gi dbang du song nas ni/_/song nas deng 'dir dbang med rnams ni sdug bsngal 'os/_/de phyir dgra bo ma rungs dbang po rnams kyi 
[\[90b.5\]]bzhin/_/dbang po rnams kyi 'gro ba 'gro bya ma yin zhing /_/dbang po rnams kyi dga' bo 'jig rten na byas nas/_/dbang po rnams kyi gshed ma 'jig rten na mthong ngo /_/rang gi g.yo ba'i sems las 'jigs pa ji lta bar/_/stag rnams las dang sbrul dang 'bar ba'i me 
[\[90b.6\]]las dang /_/dgra bo las ni de ltar 'jigs par mi bya ste/_/gang gis sbrang rtsi dang ni yang dag rtog pa med/_/lcags kyu med pa'i gnyis 'thung myos pa lta bu dang /_/yal ga'i [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]shing la rab tu ma zhi ltar/_/sems ni ji ltar 'dod bzhin phyogs su skor ba ste/_/g.yo zhing 
[\[90b.7\]]bskyod pa de la go skabs sbyin mi bya/_/de ni rang dbang yin na zhi ba yod min zhing /_/de yi gnas pa yin na byed pa byas pa nyid/_/de phyir sems 'di ji ltar rnam par mi g.yo ba/_/de ltar rab tu 'bad pa yis ni gyis mdzod cig_/bza' ba tsam ni sman bzhin nges par bsten 
[\[91a\]]
[\[91a.1\]]bya ste/_/'dir ni khong khro bya ba ma yin chags pa min/_/ji srid bkres pa nyid ni ldog pa'i ched du dang /_/lus kyi 'khrul 'khor gyis 'dzin pa yi ched du'o/_/ji ltar nags su bung ba rnams kyis me tog ro/_/'thung ste me tog rnams ni nyams par byed 
[\[91a.2\]]ma yin/_/de bzhin dus kyi dbang las bsod snyoms rgyu ba ste/_/pha rol dad pa rnams ni nyams par byed ma yin/_/drang pos rtag par khur bar byed ces khur bya dang /_/skye bo sbyin pa po yang yod pa ma yin te/_/'di na sbyor ba min las khur ba ltung ba ste/_/ji ltar khur bu de 
[\[91a.3\]]ltar sbyin pa po yin no/_/rnal 'byor rim las nyin mo mtha' dag phul nas dang /_/mtshan mo'i dang po'i thun dang cig shos dag kyang ste/_/dran pa dang bcas snga ma'i cig shos la nyol la/_/gnyid kyi tshe ni don med byed pa ma yin no/_/dus kyi me yis 'di na 'gro ba sreg pa na/_/
[\[91a.4\]]mtshan mo mtha' dag la ni gnyid par 'os pa su/_/nyon mongs dgra bo dang mtshungs 'joms pa po rnams ni/_/snying la gnas pa yin na su yi gnyid log 'gyur/_/de phyir khyim na nag po'i sbrul rnams nyal ba ltar/_/yid la nyes skyon sbrul rnams rab tu nyal ba rnams/_/rigs pa dang ni 
[\[91a.5\]]rkang pa sman gyis ston nas su/_/gnyid log par 'os 'di las gzhan du ngo tsha'o/_/ngo tsha rgyan nyid dang ni mchog gi gos yin zhing /_/ngo tsha lam las 'khyog pa rnams kyi lcags kyus te/_/ngo tsha ldan pas de ltar gyur na 'jug bya zhing /_/ngo tshas rnam par spangs pa'i yon tan 
[\[91a.6\]]rnams kyis spangs/_/ji srid pa la skyes bu ngo tsha dang bcas pa/_/de srid de yi mi yi ngo tsha nyid yin te/_/don dang don min rnams la rnam dpyod med pa ni/_/ngo tshas rnam par dman pa phyugs dang mnyam pa'o/_/gang yang yan lag nying lag rnams ni ral gris gcod/_/der yang 
[\[91a.7\]]yid kyis nyes pa bya ba ma yin zhing /_/zhi ba ma yin dag ni brjod par mi bya ste/_/bdag gi de yis khyed cag rnams kyi bar chod yin/_/bzod pa dang mnyam dka' thub yod pa ma yin zhing /_/gang gi bzod pa de yis stobs dang brtan pa ste/_/gang rnams pha rol rnams las 
[\[91b\]]
[\[91b.1\]]rtsub mo mi bzod pa/_/de rnams chos byed rnams kyi lugs min grol ba min/_/chos kyi 'byed pa po dang grags pa'i 'joms pa po/_/gzugs kyi dgra bo dang ni snying gi me yin te/_/khro ba de yis go skabs rab 'byam mi sbyin zhing /_/yon tan rnams kyi dgra bo de 'dra gzhan ma yin/_/
[\[91b.2\]]yongs su bsil ba'i chu las glog gi me bzhin du/_/ji ltar rab byung las ni khro ba 'gal ba ste/_/de ltar khyim pa rnams kyi skyes pa mi 'gal te/_/chags ldan de rnams de la dam bca' ma yin no/_/mdzes pa'i skra ni rgyang ring mgo la reg nas dang /_/rnam par 'gyur ba'i gos dang 
[\[91b.3\]]lhung bzed mthong nas dang /_/gzhan las mthar gyur spyod pa rnam par bsams nas su/_/snying la skyes pa'i nga rgyal rab tu spang bar bya/_/khyim la brten pa khengs pa ldan pa rnams kyi yang /_/nga rgyal rnam par 'phel ba 'joms pa'i phyir yin na/_/slong mo za bas bdag nyid 
[\[91b.4\]]yongs su rtog pa po/_/thar pa la dmigs skra bzhar rnams kyi sngon nyid do/_/ngan g.yo dang ni chos kyi mtshams sbyor yod min te/_/gang phyir 'di las 'khyog po nyid ni bsnyen mi bya/_/ngan g.yo nyid dang sgyu ni slu ba'i don du ste/_/chos kyi bdag nyid rnams kyi bslu bya yod ma yin/_/
[\[91b.5\]]gang zhig rgya chen rnams las sdug bsngal 'ongs pa ste/_/nyung ngu 'dod la 'ong ba ma yin de dang bral/_/de yi phyir yang nyung ngu 'dod nyid bsnyen bya ste/_/gang zhig yon tan rnams kyis rdzogs byed kye ma ci/_/de ni bkren pa'i lta ba rnams la mi 'jigs shing /_/dbang po 
[\[91b.6\]]rnams las gang zhig cung zad 'jigs min la/_/gang zhig thos nas med ces mya ngan med pa ste/_/gang gis nyung ngu 'dod de de yi thar pa min/_/gal te grol bar 'dod na tshim pa bsnyen mdzod dang /_/tshim pa na ni 'dir bde de 'di chos yin zhing /_/tshim pa rnams ni sar yang bde bar 
[\[91b.7\]]gnyid log ste/_/tshim med rnams ni mtho ris na yang rnam par sreg_/phyug kyang tshim pa med pa rtag tu dbul po ste/_/dbul yang tshim pa dang ldan rtag tu phyug po nyid/_/tshim pa med pa sdug pa'i yul rnams dang 'bye zhing /_/tshim pas 'gro ba ngal ba ldan la mya ngan byed/_/zhi 
[\[92a\]]
[\[92a.1\]]ba'i bde ba dam pa thob par 'dod rnams kyis/_/de ltar tshogs la dga' ba rnams su 'gyur mi bya/_/zhi ba'i bdag nyid gcig tu rgyu phyir 'jig rten na/_/brgya byin la sogs lha rnams kyis kyang re 'dod byed/_/chags po sdug bsngal gyi ni gnas pa'i shing yin 
[\[92a.2\]]te/_/rang gi skye bo la 'am skye la chags pa spangs/_/chags pa rnam rgyas sdug bsngal la ni 'jig rten na/_/gnyis 'thung 'khogs pa 'dam la bzhin du chags par 'gyur/_/rtag par rab zhugs chu yi rgyun ni mnyen na yang /_/rdo yi logs la dus su rnam par 'bigs pa ste/_/brtson 
[\[92a.3\]]'grus kyis ni rnyed par dka' ba ci yang med/_/de phyir khur rnams mi 'bor bar ni bzod bgyis dang /_/gang zhig yang yang ngal bso nas ni srub byed pa/_/de yi shing la me ni rnyed par sla min zhing /_/brtson 'grus brtsams pas rnyed par sla bar 'gyur ba ste/_/de phyir sbyor ba yod na bya ba 
[\[92a.4\]]'grub pa nyid/_/dran pa yod na skyon rnams 'jug pa ma yin te/_/dran pa dang mnyam mdza' bo med cing bsrung ba med/_/dran pa nyams las nges par thams cad nyams pa ste/_/de phyir lus la song ba'i dran pa spang mi bya/_/ji ltar dpa' bo rnams ni go cha bzung nas su/_/dgra bo'i 
[\[92a.5\]]sde la 'jigs pa med rnams 'jug pa ste/_/de bzhin lus la dran pa'i go cha bgos nas su/_/brtan pa rnams ni yul gyi g.yul rnams la spyod do/_/mnyam par gzhag pa'i blo dang sems ni yongs bsdams shing /_/'jig rten dag gi skye dang 'god pa shes pa ste/_/de phyir ting nge 
[\[92a.6\]]'dzin la yongs su 'jug bya ste/_/ting 'dzin rab tu thob pa'i sems nad yod ma yin/_/rnam par 'thor ba'i chu ni rnam par 'dzin don du/_/ji ltar 'bad pas dong dang 'obs ni byed pa ste/_/ye shes chu ni gnas pa'i don du ting nge 'dzin/_/de ltar dong dang 'obs dang mtshungs par rab 
[\[92a.7\]]tu bstan/_/shes rab ldan pa yongs su 'dzin pa btang ba yi/_/chos 'di nang la mchog tu gzhol ba dag gis te/_/de ltar gnas pa'i grol ba yod cing 'di yi ni/_/phyin ci log ni khyim pa ma yin dge slong min/_/shes rab dga' dang 'chi ba'i mtsho chen dag la gru/_/rmongs 
[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]pa'i mun pa rnams la ji ltar sgron ma ste/_/nad rnams thams cad 'joms pa dag gi sman yin zhing /_/nyon mongs ljon shing 'joms byed mtshon cha rnon po yin/_/de phyir thos pa bsam pa bsgom pa rnams kyi ni/_/shes rab rnam par 'phel la rab tu 'jug par bya/_/gang gi shes 
[\[92b.2\]]rab rang bzhin lta byed mig yod pa/_/de ni mig dang bral yang mig dang ldan pa nyid/_/sems la rgyu ba'i spros pa de dang de rnams kyis/_/khyim na gnas pa spangs nas kyang ni grol min nyid/_/mchog tu zhi ba thob par 'dod pa rnams kyis 'di/_/shes nas spros pa med pa rnams su 
[\[92b.3\]]'gyur bar bya/_/de phyir bag yod bla ma bzhin du bsnyen mdzod la/_/bag med dgra bo bzhin du rnam par spongs mdzod dang /_/bag yod pa las brgya byin gyi na dpal thob cing /_/bag med pa las lha min khengs rnams rnam par nyams/_/phan pa dang ldan rjes su brtse ba mnga' ba yi/_/
[\[92b.4\]]ston pa thugs rje mnga' bas mdzad bya gang yin pa/_/de ni bdag gis byas shing dang ni rab bsdams pa/_/khyed cag rnams kyi sems ni zhi bas byas pa'o/_/ci ste ri rnams dang ni khang pa stong pa dang /_/dgon pa de rnams gang dang der ni yod pa na/_/rab tu sbyor la rtag tu 'bad pa bya ba ste/_/
[\[92b.5\]]phyi nas yongs su gdung ba ldan par ma gyur cig_/sman pa rang bzhin yang dag legs par shes nas ni/_/nad pa rnams la dam pa'i sman ni brjod bya ste/_/de yi dus kyis sbyar bar nges par bsten pa la/_/nad pa gang de bdag po yin te sman pa min/_/dpal ldan drang zhing mnyam la 'jigs 
[\[92b.6\]]pa med pa yi/_/lam ni lam mkhan gyis ni rnam par bstan pa na/_/thos nas gang zhig mi 'gro de yi nyams pa ste/_/bstan pas 'dren pa po ni bu lon yod ma yin/_/sdug bsngal la sogs bden pa bzhi po kho bo yis/_/bstan pa rnams la 'di ni gang gi 'dod pa yod/_/de ni myur zhing 
[\[92b.7\]]spobs par bdag la smros shig dang /_/the tshom shin tu rnam par dpyad bya bcad par bgyi/_/de ltar mthon por gsungs pa'i drang srong chen po la/_/the tshom med pa de rnams ci yang ma gsol to/_/sems kyis de dag rnams kyi sems kyi gting dpags nas/_/byed ldan ma 'gags pas 
[\[93a\]]
[\[93a.1\]]kyang tshig 'di smras pa'o/_/rlung ni g.yo ba ma yin nyi ma bsil ba dang /_/ri bong mtshan ma can ni dro ba nyid na yang /_/'jig rten dag na rkang pa bzhi po de rnams ni/_/bden pa min par byed par nus pa ma yin no/_/gang zhig sdug bsngal brjod pa de ni bde ba min/_/
[\[93a.2\]]'di yi rgyu gang rab tu skye bo de gzhan min/_/rgyu ni 'gags pa las ni nges par thar pa ste/_/gang zhig 'di yi lam ni thabs ma yin pa min/_/de phyir bdag nyid chen po bden pa bzhi rnams la/_/dge slong skye bo des 'di gtam ci yod ma yin/_/ston pa mya ngan 'da' ba rnyed par 'gyur 
[\[93a.3\]]zhes te/_/gang zhig don ni rdzogs pa ma yin sdug bsngal te/_/gang zhig tshogs pa 'di ni brtul zhugs gzhon nyid las/_/sngon du don ni mthong bar gyur pa ma yin pa/_/de yang khyed kyi gsung 'dis da ni de ma thag_/glog gi 'od kyis lam bzhin yang dag mthong bar gyur/_/gang 
[\[93a.4\]]gi don gyi lhag ma yod pa ma yin zhing /_/gang yang srid pa'i mtsho yi pha rol phyin pa ste/_/mgon po shin tu skyen par nges par gshegs thos te/_/de dag gi yang snying la bsams par gyur pa nyid/_/de ltar 'phags pa ma 'gags pa yi tshig de yis/_/don ni rnam par shes kyang 
[\[93a.5\]]shes nas slar yang ni/_/gus ldan rnams kyi blo gros brtan po mdzad bzhed pa/_/byams pas slar yang sangs rgyas kyis ni bka' stsal to/_/gang phyir skal bar gnas kyang de ni nyams pa ste/_/phan tshun du ni 'du ba nges par yod ma yin/_/bdag nyid kyi dang gzhan gyi don ni byas nas 
[\[93a.6\]]ni/_/kho bo gnas pa la ni dgos pa yod ma yin/_/gang rnams mkhar dang sa na bdag gi gdul bya rnams/_/de rnams thar par byas shing rgyun la bcug pa ste/_/'di las gzhan du slob ma rim dang rim rnams kyis/_/nga yi chos 'di skye dgu rnams la gnas par 'gyur/_/de 
[\[93a.7\]]phyir 'gro ba'i rang bzhin rnam par shes byas nas/_/bsam mno ma byed nges par rnam par 'bral ba ste/_/rnam pa de ltar 'gro ba shes nas su/_/ji ltar 'di ltar ma yin de ltar gyis mdzod cig_/ye shes sgron mas mun pa rnam par bsal byas nas/_/srid pa rnams la snying po 
[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]med par mthong nas su/_/ji ltar nad ni nye bar zhi ba de ltar ni/_/tshe ni rnam par 'gags na tshim pa nyid yin no/_/dor bar bya zhing rtsod ldan lus zhes bya ba ni/_/srid pa'i mtsho yi rgyun ni rnam par 'gags pa na/_/don med byed pa'i dgra bo'i rnam par nyams las bzhin/_/tshe ni nye bar 
[\[93b.2\]]zhi bas tshim pa ma yin su/_/rgyu dang mi rgyu thams cad rnam par 'god pa ste/_/de phyir khyed cag rnams ni legs par bag yod gyis/_/mya ngan 'da' ba'i dus ni yongs su gyur pa ste/_/khyed cag ma smra 'di ni bdag gi phyi ma'i tshig_/de nas bsam gtan rig pa rnams kyi mchog 
[\[93b.3\]]gis ni/_/yud tsam de la bsam gtan dang po thob mdzad nas/_/de las rnam par bzhengs nas gnyis par gshegs pa ste/_/rim gyis ma tshang med par thams cad la gshegs so/_/de nas rjes su rim pas rab tu byed pa dgu/_/snyoms par 'jug pa'i tshogs ni mtha' dag 
[\[93b.4\]]bsam gtan mdzad/_/de nas drang srong chen pos slar yang rim gyis ni/_/bsam gtan dang po de nyid la ni bab pa'o/_/de las rnam par bzhengs nas kyang ni rim gyis ni/_/bsam gtan bzhi pa la ni slar yang 'dzegs pa ste/_/bsam gtan bzhi pa'i cho ga las de rnams 
[\[93b.5\]]bzhengs shing /_/mngon sum nyid du nye bar zhi ba la gshegs so/_/de nas thub pa mya ngan 'da' bar gshegs pa na/_/char gyis snun pa'i ba lang bzhin du sa g.yos shing /_/phyogs kyi glang po rnams kyis sbyangs pa bzhin du ni/_/nam mkha' las kyang sgron ma rnams ni lhung bar gyur/_/
[\[93b.6\]]lha yi nags tshal sna tshogs shing rta sreg 'dod pa'i/_/nags kyi me ni bar snang dag la byung ba bzhin/_/bud shing med cing rlung med du ba med pa'i me/_/phyogs ni sreg par byed pa byung bar gyur pa nyid/_/lha min 'joms par 'dod pa g.yul du 'jug pa na/_/khro ba brgya bas khro ba 
[\[93b.7\]]rnam par gtong ba bzhin/_/de bzhin me stag brgya pa'i me ni skyug pa yi/_/dbang po'i rdo rje mi bzad pa rnams lhung bar gyur/_/'jig pa'i rlung rnams dag gis btags pa rnams kyi bzhin/_/'dzin pa 'dzin pa rnams kyi rtse mo rnams lhung zhing /_/'khri shing shing rnams dag ni 
[\[94a\]]
[\[94a.1\]]zug rngu byed pa yi/_/phyag dar khur ba'i rlung ni drag tu g.yos par gyur/_/'dam gyi chus bsgos sdong du gzhon nu rnams kyis ni/_/lus ni yongs su dkris pa'i ngang pa'i rgyal po bzhin/_/cung zhig 'od zer kha dog ngan pa de rnams kyis/_/'od ni bcom ste zla 
[\[94a.2\]]ba mngon par gsal bar gyur/_/sprin dang bral yang mkha' la zla ba gnas na yang /_/mun pa mi bzad rnams kyis phyogs rnams rgyas pa ste/_/yud tsam de la mya ngan gyis ni zil non bzhin/_/chu bo rnams ni de ma thag tu chu dron 'bab/_/de nas nye sar skyes pa'i sha la'i shing dag gis/_/
[\[94a.3\]]rang gis me tog mdzes pa 'jug pa dus min la/_/rnam par btud nas drang srong chen po'i sku lus ni/_/gser gyi stegs bu'i gzugs brnyan la ni yang dag gtor/_/mkha' la mgo bo lnga rnams mi g.yo'i klu rnams ni/_/mya ngan gyis slar so sor dmar ba'i mig can rnams/_/rab 
[\[94a.4\]]tu gnyid log gdengs ka stod pa'i lus rnams kyis/_/thub pa la ni gus pas lta bzhin gnas par gyur/_/nang du song ba'i kun nas gdung ba rnams/_/de rnams shin tu dron po'i dbugs ni brgyus pa ste/_/'gro ba'i rang bzhin mi rtag pa ni rtogs nas su/_/mya ngan 'ongs pa min rnams 
[\[94a.5\]]mya ngan smad par gyur/_/nges par legs pa'i chos kyis cho gar rab zhugs pa/_/rgyal po rnam thos sras kyi 'dus pa dam pa de/_/chos la mngon par chags pas mya ngan ma byas shing /_/lha yi rten la mchi ma phyung bar ma gyur to/_/gtsang mar lhag par gnas pa'i byed ldan lha rnams 
[\[94a.6\]]kyang /_/drang srong chen po'i yang dag shin tu gus ldan rnams/_/rang gnas gyur zhing rnam par 'gyur ba ma song ste/_/'jig rten dag ni rang bzhin rnam par smad pa'o/_/dri za'i rgyal po rnams dang de nas klu yi rgyal po rnams/_/gnod sbyin rnams dang dam chos la dga' lha rnams ni/_/mya 
[\[94a.7\]]ngan byed cing mya ngan mchog la gzhol rnams kyis/_/rigs chen po yi nyam thag bzhin du kha la gyur/_/yid la re ba thob pa'i bdud kyi sde rnams ni/_/rab tu dga' bas skad ni 'thon zhing dgod pa ste/_/'phyo zhing glu dbyangs gar byas dga' bar gyur pa rnams/_/rnga bo che dang rdza rnga pa Ta 
[\[94b\]]
[\[94b.1\]]ha rnams brdungs/_/de nas rdo rje rtse mo bcom pa'i ri bo bzhin/_/chang dang bral ba'i gnyis 'thung dga' ba med pa bzhin/_/nog gis rnam par dman pa'i dbyibs can khyu mchog bzhin/_/thub pa'i khyu mchog nub pa na ni 'jig rten gyur/_/nyin mor byed pas rab tu dman pa'i nam mkha' 
[\[94b.2\]]bzhin/_/ba mos bsregs pa'i pad+ma dang ldan rdzing bu bzhin/_/nor dang bral zhing bya ba med pa'i rigs bzhin du/_/srid pa nyams mdzad rab tu nyams las 'gro bar gyur/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba chen po'i le'u ste nyi 
[\[94b.3\]]shu rtsa drug pa'o//_//de nas ngang pa stong dang ldan pa'i lha yi ni/_/gzhal med khang nas gdong ni cung zhig rab btud nas/_/yud tsam thams cad mkhyen pa la ni bltas nas su/_/lha na gnas pa chen po gzhan zhig gis smras pa'o/_/kye ma 'gro na dngos po thams cad 
[\[94b.4\]]mi rtag cing /_/skye ba'i chos can dang ni 'dzad pa'i chos can la/_/skye ba rnams ni sdug bsngal skyed par byed pa ste/_/de dag rnams ni lhag med zhi bas zhi ba nyid/_/skye ba'i bud shing tshad ma min pa bsregs mdzad nas/_/ye shes me lce grags pas rnam khyab du ba can/_/chu yis 
[\[94b.5\]]me bzhin du ni dus kyi chu yis ni/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i me de mya ngan 'da' bar bya/_/de nas mchog gi drang srong dang mtshungs drang srong gzhan/_/mtho ris gnas kyang 'bras bu rnams la ma chags pas/_/rab tu zhi ba'i dgra bcom drang srong mthong nas su/_/ri dbang brtan pas tshig 
[\[94b.6\]]ni rnam par smras pa'o/_/'jig rten dag na 'ga' zhig nyams par mi 'gro med/_/de ltar 'gro ba med cing song ba med pa ste/_/mchog dang mchog min mkhyen pa mchog gi don rig pa/_/ston pa mtshungs med gang du rab tu zhi bar gyur/_/shes rab rnam par dag cing mchog gi spyan mnga' 
[\[94b.7\]]ba/_/yongs su 'dren pa po nyid 'di yis dman pa ni/_/rmongs pa nges par mig long gson pa'i 'jig rten ni/_/'du shes nyams shing lam ngan la ni gnas par 'gyur/_/de nas 'gro ba'i 'gal ba ma yin ma 'gags pa/_/rjes su chags pa nyams shing skye ba 'gags byas 
[\[95a\]]
[\[95a.1\]]pas/_/'jig rten snang bas dman pa mthong nas su/_/thub ba zhi ba na ni zhi ba'i yid kyis smras/_/shes rab ldan pa 'du byed cho ga la gnas pa/_/dus 'dir nges par blo gtad bya ba ma yin zhing /_/mi rtag nyid kyi rdo rje nges par btung ba yis/_/gang du thub 
[\[95a.2\]]ba ri bo chen po bcom par 'gyur/_/snying po med cing nga med rnam par nyams pa'i chos/_/'jig rten gson pa'i 'jig rten kye ma smod par shog_/gang du thub dka' drang srong chen po seng ge yang /_/nyes pa'i gnyis 'thung rnams ni nyams par mdzad nas nyams/_/'dod chags bral ba 
[\[95a.3\]]min pa'i 'gro ba 'jug pa la/_/da ni 'jigs pa chen po ma yin su yi lags/_/thun mong las ni gang du de bzhin gshegs pa yang /_/gser gyi ka ba lta bu kun nas chag par gyur/_/sa bon drug dang myu gu gcig dang lo ma gcig_/rtsa ba drug dang 'bras bu lnga dang yal ga gnyis/_/
[\[95a.4\]]phung po gsum dang bdag nyid gcig la nyon mongs kyi/_/sdong po 'di ni bcag nas thub pa gnyis 'thung gzims/_/'gro rgyal mda' bzhin dgra bo rnams ni bcom nas su/_/chu med pa na rma bya bzhin du chags bral zhing /_/mgyogs 'gro rta bzhin du ni rnam rgyal lam song ba/_/me bzhin 
[\[95a.5\]]thub pa skye ba med cing zhi bar gyur/_/mtho ris dbang phyug rdo rje 'dzin pa mig 'phel na/_/rab tu tshim byed rgyun bzhin gsung rnams btang nas su/_/'od kyis nyam thag ba lang bzhin sar rnam brgyus nas/_/grags pas phyogs ni mngon par bkang nas bla ma gzims/_/snyan grags 
[\[95a.6\]]ldan pa nyid dang 'od zer ldan pa ste/_/chu bo sin d+hu bzhin du gser skyug shugs ldan pa/_/rnam thos bu yi rdzu 'phrul mi skyong sder bcas pa/_/lam la bcug nas mi yi nyi ma nub par gyur/_/rmug pa'i do shal gyi ni phyogs rnams dkris pa bzhin/_/rgya chen sprin rnams kyis dkris nyi ma'i 'od rnams 
[\[95a.7\]]bzhin/_/mchod sbyin rdzogs tshe mar gyis sman pa'i me bzhin du/_/thub pa zhi tshe de ring 'jig rten gsal ma gyur/_/mdud pa med pa bden pa'i lam ni bsnyen nas su/_/mdud pa med pa zhi ba'i chos 'di brnyes nas ni/_/rdzu 'phrul gyis ni sku lus 'dren par nus na yang /_/sdug bsngal 
[\[95b\]]
[\[95b.1\]]dag gi gter ni lus yin zhes spangs so/_/mun pa mun pa nyi mas bzhin du zil mnan nas/_/rdul ni rdul ni char pa bzhin du mngon bcom nas/_/slar yang slar yang sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khor mi len te/_/thub pa thub pa skar rgyal bzhin du mngon par gshegs/_/gang zhig skye ba'i 
[\[95b.2\]]sdug bsngal 'joms phyir skyes pa ste/_/gang la 'jig rten pas ni zhi ba'i ched du bsnyen/_/gang zhig 'od zer mchog gis rnam par gsal ba dang /_/khyad par 'phags pa'i blo yis rnam par gsal bar gyur/_/gang gis skye dgu rnams ni dge legs la bcug ste/_/yon tan rgya chen rnams kyis sa 
[\[95b.3\]]la rgyas pa nyid/_/'dod pa'i snyan grags gsal ba rnams kyis 'phel ba ste/_/khyim na gnas pa yin kyang grags pas 'phel ba nyid/_/gang gis mkhyen zhing smod pas 'o dod ma bos shing /_/mi ni nyam thag rnams la brtse bas bos pa ste/_/bza' ba ngan pa rab tu 'joms shing ma gsol la/_/bza' 
[\[95b.4\]]ba legs pa rjes su 'gro zhing ma gsol to/_/mi gnas dbang po zhi ba gang zhig yul rnams la/_/stobs rab dbang po nyid las yang dag mi gnas shing /_/gzhan rnams kyis ma thob pa'i lam [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]thob pa ste/_/ro mkhyen pas ni nges par 'byung ba'i ro gsol to/_/
[\[95b.5\]]gang gis sngar ma byin pa'i sbyin pa byin pa ste/_/'bras bu 'dod pas cung zad zhig ni mi byin la/_/mnyam nyid rjes mthun phun sum tshogs pa dor ba ste/_/dam pa rnams kyi yid rnams yon tan rnams kyis phrogs/_/brtan pa rnams kyi g.yo ba'i spyan ni bsrungs pa ste/_/
[\[95b.6\]]brtan par gshegs shing sems ni bsrung du bcug gyur la/_/dge legs rnams ni bsrungs shing 'phel bar mdzad pa ste/_/chos skyes pa ni cung zhig 'dod par ma mdzad do/_/brtan pas mi dge ba las mi dge rnam spangs nas/_/dge legs kyi ni dgra bo skyon rnams spangs pa 
[\[95b.7\]]ste/_/gang zhig blo yis nyes pa rnams ni kun spangs la/_/de ni 'phags min mi rtag nyid kyis nub par gyur/_/gang la chos ni 'dod pa yang dag rjes song zhing /_/dga' bas mchog tu nges pa rnam par bzung ba ste/_/me yis nor ni zos pa ji lta ba bzhin du/_/khyab bdag ye 
[\[96a\]]
[\[96a.1\]]shes nor can de yang srog song ngo /_/brgyad po rnams la mchog tu sde [(]tshen [,]tshan [)]lnga thul zhing /_/gsum gzigs spyod pa gsum ni zad byed spyan gsum pa/_/gcig ni bsrungs nas gcig ni thob nas gcig rtogs nas/_/lci ba bdun rnams spangs nas 
[\[96a.2\]]bla ma gzims par gyur/_/gang gi zhi ba'i ched du lam ni gsal mdzad de/_/legs pa'i skye bo zhi bas rab tu dang mdzad cing /_/nyon mongs ljon shing rab tu chags pa bcad pa ste/_/srid pa rnams las gus pa ldan pa rnams bsgral to/_/gsung gi bdud rtsis 'jig rten shin tu tshim 
[\[96a.3\]]mdzad cing /_/bzod pa yis ni khro ba yongs su bzung ba ste/_/slob ma'i sde ni dge legs la ni rol mdzad la/_/dge legs don du gnyer rnams phra mo rtogs su bcug_/legs pa rnams la chos kyi sa bon skyed mdzad cing /_/rgyu yi bdag nyid 'phags pa'i lam ni thob mdzad la/_/
[\[96a.4\]]'jig rten 'das pas 'phags pa min pa ma brten zhing /_/chos las ma gtogs gzhan la sbyar ba ma yin no/_/kA shi gnas su chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba ste/_/blo dang ldan pa nyid kyis 'jig rten tshim mdzad cing /_/gdul bya rnams ni chos kyi cho gar spyad mdzad la/_/bdag cag 
[\[96a.5\]]rnams la phan pa'i ched du dge bar mdzad/_/gzhan rnams kyis ma mthong ba'i de nyid mthong mdzad cing /_/yon tan rnams kyis chos byed rnams la sbyar ba ste/_/gzhan gyi lugs rnams tshar bcad nas ni skul gyur la/_/rtogs par dka' ba'i don ni rnam par shes par mdzad/_/
[\[96a.6\]]thams cad mi rtag pa dang bdag med par gsungs shing /_/srid pa rnams su bde ba cung zad ma bsrungs la/_/snyan pa'i ba dan gyen du rnam par bsgrengs pa ste/_/nga rgyal ka ba mthon po rnam par bsgyel ba mdzad/_/smad kyang rang gi thugs ni bskyod pa ma yin zhing /_/thams cad 
[\[96a.7\]]la ni 'jug pa rnams la sred ma mdzad/_/rang nyid rgal zhing bying ba bsgral bar mdzad pa ste/_/zhi zhing pha rol 'khrul pa zhi bar mdzad gyur la/_/grol zhing pha rol bcings pa grol bar mdzad pa ste/_/rtogs shing pha rol rmongs pas rtogs par mdzad pa'o/_/thub pa thub pas tshul dang 
[\[96b\]]
[\[96b.1\]]tshul min mkhyen 'di des/_/sems can rnams ni yang dag gtam gyis rjes bzung nas/_/tshul min la dga' tshul min 'byung po rnams la ni/_/bskal pa 'jig pa'i tshe na chos bzhin rab tu gzims/_/chu ldan sprin bzhin sa 'dzin ri bo nags tshal bzhin/_/'od ldan rgan po 
[\[96b.2\]]bzhin du 'od ldan gzhon nu bzhin/_/'jig rten dag gi lta byed bcom nas 'jig rten gyi/_/lta byed nye bar phrogs nas 'gros su 'di gshegs so/_/dbang phyug dam pa gang la ltung bas me reg cing /_/gang gis mchog tu zhi ba'i lam ni brnyes pa ste/_/zhi ba brnyes pa de ni gus 
[\[96b.3\]]pas mthong nas su/_/de ring 'jig rten gnyen 'dun med cing rgud pa 'dra/_/'joms par 'dod cing stobs ni rab zhugs bdud kyang ni/_/sde dang bcas pas gang ni thub par ma nus pa/_/'joms par 'dod cing stobs zhugs bdud kyis de ring ni/_/de ni dus kyi sbyor las 'joms 
[\[96b.4\]]par nus par gyur/_/'byung po rnams ni thams cad rnams ni 'dus pa rnams/_/sdug bsngal rnams kyis zil non lha dang bcas pa rnams/_/'khor ba'i 'jigs pa gang rnams zad pa ma yin zhing /_/de ltar mchog tu mya ngan 'das pa thob ma yin/_/skye dgu rnams kyi snang mdzad 
[\[96b.5\]]gang gis 'jig rten ni/_/nye bar skye ba me long bzhin du gzigs pa ste/_/rgyang ring du'am nye bar mtho ris su'am mthar/_/lha yi rna bas sgra ni rnam par thos pa'o/_/bar snang dag la skar ma'i khang par 'dzegs pa ste/_/sa la rab tu zhugs kyang theg pa ma yin zhing /_/chu la rab 
[\[96b.6\]]tu gshegs kyang nub pa ma yin la/_/sku yis rnam par 'gyur ba mang po rnams mdzad do/_/de rnams de rnams gnas rnams lam 'gros lam na bzhin/_/gang gis skye ba du ma rnams kyang dran pa ste/_/yid kyis pha rol rnams kyi yid kyi spyod pa rnams/_/dbang po las 'das sna 
[\[96b.7\]]tshogs rnams kyang mkhyen ba'o/_/thams cad mnyam par rab zhugs thams cad mkhyen pa des/_/zag pa thams cad bcad cing bya ba thams cad mdzad/_/ye shes kyis ni nyon mongs thams cad rnam spangs nas/_/ye shes de nyid kun nas brnyes nas gzims par gyur/_/skyes bu 
[\[97a\]]
[\[97a.1\]]rnams kyi rno ba'i sems rnams btul ba ste/_/rno ba ma yin blo rnams rim gyis drag par mdzad/_/rigs las chos kyi nyes pa spang bar mdzad pa ste/_/'chi med ched du chos ni su yis ston par 'gyur/_/rten dang bral ba'i 'jig rten nyam thag pa la 
[\[97a.2\]]ni/_/zhi ba'i ched du chos kyi 'thor ma su yis ster/_/thugs rje'i bdag nyid ldan la rang gi don mdzad nas/_/gzhan gyi nyon mongs dra ba su yis 'byed par 'gyur/_/'khor ba'i rgya mtshor gzhol ba'i 'jig rten dag gi ni/_/zhi ba'i ched du ye shes dam pa su yis smra/_/shes 
[\[97a.3\]]pa med par gzhol ba'i 'jig rten dag gi ni/_/dge ba'i ched du ye shes dam pa su yis smra/_/ji ltar nyin mor byed pa 'od zer med pa dang /_/ji ltar chu bo sin d+hu 'bab pa med pa dang /_/sa bdag rgyal srid dang ni rnam par bral ba'am/_/de phyir 'jig rten 'jig rten mkhyen 
[\[97a.4\]]pas rnam par dman/_/blo yis rig pa bzhin du bde bas dbyangs bzhin du/_/dpal gyis mi skyong bzhin du bzod pas chos bzhin du/_/de bzhin skyes bu mchog gis rnam par dman pa ni/_/'gro ba de ni yod pa dang ni med pa nyid/_/ji ltar kha lo sgyur bas shing rta dor ba 
[\[97a.5\]]dang /_/skya ba 'dzin pas gru dang tshul gyis sde dang ni/_/tshong dpon gyis ni tshong pa sman pas nad pa ste/_/de bzhin 'jig rten bde bar gshegs pas rnam par sman/_/dbyar tha dang ni ston rar mkha' la sprin med na/_/rlung gis kyang ni mkha' la bsnyen pa med pa na/_/'tsho bar 
[\[97a.6\]]'dod pa rnams kyi sdug bsngal ji lta ba/_/de ltar thar 'dod rnams kyi nga ni nyam thag 'di/_/de ltar dam pa'i don mdzad dgra bcom yin na yang /_/byed pa shes pa nyid kyis bla ma la zhugs pa/_/de ni chags bral yin kyang srid pa'i skyon rnams dang /_/ston pa'i yon tan rnams 
[\[97a.7\]]ni mang po rnams smras so/_/de nas chags bral min rnams mchi ma 'thon pa ste/_/brtan pa btang nas dge slong tshogs ni mya ngan dang /_/'jig rten 'god pa'i bdag nyid can zhes byar bzung nas/_/bya ba'i 'khor lo rnams ni brtan las ma g.yos so/_/de nas dus kyi dbang gis 
[\[97b\]]
[\[97b.1\]]gyad rnams kyis thos nas/_/gus pa'i dbang gis skyen par 'thon zhing song ba ste/_/khra yi stobs kyis zil mnan khrung khrung rnams bzhin du/_/kyi hud 'o brgyal zhes ni nyam thag 'thon pa'o/_/mun pa rab tu rgyas phyir thub pa rab gzims pa/_/nyi ma 'od med bzhin du de 
[\[97b.2\]]rnams kyis mthong nas/_/seng ges khyu mchog rmugs tsha ba lang rnams bzhin du/_/gus pas drag tu cho nges btab cing ngus pa'o/_/chos dga' chos kyi bla ma rab tu zhi ba na/_/gus pas ji lta ba dang bsam pa ji lta bar/_/'o dod 'bod cing mchi mas mig bcom de 
[\[97b.3\]]rnams kyi/_/dpal ldan dam pa 'ga' zhig 'di ni smras pa'o/_/gang zhig gson pa'i 'jig rten gnyid tshe rab sad pa/_/'di de mthar thug gzims mal la ni gzims pa ste/_/chos ni rnam par 'dzin pa'i rgyal mtshan 'di de ni/_/mchod sbyin mtha' na dbang po'i rgyal 
[\[97b.4\]]mtshan bzhin du btul/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyi ma nges par blo gsal zhing /_/brtson 'grus tshad gdung ye shes 'od zer stong ldan pa/_/nub par gyur tshe mi shes mun pa rnams bcom nas/_/'jig rten dag na slar yang mun pa 'di mdzad do/_/'das dang ma 'ongs 
[\[97b.5\]]pa dang da ltar gzigs nas su/_/da ni nges par 'jig rten mig ni sgrib pa ste/_/sdug bsngal rgya mtsho chen po rgod pa'i dba' rlabs las/_/'dren mdzad zam pa 'di ni nges par de ni rgud/_/de ltar 'di na kha cig snying rje bar zlos shing /_/gzhan rnams shing rta'i rta rnams dud 
[\[97b.6\]]pa bzhin du sems/_/gzhan rnams skad bton gzhan rnams sa la 'gyel ba ste/_/bya ba de rnams de rnams sems can dbang gis byas/_/de nas dus su gser gyis spras shing sna tshogs pa/_/mche ba rin thang chen po longs ma spyad pa'i khyogs/_/gyad rnams ngu bzhin 
[\[97b.7\]]drang srong la ni glang chen gyi/_/lag pa dang mtshungs lag pa rnams ni btsugs pa'o/_/de nas dus dang mthun pa'i cho ga byas nas su/_/de la phreng ba yid 'ong rnam pa sna tshogs dang /_/dri mchog dam pa rnams kyis de rnams kyis mchod cing /_/de nas dga' dang gus pas 
[\[98a\]]
[\[98a.1\]]mtha' dag bzung ba'o/_/de nas shing gzhon lus can gzhon nu ma rnams kyis/_/gdu bu rab 'khrol zangs mdog lag pa rnams kyis ni/_/glog gi 'phan rnams dag gis sprin ni dkar po bzhin/_/'di la rin thang mchog gi bla re bzung ba'o/_/de bzhin 
[\[98a.2\]]mi rnams kha cig dag gis gdugs rnams ni/_/phreng ba dkar po ldan rnams kun nas bzung ba ste/_/kha cig dag gis gser gyi yo byad dang ldan pa'i/_/rnga yab dkar po rnams ni rnam par g.yabs pa'o/_/de nas khyu mchog rnams kyis bzhin du ngal ba yis/_/mig 
[\[98a.3\]]dmar gyad rnams dag gis khyogs ni bzung ba ste/_/sil snyan rnams ni rna ba'i yid du 'ong ba rnams/_/dbyar dus chu gter bzhin du mkha' la rab tu grags/_/phyogs kyi glang po'i dbang po rnams kyi bskul ba yis/_/mdzes pa dang ni rtsub 'gyur shing rnams las bzhin du/_/me tog 
[\[98a.4\]]pad+ma la sogs de rnams de rnams ni/_/lha yi me tog rnams ni lha nas lhung bar gyur/_/dbang po'i glang po las skyes glang po che rnams kyis/_/nor gnas snying po'i chu skyes pad+ma rnams dang ni/_/man dA ra yi me tog 'dab bcas chags pa rnams/_/chu thigs zer mas 'thor 
[\[98a.5\]]ba rnams kyis mngon par 'thor/_/de nas dga' ba'i tshal skyes lus mchog ldan pa yi/_/tsan+dan dmar po'i ro ni rnam par dul ba dang /_/'bad pas ma bsgrubs bgo ba rnams ni dkar po rnams/_/dri za'i bu mo rnams kyis yang dag 'thor ba'o/_/rab tu g.yo ba'i ba dan rnams ni 
[\[98a.6\]]bzung ba ste/_/phreng ba rnam pa sna tshogs rnams kyis bkram pa ste/_/sil snyan rgya chen rnams kyis rjes su 'gro ba ste/_/zhi ba'i ched du zhi ba'i lam las khyogs te drangs/_/gus pa dang ldan gyad rnams kyis de yongs bzung nas/_/thub pa'i mthu las lan brgyar mngon 
[\[98a.7\]]par phyag 'tshal zhing /_/de yi rnam par nyams las kun nas ngu ba ste/_/grong khyer gyi ni dbus nas yongs su drangs pa'o/_/klu yi sgo nas zhugs te phyi rol byung nas ni/_/chu bo gser ldan zhes pa yang dag rgal nas su/_/cod pan zhes bya'i mchod rten gyi ni rtsa ba ru/_/grags 
[\[98b\]]
[\[98b.1\]]pas bsags phyir bsags pa de rnams kyis byas so/_/de nas shun pa 'dab ma dri [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]a ka ru/_/tsan+dan e la klu rnams kyis bsags bsags pa ru/_/mya ngan las ni sbrul bzhin dbugs rgyu de rnams ni/_/brtan min mig can rnams kyis thub pa'i sku bzhus so/_/
[\[98b.2\]]de nas lan gsum bkod kyang me yi sgron ma yis/_/de tshe drang srong chen po'i bsags pa ma 'bar te/_/bag med ldan pa gzhu ni rnam par 'khrugs pa yis/_/mda' ni rang bzhin sa yi bdag po'i dpal bzhin no/_/lam dag tu ni rnam par 'ong ba'i 'od srungs ni/_/rnam 
[\[98b.3\]]par dag pa'i sems kyis sems ni gyur pa ste/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi sku gdung tshang ba lta 'dod de/_/gang gi mthu las me yi 'bar ba ma yin no/_/de nas de tshe bla ma blta ba'i don du ni/_/der ni dge slong skyen par nye bar song ba na/_/thub pa mchog la rab tu btud pa byas pa na/_/
[\[98b.4\]]de ma thag tu me ni rang nyid 'bar bar gyur/_/nyon mongs rnams kyis ma tshig thub pa'i sku gdung la/_/pags pa sha dang spu dang cha shas rnams tshig nas/_/mar dang rlung dang bud shing yongs su rgyas na yang /_/me yis rus pa tshig par nus pa ma yin no/_/de nas bdag nyid chen 
[\[98b.5\]]po 'das pa'i de rnams ni/_/chu mchog gis ni dang bar byas nas dus su ni/_/mngon par bstod cing gyad rnams dag gis grong khyer du/_/gser gyi bum pa rnams kyis rab tu spyan drangs so/_/'di ni ri bo chen po'i rin chen khams bzhin no/_/dge legs gang ba chen po rnams ni 
[\[98b.6\]]'dzin pa ste/_/mtho ris dag na lha yi gtso bo'i khams bzhin du/_/khams ni me yis log par sbyor ba ma yin nyid/_/byams pas yongs su rnam par bsgoms pa 'di rnams ni/_/'dod chags me yis tshig pas rnam par spangs pa ste/_/de yi gus pa'i dbang gis gzung 
[\[98b.7\]]ba'i gdung rus rnams/_/bsil ba yin kyang bdag cag rnams kyi yid rnams bsregs/_/sred pa 'joms mdzad gang gi mthu las khyab 'jug gi_/mkha' lding gis kyang khur bar nus pa ma yin pa/_/'jig rten dag na mtshungs med de yi rus pa rnams/_/mi rus gyur 
[\[99a\]]
[\[99a.1\]]pa kho bo cag gis khur bar gyis/_/e ma 'jig rten chos ni nges par stobs ldan te/_/chos la nus kyang nus pas gang gis ma bzlog cing /_/gang zhig snyan pas 'gro ba mtha' dag khyab pa ste/_/de yi sku yi cha shas 'di ni bum par gnas/_/gang zhig bdag gi snang bas nyi ma gzhan snang zhing /_/de yi 
[\[99a.2\]]sa ni snang bas rnam par mdzad pa ste/_/gser gyi mdog dang ldan pa de yi sku lus ni/_/me rnams kyis ni rus pa lhag mar byas pa'o/_/gang gis nyon mongs ri bo rgyas pa rnams phye ste/_/sdug bsngal thob pa na yang bstan pa ma phye zhing /_/sdug bsngal mtha' dag rnams ni bkag par gyur pa ste/_/drang srong de yi sku lus 
[\[99a.3\]]me yis bsregs pa'o/_/g.yul du dgra bo rnams kyis mchi ma phyung nas su/_/skyabs 'ongs rnams kyi mchi ma rnam par btul nas su/_/sdug pa na yang mchi ma bstan pas ma bton pa/_/lam du mchi ma 'thon zhing de rnams rnam par zlos/_/de rnams nga rgyal ldan zhing lag pa stobs dang ldan na yang /_/de ltar 
[\[99a.4\]]mya ngan byed cing nags bzhin grong khyer der zhugs shing /_/lam rnams su ni gdung de rnams ni skye bos mchod pa ste/_/mchod pa'i don du gzhal yas khang par mdzes par byas pa'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po las/_mya ngan las 'da' ba bsngags pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa bdun pa'o//_//
[\[99a.5\]]ji lta ba bzhin nyin zhag 'ga' zhig de rnams kyis/_/de rnams mchod pa dam pa rnams kyis mchod pa ste/_/chab 'og rgyal phran pho nya bdun rnams rim gyis ni/_/de yi ched du grong khyer der ni nye bar 'ongs/_/de nas de'i tshe gang rnams thos nas dus su ni/_/nga rgyal las dang de yi gus pa'i dbang las ni/_/
[\[99a.6\]]gyad rnams 'thab mo la yang nges par song ba ste/_/sbyin pa la ni yid rnams byas pa ma yin no/_/de nas de dag rnams kyis rnam gzhag 'di rtogs nas/_/rlung bdun bzhin du mi dbang bdun po de rnams ni/_/gyen du zhugs pa'i gang gA'i shugs 'dra stobs tshogs ni/_/shin tu drag po rnams kyis ku sha 
[\[99a.7\]]zhes byar 'ongs/_/de nas grong pa'i skye bo nags mtha' nas skyen par/_/mi skyong de dag rnams kyi mgyogs 'gro'i sgra yis ni/_/khros pa las ni mgo bo rnams la bzung ba bzhin/_/'jigs pas rgyas pa'i gdong rnams grong khyer la zhugs so/_/de nas mi skyong de rnams grong khyer de bkag nas/_/glang 
[\[99b\]]
[\[99b.1\]]po'i dbang po rnams bcings rtsed mo'i nags rnams su/_/gyad mchog rnams kyis mi mthun spyad du gyur pa rnams/_/rigs kyi rjes mthun cha lugs ldan rnams la gsos so/_/de nas mda' yab mdzes shing ring pa'i smin ma de/_/sgo yi mig ni rnam par btsums shing grong khyer de/_/mi dga' ba dang 'dus pa'i yan 
[\[99b.2\]]lag ma bzhin du/_/dpung pa'i khyogs ni bteg pa nyam thag par gyur to/_/rgyal po bdun po rnams ni bya ba gcig pa ste/_/dpal gyis 'bar zhing shugs kyis 'phro ba rnams kyis ni/_/mnyam du dus gcig la ni gza' bdun rnams kyi ni/_/'jigs pa dang bcas nam mkha' bzhin dus gyur to/_/
[\[99b.3\]]sna rnams gnyis 'thung myos pa'i chang dri rnams kyis dang /_/mig rnams dpung gis bsnun pa'i rdul rnams dag gis dang /_/rna ba rnams ni rta glang rnga yi dbyangs rnams kyis/_/mi mo'i skye bo yi yang der ni bcom par gyur/_/de nas skyogs rnams nang du khu ba 'bab pa ni/_/mdun du bdar rnams bsdogs byas 
[\[99b.4\]]rta pa'i tshogs rnams dang /_/glang po rnams kyis yongs bskor sgo ni phyed bkag ste/_/'gegs shing g.yul la phyogs rnams kyis byas so/_/de nas shag ti ral gri mda' lag grong khyer pa/_/skrag pas da ni dpa' bas ngo tsha btang ba rnams/_/khra rnams bzhin du dgra bo rnams la lta bzhin du/_/nye ba'i 'thab ra rnams su kun 
[\[99b.5\]]nas gnas pa'o/_/brtan min ldan pa kha cig 'o dod 'bod pa ste/_/de bzhin gzhan rnams tshogs nas dung ni rab tu sgrogs/_/drag tu dregs rnams kha cig rab tu 'phen pa ste/_/de bzhin gzhan rnams ral gri sngon po rnams 'debs so/_/de nas rgyal ba'i ched du gyad rnams 'thab 'dod cing /_/gyad rnams bzhin du mtshan rnams sgrogs 
[\[99b.6\]]pa mthong nas ni/_/g.yul 'gyed yan lag ma rnams dag gi sems rnams dang /_/sman rnams dang mnyam mchod yon rnams ni g.yos par gyur/_/g.yul der thog mar 'gro bar 'dod pa'i bu la ni/_/g.yul 'gyed yan lag ma rnams der ni yongs 'dar zhing /_/go cha bcings nas zhi ba'i shes pa byed pa ste/_/sems khral gdong 
[\[99b.7\]]rnams mchi ma bton pa ma yin no/_/sems khral gdong las gzhan rnams [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]mo bzhin du/_/khyim thab 'gro bar 'dod pa'i gzhu la 'phyangs pa ste/_/gang gi gdong ni 'thab la dpa' ba de mthong nas/_/bcings pa'i 'phro spong song ba ma yin gnas ma yin/_/bum pa'i nang du yang dag bkag pa'i sbrul rnams bzhin/_/stobs 
[\[100a\]]
[\[100a.1\]]kyis de ltar rab tu 'phro ba de rnams ni/_/de ltar rnam par gzhag pa'i gyad rnams mthong nas ni/_/mi dbang rnams kyis g.yul gyi ched du blo bsams so/_/shing rta glang po rta dang rkang thang dregs pa ni/_/g.yul la kun nas nye bar gzhol ba mthong nas ni/_/
[\[100a.2\]]mkhas pa nyid kyis dang ni mdza' bo nyid kyis ni/_/gnyis skyes bre bos tshig ni 'di skad ces smras so/_/g.yul du dgra bo rnams kyi srog dang dregs pa ni/_/khyed cag rnams kyi mda' yis 'joms par nus pa ste/_/rdzod pa tha dad gnas rnams bde bar 
[\[100a.3\]]mi nus na/_/pha rol bya ba gcig pa rnams ni smos ci dgos/_/ci ste kun nas bkag pas pha rol 'joms pa la/_/blo ni byas rnams de ni rnam par zlog mdzod dang /_/nyes pa med pa'i grong pa'i skye bo 'gegs pa yi/_/gtse bar byed pa de ni chos ni ci zhig yin/_/dong du zhugs 
[\[100a.4\]]pa'i nag po'i sbrul rnams bgrod pa ni/_/gcig la gnas pa rnams kyis dus su rmugs pa bzhin/_/bkag nas rgyal ba mtha' gcig yod pa ma yin zhing /_/brgya la bkag pa rnams kyis rgyal ba 'thob par 'gyur/_/grong gi nang du bkag pa'i gtad rnams 
[\[100a.5\]]zhugs pa rnams/_/yid dang ldan pa nyid las mi rnams kyis thos nas/_/bsreg bya rnams kyis 'thor ba'i chung ngu'i me bzhin du/_/gzi brjid dman kyang legs pa chen por 'gro bar 'gyur/_/chos kyi bdag nyid can rnams grong du bkag na yang /_/'joms 'dod 
[\[100a.6\]]thob pa rnams ni dka' thub [(]gyis [,]kyis [)]gtor te/_/dpung ni zad par gyur kyang kA sha'i grong khyer du/_/lag pa sgra byed chos kyi stobs las rnam par rgyal/_/snyan grags ched dam yul ni 'thob pa'i ched du sa/_/mtha' dag gang rnams kyis kyang mngon par thob pa ste/_/ba lang 
[\[100a.7\]]rnams kyis chu gnas nas chu 'thungs nas bzhin/_/bor nas nor 'dzin bdag po de rnams sa la song /_/de phyir chos dang don ni yang dag mthong nas su/_/khyed cag rnams kyis zhi bas 'bad pa mdzod cig dang /_/mda' yis 'pham pa rnams ni slar yang 'bar ba ste/_zhi bas 
[\[100b\]]
[\[100b.1\]]'pham pa rnams kyi rnam 'gyur yod ma yin/_/'di kun khyed cag rnams kyi tshad ma ma yin te/_/khyed kyi stobs kyi dgra yi stobs las 'gro bar nus/_/shAkya thub pa gang la mchod par blo yod na/_/de nyid kyi ni gsung la bzod pas bsnyen mdzod cig_/de skad ces sogs 'dod pa 
[\[100b.2\]]rnam par nges pa ni/_/bram ze'i drang pos dang ni rab tu dga' ba yis/_/rgyal po yin yang rjes su bstan nas legs pa des/_/dge legs smras shing de nas de rnams kyis smras so/_/dus dang sbyar te mkhas shing phan la bshes kyis ni/_/tshig gang 'di de khyed kyis rnam par smras pa ste/_/
[\[100b.3\]]chos la dga' ba nyid dang stobs la brten pa las/_/rgyal po rnams kyis bsam pa nges par shes par mdzod/_/'dod pa'i phyir dang khro phyir stobs phyir 'chi ba'i phyir/_/phal che bar ni bya bar song zhing 'gro ba ste/_/mngon pa'i nga rgyal dang bcas kho bo cag rnams kyis/_/sangs rgyas nyid la 
[\[100b.4\]]mchod pa la ni gzhu rnams bzung /_/nga rgyal don du mchod yon blangs la rtsod pa ni/_/byis pa skyong dang drag po dag gis khyab 'jug byung /_/nga rgyal spang phyir mchod pa'i don du nged rnams kyis/_/tshe rnams dag kyang 'dor bar mi bgyi gang las yin/_/bu mo la ni sa skyong dag gi rgyal 
[\[100b.5\]]po rnams/_/gtum po long byed rnams ni rtsod par song ba ste/_/'dod pa 'joms phyir mchod pa'i don du nged rnams kyis/_/tshe rnams dag kyang 'dor bar mi bgyid gang las yin/_/rgyal rigs zad phyir drang srong drag tu khros pa ni/_/ngan spong bu yis g.yul du mtshon rnams bzung 
[\[100b.6\]]ba ste/_/khro ba 'joms phyir mchod pa'i don du nged rnams kyis/_/tshe rnams dag kyang 'dor bar mi bgyid gang las yin/_/lha mo sI tar mngon brjod 'chi ba yongs bzung nas/_/srin po shin tu dregs pa yang ni nyams pa ste/_/yongs 'dzin spong phyir mchod pa don du nged rnams 
[\[100b.7\]]kyis/_/tshe rnams dag kyang 'dor bar mi bgyid gang las yin/_/e Di lam ni pa ka zhes ni dgra 'phel zhing /_/rgyal rigs bye ba rnams ni de ltar nyams pa ste/_/rmongs mun gtor phyir mchod pa'i don du nged rnams kyis/_/tshe rnams dag kyang 'dor bar mi bgyid gang las yin/_/
[\[101a\]]
[\[101a.1\]]de skad ces sogs 'jig rten dag na 'thab mo ni/_/skyon las skyes pa mang po rnams ni byung gyur te/_/ston pa bla na med pa'i gus pas mchi ba dang /_/bcas pa yin kyang 'thab mo mi bgyid gang las yin/_/de lta na yang bdag cag rnams kyi nges pa 'di/_/
[\[101a.2\]]khyed kyi pho nyar gyur cig skyen par gshegs mdzod la/_/ji ltar don ni 'thab mo min pas 'grub pa cig_/de ltar bdag nyid kun gyis 'bad pa gyis mdzod cig_/khyed kyis chos ldan tshig rnams kyis ni nged cag rnams/_/'thab par 'dod cing mda' yi mche ba rnon po can/_/
[\[101a.3\]]nang du rab tu 'phel ba'i dug ni 'thungs pa rnams/_/sngags rnams kyis ni sbrul rnams bzhin du bkag pa'o/_/de ltar bgyi zhes rgyal po rnams kyi tshig bzung nas/_/bram ze de ni grong khyer de la rab zhugs shing /_/ji ltar rim gyis gyad rnams dag ni mthong ba ste/_/mthong nas 
[\[101a.4\]]dus su tshig ni 'di skad ces smras so/_/gzhu rnams lag na gnas pa'i mi dbang 'di rnams ni/_/nyi ltar byi dor go cha 'bar rnams kyis/_/khyed cag rnams kyis grong 'dir nges par ro myang zhing /_/seng ge bzhin du rnam par 'gying zhing gnas pa'o/_/de rnams kyi ni shubs su bkod 
[\[101a.5\]]pa'i ral gri dang /_/gser gyi rgyab ldan gzhu rnams nges par rtogs pa ste/_/grub pa'i chos dran nas ni chos min gyis 'jigs shing /_/zhu ba gsol bar byed de g.yul la 'jigs rnams min/_/de rnams mngon par 'ongs rnams sa yi phyir min zhing /_/nor phyir ma yin 
[\[101a.6\]]rgyags phyir ma yin dgra phyir min/_/thub pa'i gus pas nged cag rnams kyi zhe sa 'di/_/de phyir khyed cag rnams kyi bya bas bkur sti mdzad/_/thub pa de la khyed cag gi dang nged cag gi_/bla ma nyid du mnyam ste 'di las ngal ba 'di/_/de phyir spun gyi tshogs ni 'dus nas 
[\[101a.7\]]'ongs pa ste/_/bla ma'i gdung rnams mchod pa la ni bya ba gcig_/ji ltar chos kyi cho ga la ni ser sna nyid/_/nor rnams la ni ser sna de ltar skyon ma yin/_/ser sna smra ba nges pa nyid du skyon yin zhing /_/skyon ni chos kyi dgra bor gyur pa nyid yin no/_/gal te mi sbyin par ni khyed 
[\[101b\]]
[\[101b.1\]]rnams kyis ngas na/_/rdzong nas 'thon nas 'gron po rnams la bsnyen par mdzod/_/gang rnams kyis stobs mnga' rnams ma yin sgo glegs rnams/_/de rnams rigs su skyes pa'i rgyal rigs ma yin no/_/byams pa dang ni gzi brjid ston par byed bzhin du/_/mi dbang rnams 
[\[101b.2\]]kyis khyed cag rnams la de skad smras/_/bdag gi smra 'dod gang yang rang gis rtogs nas su/_/dga' las bdag gi de ni khyed cag rnams kyis nyon/_/'ga' zhig rtsod pa dge ba ma yin chos min te/_/rnam par 'gras par ma 'gro zhi ba bsnyen par mdzod/_/thub pa des ni bzod 
[\[101b.3\]]pa rab tu bsngags pa ste/_/gang gis gus pas me ni kun nas 'phel bar 'gyur/_/nor dang 'dod pa dag gi gcig gi ched du ni/_/rnam par rtsod pa tshul ni nye bar 'gyur ba ste/_/chos kyi ched du slar yang 'phags par gyur pa ni/_/zhi ba dang ni dgra bo rnam par rtsod par smra/_/gang zhig nye 
[\[101b.4\]]bar zhi zhing byams pa'i thugs kyis ni/_/sems can thams cad rnams la brtse ba gsungs pa ste/_/thugs rje chen po mnga' ba de la mchod pa ni/_/'tshe ba byas nas bdag gi lugs dang mtshungs ma yin/_/de phyir gdung ni rab tu sbyin las 'di rnams la/_/grags pa dang ni chos kyis 
[\[101b.5\]]yang dag bgo ba mdzod/_/de ltar gyur na khyed kyis rtsod min de dag dang /_/de dag gis kyang chos dang grags pa 'thob par 'gyur/_/chos ldan skyes bu rnams kyis chos las g.yos pa ni/_/chos la 'bad pa las kyang nged rnams kyis sbyor na/_/gang bdag rnams kyis gzhan dag chos la sbyor 
[\[101b.6\]]byed pa/_/de dag rnams kyis chos ni gnas pa'i ched du 'jug_/drang srong dam pa mchog gis chos kyi sbyin pa ni/_/sbyin pa thams cad kyi ni khyad par 'phags par gsungs/_/gang zhig la las nor la sbyor bar byed pa ste/_/chos kyi sbyin pa po ni rnam par bsags pa yin/_/de nas 
[\[101b.7\]]snyan grags dang ni bde ba 'dren pa dang /_/chos kyi tshig ni de las nye bar thob nas ni/_/ngo tsha dang bcas phan tshun lta ba de rnams kyis/_/bre bo'i rigs mnyam bram ze la ni smras pa'o/_/e ma mdza' bo'i dge la khyod kyis nges pa ste/_/yon tan rnams la bram ze'i rjes su mthun pas 
[\[102a\]]
[\[102a.1\]]chags/_/lam gzhan gyis ni 'khrul ba'i rta ngan rnams bzhin du/_/gang gis legs par bsnyen pa'i lam la bkod par 'gyur/_/ji ltar smras pa de ltar de ni ldan 'gyur zhing /_/brtse zhing mnyen pa'i tshig 'di 'dzin par rigs pa ste/_/mdza' bo'i skye bo'i tshig la 
[\[102a.2\]]shin tu 'das nas su/_/phyi nas sdug bsngal nyid du lhung rnams mya ngan 'gyur/_/de nas 'gro ba'i khams rig gdung ni gyad rnams kyis/_/de rnams brgyad du gus pas yon tan las bgos so/_/de nas rang nyid kyis ni cha shas bzung nas ni/_/bdun po so so so sor gzhan rnams la byin to/_/
[\[102a.3\]]gyad rnams dag gis mngon mchod sa skyong de rnams kyang /_/dga' zhing don byas rnams ni rang yul rnams su gshegs/_/de nas drang srong gis gdung de rnams cho ga bzhin/_/rang grong rnams su mchod rten rab gnas rnams de byas/_/de nas rang gi yul du thub pa'i mchod rten ni/_/byed 'dod 
[\[102a.4\]]bre bos so sor bgos pa'i bum pa blangs/_/sol ba lhag ma rnams kyang pi sa la zhes pa'i/_/gus pas rjes su rab ldan rnams kyis bslangs par gyur/_/de nas gdung gi snying po can gyi sngon du brgyad/_/brag ri dkar po dang mtshungs mchod rten rnams gyur zhing /_/de yi bum pas 
[\[102a.5\]]bsnyen pa bram ze'i dgu pa ste/_/sol ba'i snying po dang ldan bcu pa byung bar gyur/_/thub pa'i mchod rten sna tshogs ba dan g.yo ba rnams/_/ti se'i gangs ri'i rtse mo dang mtshungs de rnams la/_/rang gi skye bo dang bcas mi skyong de rnams dang /_/skye dgu dang bcas bram ze rnams kyis 
[\[102a.6\]]sa na mchod/_/phreng ba mngon sbyar rnams dang dri [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]mchog rnams dang /_/phreng ba dge ba rnams dang sil snyan dbyangs rnams kyis/_/mchod pa dam pa mi skyong de rnams de rnams kyis/_/rgyal ba'i gdung ni 'dzin pa de rnams la byas so/_/de nas dus su dgra bcom rnams kyi lnga brgya 
[\[102a.7\]]rnams/_/ri bo lngas mtshan grong khyer du ni yongs 'dus nas/_/slar yang ji lta ba bzhin chos ni gnas pa'i phyir/_/thub pa'i gsung ni ri yi logs su yang dag bsdus/_/gnas skabs kun du drang srong che las thos pa ni/_/kun dga' bo nyid yin zhes nges par gyur pa yi/_/dge 
[\[102b\]]
[\[102b.1\]]slong de rnams kyis ni 'dus pa mthun pa ru/_/gsung bar zhu zhes lus 'phags thub pa la zhus so/_/de nas de dag rnams kyi dbus su de bzhugs nas/_/smra ba rnams kyi mchog gis ji ltar gsungs pa'i tshig_/gang du gang la gang gi tshe na gang zhig dang /_/'di skad bdag 
[\[102b.2\]]gis 'di ni thos zhes gsungs par gyur/_/de phyir dgra bcom rnams kyis rjes su bcings pa des/_/thub pa chen po'i chos kyi bstan bcos gnas pa ste/_/gang zhig 'bad pas yang dag rab tu thob pa ni/_/mya ngan 'das shing 'da' la mya ngan 'da' bar 'gyur/_/dgra bo khengs 
[\[102b.3\]]pa rnams kyi mya ngan ster ba po/_/skye dgu sdug bsngal rnams kyi mya ngan gcod pa po/_/me tog 'bras ldan mya ngan med ltar blta na sdug_/dus su de gus sa bdag mya ngan med pa byung /_/'phags pa mau rgya'i dpal des skye dgur phan don du/_/'jig rten kun la mchod rten mtshan ma 
[\[102b.4\]]byed du bcug_/gtum po mya ngan med nyid thob nas sa la ni/_/chos rgyal mya ngan med nyid las de thob par gyur/_/mngon du byas pa'i mchod rten bdun po de rnams las/_/drang srong de yi gdung rnams mau rgya des blangs nas/_/dpal ldan mchod rten ston ka'i sprin gyi 'od rnams kyi/_/
[\[102b.5\]]stong phrag brgyad cur babs pa dus su zhag gis byas/_/dga' byed grong gnas mchod rten brgyad pa dang po ni/_/de yi dus su gus ldan klu rnams kyis bsrungs te/_/de phyir gdung rnams thob pa ma yin rgyal po des/_/der byas pa la ma dad rgya chen gyur pa'o/_/de'i phyir yid kyis 
[\[102b.6\]]dgra bo 'bras bu rnams la ni/_/kun nas gnas shing g.yo ba'i dpal ni bsrungs na yang /_/mi skyong des ni ngur smrig gon pa las gzhan du/_/lta ba rnam par dag cing 'bras bu dang po thob/_/de las gzhan du gzhan gyis thub pa'i mchod pa ni/_/gang gis byas shing byed la byed 
[\[102b.7\]]par 'gyur ba ste/_/dam pa'i skye bos bsnyen pa'i 'bras bu bla na med/_/de yis thob cing 'thob la 'thob par 'gyur ba nyid/_/drang srong bzhugs pa la ni mchod pa sbyar nas sam/_/yongs su mya ngan 'das pa'i gdung la phyag byas nas/_/yid kyi dang ba mnyam na 'bras bu mnyam pa 
[\[103a\]]
[\[103a.1\]]ste/_/sangs rgyas yon tan rnams ni mkhas rnams shes par mdzod/_/de phyir rtag tu mchog gi chos ni mkhyen pa'o/_/mchog tu mi 'gyur 'pho ba med pa brnyes ldan pa/_/brtse ba dang bcas thub pa blo gros chen po ni/_/mchod 'os mchog la mchod la dam 
[\[103a.2\]]pa 'ongs pa'o/_/mchog tu thugs rje dang ldan mchog gi thugs mkhyen pa/_/gzhan la phan phyir mchog tu ngal bar byon pa de/_/mdzad pa mkhyen pa nyid kyis dam pa mkhas rnams kyi/_/'di na gtang rag mchod pa gang phyir 'os ma yin/_/sa na 'chi dang rga ba dang mnyam 
[\[103a.3\]]'jigs pa dang /_/mtho ris dag na 'pho ba dang mnyam yod min te/_/'gro ba'i de gnyis mkhyen nas gang zhig gnas pa ste/_/de phyir mchod par bya ba dam pa su 'dra yod/_/skye ba yod na bde ba min pa 'byung ba ste/_/yang srid med las shin tu bde bas bde ba med/_/de 
[\[103a.4\]]ni brnyes shing gang gis 'gro la byin pa ste/_/de'i phyir mchod par bya ba dam pa su 'dra yod/_/de ltar thub pa'i skyes mchog de yi gus pa yis/_/mkhas nyid yon tan las min snyan dngags nus pas min/_/thub pa'i lung gi rjes su 'brangs nas 'di brtsams te/_/skye dgu 
[\[103a.5\]]rnams kyi phan dang bde ba'i phyir gyur cig_/yul shA se ta ka'i gser mig ma'i bu dge slong slob dpon snyan dngags mkhan chen po/_/smra ba dang ldan pa phyogs su rnam par grags pa/_btsun pa rta dbyangs kyis mdzad pa'i/_sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa zhes bya ba'i snyan dngags chen po 
[\[103a.6\]]las sku gdung rnam par bgo ba'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa brgyad pa yongs su rdzogs so//_//sa stengs rgya mtsho'i mthar thug bstan pa'i bdag po mchog_/yon tan mtshungs med phul byung du ma'i gter chen po/_/mkhas pa rnams kyi bla ma skye dgu rnams kyi dpal/_/
[\[103a.7\]]bla ma dam pa chos kyi rgyal po'i bka' lung gis/_/'jig rten kun na zla bral skyes mchog de nyid kyi/_/phrin las sgrub pa snye ba'i thugs dgongs rnam dag dang /_/mi rje lha sras gung thang rgyal mo yum sras dang /_/chos la gcig tu dkar ba'i lha gcig kun dga' 'bum/_/mang yul 
[\[103b\]]
[\[103b.1\]]skyi rong bzang mo yon tan skyid la sogs/_/chos ldan dam pa rnams kyis sbyin bdag bgyis pa'i ngor/_/'phags pa rta dbyangs kyis mdzad thub pa'i mdzad pa 'di/_/sa dbang bzang po dang ni blo gros rgyal pos bsgyur/_/'di bsgyur bsod nams rgyal po gang des pha ma dang /_/'gro 
[\[103b.2\]]rnams lam gyi rgyal po dam pa 'dir zhugs te/_/nyes tshogs tshang tshing kun dang bdud bzhi'i rgyal po rnams/_/bcom nas chos kyi rgyal po dam pa thob par shog/_//[
